vistīrṇe madhyame kūṭe hemadhā tuvibūṣite।
varadaḥ sevyate sirddhairlokakarttā sanātanaḥ ॥ 1.41.50 ॥
tasyaivābyantare kūṭe nānādhātuvibhūṣite।
taṭe niṣadhakūṭasya ślakṣṇacāruśilātale ॥ 1.41.51 ॥
rukmakāñcananiryūhaṃ taptakāñcanatoraṇam।
harmyaprāsādabaddhañca muditañcātivistaram ॥ 1.41.53 ॥
udyānamālākalitaṃ triṃśadyojanamāyatam।
duḥprasahyamamitraistat pūrṇamāśīviṣopamaiḥ।
ulaṅghīnāṃ pramuditaṃ rakṣasāṃ rākṣasaṃ puram ॥ 1.41.54 ॥
tasyaiva dakṣiṇe pārśve naikadaityagaṇālaye।
guhāpraveśaṃ nagaraṃ śailakukṣau durāsadam ॥ 1.41.55 ॥
tathaiva paścime kūṭe pārijātaśiloccaye।
devadānavabhāgānāṃ samṛddhāni purāṇi tu ॥ 1.41.56 ॥
tatra somaśilā nāma girestasya mahātaṭe।
somo yatrāvatarati sadā parvasu parvasu ॥ 1.41.57 ॥
upāsate'tra śrīmantaṃ tārā patimaninditam।
ṛṣikinnaragandharvāḥ sākṣāddevaṃ tamonudam ॥ 1.41.58 ॥
tatraiva cottare kūṭe brahmapārśvamiti smṛtam।
sthānaṃ tatra sureśasya brahmaṇaḥ prathitaṃ divi ॥ 1.41.59 ॥
ijyāpūjānamaskāraistatra siddhāḥ svayambhuvam।
tathaivāyatanaṃ vahneḥ sarvalokeṣu viśrutam।
tatra vigrahavān vāhniḥ sevyate siddhacāraṇaiḥ ॥ 1.41.61 ॥
tathaiva cottare ramye triśrṛṅge varaparvate।
ṛṣisiddhānucarite nānābhūtagaṇālaye।
puraṃ tat triṣu lokeṣu hemacitrantu viśrutam ॥ 1.41.62 ॥
trayāṇāṃ devamukhyānāṃ trīṇyevāyatanāni ca।
nārāyaṇasyāyatanaṃ pūrvakūṭe dvijottamāḥ ।
madhyame brahmaṇaḥ sthānaṃ śaṅkarasya tu paścime ॥ 1.41.63 ॥
daityadānavagandharvairyakṣarākṣasapannagaiḥ।
ijānā abhipūjyante devadevā mahābalāḥ ॥ 1.41.64 ॥
tathā purāṇi ramyāṇi deśe caiva vkacit vkacit।
tathaiva cottare deśe cārudhau devaparvate।
anekaśrṛṅgakalite siddhasādhu niṣevite ॥ 1.41.66 ॥
nāgānāṃ rākṣasānāñca daityānāñca mahābale ॥ 1.41.67 ॥
kūṭe tu madhyame tasya siddhasaṅgha niṣevite।
ramye devarṣicarite ratnadhātuvibhūṣite ॥ 1.41.68 ॥
pajhotpalavanaiḥ phullaiḥ saugandhikavanaistathā।
tathā kumudakhaṇḍaiśca vikacairupaśobhite ॥ 1.41.69 ॥
vihaṅgasaṅghasaṃghuṣṭaṃ nānāsatvaniṣevitam।
cārutīrthasusambādhaṃ triṃśadyojanamaṇḍalam ॥ 1.41.71 ॥
siddhairupaspṛṣṭajalaṃ jaladoṣavivarjitam।
tatrānandajalannāma mahāpuṇyajalaṃ saraḥ ॥ 1.41.72 ॥
tatra nāgapati ścaṇḍa ścaṇḍo nāma durāsadaḥ।
śataśīrṣā mahābhāgo viṣṇucakrāṅkacihnitaḥ।
ityevamaṣṭau vijñeyā vicitrā devaparvatāḥ ॥ 1.41.73 ॥।
purairāyatanaiḥ puṇyaiḥ puṇyodaiśca sarovaraiḥ।
suvarṇaparvatairnaikaistathā rajataparvataiḥ ॥ 1.41.74 ॥
nānāratnaprabhāsaiśca naikaiśca maṇiparvataiḥ।
haritālaparvatairnaikaistathā hiṅgulakāñcanaiḥ ॥ 1.41.75 ॥
śuddhairmanaḥ śilājālairbhāsvarairaruṇaprabhaiḥ ।
nānādhātuvicitraiśca naikaiśca maṇiparvataiḥ ॥ 1.41.76 ॥
pūrṇā vasumatī sarvā giribhirnaikavistaraiḥ।
nadīkandaraśailādyairanekaiścitrasānubhiḥ ॥ 1.41.77 ॥
ityevamacalairyuktairdaityarākṣasasādhubhiḥ।
kinnaroragagandharvairvicitraiḥ siddhacāraṇaiḥ ॥ 1.41.78 ॥
gandharvairapsarobhiśca sevitā naika vistarāḥ।
puṇyakṛdbhiḥ samākīrṇā kesarākṛtayo nagāḥ ॥ 1.41.79 ॥
girijālantu tanmeroḥ siddhalokamiti smṛtam।
citraṃ nānāśrayopetaṃ pracāraṃ sukṛtātmanām ॥ 1.41.80 ॥
nātyugrakarmasiddhānāṃ pratimā madhyamāḥ smṛtāḥ।
sa hi svarga iti khyātaḥ kramastveṣa prakīrtitaḥ ॥ 1.41.81 ॥
caturmahādvīpavatī seyamurvī prakīrtitā ।।
nānāvarṇapramāṇairhi nānāvarṇabalaistathā ॥ 1.41.82 ॥
nānābhakṣyānnapānaiśca nānācchādanabhūṣaṇaiḥ।
prajāvikārairvividhaiścitrairadhyuṣitaiḥ saha॥ 1.41.83 ॥
catvāro naikavarṇādyā mahādvīpāḥ pariśrutāḥ।
saiṣā caturmahādvīpā nānādvīpasamākulā।
pṛthivī kīrtitā kṛtsnā padmākārā mayā dvijāḥ ॥ 1.41.85 ॥
padmetyabhihitā kṛtsnā pṛthivī bahuvistarā ॥ 1.41.86 ॥
sabrahmasadanaṃ lokaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣam।
trilokamiti vikhyātaṃ yatsattvairvyavahāryate ॥ 1.41.87 ॥
candrādityāvataptaṃ yattajjagat parigīyate।
gandhavarṇarasopetaṃ śabdasparśaguṇānvitam ॥ 1.41.88 ॥
taṃ lokapadmaṃ śrutibhiḥ padmamityabhidhīyate।
eṣa sarvapurāṇeṣu kramaḥ supariniścitaḥ ॥ 1.41.89 ॥


## 1.42

।।sūta uvāca।।
sarovarebhyaḥ puṇyodā deva nadyo vinirgatāḥ।

ākāśāmbhonidheryo'sau soma ityabhidhīyate।

saptamenānilapathā prayātā vimalodakā ॥ 1.42.3 ॥


māhendreṇa gajendreṇa ākāśapathayāyinā।
krīḍitā hyantaratale yā sā vikṣobhitodakā ॥ 1.42.5 ॥

naikairvimānasaṅghātaiḥ prakrāmadbhirnabhastalam।
siddhairupaspṛṣṭajalā mahāpuṇyajalā śivā ॥ 1.42.6 ॥

vāyunā preryamāṇā ca anekābhogagāminī।
parivarttatyaharaharyathā sūryastathaiva sā ॥ 1.42.7 ॥

vegena kurvatī meruṃ sā prayātā pradakṣiṇam ॥ 1.42.8 ॥

vibhidyamānā salilaistaijasenānilena ca।
meroruttarakūṭeṣu patitātha caturṣvapi ॥ 1.42.9 ॥

vikīryamāṇa salilā caturddhāsaṃsṛtodakā ॥ 1.42.10 ॥

ṣaṣṭiyojanasāhasraṃ nirālambanamambaram।
nipapāta mahābhāga merostasya caturddiśam ॥ 1.42.11 ॥

sā caturṣvabhitaścaiva mahāpādeṣu śobhanā।
puṇyā mandarapūrveṇa patitā hi mahānadī ॥ 1.42.12 ॥

pūrveṇāṃśena devānāṃ sarvasiddhagaṇālayam।
suvarṇacitrakaṭakaṃ naikanirjharakandaram ॥ 1.42.13 ॥

plāvayantī saśailendraṃ mandarañcārukandaram।
vaprapratāpaśamanairanekaiḥ sphāṭikodakaiḥ ॥ 1.42.14 ॥

tathā caitrarathaṃ ramyaṃ plāvayantī pradakṣiṇam।
praviṣṭā hyambaranadī hyaruṇodasarovaram ॥ 1.42.15 ॥

aruṇodānnivṛttā'tha śītānte ramyanirjhare।
śaile siddhagaṇāvāse nipapāta sugāminī ॥ 1.42.16 ॥

sītā nāma mahā puṇyā nadīnāṃ pravarā nadī।
sā nikuñjaniruddhā tu anekābhogagāminī ॥ 1.42.17 ॥

śītāntāśikharādbhraṣṭā sukuñje varaparvate।
nipapāta mahābhāgā tasmādapi sumañjasam ॥ 1.42.18 ॥

tasmānmālyavataṃ śailaṃ bhāvayantī varāpagā।
vaikaṅkaṃ samanuprāptā vaikaṅkānmaṇiparvatam।
maṇiparvatānmahāśailaṃ ṛṣabhaṃ naikakandaram ॥ 1.42.19 ॥

patitā'tha mahāśaile jaṭhare siddhasevite ॥ 1.42.20 ॥

tasmādapi mahāśailaṃ devakūṭaṃ taraṅgiṇī।
tasya kukṣisamudrāntā krameṇa pṛthivīṃ gatā ॥ 1.42.21 ॥


srāvayantī mahābhāgā visphāreṣvavalīkadā।
nadī sahasrānugatā pravṛttā ca mahānadī ॥ 1.42.23 ॥

bhadrāśvaṃ sumahādvīpaṃ plāvayantī varāpagā।
praviṣṭā hyarṇavaṃ pūrvaṃ pūrve dvīpe mahānadī ॥ 1.42.24 ॥

dakṣiṇe'pi prapannā yā śailendre gandhamādane।
citraiḥ prapātairvidhairnaikavisphālitodakā ॥ 1.42.25 ॥

tadghandhamādanavanaṃ nandanaṃ devanandanam।
plāvayantī mahābhāgā prayātā sā pradakṣiṇam ॥ 1.42.26 ॥

nāmnā hyalakanandeti sarvalokeṣu viśrutā।
praviśatyuttarasaro mānasaṃ devamānasam ॥ 1.42.27 ॥

trikūṭācchailaśikharāt kaliṅgaśikharaṃ gatā ॥ 1.42.28 ॥

kaliṅgaśikharādbhraṣṭā rucake nipapāta sā।
rucakānniṣadhaṃ prāptā tāmrābhaṃ niṣadhādapi ॥ 1.42.29 ॥

tāmrābhaśikharārdbhraṣṭā gatā śvetodaraṃ girim ।
tasmātsumūlaṃ śailendraṃ vasudhārañca parvatam ॥ 1.42.30 ॥

hemakūṭaṃ gatā tasmāddevaśrṛṅge tato gatā।
tasmādgatā mahāśailaṃ tataścāpi piśācakam ॥ 1.42.31 ॥

piśācakācchailavarāt pañcakūṭaṅgatā punaḥ।
pañcakūṭāttu kailāsaṃ devāvāsaṃ śiloccayam ॥ 1.42.32 ॥

tasya kukṣiṣu vibhrāntā naikakandarasānuṣu।
himavatyuttamanadī nipapātācalottame ॥ 1.42.33 ॥

sthalīśatānyanekāni plāvayantyāśugāṇinī ॥ 1.42.34 ॥

srāvayantī mahābhāgā prayātā dakṣiṇodadhim ॥ 1.42.35 ॥

ramyā yojanavistīrṇā śailakukṣiṣu saṃvṛtā।

pāvanī dvijaśārdūla ghorāṇāmapi pāpmanām।
śaṅkarasyāṅkasaṃsparśānmahādevasya dhīmataḥ।
dviguṇaṃ pavitrasalilā sarvaloke mahānadī ॥ 1.42.37 ॥

anuśailaṃ samantācca nirgatā bahubhirmukhaiḥ।
atho'nyenābhidhānena khyātā nadyaḥ sahasraśaḥ ॥ 1.42.38 ॥

tasmāddhimavato gaṅgā gatā sā tu mahānadī।
evaṃ gaṅgeti nāmnādiprakāśā siddhasevitā ॥ 1.42.39 ॥

dhanyāste sattamā deśā yatra gaṅgā mahānadī।
rudrasāddhyānilādityairjuṣṭatoyā yaśasvatī ॥ 1.42.40 ॥

mahāpādaṃ pravakṣyāmi merorapi hi paścimam।

vipulaṃ śailarājānaṃ vipulodarakandaram ।
nitambakuñja kaṭakairvimalairmmaṇḍitodaram ॥ 1.42.42 ॥

api yā tryambakasyaiṣā tridaśaiḥ sevitodakā।
vāyuvegā gatābhogā lateva bhrāmitā punaḥ ॥ 1.42.43 ॥

merukūṭataṭādbhraṣṭā prahataiḥ svāditodakā।
vistīryyamāṇasalilā nirmmalāṃśukasannibhā ॥ 1.42.44 ॥

pradakṣiṇamathāvṛtya patitā sā tu gāminī ॥ 1.42.45 ॥

devabhrājaṃ mahābhrājaṃ savaibhrājaṃ mahāvanam।
plāvayantī mahābhāgā nānāpuṣpaphalodakā ॥ 1.42.46 ॥

pradakṣiṇaṃ prakurvāṇā nānāvanavibhūṣitā।
praviṣṭā paśvimasaraḥ sitodaṃ vimalodakam ॥ 1.42.47 ॥

sā sitodādviniṣkrāntā supakṣaṃ parvvataṃ gatā।
supakṣatastu puṇyodāttato devarṣisevitā ॥ 1.42.48 ॥

supakṣakūṭataṭagā tasmācca saṃśitodakā।
nipapāta mahābhāgā ramaṇyaṃ śikhiparvvatam ॥ 1.42.49 ॥

śikheśca parvatāt kaṅkaṃ kaṅkādvaiḍhūryyaparvvatam।
vaiḍhūryyāt kapilaṃ śailaṃ tasmācca gandhamādanam ॥ 1.42.50 ॥

tasmādgirivarāt prāptā piñjaraṃ varaparvvatam।
piñjarāt sarasaṃ yātā tasmācca kumudācalam ॥ 1.42.51 ॥

madhumantaṃ janañcaiva mukuṭañca śiloccayam।
mukuṭācchailaśikharāt kṛṣṇaṃ yātā mahāgirim ॥ 1.42.52 ॥

kṛṣṇāt śvetaṃ mahāśailaṃ mahānaganiṣevitam।
śvetāt sahasraśikharaṃ śailendraṃ patitā punaḥ ॥ 1.42.53 ॥

anekābhiḥ sravantībhirāpyāyitajalā śivā ।
pārijāte mahāśaile nipapātāśugāminī ॥ 1.42.54 ॥

anekanirjharanadī guhāsānuṣu rājate।
tasya kukṣiṣvanekāsu bhrāntatoyā taraṅgiṇī ॥ 1.42.55 ॥

vyāhanyamānasaṃvegā gaṇḍaśailairanekaśaḥ।
saṃvidyamānasalilā gatā ca dharaṇītale ॥ 1.42.56 ॥

ketumālaṃ mahādvīpaṃ nānāmleccha gaṇairyutam ।
plāvayantī mahābhāgā prayātā paścimārṇavam ॥ 1.42.57 ॥

suvarṇacitrapārśve tu supārśve'pyuttare girau ।
merościtramahāpāde mahāsattvaniṣevite ॥ 1.42.58 ॥

merukūṭataṭādbhraṣṭā pavaneneritodakā।
anekābhogavakrāṅgī kṣipyamāṇā nabhastale ॥ 1.42.59 ॥

ṣaṣṭiyojanasāhasre nirālambe'mbare śubhe।
vikīryamāṇā māleva nipapāta mahānadī ॥ 1.42.60 ॥

evaṃ kūṭataṭairbhraṣṭā naikairdevarṣisevitaiḥ।

nānāratnavanoddeśamaraṇyaṃ saviturvvanam।
mahāvanaṃ mahābhāgā plāvayantī pradakṣiṇam ॥ 1.42.62 ॥

tatrāviveśa kalyāṇī mahābhadraṃ sitodakā ॥ 1.42.63 ॥

bhadrasometi nāmnā hi mahāpārā mahājavā।
mahānadī mahāpuṇyā mahābhadrā vinirgatā ॥ 1.42.64 ॥

naikanirjharavaprāḍhyā śaṃkhakūṭataṭe tu sā।
tatra kūṭe giritaṭe nipapātāśugāminī ॥ 1.42.65 ॥

śaṃkhakūṭataṭādbhraṣṭā papāta vṛṣaparvvatam।
vṛṣaparvvatādvatsagiriṃ nāgaśailaṃ tato gatā ॥ 1.42.66 ॥

tasmānnīlaṃ nagaśreṣṭhaṃ saṃprāptā varṣaparvvatam।
nīlāt kapiñjalañcaiva indranīlaṃ ca nimnagā ॥ 1.42.67 ॥

tataḥ paraṃ mahānīlaṃ hemaśrṛṅgañca sā yayau ।
hemaśrṛṅgādgatā śvetaṃ śvetācca sunagaṃ yayau ॥ 1.42.68 ॥

sunagāt śataśrṛṅgañca saṃprāptā sā mahānadī।
śataśrṛṅgānmahāśailaṃ puṣkaraṃ puṣpamaṇḍitam ॥ 1.42.69 ॥

puṣkarācca mahāśailaṃ dvirājaṃ sumahābalam ।
varāhaparvvataṃ tasmānmayūrañca śiloccayam ॥ 1.42.70 ॥

mayūrāccaikaśikharaṃ kandarodaramaṇḍitam ।
jārudhiṃ śaila śikharaṃ nipapātāśugāminī ॥ 1.42.71 ॥

evaṃ girisahasrāṇi dārayantī mahānadī।
triśrṛṅgaṃ śrṛṅgakalilaṃ maryādāparvataṃ gatā ॥ 1.42.72 ॥

triśrṛṅgataṭavibhraṣṭā mahābhāganiṣevitā।
merukūṭataṭādbhraṣṭā pavaneneritodakā ॥ 1.42.73 ॥

viruddhaṃ parvatavaraṃ papāta vimalodakā।
plāvayantī mahābhāgā prayātā paścimārṇavam ॥ 1.42.74 ॥

suvarṇabhuvi pārśve tu supārśvepyuttare girau ।।

merośvitre mahāpāde mahāsattvaniṣevite ॥ 1.42.75 ॥

kandarodaravibhraṣṭā tasmādapi taraṅgiṇī।
naikabhogā papātorvīṃ citrapuṣpoḍhupotkacā ॥ 1.42.76 ॥

plāvayantī pramuditā uttarān sā kurūn śivā।
mahādvīpasya madhyena prayātā sottarārṇavam ॥ 1.42.77 ॥

evaṃ tāstu mahānadyaścatasro vimalodakāḥ।
mahāgiritaṭabhraṣṭāḥ saṃprayātāścaturdiśam ॥ 1.42.78 ॥

tatseyaṃ kathitaprāyā pṛthivī bahuvistarā।
meruśailamahākīrṇā'viśacca sarvatodiśam ॥ 1.42.79 ॥

caturmahādvīpavatī caturākrīḍakānanā।
catuṣketumahāvṛkṣā caturvarasarasvatī ॥ 1.42.80 ॥

caturmahāśailavatī caturbhujagasaṃśrayā।
aṣṭottaramahāśailā tathāṣṭavaraparvatā ॥ 1.42.81 ॥


## 1.43

।।sūta uvāca।।



tasya pītvā phalarasaṃ jīvanti hi samāyutam ॥ 1.43.4 ॥






।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
pramāṇaṃ varṇamāyuśca yāthātathyena kīrtitam।

।।sūta uvāca।।
bhadrāśvānāṃ yathā cihnaṃ kīrtitaṃ kīrtivarddhanāḥ।
tacchṛṇudhvaṃ tu kārtsnyena pūrvasiddhairudāhṛtam ॥ 1.43.11 ॥

devakūṭasya sarvasya prathitasyeha yatparam।
pūrveṇa dikṣu sarvāsu yathāvacca prakīrtitam ॥ 1.43.12 ॥

kulācalānāṃ pañcānāṃ nadīnāñca viśeṣataḥ।

śaivālo varṇamālāgraḥ korañjaścācalottamaḥ।
śvetavarṇaśca nīlaśca pañcaite kulaparvatāḥ ॥ 1.43.14 ॥

teṣāṃ prasūtiranyepi parvatā bahuvistarāḥ।

tairvimiśrā janapadairnānāsattvasamākulāḥ।
nānāprakārajātīyāstvanekanṛpapālitāḥ ॥ 1.43.16 ॥

nāmadheyaiśca nikrāntaiḥ śrīmadbhiḥ puruṣarṣabhaiḥ।
adhyāsitā janapadāḥ kīrtanīyāśca śobhitāḥ ॥ 1.43.17 ॥

teṣāntu nāmadheyāni rāṣṭrāṇi vividhāni ca।
giryantaraniviṣṭāni sameṣu viṣameṣu ca ॥ 1.43.18 ॥

tathā sumaṅgalāḥ śuddhāścandrakāntāḥ sunandanāḥ।
vrajakā nīlaśaileyāḥ sauvīrā vijayasthalāḥ ॥ 1.43.19 ॥

mahāsthalāḥ sukāmāśca mahākeśāḥ sumūrddhajāḥ।
vātaraṃhāḥ sopasaṅgāḥ parivāyāḥ parācakāḥ ॥ 1.43.20 ॥

sambhavakrā mahānetrāḥ śaivālāstanapāstathā।
kumudāḥ śākamuṇḍāśca uraḥ saṅkīrṇabhaumakāḥ ॥ 1.43.21 ॥

sodakā vatsakāścaike vārāhā hāravāmakāḥ।
śaṅkhākhyā bhāvisandrāśca uttarā haimabhaumakāḥ ॥ 1.43.22 ॥

kṛṣṇabhaumāḥ subhaumāśca mahābhaumāśca kīrtitāḥ।

te pibanti mahāpuṇyāṃ mahāgaṅgāṃ mahānadīm।
ādau trailokyavikhyātā śītā śītāmbuvāhinī ॥ 1.43.24 ॥

tathā ca haṃsavasatirmahācakrā ca nimnagā।
cakrā vakrā ca kañcī ca surasā cāpagottamā ॥ 1.43.25 ॥

śākhāvatī cendranadī meghā maṅgāravāhinī।
kāverī haritoyā ca somāvarttā śatahradā ॥ 1.43.26 ॥

vanamālā vasumatī pampā pampāvatī śubhā।
suvarṇā pañca varṇā ca tathā puṇyā vapuṣmatī ॥ 1.43.27 ॥

maṇivaprā suvaprā ca brahmabhāgā śilāśinī।
kṛṣṇatoyā ca puṇyodā tathā nāgapadī śubhā ॥ 1.43.28 ॥

śaivālinī maṇitaṭā kṣārodā cāruṇāvatī।
tathā viṣṇupadī caiva mahāpuṇyā mahānadī ॥ 1.43.29 ॥

hiraṇyavāhinīlā ca skandamālā surāvatī ।
vāmodā ca patākā ca vetālī ca mahānadī ॥ 1.43.30 ॥

etā gaṅgā mahānadyo nāyikāḥ parikīrtitāḥ।

pūrvadvīpasya vohinyaḥ puṇyavatyaśca kīrtitāḥ।
kīrttanenāpi caitāsāṃ pūtaḥ syāditi me matiḥ ॥ 1.43.32 ॥

samṛddharāṣṭraṃ sphītañca nānājanapadākulam।
nānāvṛkṣavanoddeśaṃ nānānaga suveṣṭitam ॥ 1.43.33 ॥

bahudhānyadhanopetaṃ nānānṛpatipālitam ।
upetaṃ kīrtanaśatairnānāratnākarākaram ॥ 1.43.34 ॥

tasmindeśe samākhyātā hemaśaṅkhadalaprabhāḥ।

sambhāṣaṇaṃ darśanañca samasthānopasevanam ।
devaiḥ saha mahābhāgāḥ kurvate tatra vai prajāḥ ॥ 1.43.36 ॥

dharmādharmaviśeṣaśca na teṣvasti mahātmasu।
ahiṃsā satyavākyañca prakṛtyaiva hi varttate ॥ 1.43.37 ॥

te bhaktyā śaṅkaraṃ devaṃ gaurīṃ paramavaiṣṇavīm।
ijyāpūjānamaskārāṃstābhyāṃ nityaṃ prayuñjate ॥ 1.43.38 ॥


## 1.44

।।sūta uvāca ।।
nisarga eṣa vikhyāto bhadrāśvānāṃ yathārthavat ।
śrṛṇudhvaṃ ketumālānāṃ vistareṇa prakīrttanam ॥ 1.44.1 ॥

niṣadhasyācalendrasya paścimasya mahātmanaḥ।
paścimena hi yattatra dikṣu sarvāsu kīrtitam ॥ 1.44.2 ॥

kulācalānāṃ saptānāṃ nadīnāñca viśeṣataḥ।
tathā janapadānāñca vistaraṃ śrotumarhatha ॥ 1.44.3 ॥

aśoko varddhamānaśca saptaite kulaparvvatāḥ ॥ 1.44.4 ॥

teṣāṃ prasūtiranye'pi parvatā bahuvistarāḥ।

tairvimiśrā janapadā nānājātisamākulāḥ।
nānāprakāravijñeyāstvanekanṛpapālitāḥ ॥ 1.44.6 ॥

te nāmadheyairvikrāntā vividhāḥ prathitāḥ bhuvi।
adhyāsitā janapadaiḥ kīrttanaiśca vibhūṣitāḥ ॥ 1.44.7 ॥

teṣāṃ sanāmadheyāni rāṣṭrāṇi vividhāni ca।
giryyantaraniviṣṭāni sameṣu viṣameṣu ca ॥ 1.44.8 ॥

yathaiha kathitāḥ paurā gomanuṣyakapotakāḥ।
tatsukhā bhramarā yūthā māheyācalakūṭakāḥ ॥ 1.44.9 ॥

sumaulāḥ stāvakāḥ krauñcāḥ kṛṣṇāṅgamaṇipuñjakāḥ.
kūṭakambalamauṣīyāḥ samudrāntarakāstathā ॥ 1.44.10 ॥

karambhavāḥ kucāḥ śvetāḥ suvarṇakaṭakāḥ śubhāḥ।
śvetāṅgāḥ kṛṣṇapādāśca vihagā kapilakarṇikāḥ ॥ 1.44.11 ॥

atyākarālagojvālā hīnānāvanapātakāḥ।
mahiṣāḥ kumudābhāśca karavāṭāḥ sahotkacāḥ ॥ 1.44.12 ॥

śu(nakā) kanāsā mahānāsā vanāsagajabhūmikāḥ।
karañjamañjamā vāhāḥ kiṣkiṇḍīpāṇḍubhūmikīḥ ॥ 1.44.13 ॥

kuberā dhūmajā jaṅgā vaṅgā rājīvakokilāḥ।
vācāṅgāśca mahāṅgāśca madhaureyāḥ surecakāḥ ॥ 1.44.14 ॥

pittalāḥ kācalāścaiva śravaṇā mattakāsikāḥ।
godāvā bakulā vāṅgā vaṅgakā modakāḥ kalāḥ ॥ 1.44.15 ॥

te pibanti mahābhāgāḥ prathamāntu mahānadīm।
suvaprāṃ puṇyasalilāṃ mahānāganiṣevitām ॥ 1.44.16 ॥

kambalāṃ tāmasīṃ śyāmāṃ sumedhāṃ bakulāṃ nadīm।
vikīrṇāṃ śikhimālāñca tathā darbhāvatīmapi ॥ 1.44.17 ॥

bhadrānadīṃ śukanadīṃ palāśāñca mahānadīm।
bhīmāṃ prabhañjanāṃ kāñcīṃ puṇyāñcaiva kuśāvatīm ॥ 1.44.18 ॥

dakṣāṃ śākavatīñcaiva puṇyo dāñca mahānadīm।
candrāvatīṃ sumūlāñca ṛṣabhāñcāpagottamām ॥ 1.44.19 ॥

nadīṃ samudramālāñca tathā campāvatīmapi।
ekākṣāṃ puṣkalāṃ vāhāṃ suvarṇāṃ nandi nīmapi ॥ 1.44.20 ॥

kālindīñcaiva puṇyodāṃ bhāratīñca mahānadīm ।
sītodāmpātikāṃ brāhmīṃ viśālāñca mahānadīm ॥ 1.44.21 ॥

pīvarīṃ kumbhakārīñca ruṣā ñcaivāpagottamām।
mahiṣīṃ mānuṣīṃ daṇḍāṃ tathā nadanadīṃ śubhām ॥ 1.44.22 ॥

etāñcānyāñca pīyante bahvayo hi saritottamāḥ ।
devarṣisiddhacaritāḥ puṇyodāḥ pāpahāḥ śubhāḥ ॥ 1.44.23 ॥

nānājanapadāsphītaṃ mahāpagāvibhūṣitam।
nānāratnaughasampūrṇaṃ nityaṃ pramuditaṃ śivam ॥ 1.44.24 ॥

udīrṇaṃ dhanadhānyārthairnaravāsaiḥ samantataḥ।
sanniviṣṭaṃ mahādvīpaṃ paścimaṃ sukṛtātmanām ।
nisargaḥ ketumālānāmeṣa vaḥ parikīrttitaḥ ॥ 1.44.25 ॥


## 1.45

।।śāṃśapāyana uvāca ।।
pūrvāparau samākhyātau dvau deśau nastvayā prabho।

।।sūta uvāca।।
dakṣiṇena tu śvetasya nīlasyaivottareṇa tu।

sarvartukāmadāḥ sattvā jarādurgandhavarjitāḥ।

tatrāpi sumahān divyo nyagrodho rohiṇo mahān।
tasya pītvā phalarasaṃ pibanto varttayantyuta ॥ 1.45.4 ॥






sarvaratna mayaṃ caikaṃ bhavanairupaśobhitam ॥ 1.45.10 ॥

uttarasya samudrasya samudrānte ca dakṣiṇe।
kuravastatra tadvarṣaṃ puṇyaṃ siddhaniṣevitam ॥ 1.45.11 ॥


gandharvāpsarasopetaṃ prakṣaranti madhūttamam ॥ 1.45.13 ॥



śuklābhijanasampannāḥ sarve ca sthira yauvanāḥ ॥ 1.45.16 ॥


mithunaṃ jāyate sadyaḥ samañcaiva vivarddhate।

anyonyamanuraktāśca cakravākasadharmiṇaḥ।


kurūṇāmapi caiteṣāṃ śrṛṇudhvaṃ vistareṇa tu।
jārudheḥ śailarājasyāpyuttareṇottarasya hi।
dikṣu sarvāsu yadyatra kīrtyamānaṃ nibodhata ॥ 1.45.21 ॥

anekakandaradarīguhānirjharamaṇḍitau।
naikakuñjavanopetau citradhātuvibhūṣitau ॥ 1.45.22 ॥

anekadhātukalilau sarvadhātuvibhūṣitau।
puṣpamūlaphalopetau siddhacāraṇasevitau ॥ 1.45.23 ॥

dvāvapyetau sumahatāvucchritau kula parvatau।
tābhyāṃ kūṭaśatairnaikaistaddvīpamupasevitam ॥ 1.45.24 ॥

yayormadhye na sā yātā bhadrasomā mahānadī ॥ 1.45.25 ॥

sahasraśaśca nadyo'nyāḥ prasannasurasodakāḥ।
paryāptodāḥ kurūṇāṃ hi snānapānāvagāhanaiḥ ॥ 1.45.26 ॥

madhumaireyavāhinyo ghṛtavāhinya eva ca ॥ 1.45.27 ॥

dadhnaḥ śatahradāścānyāstataḥ svādvannaparvataḥ।
amṛtasvādukalpāni phalāni vividhāni ca ॥ 1.45.28 ॥

pañcayojanamānāni mahāgandhāni sarvaśaḥ ॥ 1.45.29 ॥

nānāvarṇaprakārāṇi puṣpāṇi ca sahasraśaḥ।
upa bhogasahasrāṇi bhadrāṇi ca mahānti ca ॥ 1.45.30 ॥

gandhavarṇarasāḍhyāni sparśopetāni sarvaśaḥ।
tamālāgurugandhānāṃ candanānāṃ vanāni ca ॥ 1.45.31 ॥

bhramarairupagītāni praphullāni sadaiva ca।
vṛkṣagulmalatāḍhayāni vanāni susukhāni ca ॥ 1.45.32 ॥

ṣaṭpadairupagītāni dvijaiścānyairdvijottamāḥ।
pajhotpalavanāḍhyāni sarāṃsi ca sahasraśaḥ ॥ 1.45.33 ॥

bhakṣyamālyasamṛddhāśca bahumālyānulepanāḥ।
manoharamukhaiścitraiḥ pakṣisaṅghairnikūjitāḥ ॥ 1.45.34 ॥

śayanāsanopabhogāśca anekaguṇavistarāḥ।
vihārabhūmayo ramyāḥ sarvartuṣu sukhapradāḥ ॥ 1.45.35 ॥

ākrīḍāḥ sarvataḥ sphītāḥ maṇihemapariṣkṛtāḥ।
śilāgṛhā vṛkṣagṛhā vareṇyāḥ kadalī gṛhāḥ ॥ 1.45.36 ॥

latāgṛhasahasrāṇi susukhāni samantataḥ।
śuddhaśaṅkhadalābhāni bhūmiveśmaśatāni ca ॥ 1.45.37 ॥

suvarṇamaṇicitrāṇi sarvatra vipulāni ca ॥ 1.45.38 ॥

mahāvṛkṣasahasrāṇi vareṇyāni ca sarvaśaḥ।
nānākārāṇi vāsāṃsi sūkṣmāṇi susukhāni ca ॥ 1.45.39 ॥

mṛdaṅga veṇupaṇavavīṇādyā bahuvistarāḥ।

sarvatraiva tathodyānaṃ sarvatraiva hi tatpuram।
sarvadvīpapramuditaṃ naranārī samākulam।
pravāti cānilastatra nānāpuṣpādhivāsitaḥ ॥ 1.45.41 ॥

nityamaṅgasukhāhlādastasmin dvīpe śramāpahe ।
tatra svargaparibhraṣṭā jāyante hi narāḥ sadā।
bhaumaṃ tadapi hi svargaṃ tatrāpi ca guṇottamam ॥ 1.45.42 ॥

candrakāntā naravarāḥ śyāmāṅkāḥ pūrvakūlajāḥ।
śyāmāvadātāḥ sukhinaḥ sūryakāntā varāḥ prajāḥ ॥ 1.45.43 ॥

tasmin deśe narāḥ śreṣṭā devasattvaparākramāḥ।
sadā vihāriṇaḥ sarve kāmavṛttyā suvarcasaḥ ॥ 1.45.44 ॥

valayāṅgadakeyūrahārakuṇḍalabhūṣitāḥ ।
sragviṇaścitra mukuṭāścitrācchādanavāsasaḥ ॥ 1.45.45 ॥

ajīrṇayauvanadharāḥ supriyāḥ priyadarśanāḥ।
prajā varṣasahasrāṇi jīvanti subahūnyuta ॥ 1.45.46 ॥

na tāḥ prasavadharmiṇyo na vaṃśaprakṣayo vidhiḥ।
mithunaṃ jāyate vṛkṣādupakṣamamanīdṛśam ॥ 1.45.47 ॥

sāmānyavibhavāḥ sarve mamatvaparivarjitāḥ।
na tatra vidyate dharmo nādharmaḥ sampravarttate ॥ 1.45.48 ॥

na vyadhirna jarā tatra na durmedhā na ca klamaḥ।
pūrṇe kāle vinaśyanti jalabudbudavacca te ॥ 1.45.49 ॥

evamatyantasukhinaḥ sarvaduḥkhavivarjitāḥ।
raktā dharmaṃ na paśyanti duḥkhāddharmo'bhijāyate ॥ 1.45.50 ॥

uttarāṇāṃ kurūṇāntu pārśve jñeyantu dakṣiṇe।
samudramūrmimālāḍhyaṃ nānāsvaravibhūṣitam ॥ 1.45.51 ॥

pañcayojanasāhasramatikramya surālayam।
candradvīpamiti khyātaṃ cadramaṇḍalasaṃsthitam ॥ 1.45.52 ॥

sahasrayojanānāntu sarvataḥ parimaṇḍalam।
śatayojanavistīrṇamucchritaṃ tāvadeva tu ॥ 1.45.53 ॥

tasya madhye girivaraḥ siddhacāraṇasevitaḥ।
candra tulyaprabhaiḥ kāntaiścandrākāraiḥ sulakṣaṇaiḥ ॥ 1.45.54 ॥

śvetavaiḍhūryakumudaiścitro'sau kumudaprabhaḥ।
anekacitrakodyāno naikanirjharakandaraḥ।
mahāsānudarīkuñjairvividhaiḥ samalaṃkṛtaḥ ॥ 1.45.55 ॥

tasmācchailānmahāpuṇyā candrāṃśuvimalodakā।
pravahatyuttamanadī candrāvarttā taraṅgiṇī ॥ 1.45.56 ॥

tatra candramasaḥ sthānaṃ nakṣatrādhipatervaram।
sadā'vatarate tatra candramā grahanāyakaḥ ॥ 1.45.57 ॥

tatra candramaso nāmnā śailaḥ sa tu pariśrutaḥ।
candradvīpaṃ mahādvīpaṃ prakāśaṃ divi ceha ca ॥ 1.45.58 ॥

candrakāntāḥ prajāḥ sarvā vimalāścandradaivatāḥ ॥ 1.45.59 ॥

atyantadhārmikāḥ saumyāḥ satyasandhāḥ sutejasaḥ।

paścimena tu dvīpasya paścimasya prakīrttitam।
caturyojanasāhasraṃ samatītya mahodadhim ॥ 1.45.61 ॥

daśayojanasāhasraṃ samantāt parimaṇḍalam।

nityaṃ pramuditaṃ sphītaṃ mahāśailaiśca śobhitam ॥ 1.45.63 ॥

tatra bhadrāsanaṃ vāyornānāratnaiśca maṇḍitam।
tatra vigrahavān vāyuḥ sadā parvasu pūjyate ॥ 1.45.64 ॥

virājante'mara prakhyāstatra citrāmbarasrajaḥ ॥ 1.45.65 ॥

vīryavanto mahābhāgāḥ pañcavarṣaśatāyuṣaḥ।
satyasandhā mudā yuktāḥ prajāstā vāyudaivatāḥ ॥ 1.45.66 ॥

।।sūta uvāca।।

uttaraśravaṇe bhūyaḥ papracchustadanantaram ॥ 1.45.68 ॥

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।
caturdaśaite manavaḥ prajāsarge bhavantyuta ॥ 1.45.69 ॥

etadveditumicchāmastanno nigada sattama।

eta dvistarato bhūyastānuvāca samāhitaḥ ॥ 1.45.71 ॥

।।sūta uvāca।।
nisarga eṣa vikhyātaḥ kurūṇāntu yathārthavat।
bhāratasya tu vakṣyāmi nisargaṃ taṃ nibodhata ॥ 1.45.72 ॥

puṇyatīrthe himavato dakṣiṇasyācalasya hi।
pūrvapaścāyatasyāsya dakṣiṇena dvijottamāḥ ॥ 1.45.73 ॥

tathā janapadānāṃ ca vistaraṃ śrotumarhatha।

idaṃ tu madhyamaṃ citraṃ śubhāśubhaphalodayam।
uttaraṃ yatsamudrasya himavaddakṣiṇaṃ ca yat ॥ 1.45.75 ॥

niruktavacanāccaiva varṣaṃ tadbhārataṃ smṛtam ॥ 1.45.76 ॥

na khalvanyatra marttyānāṃ bhūmau karma vidhīyate ॥ 1.45.77 ॥

samudrāntaritā jñeyāste tvagamyāḥ parasparam ॥ 1.45.78 ॥

indradvīpaḥ kaseruśca tāmravarṇo gabhastimān।
nāgadvīpastathā saumyo gandharvastvatha vāruṇaḥ ॥ 1.45.79 ॥


tiryaguttaravistīrṇaḥ sahasrāṇi navaiva tu ॥ 1.45.81 ॥


ijyāyuddhavaṇijyābhirvarttayanto vyavasthitāḥ ॥ 1.45.83 ॥



yastvayaṃ navamo dvīpastiryagāyata ucyate।
kṛtsnaṃ jayati yo hyenaṃ sa samrāḍiha kīrttyate ॥ 1.45.86 ॥


vindhyaśca pāriyātraśca saptaite kulaparvatāḥ ॥ 1.45.88 ॥


mandaraḥ parvata śreṣṭo vaihāro dardurastathā।
kolāhalaḥ sasurasaḥ maināko vaidyutastathā ॥ 1.45.90 ॥

gantuprasthaḥ kṛṣṇagirirgodhano girireva ca ॥ 1.45.91 ॥

puṣpagiryujjayantau ca śailo raivatakastathā।





vedasmṛtirvedavatī vṛtraghnī sindhureva ca।

parā carmmaṇvatī caiva vidiśā vetravatyapi।













sahyasya cottarārddhe tu yatra godāvarī nadī।

tatra govarddhano nāma surarājena nirmitaḥ।

antaḥpuravanoddeśastena jajñe manoramaḥ ॥ 1.45.113 ॥



ramaṭā raddhakaṭakāḥ kekayā daśamānikāḥ।


lampākā stanapāścaiva pīḍikā juhuḍaiḥ saha ॥ 1.45.118 ॥








paunikā maunikāścaiva asmakā bhogavarddhanāḥ।

śūrpākārāḥ kolavanāḥ durgāḥ kālītakaiḥ saha ॥ 1.45.127 ॥









catvāri bhārate varṣe yugāni kavayo viduḥ।


## 1.46

।।sūta uvāca।।
etacchrutvā tu ṛṣaya uttaraṃ punareva te।

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
yacca kimpuruṣaṃ varṣaṃ harivarṣaṃ tathaiva ca।


।।sūta uvāca।।


jāyante mānavāstatra nistaptakanakaprabhāḥ ॥ 1.46.6 ॥

tasya kimpuruṣāḥ sarve pibanti rasamuttamam ॥ 1.46.7 ॥



harivarṣe tu jīvanti sarve muditamānasāḥ।
na jarā bādhate tatra jīryanti na ca te narāḥ ॥ 1.46.10 ॥

na tatra sūryastapati na ca jīryanti mānavāḥ ॥ 1.46.11 ॥


manasvino bhuktabhogāḥ satkarmaphalabhoginaḥ ॥ 1.46.13 ॥

meroḥ pratidiśaṃ te tu navasāhasravistṛte ॥ 1.46.15 ॥


merostu paścime bhāge navasāhasrasaṃmite।
catustriṃśatsahasrāṇi gandhamādanaparvataḥ ॥ 1.46.17 ॥


dakṣiṇena tu nīlasya niṣadhasyottareṇa tu ॥ 1.46.19 ॥



anāvṛttāścatuṣkeṇa bhidyante madhyamāgatāḥ ॥ 1.46.22 ॥


nityapuṣpaphalopetaḥ siddhacāraṇasevitaḥ ॥ 1.46.24 ॥



patamānāni tānyurvyāṃ kurvanti vipulaṃ svanam।
tasyā jambvāḥ phalaraso nadībhūya prasarpati ॥ 1.46.27 ॥


jambūrasaphalaṃ pītvā na jarāṃ prāpnuvanti te।
na ca dhruvaṃ na rogaṃ tu na ca mṛtyuṃ tathāvidham ॥ 1.46.29 ॥


skannaṃ bhavati tacchukraṃ kanakaṃ devabhūṣaṇam ॥ 1.46.31 ॥








## 1.47

।।sūta uvāca।।
apsarogaṇasaṃyukto modate hyalakādhipaḥ ॥ 1.47.1 ॥

mandaṃ nāmnā kumudvantaṃ śaradambudasannibham ॥ 1.47.2 ॥

divyañca nandanaṃ tatra tasyāstīre mahadvanam ॥ 1.47.3 ॥

prāguttareṇa kailāsāddivyasattvauṣadhaṃ girim।
suradhātumayaṃ citraṃ suvarṇaṃ parvataṃ prati ॥ 1.47.4 ॥

candraprabho nāma giriḥ saśuddhāṃ ratnasannibhaḥ।
tasya pāde mahaddivyamacchodaṃ nāma tatsaraḥ ॥ 1.47.5 ॥

tasmāddivyā prabhavati hyacchodā nāma nimnagā।

tasmin girau nivasati maṇibhadraḥ sahānugaḥ।

mahīmaṇḍalamadhyena praviṣṭe te mahodadhim ॥ 1.47.8 ॥


tasya pāde mahaddivyaṃ lohitaṃ nāma tatsaraḥ ॥ 1.47.10 ॥

tasmāt puṇyaḥ prabhavati lauhityaḥ sadano mahān।

tasmin girau nivasati yakṣo maṇivaro vaśī।
saumyaiḥ sudhārmikaiścaiva guhyakaiḥ parivāritaḥ ॥ 1.47.12 ॥

vṛtrakāyāt kilotpannamañjanaṃ trikakutprati ॥ 1.47.13 ॥

sarvadhātumayastatra sumahān vaidyuto giriḥ।


kuberānucarastatra prahetṛtanayo vaśī।
brahma pāto nivasati rākṣaso'nantavikramaḥ।
antarikṣacarairghorairyātudhānaśatairvṛtaḥ ॥ 1.47.16 ॥

apareṇa tu kailāsānmukhyasattvauṣadhiṃ girim।

bhavasya dayitaḥ śrīmān parvato meghasannibhaḥ।


tasmin girau nivasati giriśo dhūmralohitaḥ।


astyuttareṇa kailāsācchivasattvauṣadho giriḥ ॥ 1.47.22 ॥

gauro nāma giristatra haritālamayaḥ śubhaḥ।

ramyaṃ bindusaro nāma yatra yāto bhagīrathaḥ ॥ 1.47.24 ॥


tatra tripathagā devī prathamantu pratiṣṭitā।
somapādaprasūtā sā saptadhā pratipadya te ॥ 1.47.26 ॥


divicchāyāpatho yastu anunakṣatramaṇḍalam ।


kṛtaṃ bindusarastatra tato bindusaraḥ smṛtam ॥ 1.47.30 ॥

tato niruddhā devī sā bhavena smayatā kila।


nirudhya tu śirasyenāṃ vegena patitāṃ bhuvi ॥ 1.47.33 ॥











sādhrāṃstu ṣārāṃstampākān pahnavān daradān śakān।


saindhavān randhrakarakān bhramarābhīrarohakān ।
śunāmukhāṃścorddhvamanūn siddhacāraṇasovitān ॥ 1.47.46 ॥








apathān bhāvayantīha indradyumnasaro'pi ca।

madhyenodyānamaskārān kuthaprāvaraṇān yayau।
indradvīpasamudre tu praviṣṭā lavaṇodadhim ॥ 1.47.55 ॥



sikatāparvatamarūn gatvā vidyādharān yayau।
nemimaṇḍalakoṣṭe tu praviṣṭā sā mahodadhim ॥ 1.47.58 ॥


vasvokasāyāstīre tu vārisurabhiviśrute।

tatrāgastyaiḥ parivṛto vidvadbhirbrahmarākṣasaiḥ।


manasvinī prabhavati tasmājjyotiṣmatī ca sā ॥ 1.47.63 ॥

avagāhya hyubhayataḥ samudrau pūrvapaścimau ।
saro viṣṇupadaṃ nāma niṣadhe parvatottame ॥ 1.47.64 ॥

tasmāddvayaṃ prabhavati gāndharvī nanvalī ca yā।
meroḥ paścāt prabhavati hradaścandraprabho mahān ॥ 1.47.65 ॥


śvetāt prabhavate puṇyaṃ sarastūttaramānasam ॥ 1.47.67 ॥

madhumatsaraḥ puṇyañca pajhamīnadvijākulam ॥ 1.47.68 ॥

kalpavṛkṣasamākīrṇaṃ madhuvatsarvataḥ sukham।
rudrakāntamiti khyātaṃ nirmitaṃ tadbhavena tu ॥ 1.47.69 ॥


yani kimpuruṣādyāni teṣu devo na varṣati ॥ 1.47.71 ॥

udbhidyānyudakānyatra pravahanti saridvarāḥ।



cakro balāhakaścaiva mainākaścaiva parvataḥ।

tatra saṃvarttako nāma so'gniḥ pibati tajjalam।

mahendrabhayavitrastāḥ pakṣacchedabhayāttadā।

ihoditasya dṛśyante tathānye'nyatra nodite ॥ 1.47.78 ॥




## 1.48

।।sūta uvāca।।
dakṣiṇenāpi varṣasya bhāratasya nibodhata।
daśayojanasāhasraṃ samatītya mahārṇavam ॥ 1.48.1 ॥

atastribhāgavistīrṇaṃ nānāpuṣpaphalodayam ॥ 1.48.2 ॥

vidyutvantaṃ mahāśailaṃ tatraikaṃ kulaparvatam।
yena kūṭataṭairnaikaistaddvīpaṃ samalaṃkṛtam ॥ 1.48.3 ॥

prasannasvādusalilāstatra nadyaḥ sahasraśaḥ।
vāpyastasya tu dvīpasya pravṛttā vimalodakāḥ ॥ 1.48.4 ॥

tasya śailasya chidreṣu vistīrṇeṣvāyateṣu ca।
anekeṣu samṛddhāni nānākārāṇi sarvvaśaḥ ॥ 1.48.5 ॥

naranārīsamāḍhyāni muditāni mahānti ca।

purāṇi sanniviṣṭāni parvatāntargatāni ca।
susambaddhāni cānyonyamekadvārāṇi cānyatha ॥ 1.48.7 ॥

dīrghaśmaśrudharātmāno nīlā meghasamaprabhāḥ।
jātamātrāḥ prajāstatra aśītiparamāyuṣaḥ ॥ 1.48.8 ॥

śākhāmṛgasadharmāṇaḥ phalamūlāśinastathā।
godharmāṇo hyanirdiṣṭāḥ śaucācāravivarjitāḥ ॥ 1.48.9 ॥

taddvīpaṃ tādṛśaiḥ pūrṇaṃ manujaiḥ kṣudramānuṣaiḥ ।
evamete'ntaradvīpā vyākhyātā ānupūrvaśaḥ ॥ 1.48.10 ॥

viṃśatrriṃśacca pañcāśat ṣaṣṭyaśītiḥ śataṃ tathā।
sahasramapi cāpyuktaṃ yojanānāṃ samantataḥ ॥ 1.48.11 ॥

vistīrṇāścāyatāścaiva nānāsattvasamākulāḥ।
barhiṇadvīpaparvāṇi kṣudradvīpāḥ sahasraśaḥ ॥ 1.48.12 ॥

jambūdvīpapradeśāttu ṣaḍanye vividhāśrayāḥ।
atra dvīpāḥ samākhyātā nānāratnākarāḥ kṣitau ॥ 1.48.13 ॥

aṅgadvīpaṃ yamadvīpaṃ malayadvīpameva ca।
śaṅkhadvīpaṃ kuśadvīpaṃ varāhadvīpameva ca ॥ 1.48.14 ॥

aṅgadvīpaṃ nibodha tvaṃ nānāsaṅghasamākulam।
nānāmlecchagaṇākīrṇaṃ taddvīpaṃ bahuvistaram ॥ 1.48.15 ॥

hemavidrumapūrṇānāṃ ratnānāmākaraṃ kṣitau।
nadīśailavanaiścitraṃ sannibhaṃ lavaṇāmbhasā ॥ 1.48.16 ॥

tatra cakragirirnāma naika nirjharakandaraḥ।
tatra sā tu darī cāsya nānāsatvasamāśrayā ॥ 1.48.17 ॥

sa madhye nāgadeśasya naikadeśo mahāgiriḥ।
koṭibhyāṃ nāganilayaṃ prāpto nadanadī patim ॥ 1.48.18 ॥

yamadvīpamiti proktaṃ nānāratnākarācitam ।
tatrāpi dyutimānnāma parvato dhātumaṇḍitaḥ।
samudragānāṃ prabhavaḥ prabhavaḥ kāñcanasya tu ॥ 1.48.19 ॥

tathaiva malayadvīpamevameva susaṃvṛtam।
maṇiratnākaraṃ sphītamākaraṃ kanakasya ca ॥ 1.48.20 ॥

ākaraṃ candanānāñca samudrāṇāṃ tathākaram।
nānāmlecchagaṇākīrṇaṃ nadīparvatamaṇḍitam ॥ 1.48.21 ॥

tatra śrīmāṃstu malayaḥ parvato rajatākaraḥ।
mahāmalaya ityevaṃ vikhyāto varaparvataḥ ॥ 1.48.22 ॥

dvitīyaṃ mandaraṃ nāma prathitañca sadā kṣitau।
āgastyabhavanaṃ tatra devāsuranamaskṛtam ॥ 1.48.23 ॥

tathā kāñcanapādasya malayasyāparasya hi।
nikuñjaistṛṇasomāṅgairāśramaṃ puṇyasevitam ॥ 1.48.24 ॥

nānāpuṣpaphalopetaṃ svargādapi viśiṣyate।
tatrāvatarate svargaḥ sadā parvasu parvasu ॥ 1.48.25 ॥

tathā trikūṭanilaye nānādhātuvibhūṣite ।
anekayojanotsedhe citrasānudarīgṛhe ॥ 1.48.26 ॥

tasya kūṭataṭe ramye hemaprākāratoraṇā।
niryūhavalabhīcitrā harmyaprāsādamālinī ॥ 1.48.27 ॥

nityapramuditā sphītā laṅkā nāma mahāpurī ॥ 1.48.28 ॥

sā kāmarūpiṇāṃ sthānaṃ rākṣasānāṃ mahātmanām ।
āvāso baladṛptānāṃ tadvidyāddevavidviṣām।
mānuṣāṇāmasambādhā hyagamyā sā mahāpurī ॥ 1.48.29 ॥

tasya dvīpasya vai pūrve tīre nadanadīpateḥ।
gokarṇanāmadheyasya śaṅkarasyālayaṃ mahat ॥ 1.48.30 ॥

tathaikarājyaṃ vijñeyaṃ śaṅkhadvīpasamāsthitam।

tatra śaṅkhagirirnāma dhautaśaṅkhadalaprabhaḥ।

śaṅkha nāgā mahāpuṇyā yasmāt prabhavate nadī।
yatra śaṅkhamukho nāma nāgarājaḥ kṛtālayaḥ ॥ 1.48.33 ॥

tathaiva kumudadvīpaṃ nānāpuṇyopaśobhitam।

kumudākhyā mahābhāgā duṣṭacittanibarhaṇī।
mahādevasya bhaginī prabhābhistābhirijyate ॥ 1.48.35 ॥

tathā varāhadvīpe ca nānāmlecchagaṇākule।

dhanadhānyayute sphīte dharmmiṣṭhajanasaṅkule।
nadīśailavanaiścitrairbahupuṣpaphalopagaiḥ ॥ 1.48.37 ॥

varāhaparvvato nāma tatra ramyaḥ śiloccayaḥ।
anekakandaradarīguhānirjharaśobhitaḥ ॥ 1.48.38 ॥

tasmātsurasapānīyā puṇyatīrthataraṅgiṇī।
vārāhī nāma varadā pravṛttāsya mahānadī ॥ 1.48.39 ॥

ananyadevatāstasmai namaskurvanti viprajāḥ ॥ 1.48.40 ॥

evaṃ ṣaḍete kathitā anudvīpāḥ samantataḥ।
bhāratadvīpadeśo vai dakṣiṇe bahuvistaraḥ ॥ 1.48.41 ॥

evamekamidaṃ varṣaṃ bahudvīpamihocyate।
samudrajalasambinnaṃ khaṇḍaṃ khaṇḍīkṛtaṃ smṛtam ॥ 1.48.42 ॥

evañcaturmahādvīpaḥ sāntaradvīpamaṇḍitaḥ।

iti mahāpurāṇe vāyuprokte bhuvanavinyāso nāmāṣṭacatvāriṃśo dhyāyaḥ ।। 48 ।

## 1.49

।।sūta uvāca।।

jambūdvīpasya vistārāddviguṇastasya vistaraḥ।



plakṣadvīpādiṣu teṣu sapta saptasu saptasu।

gomeda ko'tra prathamaḥ parvato meghasannibhaḥ।
khyāyate tasya nāmnā vai varṣaṃ gomedakantu tat ॥ 1.49.6 ॥

dvitīyaḥ parvataścandraḥ sarvauṣadhisamanvitaḥ।

tatrācale samutpannau pūrvaṃ nāradaparvatau ॥ 1.49.8 ॥

caturthastatra vai śailo dundubhirnāma nāmataḥ।



yasmādvibhrājate'rcirbhirvaibhrājastena sa smṛtaḥ ॥ 1.49.12 ॥


candrasya śikharaṃ nāma nāradasya sukhodayam।
ānandaṃ dundubhervarṣa somakasya śivaṃsmṛtam।

viharanti ramante ca dṛśyamānāstu taiḥ saha ॥ 1.49.15 ॥





adhaḥsarpiṇī na teṣvasti tathaivotsarpiṇī na ca ॥ 1.49.20 ॥

na tatrāsti yugāvasthā caturyugakṛtā vkacit।
tretāyugasamaḥ kālaḥ sarvadā tatra varttate ॥ 1.49.21 ॥



sukhamāyurbalaṃ rūpamārogyaṃ dharma eva ca।

divyauṣadhi phalopetaḥ sarvauṣadhivanaspatiḥ ॥ 1.49.25 ॥


sa tatra pūjyate sthāṇurmadhye janapadasya hi।
plakṣadvīpasya caiveha vaipulyādvistareṇa tu ॥ 1.49.27 ॥


śālmalena samudrastu dvīpenekṣurasodakaḥ।



dvitīyaḥ parvatastasya unnato nāma viśrutaḥ।


caturthaḥ parvato droṇo yatrauṣadhyo mahābalāḥ।

kaṅkastu pañcamastatra parvataḥ sumahodayaḥ।

ṣaṣṭhastu parvatastatra mahiṣo meghasannibhaḥ।
yasmin so'gnirnivasati mahiṣo nāma vārijaḥ ॥ 1.49.36 ॥

saptamaḥ parvatastatra kakujhānnāma bhāṣyate।
prajāpatimupādāya prājāpatye vidhistvayam ॥ 1.49.37 ॥

kumudāt prathamaṃ śvetamunnatasya tu lohitam ॥ 1.49.38 ॥

kaṅkasya vaidyutaṃ nāma mahiṣasya tu mānasam ॥ 1.49.39 ॥

kakudaḥ suprabhaṃ nāma saptaitāni tu saptadhā।




śālmalirvipulaskandha stasya nāmnā sa ucyate।
śālmalistu samudreṇa surodena samantataḥ।
vistārācchālmalasyaiva samena tu samantataḥ ॥ 1.49.44 ॥



saptaiva girayastatra varṇyamānānnibodhata।

kukhadvīpe tu vijñeyaḥ parvato vidrumoccayaḥ।
dvīpasya prathamastasya dvitīyo hemaparvataḥ ॥ 1.49.48 ॥






na teṣu dasyavaḥ santi mlecchajātyastathaiva ca।
gauraprāyo janaḥ sarvaḥ kramācca mriyate tathā ॥ 1.49.54 ॥

pavitrā santatiścaiva dyutigarbhā mahī tathā ॥ 1.49.55 ॥




ghṛtodakasamudro vai krauñcadvīpena saṃvṛtaḥ ॥ 1.49.59 ॥


divāvṛtaḥ paraścapi divindo girirucyate ॥ 1.49.61 ॥


ete ratnamayāḥ sapta krauñcadvīpasya parvatāḥ।




munideśātparaścaiva procyate dundubhisvanaḥ।

gaurī kumudvatī caiva sandhyā rātrirmanojavā ।




na śakyaṃ vistarādvaktu mapi varṣaśatairapi ।


krauñcadvīpasya vistārād dviguṇastasya vistaraḥ।
parivārya samudraṃ sa dadhimaṇḍodakaṃ sthitaḥ ॥ 1.49.74 ॥



devarṣigandharvayutaḥ prathamo merurucyate।

tatra meghāstu vṛṣṭyarthaṃ prabhavanti ca yānti ca।

tasmānnityamupādatte vāsavaḥ paramaṃ jalam।

tasyāpare raivatako yatra nityaṃ pratiṣṭitā ।


āmbikeyātparo ramyaḥ sarvauṣadhisamanvitaḥ ।
sa caiva keśarītyukto yato vāyuḥ pravāyati ॥ 1.49.82 ॥

udayasyodayaṃ varṣaṃ jaladaṃ nāma viśrutam ॥ 1.49.83 ॥

dvitīyaṃ jaladhārasya sukumāramiti smṛtam।

āmbikeyasya modākaṃ kesareṣu mahādrumam ॥ 1.49.85 ॥

dvīpasya parimāṇañca hrasvadīrghatvameva ca।
śākadvīpena vikhyātastasya madhye vanaspatiḥ ।


teṣu nadyaśca saptaiva prativarṣaṃ samudragāḥ ।

anu taptā ca nāmnaiva nadī samparikīrttitā ॥ 1.49.89 ॥




anugacchanti tāstvanyā nadīnadyaḥ sahasraśaḥ।


nadījalaiḥ praticchannaḥ parvataiścābhrasannibhaiḥ ॥ 1.49.95 ॥

sarvadhātuvicitraiśca maṇi vidrumabhūṣitaiḥ।
puraiśca vividhākāraiḥ sphītairjanapadairapi ॥ 1.49.96 ॥




viparyayo na teṣvasti etat svābhāvikaṃ smṛtam ॥ 1.49.100 ॥

svadharmeṇaiva dharmajñāste rakṣanti parasparam ॥ 1.49.101 ॥



citrai rmaṇimayaiḥ śīlaiḥ śikharaistu samucchritaiḥ ॥ 1.49.104 ॥

dvīpasya tasya pūrvārddhe citrasānuḥ sthito mahān।

dvīpārddhasya paristomaḥ parvato mānasottamaḥ ॥ 1.49.106 ॥

sthito velāsamīpe tu navacandra ivoditaḥ।



tasmin dvīpe smṛtau dvau tu puṇyau janapadau śubhau।
abhito mānasasyātha parvatasyānumaṇḍalau ॥ 1.49.110 ॥

tasyaivābhyantare yattu dhātakīkhaṇḍamucyate ॥ 1.49.111 ॥


samamāyuśca rūpañca tasmin varṣadvaye sthitam।

nigraho na ca daṇḍo'sti na lobho na parigrahaḥ ॥ 1.49.114 ॥

satyānṛtaṃ na tatrāsti dharmādharmau tathaiva ca।

varṣadvaye sarvametat puṣkarasya na vidyate ॥ 1.49.116 ॥

udbhijjānyudakānyatra giriprasravaṇāni ca ॥ 1.49.117 ॥

sarvatra susukhastatra jarāklamavivarjitaḥ ॥ 1.49.118 ॥


vistarānmaṇḍalāccaiva puṣkarasya tathaiva ca ॥ 1.49.120 ॥

dvīpasyānantaro yastu samudrastu samantataḥ ॥ 1.49.121 ॥


ṛṣayo nivasantyasmin prajā yasmāccaturvidhāḥ।
tasmādvarṣamiti proktaṃ prajānāṃ sukhadantu tat ॥ 1.49.123 ॥


iti prabandhanāt siddhaṃ varṣatvaṃ tena teṣu tat ।। 49.125 ।।

śuklapakṣe candravṛddhau samudraḥ pūryate tadā।


ukhāsthamagnisaṃyogāt jalamudricyate yathā।
tathā mahodadhigataṃ toyamudricyate tataḥ ।। 49.128 ।।

anyūnāhyatiriktāśca varddhantyāpo hrasanti ca।


udakasyādhānaṃ yasmācca tasmādudadhirucyate ।। 49.131 ।।

plakṣadvīpe tu gomedaḥ parvatastena cocyate ।। 49.132 ।।

kuśadvīpe kuśastambastasya nāmnā sa ucyate ।। 49.133 ।।

krauñcadvīpe giriḥ krauñco madhye janapadasya ha।

nyagrodhaḥ puṣkaradvīpe tatra taiḥ sa namaskṛtaḥ।

upāsate tatra devāstrayastriṃśanmaharṣibhiḥ।
sa tatra pūjyate caiva devairdevottamottamaḥ ।। 49.136 ।।

jambūdvīpātpravarttante ratnāni vividhāni ca।

sarvaso brahmacaryeṇa satyena ca damena ca।

etasmin puṣkaradvīpe yaduktaṃ varṣakadvayam।


bhojanañcāprayatnena tatra svayamupasthitam ।




ālokastasya cārvāktu nirālokastataḥ param।

āloke lokaśabdastu nirāloke salokatā।
lokārthaṃ sammato loko nirālokastu bāhyataḥ ।। 49.146 ।।

lokavistāramātrantu ālokaḥ sarvato bahiḥ।

bhūrloko'tha bhuvarlokaḥ svarloko'tha mahastathā ।। 49.148 ।।

janastapastathā satya etāvān lokasaṅ‌grahaḥ।



daśādhikyena cānyonyaṃ dhārayanti parasparam।


bāhyato ghanatoyasya tiryagūrddhvānumaṇḍalam।


mahān vyāpto hyanantena avyaktena tu dhāryate ।। 49.156 ।।

anantamaparivyaktandaśadhā sūkṣma eva ca।
anantamakṛtātmānamanādinidhanañca tat ।। 49.157 ।।

atītya para to ghoramanālambamanāmayam।

tama eva nirālokamamaryādamadeśikam।
devānāmapyavidiṃtaṃ vyavahāravivarjjitam ।। 49.159 ।।


mahato devadevasya maryādāyāṃ vyavasthitam ।। 49.161 ।।


rasātalatalāt sapta saptaivorddhvatalāḥ kṣitau।


vicitrā jagataḥ sā vai pravṛttiranavasthitā ।। 49.165 ।।

yathaitad bhautikaṃ nāma nisargabahuvistaram।
atīndriyairmahābhāgaiḥ siddhairapi na lakṣyate ।। 49.166 ।।

īśvarasya tu devasya anantasya dvijottamāḥ ।। 49.167 ।।

ananta eṣa sarvatra sarvasthāneṣu paṭyate।

ya eṣa śivanāmnā hi tadvaḥ kārtsnyena kīrtitam।
sa eṣa sarvatra gataḥ sarvasthāneṣu pūjyate ।। 49.169 ।।

bhūmau rasātale caiva ākāśe pavane'nale।

tathā tapasi vijñeya eṣa eva mahādyutiḥ।
sarvalokeṣu lokeśa ijyate bahudhā prabhuḥ ।। 49.171 ।।

evaṃ parasparotpannā dhāryante ca parasparān।

pṛthvyādayo vikārāste paricchinnāḥ parasparam।




bhavantyanyonyahīnāni parasparasamāśrayāt।

yāvadetāni bhūtāni tāvadutpatti rucyate ।। 49.178 ।।

pratyākhyāya ca bhūtāni kāryotpattirna vidyate ।। 49.179 ।।



vistārānmaṇjalāccaiva prasaṃkhyātena caiva hi ।

adiṣṭānaṃ bhaganato yasya sarvamidaṃ jagat ।

etāvadeva śrotavyaṃ sanniveśe tu pārthiva ।। 49.184 ।।

sapta prakṛtayastvetā dhārayanti parasparam।
tāsvalpaparimāṇena prasaṅkhyātumihocyate।



## 1.50

।।sūta uvāca।।




anantaḥ paṭyate caiva vyāpakaḥ sarvasambhavaḥ ॥ 1.50.5 ॥



evamantamanantasya bhautikasya na vidyate।
purā surairabhihitaṃ niścitantu nibodhata ॥ 1.50.8 ॥

sthitireṣā tu vijñeyā saptame'smin rasātale ॥ 1.50.9 ॥

daśayojanasāhasramekabhaumaṃ rasātalam।
sādhubhiḥ parivikhyātamekaikaṃ bahuvistaram ॥ 1.50.10 ॥

prathamamatalañcaiva sutalantu tataḥ param।
tataḥ parataraṃ vidyādvitalaṃ bahuvistaram ॥ 1.50.11 ॥

tato gabhastalaṃ nāma parataśca mahātalam।
śrītalañca tataḥ prāhuḥ pātālaṃ saptamaṃsmṛtam ॥ 1.50.12 ॥

pāṇḍubhaumaṃ dvitīyantu tṛtīyaṃ raktamṛttikam ॥ 1.50.13 ॥

pītabhaumañcaturthantu pañcamaṃ śarkarātalam।
ṣaṣṭhaṃ silāmayañcaiva sauvarṇaṃ saptamantalam ॥ 1.50.14 ॥

prathame tu tale khyātamasurendrasya mandiram।
namucerindraśatrorhi mahānādasya cālayam ॥ 1.50.15 ॥

purañca śaṅkukarṇasya kabandhasya ca mandiram।


dhanañjayasya ca puraṃ māhendrasya mahātmanaḥ ।
kāliyasya ca nāgasya nagaraṃ kalasasya ca ॥ 1.50.18 ॥


dvitīye'pi tale viprā daityendrasya surakṣasaḥ।
mahājambhasya ca tathā nagaraṃ prathamasya tu ॥ 1.50.20 ॥

hayagrīvasya kṛṣṇasya nikumbhasya ca mandiram।
śaṅkhākhyeyasya ca puraṃ nagaraṃ gomukhasya ca ॥ 1.50.21 ॥

rākṣasasya ca nīlasya meghasya krathanasya ca।

kambalasya ca nāgasya puramaśvatarasya ca।
kadruputrasya ca puraṃ takṣakasya mahātmanaḥ ॥ 1.50.23 ॥


anuhlādasya ca puraṃ detyendrasya mahātmanaḥ ॥ 1.50.25 ॥


cyavanasya ca vijñeyaṃ rākṣasasya ca mandiram।
rākṣasendrasya ca puraṃkumbhilasya kharasyaca ॥ 1.50.27 ॥

virādhasya ca krūrasya puramulkāmukhasya ca।
hemakasya ca nāgasya tathā pāṇḍurakasya ca ॥ 1.50.28 ॥

maṇimantrasya ca puraṃ kapilasya ca mandiram।
nandasya coragapaterviśālasya ca mandiram ॥ 1.50.29 ॥


caturthe daityasiṃhasya kālanemermahātmanaḥ।
gajakarṇasya ca puraṃ nagaraṃ kuñjarasya ca ॥ 1.50.31 ॥

rākṣasendrasya ca puraṃ sumālerbahuvistaram।
muñjasya lokanāthasya vṛkavaktrasya cālayam ॥ 1.50.32 ॥

bahuyojanasāhasraṃ bahupakṣisamākulam ।
nagaraṃ vainateyasya caturthe'smin rasātale ॥ 1.50.33 ॥

pañcame śarkarābhaume bahuyojanavistṛte।
virocanasya nagaraṃ daityasiṃhasya dhīmataḥ ॥ 1.50.34 ॥

purañca vidyujjihvasya rākṣasasya ca dhīmataḥ ॥ 1.50.35 ॥

karmmārasya ca nāgasya svastikasya jayasya ca ॥ 1.50.36 ॥


ṣaṣṭe tale daityapateḥ kesarernagarottamam।
suparvvaṇaḥ sulomnaśca nagaraṃ mahiṣasya ca।
rākṣasendrasya ca puramutkrośasya mahātmanaḥ ॥ 1.50.38 ॥



saptame tu tale jñeyaṃ pātāle sarvvapaścime ।

mucukundasya daityasya tatra vai nagaraṃ mahat ॥ 1.50.42 ॥

tathaiva nāganagaraiṛddhimadbhiḥ sahasraśaḥ ॥ 1.50.43 ॥




prabhurmukhasahasreṇa śobhate vai sa kuṇḍalī ॥ 1.50.47 ॥


sa tu netrasahasreṇa dviguṇena virājatā।

tasya kundenduvarṇasya akṣamālā virājate ।
taruṇādi tyamāleva śvetaparvvatamūrddhani ॥ 1.50.50 ॥

jaṭākarālo dyutimān lakṣyate śayanāsane।




ataḥparamanālokyamagamyaṃ siddhasādhubhiḥ।
devā mānapyaviditaṃ vyavahāravivarjjitam ॥ 1.50.55 ॥


ata ūrddhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi sūryācandramasorgatim।
sūryācandramasāvetau bhramantau yāvadeva tu।

vistarārddhaṃ pṛthivyāstu bhavedanyatra bāhyataḥ ॥ 1.50.58 ॥


avati trīnimān lokān yasmāt sūryaḥ paribhraman।

mahitatvānmahīśabdo hyasmin varṣe nipātyate ॥ 1.50.61 ॥

asya bhāratavarṣasya viṣkambhantu suvistaram।

navayojanasāhasro vistāro bhāskarasya tu।



abhimānivyatītā ye tulyāste sāmpratairiha।
devā ye vai hyatītāste rūpairnāmabhireva ca ॥ 1.50.66 ॥

tasmāttu sāmpratairdevairvakṣyāmi vasudhātalam।
divastu sanniveśo vai sāmprataireva kṛtsnaśaḥ ॥ 1.50.67 ॥

tasyā vārdhapramāṇena merorvai cāturantaram ॥ 1.50.68 ॥



pṛthivyā vistaraṃ kṛtsnaṃ yojanaistannibodhata।



ityetadvai prasaṅkhyātaṃ pṛthivyantasya maṇḍalam ॥ 1.50.74 ॥



uparyupari lokānāṃ chatravatparimaṇḍalam ॥ 1.50.77 ॥


bhūrlokasva bhuvaścaiva tṛtīyaḥ svariti smṛtaḥ ।
maharloko janaścaiva tapaḥ satyaśca saptamaḥ ॥ 1.50.79 ॥




ghanodadhipareṇātha dhāryyate ghanatejasā।
bāhyato ghanatejastu tiryyagūrddhvantu maṇḍalam ॥ 1.50.83 ॥





dakṣiṇena punarmerormānasasyaiva mūrddhani।
vaivasvato nivasati yamaḥ saṃyamane pure ॥ 1.50.87 ॥

pratīcyāntu punarmerormānasasyaiva mūrddhani।

diśyuttarasyāṃ merostu mānasasyaiva mūrddhani।

sthitā dharmmavyavasthāyai lokasaṃrakṣaṇāya ca ॥ 1.50.90 ॥


dakṣiṇe prakrame sūryyaḥ kṣipteṣuriva sarpati।

vaivasvate saṃyamane udayastatra ucyate ॥ 1.50.93 ॥

sukhāyāmarddharātrañca madhyagaḥ syādraviryadā।



evamevottareṣvarko bhavaneṣu virājate ॥ 1.50.97 ॥


rātryarddhaṃ cāmarāvatyāmastameti yamasya ca।


sa śīgrameti paryyeti bhāskaro'lātacakravat।


pūrvvāhne cāparāhne tu dvau dvau devālayau tu saḥ।
tapatyekantu madhyāhne taireva tu saraśmibhiḥ ॥ 1.50.103 ॥

udito varddhamānābhirāmadhyāhnaṃ tapan raviḥ।
ataḥ paraṃ hrasantībhirgobhirastaṃ sa gacchati ॥ 1.50.104 ॥



sarveṣāmuttare merurlokālokastu dakṣiṇe।
hriyante raśmayo yasmāttena rātrau na dṛśyate ॥ 1.50.107 ॥

ucchrāyasya pramāṇena jñeyamastamanodayam ॥ 1.50.108 ॥


lekhayāvasthitaḥ sūryo yatra yatra tu dṛśyate।

prabhā hi saurī pādena astaṅgacchati bhāskare।

saṃyukto vahninā sūryastataḥ sa tapate divā ॥ 1.50.112 ॥


uttare caiva bhūmyarddhe tathā tasmiṃśca dakṣiṇe।

tasmācchuklā bhavantyāpo naktamahnaḥ praveśanāt ॥ 1.50.115 ॥

etena kramayogena bhūmyarddhe dakṣiṇottare।
udayāstamane'rkasya ahorātraṃ viśatyapaḥ ॥ 1.50.116 ॥

evaṃ puṣkaramadhyena yadā sarpati bhāskaraḥ।



paryyāgacchettadādityo māghe kāṣṭāntameva hi ॥ 1.50.120 ॥




maṇḍalaṃ viṣuvadyāpi yojanaistannibodhata।







te tu vakṣyāmi saṅkhyāya yojanaistannibodhata ॥ 1.50.131 ॥

ekaikamantarantasyā niyutānyekasaptatiḥ।

abhyantarantu paryeti maṇḍalānyuttarāyaṇe ॥ 1.50.133 ॥

bāhyato dakṣiṇe caiva satatantu yathākramam।


śate dve punarapyanye yojanānāṃ prakīrttite ॥ 1.50.136 ॥

ekaviṃśatibhiścaiva yojanairadhikairhi te।

viṣkambho maṇḍalasyaiṣa tiryak sa tu vidhīyate।
pratyahañcarate tāni sūryo vai maṇḍalakramam ॥ 1.50.138 ॥

kulālacakraparyanto yathā śīghraṃ nivarttate।



kulālacakramadhyastu yathā mandaṃ prasarpati।
tathodagayane sūryaḥ sarpate mandavikramaḥ ॥ 1.50.142 ॥


aharbhavati taccāpi carate mandavikramaḥ ॥ 1.50.144 ॥


tato mandataraṃ tābhyāñcakraṃ bhramati vai yathā।
mṛtpiṇḍa iva madhyastho dhruvo bhramati vai yathā ॥ 1.50.146 ॥


kulālacakranābhistu yathā tatraiva varttate।
dhruvastathā hi vijñeyastatraiva parivarttate ॥ 1.50.148 ॥


uttare prakrame tvindo rdivā mandā gatiḥ smṛtā।
tathaiva ca punarnaktaṃ śīghrā sūryasya vai gatiḥ ॥ 1.50.150 ॥


tathā vicarate mārgaṃ samena viṣameṇa ca ॥ 1.50.152 ॥





abhyantaraṃ prakāśante lokālokasya vai gireḥ ॥ 1.50.157 ॥

etāvāneva lokastu nirālokastvataḥ param।
lokāloka ekadhā tu nirālokastvanekadhā ॥ 1.50.158 ॥

lokālokantu sandhatte yasmāt sūryaḥ parigraham।


prārthayanti sahasrāṃśumudayanti dine dine।
tāpayanto durātmānaḥ sūryamicchanti khāditum ॥ 1.50.161 ॥

sandhyeti samupāsantaḥ kṣepayanti mahājalam ॥ 1.50.162 ॥

tena dahyanti te daityā vajrabhūtena vāriṇā ॥ 1.50.163 ॥





lekhāprabhṛtyathāditye trimuhūrttāgate tu vai।


traya eva muhūrttāstu tasmāt kālācca madhyamāt ॥ 1.50.170 ॥


daśapañcamuhūrttādvai rātrindivamiti smṛtam ॥ 1.50.172 ॥

vardhdhate hrasate caiva ayane dakṣiṇottare।
ahastu grasate rātriṃ rātristu grasate tvahaḥ ॥ 1.50.173 ॥

śaradvasantayormadhye viṣuvantadvibhāvyate।

ṛtutrayamayanaṃ syāddvai'yane varṣamucyate ॥ 1.50.175 ॥


śataghnaikonakāstriṃśanmātrātriśat ṣaḍuttarā।

saptatiñcāpi tatraiva navatiṃ viddhi nisvaye ॥ 1.50.178 ॥

catvāryeva śatānyāhurvidyutau vaidhasaṃyuge।

niścayaḥ sarvakālasya yuga ityabhidhīyate ॥ 1.50.180 ॥

saṃvatsarastu prathamo dvitīyaḥ parivatsaraḥ।
idvatsarastṛtīyastu caturthaścānuvatsaraḥ।




mānaṃ taccitrabhānostu vijñeyaṃ bhuvanasya tu ॥ 1.50.185 ॥



taiścāpi śrṛṅgavānnāma sarvataścaiva viśrutaḥ।
ekamārgaśca vistāro viṣkambhaścāpi kīrtitaḥ ॥ 1.50.188 ॥

tasya vai sarvataḥ śrṛṅgaṃ madhyamantaddhiraṇmayam।

sarvaratnamayaṃ caikaṃ śrṛṅgamuttaramuttamam।

yattadviṣuvataṃ śrṛṅgantadarkaḥ pratipadyate।
śaradvasantayormadhye madhyamāṃ gatimāsthitaḥ।
ahastulyāmatho rātriṃ karoti timirāpahaḥ ॥ 1.50.191 ॥

anuliptā ivābhānti pajharaktairgabhastibhiḥ ॥ 1.50.192 ॥





samā rātrirahaścaiva yadā tadviṣuvadbhavet।
brāhmaṇebhyo viśeṣeṇa mukhametattu daivatam ॥ 1.50.197 ॥


tapastapasyau madhumādhavau ca śukraḥ śuciścāyanamuttaraṃ syāt।



evaṃ jñātvā na muhyeta daive pitrye ca mānavaḥ।

ālokāntaḥ smṛto loko lokānto loka ucyate।
lokapālāḥ sthitāstatra lokālokasya madhyataḥ ॥ 1.50.203 ॥




tatrāsate prajāvanto munayo hyagnihotriṇaḥ ।


calitante punarddharmaṃ sthāpayanti yuge yuge।



icchādveṣaprakṛtyā ca maithunopagamena ca ॥ 1.50.212 ॥

tathā kāyakṛteneha sevanādviṣayasya ca।
prajaiṣiṇaste munayo dvāpareṣviha jajñire ॥ 1.50.213 ॥

nāgavīthyuttare yacca saptarṣibhyaśca dakṣiṇam ।

satatante jugupsante tasmānmṛtyurjjitastu taiḥ ॥ 1.50.215 ॥


ityetaiḥ kāraṇaiḥ śuddhaiste' matatvaṃ hi bhejire।

trailokyasthitikālo'yamapunarmārgagāminaḥ।

ūrddhvottaramṛṣibhyastu dhruvo yatrāsti vai smṛtam।



## 1.51

।।sūta uvāca।।
svāyambhuve nisarge tu vyākhyātānyuttarāṇi tu।

etacchrutvā tu munayaḥ papracchurlomaharṣaṇam।

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।
kaśca bhrāmayate tāni bhramanti yadi vā svayam ॥ 1.51.3 ॥

etadveditumicchāmastanno nigada sattam।
bhūtasammohanantvetacchrotumicchā pravarttate ॥ 1.51.4 ॥

।।sūta uvāca।।
bhūtasammohanaṃ hyetad bruvato me nibodhata।
pratyakṣamapi dṛsyaṃ yattat saṃmohayate prajāḥ ॥ 1.51.5 ॥

yo'sau caturdiśaṃ pucche śiśumāre vyavasthitaḥ।
uttānapādaputro'sau meḍhībhūto dhruvo divi ॥ 1.51.6 ॥

sa hi bhraman bhrāmayate candrādityau grahaiḥ saha।
bhramantamanugacchanti nakṣatrāṇi ca cakravat ॥ 1.51.7 ॥

dhruvasya manasā cāsau sarpate bhagaṇaḥ svayam।

vātānīkamayairbandhairdhruve baddhāni tāni vai।

astodayau tathotpātā ayane dakṣiṇottare।
viṣuvad‌grahavarṇāśca dhruvātsarvaṃ pravarttate ॥ 1.51.10 ॥



parivartta kramādviprā bhābhirālokayan diśaḥ।
jagato jalamādatte kṛtsnasya dvijasattamāḥ ॥ 1.51.13 ॥


yatsomāt sravate sūryastadagreṣvavatiṣṭhate।
meghā vāyunighātena visṛjanti jalambhuvi ॥ 1.51.15 ॥

ekamutkṣipyate caiva patate ca punarjjalam।
nānāprakāramudakantadeva parivarttate ॥ 1.51.16 ॥



sarvalaukikamambho vai yatsomānnabhasaḥ snutam।
somādhāraṃ jagatsarvametattathyaṃ prakīrtitam ॥ 1.51.19 ॥


somaputrapurogāśca mahānadyo dvijottamāḥ ॥ 1.51.21 ॥


ārkantejo hi bhūtebhyo hyādatte raśmibhirjalam ॥ 1.51.23 ॥

samudrādvāyusaṃyogādvahantyāpo gabhastayaḥ।
yacchatyapo hi meghebyaḥ śuklāḥ śuklagabhastibhiḥ ॥ 1.51.24 ॥


vāyavyaṃ stanitañcaiva vaidyutañcāgnisaṃbhavam ॥ 1.51.26 ॥

mehanācca miherddhātormeghatvaṃ vyañjayanti ca।
na bhraśyanti yatastvāpastadabhraṃ kavayo viduḥ ॥ 1.51.27 ॥

meghānāṃ punarutpattistrividhā yonirucyate।


bhūtvā dharaṇimabhyetya vicaranti ramanti ca ॥ 1.51.30 ॥



parvatāgranitambeṣu varṣanti ca ramanti ca।

te hi vidyudguṇopetāḥ stanayanti svanapriyāḥ ॥ 1.51.34 ॥








tasmādbrahmā samutpannaścaturvaktraḥ svayambhuvaḥ।



parjanyo diggajāścaiva hemante śītasambhavāḥ।

divyāmatijalāṃ puṇyāṃ vidyāṃ svargapathisthitām ॥ 1.51.46 ॥


udag himavataḥ śailāduttarasya ca dakṣiṇe।

tasminnipatitaṃ varṣaṃ yattuṣārasamudbhavam ।
tatastadāvaho vāyurhimaśailāt samudvahan।
ānayatyātmayogena siñcamāno mahāgirim ॥ 1.51.49 ॥


meghāvāpyāyataścaiva sarvametat prakīrttitam ।



ataḥ sūryarathasyātha sanniveśaṃ nibodhata।

hiraṇmayena bhagavān parvaṇā tu mahaujasā।
cakreṇa bhāsvatā sūryaḥ syandanena prasarppati ॥ 1.51.55 ॥


asaṅgaḥ kāñcano divyo yuktaḥ paramagairhayaiḥ ॥ 1.51.57 ॥

chandobhirvājirūpaistu yataḥ śukrastataḥ sthitaḥ।
te nā'sau sarpativyomni bhāsvatā tu divākaraḥ ॥ 1.51.58 ॥

athemāni tu sūryasya pratyaṅgāni rathasya tu।
saṃvatsarasyāvayavaiḥ kalpitāni yathā kramam ॥ 1.51.59 ॥


rathanīḍaḥ smṛto hyabdastvayane kūbarāvubhau ।


rātrirvarūtho gharmo'sya dhvaja ūrddhvasamucchritaḥ।


akṣe cakraṃ nibaddhantu dhruve tvakṣaḥ samarpitaḥ ॥ 1.51.65 ॥

sahacakro bhramatyakṣaḥ sahākṣo bhramati dhruvaḥ।
akṣaḥ sahaiva cakreṇa bhramate'sau dhruveritaḥ ॥ 1.51.66 ॥

evamartha vaśāttasya sanniveśo rathasya tu।
tathā saṃyogabhāgena saṃsiddho bāsvaro rathaḥ ॥ 1.51.67 ॥

tenā'sau taraṇirdevastarasā sarpate divi।
yugākṣakoṭisambaddhau raśmī dvau syandanasya hi ॥ 1.51.68 ॥

dhruveṇa bhramato raśmī vicakrayugayostu vai।
bhramato maṇḍalāni syuḥ khecarasya rathasya tu ॥ 1.51.69 ॥

dhruveṇa saṃgṛhīte vai dvicakraśvetarajjuvat ॥ 1.51.70 ॥

bhramantamanugacchetāṃ dhruvaṃ raśmī tu tāvubhau ।

kīlāsakto yathā rajjurbhramate sarvato diśam।

varddhete dakṣiṇe caiva bhramato maṇḍalāni tu।
dhruveṇa saṃgṛhītau tu rasmī vai nayato ravim ॥ 1.51.73 ॥



udveṣṭayan sa vegena maṇḍalāni tu gacchati ॥ 1.51.76 ॥


## 1.52

।।sūta uvāca।।
ete vasanti vai sūrye dvau dvau māsau krameṇa tu।
tumbururnāradaścaiva gandharvau gāyatāṃ varau ॥ 1.52.3 ॥
kratusthalyapsarāścaiva tathā vai puñjikasthalī।
madhumādhavayoreṣa gaṇo vasati bhāskare ॥ 1.52.5 ॥
vāsantau graiṣmikau māsau mitraśca varuṇaśca ha।
menakā sahajanyā ca gandharvau ca hahā huhūḥ।
rathasvanaśca grāmaṇyo rathacitraśca tāvubhau ॥ 1.52.7 ॥
pramloceti ca vikhyātā nimloceti ca te ubhe।
nabhonabhasyayoreṣa gaṇo vasati bhāskare ॥ 1.52.11 ॥
viśvāvasuśca gandharvāstathaiva surabhiśca yaḥ।
vasantyete tu vai sūrye māsayośca iṣorjayoḥ ॥ 1.52.15 ॥
haimantikau tu dvau māsau vasanti tu divākare।
citrasenaśca gandharva ūrṇāyuścaiva tāvubhau ॥ 1.52.17 ॥
urvaśī vipracittiśca tathaivāpsarasau śubhe।
sahe caiva sahasye ca vasantyete divākare ॥ 1.52.19 ॥
ṛtajitsatyajiccaiva grāmaṇyau lokaviśrutau ॥ 1.52.22 ॥
brahmopetastathā dakṣo yajñopetaśca sa smṛtaḥ।
ete devā vasantyarke dvau māsau tu krameṇa tu ॥ 1.52.23 ॥
sūryamāpyāyayantyete tejasā teja uttamam ॥ 1.52.24 ॥
prathitaistairvacobistu stuvanti munayo ravim।
gandharvāpsarasaścaiva gītanṛtyairupāsate ॥ 1.52.25 ॥
vālakhilyā nayantyastaṃ paricāryodayādravim ॥ 1.52.26 ॥
yathā tapatyasau sūryasteṣāṃ siddhastu tejasā।
ityete vai vasantīha dvau dvau māsau divākare ॥ 1.52.28 ॥
ete tapanti varṣanti bhānti vānti sṛjanti ca।
duritaṃ hi pracārāṇāṃ vyapohanti vkacit vkacit ॥ 1.52.31 ॥
vimāne'vasthitā divye kāmagā vātaraṃhasaḥ।
ete sahaiva sūryeṇa bhramanti divasānugāḥ ॥ 1.52.32 ॥
evaṃ vasanti vai sūrye saptakāste caturddiśam।
caturddaśasu sargeṣu gaṇā manvantareṣu ca ॥ 1.52.35 ॥
sudhāmṛtaṃ tatpitaraḥ pibanti devāśca saumyāśca tathaiva kavyam ॥ 1.52.38 ॥
annena śaśvattu dadhāti martyān sūryaḥ svayaṃ tacca bibharti gobhiḥ ॥ 1.52.40 ॥
ayaṃ haristairharibhisturaṅgamairayan hi cāpo haratīti raśmibhiḥ।
harirharidbhirhriyate turaṅgamaiḥ pibatyathāpo haribhiḥ sahasradhā।
tataḥ pramuñcatyapi tāstvasau hariḥ sa muhyamāno haribhisturaṅgamaiḥ ॥ 1.52.42 ॥
bhadraistairakṣatairaśvaiḥ sarpate'sau divi kṣaye ॥ 1.52.43 ॥
ahorātrādrathaināsau ekacakreṇa tu bhraman।
saptadvīpasamudrāntaṃ saptabhiḥ saptabhirhayaiḥ ॥ 1.52.44 ॥
chandobhiraśvarūpaistairyataścakrantataḥ sthitaiḥ।
kāmarūpaiḥ sakṛdyuktairamitaistairmanojavaiḥ ॥ 1.52.45 ॥
haritairavyayaiḥ piṅgairīśvarairbrahmavādibhiḥ।
bāhyamabhyantarañcaiva maṇḍalaṃ divasakramāt ।
kalpādau samprayuktāste vahantyābhūtasamplavāt।
prathitairvacobhaṅiragryaiḥ stūyamāno maharṣibhiḥ।
saivyate gītavṛtyaiśca gandharvairapsarogaṇaiḥ।
pataṅgaḥ pata gairaśvairbhramamāṇo divaspatiḥ ॥ 1.52.48 ॥
śatāraiśca tribhiścakrairyuktaḥ śulkairhayottamaiḥ ॥ 1.52.50 ॥
daśa bhistu kṛśairdivyairasaṅgaistairmanojavaiḥ।
sakṛdyukte rathe tasmin vahante cāyugakṣayāt ॥ 1.52.51 ॥
aśvā stamekavarṇāste vahante śaṅkhavarccasam ॥ 1.52.52 ॥
asvo vāmasturaṇyaśca haṃso vyomī mṛgastathā ॥ 1.52.53 ॥
ityete nāmabhiḥ sarve daśa candramaso hayāḥ ।
ete candramasaṃ devaṃ vahanti divasakṣayāt ॥ 1.52.54 ॥
devaiḥ parivṛtaḥ saumyaḥ pitṛbhiścaiva gacchati।
somasya śukla pakṣādau bhāskare purataḥ sthite।
tato dvitīyāprabhṛti bahulasya caturddaśī।
apāṃ sāramayasyendo rasamātrātmakasya ca।
pibantyambumayaṃ devā madhu saumyaṃ sudhāma yam ॥ 1.52.60 ॥
aparāhne pitṛgaṇairjaghanyaḥ paryupāsyate ॥ 1.52.65 ॥
saumyā barhiṣadaścaiva agniṣvāttāstathaiva ca।
kavyāścaiva tu ye proktāḥ pitaraḥ sarva eva te ॥ 1.52.67 ॥
amāvasyāntadā tasya antamāpūryate param।
toyatejomayaḥ śubhraḥ somaputrasya vai rathaḥ।
savarūthaḥ sānukarṣaḥ sūto divyo rathe mahān।
sopāsaṅgapatākastu sadhvajo meghasannibhaḥ ॥ 1.52.73 ॥
pṛthivīsambhavairyukto nānāvarṇairhayottamaiḥ ॥ 1.52.74 ॥
asaṅgairlauhitairaśvaiḥ sarvagairagnisambhavaiḥ।
sarpate'sau kumāro vai ṛjuvakrānucakragaḥ ॥ 1.52.76 ॥
śoṇairaśvaiḥ kāñcanena syandanena prasarpati ॥ 1.52.77 ॥
yuktastu vājibhirdivyairaṣṭābhirvātasammitaiḥ।
nakṣatre'bdannivasati savegastena gacchati ॥ 1.52.78 ॥
rathantamomayantasya sakṛdyuktā vahantyuta ॥ 1.52.80 ॥
ādityameti somācca punaḥ saureṣu parvasu ॥ 1.52.81 ॥
sarve dhruvanibaddhāste prabaddhā vātaraśmibhiḥ ॥ 1.52.83 ॥
ete vai bhrāmyamāṇāstu yathā yogaṃ bhramanti vai।
paribhramanti tadbaddhāścandrasūryagrahā divi।
bhramantamanugacchanti dhruvante jyotiṣāṃ gaṇāḥ ॥ 1.52.85 ॥
yathā nadyudake naustu salilena sahohyate।
tasmātsarveṇa dṛsyante vyomni devagaṇāstu te ॥ 1.52.86 ॥
sarvā dhruvanibaddhāstā bramantyo bhrāmayanti tam ॥ 1.52.87 ॥
alātacakravadyānti vātacakreritāni tu।
evaṃ dhruvanibaddho'sau sarpate jyotiṣāṃ gaṇaḥ।
uttānapādastasyātha vijñeyo hyuttaro hanuḥ ॥ 1.52.92 ॥
varuṇaścāryamā caiva paścime tasya sasthini।
pucche'gniśca mahendraśca marīciḥ kaśyapo dhruvaḥ।
eka eva bhramatyeṣa meruparvatamūrddhani ॥ 1.52.98 ॥


## 1.53

।। śāṃśapāyana uvāca ।।
papracchuruttaraṃ bhūyastadā te lomaharṣaṇam ॥ 1.53.1 ॥
।।ṛṣaya ucuḥ ।।
tadvo'haṃ sampravakṣyāmi sūryācandramasorbhavam।
ataḥ paraṃ trividhāgnervakṣye'hantu samudbhavam।
divyasya bhautikasyāgnerathāgneḥ pārthivasya ca ॥ 1.53.5 ॥
caturbhūtāvaśiṣṭe'smin pārthivaḥ so'gnirucyate।
tasmādapaḥ piban sūryo gobhidīrpyatyasau divi ॥ 1.53.8 ॥
pādena pārthivasyāgnestasmādagnistapatyasau ॥ 1.53.12 ॥
uttare caiva bhūmyarddhe tasmādasmiṃśca dakṣiṇe।
etena kramayogena bhūmyarddhe dakṣiṇottare ।
udayāstamaye nityamahorātraṃ viśatyapaḥ ॥ 1.53.16 ॥
yaścāsau tapate sūrye pibannambho gabhastibhiḥ।
manuṣyānauṣadheneha svadhayā ca pitṝnapi।
amṛtena surān sarvāṃstrīṃstribhistarpayatyasau ॥ 1.53.24 ॥
vasante caiva grīṣme ca sataiḥ sutapate tribhiḥ।
varṣāsvatho śaradi ca caturbhiḥ samprakarṣati ॥ 1.53.25 ॥
hemante śiśire caiva himaṃ sa sṛja te tribhiḥ।
oṣadhīṣu balandhatte svadhayā ca pitṝnapi।
sūryo'maratvamamṛtatrayantriṣu niyacchati ॥ 1.53.26 ॥
evaṃ raśmisahasrantat sauraṃ lokārtha sādhakam।
rudro vaivasvataḥ sākṣāddharmo loke prabhuḥ svayam।
devāsuragurū dvau tu bhānumantau mahāgrahau।
daityo mahendraśca tayorādhipatye vinirmitau ॥ 1.53.33 ॥
bhavatyasya jagatkṛtsnaṃ sadevāsuramānuṣam ॥ 1.53.34 ॥
rudrendropendracandrāṇāṃ viprendrāstridivaukasām।
dyutirdyutimatāṃ kṛtsnā yattejaḥ sārvalaukikam ॥ 1.53.35 ॥
sarvātmā sarvalokeśo mūlaṃ paramadaivatam।
tataḥ saṃjāyate sarvaṃ tatra caiva pralīyate ॥ 1.53.36 ॥
yatra gacchanti nidhanaṃ jāyante ca punaḥ punaḥ।
jagatpratāpanamṛte bhāskaraṃ vāritaskaram ॥ 1.53.41 ॥
suṣumno harikeśaśca viśvakarmā tathaiva ca।
tiryagūrddhvaprabhāvo'sau suṣumnaḥ parikīrtyate ॥ 1.53.46 ॥
dakṣiṇe viśvakarmā tu raśmirvarddhayate budham ॥ 1.53.47 ॥
sampadvasuśca yo raśmiḥ sā yonirlohitasya ca ॥ 1.53.48 ॥
varddhante viditāḥ sarvā viśvañcaidaṃ punarjagat ।
suvati spandanārthe ca dhātureṣa vibhāvyate।
bahvarthaścandra ityeṣa hlādane dhāturiṣyate ।
suryācandramasordivye maṇḍale bhāsvare khage।
jvalattejomaye śukle vṛttakumbhanibhe śubhe ॥ 1.53.56 ॥
sauraṃ sūryo viśasthānaṃ saumyaṃ somastathaiva ca ॥ 1.53.59 ॥
bṛhadbṛhaspatiścaiva lohitaścaiva lauhitaḥ ।
tulyastayostu svarbhānurbhūtvādhastāt prasarpati।
svarbhānostu bṛhat sthānannirmitaṃ yattamomayam।
ādityāttacca niṣkramya somaṃ gacchati parvasu ॥ 1.53.64 ॥
ādityameti somācca punaḥ somañca parvasu।
svarbhāsānudate yasmāttataḥ svarbhānurucyate ॥ 1.53.65 ॥
buhaspateḥ pādahīnau kujasaurāvubhau smṛtau ।
yojanānyarddhamātrāṇi tebhyo hrasvaṃ na vidyate ॥ 1.53.70 ॥
tebhyo'dhastāttu catvāraḥ punaranye mahāgrahāḥ।
gatistāstveva sūryasya nīcoccatve'yanakramāt।
kalpādau saṃpravṛttāni nirmitāni svayambhuvā।
manvantareṣu sarveṣu devatāyatanāni vai।
varttante varttamānaiśca sthānāni svaiḥ suraiḥ saha ॥ 1.53.78 ॥
vivasvānaditeḥ putraḥ sūryo vaivasvate'ntare ॥ 1.53.79 ॥
tviṣimāndharmaputrastu somadevo vasuḥ smṛtaḥ।
śukro devastu vijñeyo bhārgavo'surarājakaḥ ॥ 1.53.80 ॥
budho manoharaścaiva tviṣiputrastu sa smṛtaḥ ॥ 1.53.81 ॥
somarkṣagrahasūrye tu kīrtitāstvabhimāninaḥ ॥ 1.53.83 ॥
navaraśmestu yūno hi lohitasthānamammayam ॥ 1.53.86 ॥
uccatvāddṛśyate śīghramabhivyaktairgabhastibhiḥ।
samānakālāstamayau viṣuvatsu samodayau ॥ 1.53.93 ॥
uttarāsu ca vīthīṣu vyantarāstamayodayau।
grahāśca candrasūryau tu divi divyena tejasā।
nityamṛkṣeṣu yujyanti gacchanti niyamakramāt ॥ 1.53.99 ॥
samāgame ca bhede ca paśyanti yugapat prajāḥ ॥ 1.53.100 ॥
parasparasthitāḥ hyete yujyante ca parasparam।
tviṣimān dharmmaputrastu somo viśvāvasustathā।
navārcirlohitāṅgastu prajāpatisuto grahaḥ ।
rohiṇīṣu samutpannau grahau candrārkamarddanau ॥ 1.53.109 ॥
bhānorgativiśeṣeṇa cakravat parivarttate ॥ 1.53.116 ॥
buddhipūrvambhagavatā kalpādau saṃprakīrtitaḥ।
pañcaite hetavo jñeyā jyotirgaṇavicintane ॥ 1.53.123 ॥


## 1.54

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
kasmin deśe mahāpuṇyametadākhyānamuttamam।

।।sūta uvāca।।



stuvanti ca mahādevaṃ yatra yathāvidhi ॥ 1.54.5 ॥


pravṛtte jyotiṣāṃ cakre madhyavyāpte divākare।
atha niyamapravṛttāśca prāṇaśeṣavyavasthitāḥ ॥ 1.54.7 ॥



।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
nīlakaṇṭeti yat proktaṃ tvayā pavanasattama।

tadvayaṃ śrotumicchāmastvatprasādātprabhañjana।


varṇasthānagate vāyau vāgvidhiḥ saṃpravartate।
jñānaṃ pūrvamathotsāha stvatto vāyo pravartate ।। 54.13 .
yatra vāco nivartante dehabandhāśca durlabhāḥ ॥ 1.54.13 ॥

tatrāpi te'sti sadbhāvaḥ sarvagastvaṃ sadānila।

eṣa vai jīvalokaste pratyakṣaḥ sarvato'nila।
vettha vācaspatiṃ devaṃ manonāyakamīsvaram ॥ 1.54.15 ॥


।।vāyuruvāca।।
purā kṛtayuge vipro vedanirṇayatatparaḥ।

prapaccha kārtikeyaṃ vai mayūravaravāhanam।


vasiṣṭhaḥ pṛcchate bhaktyā kārtikeyaṃ mahābalam ॥ 1.54.20 ॥

।।vasiṣṭha uvāca।।
namaste haranandāya umāgarbha namo 'stu te।
namaste agnigarbhāya gaṅgāgarbha namo'stu te ॥ 1.54.21 ॥

namaste śaragarbhāya namaste kṛttikāsuta।

namaste śaktihastāya divyaghaṇṭāpatākine।
evaṃ stutvā mahāsenaṃ papraccha śikhivāhanam ॥ 1.54.23 ॥

tatkimarthaṃ samutpannaṃ kaṇṭe kundendusaṃprabhe ॥ 1.54.24 ॥





kailāsaśikhare ramye nānādhātuvicitrite।



kokilārāvamadhure siddha cāraṇasevite।
saurabhaiyīninādāḍhye adhastanitaniḥsvane ॥ 1.54.32 ॥

vināyakabhayodvigne kuñjarairyuktakandare।

dolālambitasampāte vanitāsaṅghasevite।

mukhamardalavāditrairbalināṃ sphoṭitaistathā।

hāsaiḥ santrāsajananairvikarālamukhaistathā।
dehagandhairvicitraiśca prakrīḍitagaṇeścaraiḥ ॥ 1.54.36 ॥

vyādhrasiṃhamukhaiśacavānyairgajavājimukhaistathā ॥ 1.54.37 ॥

hrasvairdīrghaiḥ kṛśaiḥ sthūlairlambodara mahodaraiḥ ॥ 1.54.38 ॥



bahunetrairmahānetrairekanaitrairanaitrakaiḥ ।
evaṃvidhairmahāyogibhūtairbhūtapatirvṛtaḥ ॥ 1.54.41 ॥

viśuddhamuktāmaṇiratnabhūṣite śilātale hemamaye manorame।

।।devyuvāca।।
tava kaṇṭe mahādeva bhrājate'mbudaśannibham ॥ 1.54.43 ॥




agre samutthitaṃ tasmin viṣaṃ kālānalaprabham ॥ 1.54.47 ॥




trailokyasyeśvarā yūyaṃ sarve vai vigatajvarāḥ।

vimānagāminaḥ sarve sarve svacchandagāminaḥ।
adhyātme cādhibhūte ca adhidaive ca nityaśaḥ।



surāsurairmathyamāne pāthodhau ca mahātmabhiḥ।

trailokyotsādi sūryābhaṃ prasphurantaṃ samantataḥ ॥ 1.54.56 ॥




yattadagre samutpannaṃ mathyamāne mahodadhau ॥ 1.54.60 ॥



ityuktvā pajhagarbhābhaḥ pajhayonirayonijaḥ ।

namaḥ pinākahastāya vajrahastāya vai namaḥ ॥ 1.54.64 ॥


brahamaṇe caiva rudrāya viṣṇave caiva te namaḥ।

rudrāya ca sureśāya devadevāya te namaḥ। 54.68।

buddhāya caiva śuddhāya muktāya kevalāya ca ॥ 1.54.68 ॥

loka trayavalidhātre ca candrāya varuṇāya ca ॥ 1.54.69 ॥

rajase caiva sattvāya tamase'vyaktayonaye ॥ 1.54.70 ॥

vyaktāya caivāvyaktāya vyaktāvyaktāya vai namaḥ ॥ 1.54.71 ॥

cintyāya caivācintyāya cintyācintyāya vai namaḥ।



dhvajine rathine caiva yamine brahmacāriṇe।
ityevamādicaritaistubhyaṃ deva namo'stu te ॥ 1.54.75 ॥

।।śrīmahādeva uvāca।।
evaṃ stutastato devaiḥ praṇipatya varānane ॥ 1.54.76 ॥

sūkṣmo'tiyogātiśayādacintyo na hi pluto vyaktamupaiti candraḥ ॥ 1.54.77 ॥

stuto'haṃ vividhaiḥ stotrai rvedavedāṅgasambhavaiḥ ॥ 1.54.78 ॥

tataḥ prīto hyahaṃ tasmai brahmaṇe sumahātmane।

bhagavan bhūtabhavyeśa lokanātha jagatpate।
kiṃ kārye te mayā brahman karttavyaṃ vada suvrata ॥ 1.54.80 ॥


surāsurairmathyamāne payodhāvambujekṣaṇa।

kālamṛtyurivodbhūtaṃ yugāntādityavarccasam ॥ 1.54.83 ॥

trailokyotsādi sūryābhaṃ visphurantaṃ samantataḥ।
agre samutthitaṃ tasmin viṣaṅkālānalaprabham ॥ 1.54.84 ॥

bhavānagryasya bhoktā vai bhavāṃśvaiva varaḥ prabhuḥ ॥ 1.54.85 ॥

nāsti kaścit pumān śaktastrailokyeṣu ca gīyate ॥ 1.54.86 ॥


tato'haṃ pātumārabdho viṣamantakasannibham।
pibato me mahāghoraṃ viṣaṃ surabhayaṅkaram।



tatastasya vacaḥ śrutvā mayā girivarātmaje।




aho balaṃ vīryaparākramaste aho punaryogabalaṃ tavaiva।
aho prabhutvaṃ tava devadeva gaṅgājalāsphālitamuktakeśa ॥ 1.54.95 ॥

tvameva viṣṇuścaturānanastvaṃ tvameva mṛtyu rvaradastvameva।
tvameva sūryo rajanīkaraśca tvameva bhūmiḥ salilaṃ tvameva ॥ 1.54.96 ॥

tvameva yajño niyamastvameva tvameva bhūtaṃ bhavitā tvameva।
tvameva cādi rnidhanaṃ tvameva sthūlaśca sūkṣmaḥ puruṣastvameva ॥ 1.54.97 ॥

tvameva sūkṣmasya parasya sūkṣmastvameva vahniḥ pavanastvameva।
tvameva sarvasya carācarasya lokasya karttā pralaye ca goptā ॥ 1.54.98 ॥

gatā vimānairanigṛhya vegairmahātmano merumupetya sarve ॥ 1.54.99 ॥

ityetatparamaṃ guhyaṃ puṇyātpuṇyamahattaram।

yastu dhārayate nityamenāṃ brahmodbhavāṃ kathām।



vivāde jayamāpnoti yuddhe śūratvameva ca।
gacchataḥ kṣemamadhvānaṃ gṛhe ca nityasampadaḥ ॥ 1.54.104 ॥


nandhitulyabalaḥ śrīmān nanditulyaparākramaḥ ॥ 1.54.106 ॥

na hanyate gatistasya anilasya yathāmbare।
mama tulyabalo bhūtvā tiṣṭhatyābhūtasamplavam ॥ 1.54.107 ॥


brāhmaṇo vedamāpnoti kṣatriyo jayate mahīm।

vyādhito mucyate rogādbaddho mucyeta bandhanāt।
gurviṇī labhate putraṃ kanyā vindati satpatim।
naṣṭañca labhate sarvamihaloke paratra ca ॥ 1.54.110 ॥

gavāṃ śatasahasrasya samyagdattasya yat phalam।

yastu dhārayate nityaṃ rudralokaṃ sa gacchati ॥ 1.54.112 ॥


krāntaṃ mayā pāpaharaṃ mahāpadaṃ nivedya tebhyaḥ pradadau prabhañjanaḥ।


## 1.55

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
guṇakarmaprabhāvaiśca ko'dhiko vadatāṃ varaḥ।
śrotumicchāmahe samyagāścaryaṃ guṇavistaram ॥ 1.55.1 ॥

।।sūta uvāca।।

baliṃ baddhvā mahaujāstu trailokyādhi patiḥ purā ॥ 1.55.3 ॥



abhigamya mahātmānaṃ stuvanti puruṣaṃ harim ॥ 1.55.6 ॥




tasyaiva ca prasādena ādau siddhatvamāgatam ॥ 1.55.10 ॥

purā tamasi cāvyakte trailokye grāsite mayā।


etasminnantare dūrāt paśyāmi hyamitaprabham।


tato māmabravīdbrahmā sarvaloka namaskṛtaḥ।
kastvaṃ kuto vā kiñceha tiṣṭhase vada me vibho ॥ 1.55.15 ॥

evamuktastadā dena brahmaṇāhamuvāca tam ॥ 1.55.16 ॥



varddhamāne tadā vahnāvatyantaparamādbhute।

tasya jvālasya madhye tu paśyāvo vipulaprabham ॥ 1.55.20 ॥


mahaujasaṃ mahāghoraṃ varddhamānaṃ bhṛśaṃ tadā।

asya liṅgasya yo'ntaṃ vai gacchate mantrakāraṇam।
ghora rūpiṇamatyarthaṃ bhindantamiva rodasī ॥ 1.55.23 ॥

tato māmabravīdbrahmā adho gaccha tvatandritaḥ।
antamasya vijānīmo liṅgasya tu mahātmanaḥ ॥ 1.55.24 ॥


tato varṣasahasrantu ahaṃ punaradho gataḥ।

tathā brahmā ca śrāntaśca na cāntantasya paśyati।
samāgato mayā sārddhaṃ tatraiva ca mahāmbhasi ॥ 1.55.27 ॥

tato vismayamāpannāvubhau tasya mahātmanaḥ ।।

māyayā mohitau tena naṣṭasaṃjñau vyavasthitau ॥ 1.55.28 ॥

tato dhyānagatantatra īśvaraṃ sarvatomukham।
prabhavaṃ nidhaऩñcaiva lokānāṃ prabhumavyayam ॥ 1.55.29 ॥

avyaktāya mahāntāya namaskāraṃ prakurmahe ॥ 1.55.30 ॥

namo'stu te lokasureśa deva namo'stu te bhūtapate mahāṃśca।
namo'stu te śāśvata siddhayone namo'stu te sarvajagatpratiṣṭha ॥ 1.55.31 ॥






tvaṃ dhārayasi lokāstrīṃstvameva sṛjasi prabho ॥ 1.55.37 ॥

pūrveṇa vadanena tvamindratvañca prakāśase।

paścimena tu vakreṇa varuṇatvaṃ karoṣi vai।
uttareṇa tu vakreṇa saumyatvañca vyavasthitam ॥ 1.55.39 ॥




tvattaḥ prasūtā deveśa sandhyā rātristathaiva ca ॥ 1.55.43 ॥

sūryāyutānāmayutaprabhā ca namo'stu te candrasahasragocara।
namo'stu te parvatarūpadhāriṇe namo'stu te sarvaguṇākarāya ॥ 1.55.44 ॥

namo'stu te paṭṭiśarūpadhāriṇe namo'stu te carmavibhūtidhāriṇe ।
namo'stu te rudrapinākapāṇaye namo'stu te sāyakacakradhāriṇe ॥ 1.55.45 ॥

namo'stu te deva hiraṇyavāsase namo'stu te deva hiraṇyabāhave ॥ 1.55.46 ॥

namo'stu te deva hiraṇyarūpa namo'stu te deva hiraṇyanābha।
namo'stu te netrasahasracitra namo'stu te deva hiraṇyaretaḥ ॥ 1.55.47 ॥

namo'stu te deva hiraṇyavarṇa namo'stu te deva hiraṇyagarbha।
namo'stu te deva hiraṇyacīra namo'stu te deva hiraṇyadāyine ॥ 1.55.48 ॥

namo'stu te deva hiraṇyamāline namo'stu te deva hiraṇyavāhine।
namo'stu te deva hiraṇyacīra namo'stu te bhairavanādanādine ॥ 1.55.49 ॥

namo'stu te bhairavavegavega namo'stu te śaṅkara nīlakaṇṭha।
mano'stu te divyasahasrabāho namo'stu te narttanavādanapriya ॥ 1.55.50 ॥


abhibhāṣyastadā hṛṣṭo mahādevo maheśvaraḥ।
vakrakoṭisahasreṇa grasamāna ivāparam ॥ 1.55.52 ॥



dundubhisvananirghoṣaparjanyaninadopamaḥ।
mukto hāsastadā tena nabhaḥ sarvamapūrayat ॥ 1.55.55 ॥

tadovāca mahāyogī prīto'haṃ surasattamau ॥ 1.55.56 ॥


prīto'haṃ yuvayoḥ samyagvaraṃ dajhi yathepsitam ॥ 1.55.58 ॥

ūcatuśca mahātmānau punareva tadānaghau ॥ 1.55.59 ॥

yadi prītiḥ samutpannā yadi deyo varaśca nau।
bhaktirbhavatu nau nityaṃ tvayi deva sureśvara ॥ 1.55.60 ॥

।।bhagavānuvāca।।
evamuktvā sa bhagavāṃstatraivāntaradhīyata ॥ 1.55.61 ॥

evameṣa mayokto vaḥ prabhāvastasya yoginaḥ।


tasmai devādhipatyāya namaskāraṃ prayuṅkta ha।

mahādeva namaste'stu maheśvara namo'stu te।
surāsuravara śreṣṭha manohaṃsa namo'stu te ॥ 1.55.65 ॥

।।sūta uvāca।।

kāmāṃśca labhate sarvān pāpebhyastu vimucyate ॥ 1.55.67 ॥

mahādevaprasādena uktaṃ brahma sanātanam।


## 1.56

।।śāṃśapāyana uvāca ।।

।।sūta uvāca।।
ailasyādityasaṃyogaṃ somasya ca mahātmanaḥ ॥ 1.56.2 ॥



etatsarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi parvāṇi ca yathākramam ॥ 1.56.5 ॥

yadā tu candrasūryau tau nakṣatreṇa samāgatau।


prasīdamānāt somācca pitrarthaṃ tatparisravāt।

dvilavaṃ kuhumātraṃ tu te ubhe tu vicārya saḥ।



sadyaḥ pakṣaratā tena saumyena madhunā ca saḥ ॥ 1.56.12 ॥

nirvāpaṇārthaṃ dattena pitreṇa vidhinā nṛpaḥ।

ṛturagnistu yaḥ proktaḥ sa tu saṃvatsaro mataḥ।









yasmāt prasravate somānmāsi māsi nibodhata।

evaṃ tadamṛtaṃ saumyaṃ sudhā ca madhu caiva ha ।

pibantyambumayīrde vāstrayastriṃśattu chandajāḥ।

pibanti dvikalaṃ kālaṃ pitaraste sudhāmṛtam ॥ 1.56.25 ॥





granthimanti yathā parvāṇīkṣuveṇvorbhavantyuta ॥ 1.56.30 ॥


agnayādhānakriyā yasmāt kriyate parvasandhiṣu।

vyatīpāte sthite sūrye lesorddhantu yugāntare।
yugāntarodite caiva lekhorddhaṃ śaśinaḥ kramāt ॥ 1.56.33 ॥

yasminkāle sa sīmānte sa vyatīpāta eva tu ॥ 1.56.34 ॥


yasmāttāmanupaśyanti pitaro daivataiḥ saha।

rañjanāccaiva candrasya rāketi kavayo viduḥ ॥ 1.56.37 ॥



anyonyaṃ candrasūryau tau yadā taddarśa ucyate ॥ 1.56.40 ॥

naṣṭacandrā pyamāvāsyā madhyasūryeṇa saṅgatā ॥ 1.56.41 ॥

dvau kālau saṅgamaścaiva madhyāhne niṣpatedraviḥ ॥ 1.56.42 ॥

nirmucyamānayormadhye tayormaṇḍalayostu vai ॥ 1.56.43 ॥






kuheti kokirenokto yaḥ kālaḥ paricihnitaḥ।



pratipatpañcadaśyośca parvakālo dvimātrakaḥ।



daśabhiḥ pañcabhiścaiva kalābhirdivasakramāt।





devāste pitaraḥ sarve devāstān bhāvayantyuta।

pitā pitāmahaścaiva tathaiva prapitāmahaḥ।
yajvāno ye tu some na somavantastu te smṛtāḥ ॥ 1.56.61 ॥

karmasveteṣu yuktāste tṛpyantyādehasambhavāt ॥ 1.56.62 ॥


ante ca naiva sīdanti śraddhāyuktena karmaṇā।
brahmacaryeṇa tapasā yajñena prajayā ca vai ॥ 1.56.64 ॥

śraddhayā vidyayā caiva pradānena ca saptadhā।
karmasveteṣu ye yuktā bhavantyā devapātanāt ॥ 1.56.65 ॥

svargatā divi modante pitṛmantamupāsate ॥ 1.56.66 ॥

prajāvatāṃ praśaṃsaiva smṛtā siddhā kriyāvatām।





parānnāni ca lipsante kampamānāstatastataḥ ॥ 1.56.72 ॥


karambhavālukāyāñca asipatravane tathā।


bhūmyāpasavyadarbheṣu dattvā piṇḍatrayantu vai।







itye te pitaro devā devāśca pitaraśca vai।



ityarkapitṛ somānāmailasya ca samāgamaḥ।


vaiśvarūpyantu sarvasya kathitañcaikadeśikam।

svāyambhuvasya hītyeṣa sargaḥ krānto mayātra vai।


## 1.57

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।
caturyugāni yānyāsan pūrvaṃ svāyambhuve'ntare ।
।।sūta uvāca।।
teṣāñcaturyugaṃ hyetat pravakṣyāmi nibodhata ॥ 1.57.2 ॥
yugañca yugabhedañca yugadharmantathaiva ca ॥ 1.57.3 ॥
yugasandhyaṃśakañcaiva yugasandhānameva ca।
laukikena pramāṇena vibuddho'bdastu mānuṣaḥ।
tenābdena prasaṅkhyāya vakṣyāmīha caturyugam ।। 57.5 .
nimeṣakālaḥ kāṣṭā ca kalāścāpi muhūrttakāḥ।
nimeṣakālatulyaṃ hi vidyāllaghvakṣarañcayat ॥ 1.57.5 ॥
ahorātre vibhajate sūryo mānuṣadaivike।
catvāra ścādhikā māsāḥ pitre caiveha kīrttitāḥ ॥ 1.57.10 ॥
etaddivyamahorātraṃ śāstre'smin niśvayo mataḥ ॥ 1.57.11 ॥
ye te rātryahanī divye prasaṅkhayāte tayoḥ punaḥ।
divyenaiva pramāṇena yugasaṅkacāprakalpanam ॥ 1.57.20 ॥
catvāri bhārate varṣe yugāni kavayo viduḥ।
dvāparaśca kali ścaiva yugānyetāni kalpayet ॥ 1.57.21 ॥
tretā trīṇi sahasrāṇi saṅkhyaiva parikīrtyate।
evaṃ saṅ‌khyātakālaśca kāleṣviha viśeṣataḥ।
kṛtatretādiyuktā sā manorantaramucyate ॥ 1.57.32 ॥
manvantarasya saṅkhyā tu varṣāgreṇa nibodhata।
catuḥ sahasrayuktaṃ vai prathamantat kṛtaṃ yugam।
yugapatsamavetārtho dvidhā vaktuṃ na śakyate।
kramāgataṃ mayā hyetattubhyaṃ proktaṃ yugadvayam।
tatra tretāyugasyādau manuḥ saptarṣayaśca te।
satyena brahmacaryeṇa śrutena tapasā ca vai।
abhivyaktāstu te mantrāstārakādyairnidarśanaiḥ।
saptarṣibhistu te proktāḥ smārtaṃ dharmaṃ manurjagau ॥ 1.57.45 ॥
ṛṣayastapasā devāḥ kalau ca dvāpareṣu vai।
saṅkalpitena manasā vācoktena svasarmaṇā।
tretāyuge tvavikalaḥ karmmārambhaḥ prasiddhyati ॥ 1.57.52 ॥
parasparavirodhena manuntāḥ punaranvayuḥ।
priyavratottānapādau pradhamantau mahīpatī ॥ 1.57.56 ॥
pracchannapāpā ye jetumaśakyā manujā bhuvi।
yajñaḥ pravartitaścaiva tadā hyevantu daivataiḥ।
sārddhaṃ viśvabhujā caiva devendreṇa mahaujasā।
svāyambhuve'ntare devairyajñāste prāk pravartitāḥ ॥ 1.57.61 ॥
kriyā dharmaśca hrasate satyadharmaḥ pravarttate ॥ 1.57.62 ॥
nyagrodhau tau smṛtau bāhū vyāmo nyagrodha ucyate।
vāmenaivocchrayādyasya sama ūrddhvantu dehinaḥ।

## 1.58

।।sūta uvāca ।।




ādye kṛte ca dharmo'sti tretāyāṃ samprapadyate।

dvaidhamutpadyate caiva yuge tasmin śrutau smṛtau ॥ 1.58.6 ॥

aniścayādigamanāddharmatattvaṃ nigadyate।
dharmatattve tu bhinnānāṃ matibhedo bhavennṛṇām ॥ 1.58.7 ॥

ayaṃ dharmo hyayaṃ neti niścayo nābhigamyate ॥ 1.58.8 ॥


eko vedaśvatuṣpādastretāsviha vidhīyate ॥ 1.58.10 ॥


mantrabrāhmaṇavinyāsaiḥ svaravarṇaviparyayaiḥ ॥ 1.58.12 ॥


anye tu prahitāstīrthaiḥ kecittān pratyavasthitāḥ ॥ 1.58.14 ॥

dvāpareṣu pravarttante bhinnavṛttāśramā dvijāḥ।

ādhvaryavasya prastāvairbahudhā vyākulaṃ kṛtam ॥ 1.58.16 ॥

vyākulaṃ dvāpare bhinne kriyate bhinnadarśanaiḥ ॥ 1.58.17 ॥

dvāpare sampravartante vinaśyanti punaḥ kalau ॥ 1.58.18 ॥


vāṅmanaḥkarmajairduḥkhairnirvedo jāyate punaḥ ।



arthaśāstravikalpaśca hetuśāstravikalpanam ॥ 1.58.23 ॥




dvāpareṣu pravarttante rogo lobho vadhastathā।

niḥśepe dvāpare tasmin tasya sandhyā tu pādataḥ ॥ 1.58.28 ॥

pratiṣṭhate guṇairhīno dharmo'sau dvāparasya tu ।

dvāparasya ca varṣe yā tiṣyasya tu nibodhata।

ete svabhāvāstiṣyasya sādhayanti ca vai prajāḥ ॥ 1.58.31 ॥


kalau pramārako rogaḥ satataṃ kṣudbhayāni vai।

na pramāṇaṃ smṛterasti tiṣye loke yuge yuge।
garbhastho mriyate kaścidyauvanasthastathāparaḥ ।
sthāvire madhyakaumāre mriyante vai kalau prajāḥ ॥ 1.58.34 ॥

anṛtabruvaśca satataṃ tiṣye jāyanti vai prajāḥ ॥ 1.58.35 ॥



saṃkṣobho jāyate'tyarthaṃ kalimāsādya vai yugam।

bhavantīha kalau tasmin śayanāsanabhojanaiḥ ॥ 1.58.39 ॥



bhṛtyāśca naṣṭasuhṛdo yugānte paryupasthite ॥ 1.58.42 ॥

māyāmātrā bhaviṣyanti yugānte pratyupasthite ॥ 1.58.43 ॥


caturāśramaśaithilyāddharmaḥ pravicaliṣyati ॥ 1.58.45 ॥

tadā hyalpaphalā devī bhavedbhūmirmahīyasī।

tadā hyaikāhiko dharmo dvāpare yaśca māsikaḥ।
tretāyāṃ vatsarasthaśca ekāhādatiricyate ॥ 1.58.47 ॥



patayaśca bhaviṣyanti bahavo'smin kalau yuge।


puruṣālpaṃ bahustrīkaṃ yugānte paryupasthite ॥ 1.58.52 ॥

bahuyācanako loko bhaviṣyati parasparam।


naraśūnyā vasumatī śūnyā caiva bhaviṣyati।

alpodakā cālpaphalā bhaviṣyati vasundharā।



śūdrā dharmañcariṣyanti yugānte paryupasthite ॥ 1.58.59 ॥

caurāścaurasya harttāro hanturharttāra eva va ॥ 1.58.60 ॥





vedavikrayiṇaścānye tīrthavikrayiṇo'pare ।

utpadyante tathā te vai saṃprāpte tu kalau yuge।

strīvadhaṃ govadhaṃ kṛtvā hatvā caiva parasparam।
upahanyustadānyonyaṃ sādhayanti tathā prajāḥ ॥ 1.58.67 ॥

moho glānistathāsaukhyaṃ tamovṛttaṃ kalau smṛtam ॥ 1.58.68 ॥

prajā tu bhrūṇahatyāyāmatha vai sampravarttate।


dhanyā dharmañcariṣyanti yugānte dvijasattamāḥ ॥ 1.58.71 ॥





gotreṇa vai candramaso nāmnā pramitirucyate।
mādhavasya tu soṃśena pūrvaṃ svāyambhuve'ntare ॥ 1.58.76 ॥


sa tadā taiḥ parivṛto mlecchān hanti sahasraśaḥ ॥ 1.58.78 ॥






pravṛttacakro balavān mlecchānāmantakṛdvibhuḥ।

mādhavasya tu soṃśena devasya hi vijajñivān ।
pūrvajanmavidhijñaiśca pramitirnāma vīryavān ॥ 1.58.85 ॥

gotreṇa vai candramasaḥ pūrve kaliyuge prabhuḥ।

parasparanimittena kopenākasmikena tu ॥ 1.58.87 ॥


tato vyatīte tasmiṃstu amātye satyasainike।

tatra sandhyāṃśake kāle samprāpte tu yugāntike।

upahiṃsanti cānyonyaṃ prapadyante parasparam ॥ 1.58.91 ॥

arājake yugavaśāt saṃśaye samupasthite।




pratyantāṃstānnipevante hitvā janapadān svakān ॥ 1.58.96 ॥

madhumāṃsairmūlaphalairvarttayanti suduḥkhitāḥ ॥ 1.58.97 ॥





cittasammohanaṃ kṛtvā tāsāntaiḥ saptamantu tat।
bhāvino'rthasya ca balāttataḥ kṛtamavartata ॥ 1.58.102 ॥

pravṛtte tu punastasmiṃstataḥ kṛtayuge tu vai।

sadā saptarṣayaścaiva tatra te ca vyavasthitāḥ ॥ 1.58.104 ॥

kalijaiḥ saha te sarve nirviśeṣāstadābhavan ॥ 1.58.105 ॥


tatasteṣu kriyāvatsu varttante vai prajāḥ kṛte ।

manvantarādhikāreṣu tiṣṭhanti munayastu vai ॥ 1.58.108 ॥


evaṃ yugādyugasteha santānastu parasparam ।
varttate hyavyavacchedādyāvanmanvantarakṣayaḥ ॥ 1.58.110 ॥


ityeṣa pratisandhirvaḥ kīrttitastu mayā dvijāḥ ॥ 1.58.112 ॥



krameṇa parivṛttā tu manorantaramucyate ॥ 1.58.115 ॥

caturyuge tathaikasmin bhavatīha yathāśrutam।
tathā cānyeṣu bhavati punastadvai yathākramam ॥ 1.58.116 ॥

sarge sarge yathā bhedā utpadyante tathaiva tu ।






asyābhimāninaḥ sarve nāmarūpairbhavantyuta।


yugasvabhāvācca tathā vidhatte vai sadā prabhuḥ ।



## 1.59

।।sūta uvāca।।
yugeṣu yāstu jāyante prajāstā vai nibodhata।

yugamātrantu jīvanti ṛte mṛtyuvadhena te ॥ 1.59.2 ॥





ityeṣa mānuṣotsedho hrasatīha yugāntike ॥ 1.59.7 ॥


āpādato mastakantu navatālo bhavettu yaḥ।
saṃhatājānubāhustu sa surairapi pūjyate ॥ 1.59.9 ॥

krameṇaitena yogena hrāsavṛddhī yuge yuge ॥ 1.59.10 ॥








manojñaistattvabhāvajñaiḥ sukhino hyupapedire ॥ 1.59.18 ॥

saditi brahmaṇaḥ śabdastadvanto ye bhavantyuta।
sāyujyaṃ brahmaṇo'tyantaṃ tena santaḥ prajakṣyate ॥ 1.59.19 ॥



śrautasmārtasya dharmasya jñānāddharmaḥ sa ucyate ॥ 1.59.22 ॥



gṛhastho brahmacārī ca vānaprastho'tha bhikṣukaḥ ॥ 1.59.25 ॥

na ca devā na pitaro munayo na ca mānavāḥ।
ayaṃ dharmo hyayaṃ neti bruvanto'bhinnadarśanāḥ ॥ 1.59.26 ॥

dharmādharmāviha proktau śabdāvetau kriyātmakau।

dhāraṇā dhṛtirityarthāddhātordharmaḥ prakīrttitaḥ ।
adhāraṇe'mahattve ca adharma iti cocyate ॥ 1.59.28 ॥


ācinoti ca śāstrārthānyamaiḥ sanniyamairyutaḥ ॥ 1.59.30 ॥

pūrvebhyo vedayitveha śrautaṃ saptarṣayo'bruvan।

manvantarasyātītasya smṛtvācāraṃ punarjagau।



taiḥ śiṣṭaiścarito dharmaḥ samyageva yuge yuge ॥ 1.59.35 ॥


dānaṃ satyantapo'lobho vidyejyāprajanī dayā।


pratyaṅgāni ca vakṣyāmi dharmasyeha tu lakṣaṇam ॥ 1.59.39 ॥

yathā bhūtapravādastu ityetatsatyalakṣaṇam ॥ 1.59.40 ॥

brahmacaryaṃ japo maunaṃ nirāhāratvameva ca।
ityetat tapaso mūlaṃ sughoraṃ taddurāsadam ॥ 1.59.41 ॥




parasvānāmanādānamalobha iha kīrtyate ॥ 1.59.45 ॥

maithunasyāsamācāro hyacintanamakalpanam।
nivṛttirbrahmacaryaṃ tadacchidraṃ dama ucyate ॥ 1.59.46 ॥

na mithyā sampravartante śamasyai tattu lakṣaṇam ॥ 1.59.47 ॥

na krudhyettu pratihataḥ sa jitātmā vibhāvyate ॥ 1.59.48 ॥

tattadguṇavate deyamityetaddānalakṣaṇam ॥ 1.59.49 ॥

dānaṃ trividhamityetat kaniṣṭhajyeṣṭhamadhayamam ।




 avyaktādyo'viśeṣācca vikāro'sminnacetane।

ṛṣibhirdharmatattvajñaiḥ pūrve svāyambhuve'ntare ॥ 1.59.55 ॥

atra vo varttayiṣyāmi vidhirmanvantarasya yaḥ ।
itaretaravarṇasya cāturvarṇasya caiva hi।
pratimanvantarañcaiva śrutiranyā vidhīyate ॥ 1.59.56 ॥

ābhūtasaṃplavasyāpi varjyaikaṃ śatarudriyam ॥ 1.59.57 ॥

vidhirhotraṃ tathā stotraṃ pūrvavatsampravartate।
dravyastotraṃ guṇastotraṃ karma stotraṃ tathaiva ca।
caturthamābhijanikaṃ stotrametaccaturvidham ॥ 1.59.58 ॥

manvantareṣu sarveṣu yathā devā bhavanti ye।
pravarttayati teṣāṃ vai brahmastotraṃ caturvidham।


mantrāḥ prādurbabhūvurhi pūrvamanvantareṣviha।



guṇasāmye varttamāne sarvasampralaye tadā।
aticāre tu devānāmatideśe tayoryathā ॥ 1.59.64 ॥

abuddhipūrvakaṃ tadvai cetanārthaṃ pravarttate ।
tena hyabuddhipūrvaṃ taccetanena hyadhiṣṭitam ॥ 1.59.65 ॥

varttate ca yathā tau tu yathā matsyodake ubhe।

karaṇatvāttathā kāryaṃ tadā tasya pravarttate।
viṣaye viṣayitvācca hyarthe'rthitvāttathaiva ca ॥ 1.59.67 ॥


bhūtabhedāstu bhedebhyo jajñire te parasparam।

yatholmukasruṭannūrddhvamekakālaṃ pravarttate।

yathāndhakāre khadyotaḥ sahasā sampradṛśyate।
tathā vivṛtto hyavyaktāt khadyota iva celvaṇaḥ ॥ 1.59.71 ॥

sa mahān saśarīrastu yatraivāgre vyavasthitaḥ।

tatraiva saṃsthito vidvāṃstamaso'nta iti śrutiḥ ॥ 1.59.73 ॥

buddhirvivarttamānasya prādurbhūtā caturvidhā ।

mahataḥ saśarīrasya vaivarttyāt siddhirucyate ॥ 1.59.75 ॥


kṣetrajñaḥ kṣetravijñānāt bhagavān matirucyate।
yasmādbuddhyā tu śete ha tasmādbodhātmakaḥ sa vai ।
saṃsiddhaye parigataṃ vyaktāvyaktamacetanam ॥ 1.59.77 ॥

kṣetrajñena parijñāto bhogyo'yaṃ viṣayastviti ॥ 1.59.78 ॥

ṛṣītyeṣa gatau dhātuḥśrutau satye tapasyatha।
etatsanniyate tasmin brahmaṇā sa ṛṣiḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 1.59.79 ॥



yasmānna hanyate mānairmahān parigataḥ puraḥ।



satyarṣayastataste vai paramāḥ satyadarśanāḥ ॥ 1.59.85 ॥

tasmāt śrutarṣayaste'pi śrutasya paridarśanāḥ ॥ 1.59.86 ॥




utathyo vāmadevaśca apojyaścaiśijastathā ॥ 1.59.90 ॥


vatsaro nagrahūścaiva bhāradvājastathaiva ca ॥ 1.59.92 ॥

bṛhadutthaḥ śaradvāṃśca agastyaścauśijastathā।

vājaśravāḥ suvittaśca suvāgveṣaparāyaṇaḥ।

īśvarā ṛṣikāścaiva ye cānye vai tathā smṛtāḥ।
ete mantrakṣakṛtaḥ sarve kṛtsnaśastānnibodhata ॥ 1.59.95 ॥

aurvo'tha jamadagniśca divaḥ sārasvata stathā ॥ 1.59.96 ॥



purukutso'tha māndhātā ambarīṣastathaiva ca।

yuvanāśvaḥ paurukutsastrasaddasyuḥ sadasyumān ॥ 1.59.100 ॥

āyāpyaśca suvittiśca vāmadevastathaiva ca ॥ 1.59.101 ॥


kāśyapaścaiva vatsāro vibhramo raibhya eva ca।
asito devalaścaiva ṣaḍete brahmavādinaḥ ॥ 1.59.103 ॥
