caturtha indrapramatiḥ pañcamastu bharadvasuḥ ॥ 1.59.105 ॥

sudyumnaścāṣṭamaścaiva navamo'tha bṛhaspatiḥ।
daśamastu bharadvājau mantrabrāhmaṇakārakāḥ ॥ 1.59.106 ॥

ete caiva hi kartāro vidharmadhvaṃsakāriṇaḥ।

heturhiteḥ smṛto dhātoryannihantyuditamparaiḥ।
atha vārthapari prāpterhinotergatikarmaṇaḥ ॥ 1.59.108 ॥


idantvidamidaṃ nedamityaniścitya saṃśayaḥ ॥ 1.59.110 ॥

idameva vidhātavyamityayaṃ vidhirucyate ।

yo hyatyantataroktaśca purākalpaḥ sa ucyate।

mantrabrāhmaṇakalpaistu nigamaiḥ śuddhavistaraiḥ ।



mantro mantrayaterdhātorbrāhmaṇo brahmaṇo'vanāt ॥ 1.59.116 ॥

alpākṣaramasandigdhaṃ sāravadviśvatomukham।
astobhamanavadyañca sūtraṃ sūtravido viduḥ ॥ 1.59.117 ॥


## 1.60

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।

।।sūta uvāca ।।
dvāpare tu parāvṛtte manoḥ svāyambhuve'ntare ।
brahmā manumuvācedaṃ tadvadiṣye mahāmate ॥ 1.60.2 ॥




punardaśaguṇaḥ kṛtsno yajño vai sarvakāmadhuk ॥ 1.60.7 ॥

evamuktastathetyuktvā manurlokahite rataḥ।
vedamekaṃ catuṣpādaṃ caturdhā vyabhajatprabhuḥ ॥ 1.60.8 ॥

tadidaṃ varttamānena yuṣmākaṃ vedakalpanam ॥ 1.60.9 ॥

pratyakṣeṇa parokṣaṃ vai tannibodhata sattamāḥ ॥ 1.60.10 ॥


brahmaṇā coditaḥ so'smin vedaṃ vyastuṃ pracakrame।

jaiminiñca sumantuñca vaiśampāyanameva ca।

ṛgvedaśrāvakaṃ pailañjagrāha vidhivad dvijam ।
yajurvedapravaktāraṃ vaiśampāyanameva ca ॥ 1.60.14 ॥

tathaivātharvavedasya sumantumṛṣisattamam ॥ 1.60.15 ॥

itihāsapurāṇasya vaktāraṃ samyageva hi।

eka āsīdyajurvedastañcaturddhā vyakalpayat।
caturhotramabhūttasmiṃstena yajñamakalpayat ॥ 1.60.17 ॥

ādhvaryavaṃ yajurbhistu ṛgbhirhotraṃ tathaiva ca।
brahmatvamakarodyajñe vedenātharvaṇena tu ॥ 1.60.18 ॥

tataḥ sṛcamuddhṛtya ṛgvedaṃ samakalpa yat।
hotṛkaṃ kalpyate tena yajñavāhaṃ jagaddhitam ॥ 1.60.19 ॥

sāmabhiḥ sāmavedañca tenodgātramarocayat ।
rājñastvatharvavedena sarvakarmāṇyakārayat ॥ 1.60.20 ॥


yacchiṣṭantu yajurvede tena yajñamathāyujat।

sa tenoddhṛtavīryastu ṛtvigbhirvedapāragaiḥ।
prayujyate hyaśvamedhastena vā yujyate tu saḥ ॥ 1.60.23 ॥




indrapramatirekāntu saṃhitāṃ dvijasattamaḥ।

satyaśravāḥ satyahitaṃ punaradhyāpayad dvijaḥ ॥ 1.60.28 ॥

so'pi satyataraṃ putraṃ punaradhyāpayadvibhuḥ।

abhavaṃstasya śiṣyā vai trayastu sumahaujasaḥ ।


janakasyasa yajñe vai vināśamagamad dvijaḥ ॥ 1.60.32 ॥

।।śāṃśapāyana uvāca।।
janakasyāśvamedhena kathaṃ vādo babhūva ha ॥ 1.60.33 ॥

sarvametadyathāvṛttamācakṣva viditantava।

।।sūta uvāca।।


iti niścitya manasā buddhiṃ cakre janādhipaḥ ।। 60.36 ।।


tasmai tadupanītaṃ hi vidyāvittaṃ dvijottamāḥ ।। 60.38 ।।


manasā gatavittāste mamedaṃ dhanamityuta।
mamaivaitanna vetyanyo brūhi kiṃ vā vikalṣyate।

tathānyastatra vai vidvān brahmavāhasutaḥ kaviḥ।
yājñavalkyo mahātejāstapasvī brahmavittamaḥ ।। 60.41 ।।

brahmaṇo'ṅgāt samutpanno vākyaṃ provāca susvaram।

yovā na prīyate vipraḥ sa me hyavatu mā'ciram ।। 60.43 ।।

tato brahmārṇavaḥ kṣubdhaḥ samudra iva samplave ।
tānuvāca tataḥ svastho yājñavalkyo hasanniva ।। 60.44 ।।


sahasradhā śubhairarthaiḥ sūkṣmadarśanasambhavaiḥ ।। 60.46 ।।

śāpottamaguṇairyuktā nṛpaughaparivarjanāḥ ।
vādāḥ samabhavaṃstatra dhanahetormahātmanām ।। 60.47 ।।

ṛṣayastvekataḥ sarve yājñavalkyastathaikataḥ।
sarve te munayastena yājñavalkyena dhīmatā ।


pūrṇastvaṃ jaḍamānena vātādhmāto yathā dṛtiḥ ।। 60.50 ।।





etacchrutvā vacastasya śākalyaḥ krodhamūrcchitaḥ।

tataḥ samabhavadvādastayorbrahmavidormahān ।। 60.56 ।।

sāgraṃ praśnasahasrantu śākalyastamucūcudat।

śāpaḥ paṇo'sya vādasya abruvan mṛtyumāvrajet ।। 60.58 ।।



vyākhyāya vai mune teṣāṃ praśrasāraṃ mahāgatiḥ ।। 60.61 ।।


devamitrastu śākalyo mahātmā dvijasattamaḥ।

tacchiṣyā abhavan pañca mudghalo golakastathā।

niruktañca punaścakre caturthaṃ dvijasattamaḥ ।। 60.65 ।।

dharmaśarmā devaśarmā sarve vratadharā dvijāḥ ।। 60.66 ।।

śākalye tu mṛte sarve brahmaghnāste babhūvire।
tadā cintāṃ parāṃ prāpya gatāste brahmaṇo'ntikam ।। 60.67 ।।

tān jñātvā cetasā brahmā preṣitaḥ pavanaḥpure।
tatra gacchata yūyaṃ vaḥsadyaḥ pāpaṃ praṇaśyati ।। 60.68 ।।

dvādaśārkaṃ namaskṛtyā tathā vai vālukeśvaram ।
kuṇḍe catuṣṭaye snātvā brahmahatyāṃ tariṣyatha ।। 60.69 ।।

sarve śīghratarā bhūtvā tatpuraṃ samupāgatāḥ।
snānaṃ kṛtaṃ vidhānena devānāṃ darśanaṃ kṛtam ।। 60.70 ।।

uttareśvaraṃ namaskṛtya vāḍavānāṃ prasādataḥ।

vāyoḥ puraṃ pavitrañca vāyunā nirmitaṃ purā ।। 60.72 ।।

añjanāgarbhasambhūtahanumānpavanātmajaḥ।
yadā jāto mahādevohanumānsatyavikramaḥ।
tadevaṃ nirmitaṃ tīrthaṃ vāyunā brahmayoninā ।। 60.73 ।।

ūrvyāṃ jātāstu ye śūdrā brāhmaṇānāṃ niveditāḥ।
vṛttyarthaṃ brahmayajñārthaṃ karasteṣu kṛto mahān ।। 60.74 ।।

anena vidhinā jātaṃ viprāṇāṃ śāsanaṃ mahat ।
goghno vāpi kṛtaghno vā surāpī gurūtalpagaḥ।


## 1.61

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
bhāradvājo yājñavalkyo gālakiḥ sālakistathā।

rathītaro niruktañca punaścakre caturthakam ॥ 1.61.2 ॥



vaiśampāyanagotro'sau yajurvedaṃ vyakalpayat।

ekastatra parityakto yājñavalkyo mahātapāḥ।

tridhā bhedāstu te proktā bhede'sminnavame śubhe ॥ 1.61.7 ॥




ityuktaḥ prāha teṣāṃ sa carakatvamabhūdyathā ॥ 1.61.11 ॥

।।sūta uvāca ।।

yo no'tra saptarātreṇa nāgacched dvijasattamāḥ।
sa kuryādbrahmavadhyāṃ vai samayo naḥ prakīrtitaḥ ॥ 1.61.13 ॥

prayayuḥ saptarātreṇa yatra sandhiḥ kṛto'bhavat ॥ 1.61.14 ॥


brahmavadyāñcaradhvaṃ vai matkṛte dvijasattamāḥ।

।।yājñavalkya uvāca।।
ahameva cariṣyāmi tiṣṭhantu munayastvime।

evamuktastataḥ kruddho yājñavalkyamathābravīt ।
uvāca yattvayādhītaṃ sarvaṃ pratyarpayasva me ॥ 1.61.18 ॥

rudhireṇa tathāktāni charditvā brahmavittamaḥ ॥ 1.61.19 ॥

sūryabrahma yaducchinnaṃ khaṃ gatvā pratitiṣṭhati ॥ 1.61.20 ॥

tāni tasmai dadau tuṣṭaḥ sūryo vai brahmarītaye।

aśvarūpāya dattāni tataste vājino'bhavan ॥ 1.61.22 ॥


ityete carakāḥ proktā vājinastānnibodhata।
yājñavalkyasya śiṣyāste kaṇvavaidheyaśālinaḥ ॥ 1.61.24 ॥

madhyandinaśca śāpeyī vidigdhaścāpya uddalaḥ।
tāmrāyaṇaśca vātsyaśca tathā gālavaśaiśirī।
āṭavī ca tathā parṇī vīraṇī saparāyaṇaḥ ॥ 1.61.25 ॥


putramadhyāpayāmāsa sumantumatha jaiminiḥ।
sumantuścāpi sutvānaṃ putramadhyāpayatprabhuḥ।

provācātha sahasrasya sukarmā sūryavarcasaḥ ॥ 1.61.28 ॥


bhāvinau te mahāvīryau śiṣyāvanalavarcasau ॥ 1.61.30 ॥

etau surau mahābhāgau mā krudhya dvijasattama ॥ 1.61.31 ॥






sakatiputraḥ sahasātyaputra etān bhedān vitta lokākṣiṇastu ॥ 1.61.37 ॥

trayastu kuthumeḥ putrā auraso rasapāsaraḥ।






provāca caiva śiṣyebhyo yebhyastāṃśca nibodhata ॥ 1.61.44 ॥





kabandhāya punaḥ kṛtsraṃ sa ca vidyādyathākramam ॥ 1.61.49 ॥

kabandhastu dvidā kṛtvā pathyāyaikaṃ punardadau।
dvitīyaṃ vedasparśāya sa caturddhākarot punaḥ ॥ 1.61.50 ॥

modo brahmabalaścaiva pippalādastathaiva ca।

punaśca trividhaṃ viddhi pathyānāṃ bhedamuttamam।

śaunakastu dvidhā kṛtvā dadāvekantu babhrave।





sāmikā ca caturthīsyātsā caiṣā pūrvasaṃhitā ॥ 1.61.58 ॥









dve sahasre śatanyūne vede vājasaneyake।




etāvadbhṛguvistāramanyaccātharvikaṃ bahu ॥ 1.61.71 ॥






manvantarāṇāṃ kriyate surāṇāṃ nāmaniścayaḥ।

śiṣyebhyaśca punardattvā tapastaptuṃ gato vanam।


āyurvedo dhanurvedo gāndharvaścaiva te trayaḥ।

tebhya ṛṣiprakṛtayo munibhiḥ saṃsitavrataiḥ ।। 61.80 ।।


dharmasyātha pulastyasya kratośca pulahasya ca।
pratyūṣasya prabhāsasya kaśyapasya tathā punaḥ ।। 61.82 ।।



parvato nāradaścaiva kaśyapasyātmajāvubhau।






sambuddhāstu svayaṃ ye tu sambuddhā ye ca vai svayam।
tapaseha prasiddhā ye garbheyaiśca praṇoditam ।। 61.91 ।।

ityete ṛṣibiryuktā devadvijanṛpāstu ye ।। 61.92 ।।



tulyairvyavaharanti sma adṛṣṭaiḥ karmahetubhiḥ ।। 61.95 ।।

agrāmyairvarttayanti sma rasaiścaiva svayaṃkṛtaiḥ।

varṇāśramavyavasthānaṃ kriyante prathamantu vai ।। 61.97 ।।

prāptai tretāyugamukhe punaḥ saptarṣayastviha।

jāyamāne pitā putreputraḥ pitari caiva hi।
evaṃ sametyāvicchedādvarttayantyāyugakṣayāt।



mantrabrāhmaṇakarttāro jāyante ha yugakṣaye ।। 61.102 ।।


bhaviṣye dvāpare caiva droṇirdvaipāyanaḥ punaḥ ।

tasmai tadbrahmaṇā brahmatapasā prāptamavyayam ।। 61.105 ।।

yaśasā prāpya satyaṃ hi satyenāpto hi cāvyayaḥ ।। 61.106 ।।

avyayādamṛtaṃ śukramamṛtāt sarvameva hi।
dhruvamekākṣaramidaṃ svātmanyeva vyavasthitam।


jagataḥ pralayotpattau yattatkāraṇasaṃjñitam।
mahataḥ paramaṃ guhyaṃ tasmai subrahmaṇe namaḥ ।। 61.109 ।।

agādhāparamakṣayyaṃ jagatsammohanālayam।

yattadavyaktamamṛtaṃ prakṛtibrahma śāśvatam ।। 61.111 ।।

pradhānamātmayoniśca guhyaṃ sattvañca śabdyate।
paramabrahmaṇe tasmai nityameva namo namaḥ ।। 61.112 ।।



yadvai darśitavāneṣa kastadanveṣṭumarhati ।

yadā yatkriyate yena tadā tatso'bhimanyate।
yenedaṃ kriyate pūrvaṃ tadanyena vibhāvitam ।। 61.116 ।।

yadā tu kriyate kiñcitkenacidvāṅmayaṃ kvacit।


pratyekavidyambhavati tretāsviha punaḥ punaḥ ।। 61.119 ।।

vyasyate hyekavidyantaddvāpareṣu punaḥ punaḥ ।
brahmā caitaduvācādau tasmin vaivasvate'ntare ।। 61.120 ।।







etena kramayogena manvantaraviniścayaḥ ।। 61.127 ।।

mantraiḥ sahorddhvaṃ gacchanti hyāvarttante ca taiḥ saha ।। 61.128 ।।

paryāptakāle samprāpte sambhūtā naiva nasya (?) tu ।। 61.129 ।।

avaśyambhāvinārthena sambadhyante tadā tu te।
tataste doṣavajjanma paśyante rāgapūrvakam ।। 61.130 ।।


janalokāttapolokaṃ gacchantīhānivarttanam।
nidhanaṃ brahmaloke vai gatāni munibhissaha ।। 61.132 ।।


pitṛbhirmunibhirdevaiḥ sārddhaṃ saptārṣibhiśca vai।

etena kramayogena kalpamanvantarāṇi tu।


vistarastu nisargasya saṃhārasya ca sarvaśaḥ।

devatānāmṛṣīṇāñca saṅkhyānārthaniśāradaiḥ।


vatsareṇaiva divyena pravakṣyāmyantarammanoḥ ।। 61.140 ।।




ityeṣa sthitikālo vai manordevarṣibhiḥ saha ।। 61.144 ।।

sarvamanvantarāṇāṃ vai pratisandhiṃ nibodhata।

kṛtatretādi saṃyuktaṃ caturyugamiti smṛtam।
tadekasaptatiguṇaṃ parivṛttaṃ tu sādhikam।


ityeṣa kīrttitaḥ sargā manoḥ svāyambhuvasya ha।
prati sandhintu vakṣyāmi tasya vai cāparasya tu ।। 61.148 ।।

manvantaraṃ yathā pūrvamṛṣibhirdaivataiḥ saha।
avaśyambhāvinārthena yathā tadvai nivarttate ।। 61.149 ।।

asmin manvantare pūrvaṃ trailokyasyeśvarāstu ye।
saptarṣayaśca devāste pitaro manavastathā।

maharlokāya te sarve unmukhā dadhire gatim ।। 61.151 ।।


devatāḥ pitaraścaiva ṛṣayo manureva ca ।। 61.153 ।।

manvantare tu sampūrṇe yadyanyadvai kalau yuge।

tathā manvantarānteṣu ādirmanvantarasya ca ।। 61.155 ।।


manvantaraṃ pratīkṣante kṣīyante tapasi sthitāḥ ।। 61.157 ।।

manvantaravyavasthārthaṃ santatyarthañca sarvaśaḥ।
pūrvavat sampravarttante pravṛtte vṛṣṭisarjjane ।। 61.158 ।।

prajāsu ca niketāsu saṃsthitāsu kvacit kvacit ।। 61.159 ।।

vārttāyāntu pravṛttāyāṃ saddharme ṛṣibhāvite ।
nirānande gate loke naṣṭe sthāvarajaṅgame ।। 61.160 ।।

agrāmanagare caiva varṇāśramavivarjite।



saptarṣayo manuścaiva ādau manvantarasya ha।
prārambhante ca karmāṇi manuṣyā daivataiḥ saha ।। 61.164 ।।

manvantarādau prāgeva tretāyugamukhe tataḥ ।
pūrvaṃ devāstataste vai sthite dharme tu sarvaśaḥ ।। 61.165 ।।




mantraiḥ sahordhvaṅgacchanti maharlokamanāmayam ।। 61.169 ।।


upasthitā ihaivānye devā ye svargavāsinaḥ ।। 61.171 ।।

satyaina brahmacaryeṇa śrutena ca samanvitāḥ ।। 61.172 ।।


teṣāmatyantaviccheda iha manvantarakṣayāt।


evamatyantavicchinnaṃ bhavatyābhūtasaṃplavāt ।। 61.176 ।।

maharjanañcaiva janantapaśca ekāntagāni sma bhavanti satye ।। 61.177 ।।

satye sthitānīha tadā tu tāni prapte vikāre pratisargakāle ।। 61.178 ।।

manvantarāṇāṃ parivarttanāni muñcanti satyantu tato'parānte।



sarvāṇi rājarṣisurarṣimanti brahmarṣidevoragavanti caiva।
sureśasaptarṣipitṛprajaiśairyuktāni samyak parivarttanāni ।। 61.182 ।।


svargīyametat paramaṃ pavitraṃ putrīyametacca paraṃ rahasyam।
japyaṃ mahatparvasu caitadagryaṃ duḥsvapnaśāntiḥ paramāyuṣeyam ।। 61.184 ।।

mamāpi vikhyāpanasaṃyamāya siddhiṃ juṣadhvaṃ sumaheśatatvam । 61.185 ।।

ityetadantaraṃ proktaṃ manoḥ svāyambhuvasya tu।



# Part 2


## 2.1

śāṃśapāyana uvāca॥
daivatānāṃ ca sarveṣāṃ ye ca yasyāntare manoḥ ॥ 2.1.1 ॥
samāsādvistarāccaiva bruvato vai nibodata ॥ 2.1.2 ॥
vakṣyāmyetān purastāttu manorvaivasvatasya ha ॥ 2.1.4 ॥
manvantaraṃ mayā coktaṃ krāntaṃ svāyambhuvasya ha ॥ 2.1.5 ॥
dhaivasyaśo'tha vāmānyo gopā devāyatastathā ।
ityete kratuputrāstu tadāsan somapāyinaḥ ॥ 2.1.10 ॥ 62.10
pracetāścaiva yo devo viśvedevāstathaiva ca ।
ityetā devatā hyāsanmanusvārociṣentare ॥ 2.1.13 ॥
ūrjau vasiṣṭhaputrastu stambhaḥ kāśyapa eva ca ।
paulastyaścaiva dattātrirātreyo niścalastathā ।
paulahasya ca dhāvāṃstu ete saptarṣayaḥ smṛtāḥ ॥ 2.1.16 ॥
etanmanvantaraṃ proktaṃ samāsānna tu vistarāt ॥ 2.1.21 ॥
na śakyo vistarastasya vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi ।
punaruktabahutvāttu prajānāṃ vaikule kule ॥ 2.1.22 ॥
tṛtīyastvatha paryāya auttamasyāntare manoḥ ।
īṣorjāśca tathā jyeṣṭo vapuṣmāṃścaiva dvādaśa ।
ityete nāmabhiḥ krāntāḥ sudhāmānastu dvādaśa ॥25 ।
anyānārādhito devo vasudhiṣṇo vivasvasuḥ ॥ 2.1.26 ॥
haṃsasvaro'hihā caiva pratarddanayaśaskarau ।
jantuvāhayatiścaiva suvittasunayastathā ।
satyānāmapi nāmāni nibodhata yathāmatam ।
dikpatirvākpatiścaiva viśvaḥ śambhustathaiva ca ॥ 2.1.30 ॥
ityete devatā hyāsannauttamasyāntare manoḥ ॥ 2.1.32 ॥
ajaśca paraśuścaiva divyo divyauṣadhirnnayaḥ ।
auttamasya manoḥ putrāstrayodaśa mahātmanaḥ ।
caturthetvatha paryāye tāmasasyāntare manoḥ ।
saptarṣayo'ntare caiva tānnibodhata sattamāḥ ॥ 2.1.39 ॥ 62.40
paulaho vanapīṭhaśca gotre vāsiṣṭha eva ca ।
caitrastathāpi paulastya ṛṣayastāmase'ntare ॥ 2.1.41 ॥
priyabhṛtyo hyavakṣiśca pṛṣṭaloḍho dṛḍhodyataḥ ।
svatravipregnibhāsasva pratyetiṣṭāmṛtastathā ।
matiśca sumatiścaiva ṛtasatyau tathaiva ca ।
āvṛtirvivṛtiścaiva mado vinaya eva ca ॥ 2.1.47 ॥
medhā medhātithiścaiva satyamedhāstathaiva ca ।
dīptimedhā yaśomedhāḥ sthiramedhāstathaiva ca ।
medhāvān medhaharttā ca kīrttitāstu sumedhasaḥ ॥ 2.1.51 ॥
satyanetrastathātreya ṛṣayo raivatāntare ॥ 2.1.53 ॥
cariṣṇavasya putrāste pañcamañcaitadantaram ॥ 2.1.54 ॥
svārociṣottamaścaiva tāmaso raivatastathā ।
priyavratānvayā hyete catvāro manavastathā ॥ 2.1.55 ॥
antarikṣo vasuhayo hyatithiśca priyavataḥ ।
sumanāśca suvetāśca raivataḥ supracetasaḥ ।
vijayaḥ sujayaścaiva manodyānau tathaiva ca ।
sumatiḥ supariścaiva vijñāto'rthapatiśca yaḥ ।
lekhāṃstathā pravakṣyāmi bruvato me nibodhata ।
vāto dhruvakṣitiścaiva adbhutaścaiva vīryavān ।
manojavo mahāvīryasteṣāmindrastadābhavat ।
madhurā treya ityete sapta vai cākṣuṣe'ntare ॥ 2.1.65 ॥
agniṣṭudatirātraśca sudyumnaśceti te nava ॥ 2.1.66 ॥
vaivasvatena saṅkhyātastasya sargo mahātmanaḥ ।
॥ṛṣaya ūcuḥ॥
tasyānvavāye ye'pyanye tanno brūhi yathātatham ॥ 2.1.69 ॥ 62.70
sūta uvāca॥ cākṣuṣasya nisargantu samāsācchrotumarhatha ।
dhruvañca kīrttimantañca ayasmantaṃ vasuṃ tathā ।
uttānapādo'janayat kanye dve ca śucismite ।
tretāyuge tu prathame pautraḥ svāyambhuvasya saḥ ।
tasmai brahmā dadau prīto jyotiṣāṃ sthānamuttamam ।
ābhūtasaṃplavaṃ hṛdyamastodayavivarjitam ॥ 2.1.78 ॥
aho sya tapaso vīryamaho śrutamaho hutam।
sthitāḥ saptarṣayaḥ kṛtvā yadenamupari dhruvam ।
nāmnodāradhiyaṃ putramindro yaḥ pūrvajanmani ॥ 2.1.84 ॥
evaṃ manvantaraṃ yuktamindratvaṃ prāptavānvibhuḥ ॥ 2.1.85 ॥
agniṣṭudatirātraśca sudyumnaśceti te nava ।
apacāreṇa venasya prakopaḥ sumahānabhūt ॥ 2.1.91 ॥
prajārthamṛṣayastasya mamanthurdakṣiṇaṃ karam ।
sa dhanvī kavacī jātastejasā prajvalanniva ।
tasya stavārthamutpannau nipuṇau sūtamāgadhau ॥ 2.1.94 ॥
pitṛbhirdānavaiścaiva gandharvairapsarogaṇaiḥ ।
sarvaiḥ puṇyajanaiścaiva vīrudbhiḥ parvataistathā ॥ 2.1.96 ॥
yathā yakṣaiḥ sagandharvairapsarobhiryathā purā ॥ 2.1.99 ॥ 62.100
kruddhairmaharṣibhiḥ pūrvaṃ tat sarvaṃ kathayasva naḥ ॥ 2.1.101 ॥
yaścemaṃ śrāvayenmartyaḥ pṛthorvainyasya sambhavam ।
yasya putro'bhavadveno nātyarthaṃ dhārmika stathā ॥ 2.1.106 ॥
vedaśāstrāṇyatikramya hyadharme nirato'bhavat ।
na yaṣṭavyaṃ na hotavyamiti tasya prajāpateḥ ।
mayi yajño vidhātavyo mayi hotavyamityapi ॥ 2.1.111 ॥
tamatikrāntamaryādamādadānamasāmpratam ।
nidhane ca prasūto'si prajāpatirasaṃśayaḥ ॥ 2.1.113 ॥
sa prahasya tu durbuddhiridaṃ vacanakovidaḥ ।
dhīvarānasṛjatso'pi venakalmaṣasambhavān ॥ 2.1.122 ॥
pṛthustasmāt samutpannaḥ karāsphālanatejasaḥ ।
samutpanne mahārātri venaśca tridivaṅgataḥ ॥ 2.1.127 ॥
samāgamya tadā vainyamabhyaṣiñcannarādhipam ।
āpastastambhire cāsya samudramabhiyāsyataḥ ।
etasminneva kāle ca yajñe paitāmahe śubhe ।
juhāvendrāya devena tataḥ sūto vyajāyata ॥ 2.1.135 ॥
pramādastatra sañjajñe prāyaścittañca karmasu ।
adharottaracāreṇa jajñe tadvarṇavaikṛtam ॥ 2.1.136 ॥
madhyamo hyeṣa sūtasya dharmaḥ kṣatropajīvanam ।
rathanāgāśca caritaṃ jaghanyañca cikitsitam ॥ 2.1.138 ॥
ṛṣibhistau niyuktau tu bhaviṣyaiḥ stūyatāmiti ।
dānadharmarato nityaṃ satyavān sa jitendriyaḥ ।
eṣa vo vṛttido vainyo bhavantviti narādhipaḥ ॥ 2.1.146 ॥
tvanno vṛttiṃ vidhatsveti maharṣervacanāttadā ।
asyārddanabhaya trastā gaurbhūtvā prādravanmahī ॥ 2.1.148 ॥
dadarśa cāgrato vainyaṃ kārmukodyatadhāriṇam ॥ 2.1.149 ॥    62.152
jvaladbhirviśikhairbāṇairdīptatejasamacyutam ।
uvāca vainyaṃ nādharmaṃ strīvadhe paripaśyasi ।
mayi lokāḥ sthitā rājan mayedandhāryate jagat ।
avadhyāśca striyaḥ prāhustiryagyoniśateṣvapi ।
matvaivaṃ pṛthivīpāla dharmaṃ na tyaktumarhasi ॥ 2.1.155 ॥
krodhaṃ nigṛhya dharmātmā vasudhāmidamabravīt ॥ 2.1.156 ॥
yasmiṃstu nihate bhadre labhante bahavaḥ sukham ।
tasminhate śubhe nāsti pātakañcopa pātakam ॥ 2.1.158 ॥ 62.162
yadi me vacanaṃ nādya kariṣyasi jagaddhitam ॥ 2.1.159 ॥
duhitṛtvañca me gaccha evametaṃ mahadvaram ।
niyacche tvāntu dharmārthaṃ prayuktaṃ ghoradarśane ॥ 2.1.162 ॥
pratyuvāca tato vainyamevamuktā satī mahī ।
vatsantu mama taṃ yaccha kṣareyaṃ yena vatsalā ।
na hi pūrvanisarge vai viṣame pṛthivītale ।
vaivasvate'ntare tasminsarvasyaitasya sambhavaḥ ॥ 2.1.168 ॥ 62.172
samatvaṃ yatra yatrāsīdbhūyastasmiṃstadeva hi ।
tatra tatra prajāstā vai nivasanti sma sarvadā ॥ 2.1.169 ॥
vainyātprabhṛti loke'sminsarvasyaitasya sambhavaḥ ॥ 2.1.170 ॥
sasyāni tena dugdhāni vainyena tu vasundharām ।
manuñca cākṣuṣaṃ kṛtvā vatsampātre ca bhūmaye ।
punaḥ stutvā devagaṇaiḥ purandarapurogamaiḥ ।
sauvarṇaṃ pātramādāya amṛtaṃ duduhe tadā ।
tenaiva varttayante ca devā indrapurogamāḥ ॥ 2.1.175 ॥
nāgaiśca stūyate dugdhā viṣaṃ kṣīraṃ tadā mahī ।
dogdhā ca jatunābhastu pitā maṇivarasya saḥ ।
tena te varttayantīti paramarṣiruvāca ha ॥ 2.1.179 ॥
padmapātre punardugdhā gandharvairapsarogaṇaiḥ ।
gandharvarājo'tibalo mahātmā sūryasannibhaḥ ॥ 2.1.183 ॥
śailaiśca stūyate dugdhā punardevī vasundharā ।
carācarasya lokasya pratiṣṭā yonireva ca ॥ 2.1.188 ॥ 62.193


## 2.2

।।sūta uvāca ।।
vasu dhārayate yasmādvasudhā tena cocyate ॥ 2.2.1 ॥
prathitā pravibhaktā ca śobhitā ca vasundharā।
pṛthureva namaskāryo brahmayoniḥ sanātanaḥ ॥ 2.2.6 ॥
yodhairapi ca saṃgrāme prārthayānairjayaṃ yudhi।
tataḥ svāyambhuve pūrvantadā manvantare punaḥ।
vatsaṃ svāyambhuvaṃ kṛtvā dugdhā grīṣmeṇa vai mahī ॥ 2.2.13 ॥ *
manau svārociṣe dugdhā mahī caitreṇa dhīmatā।
manuṃ svārociṣaṃ kṛtvā vatsaṃ sasyāni vai purā ॥ 2.2.14 ॥
uttame'nuttamenāpi dugdhā devabhujena tu ।
pṛthostu putrau vikrāntau jajñāte'ntarddhipālinau।
sarve pracetaso nāma dhanurvedasya pāragāḥ।
apṛthagdharmacaraṇāste'tapyanta mahattapaḥ।
tadupaśrutya tapasā sarve yuktāḥ pracetasaḥ।
mukhebhyo vāyumagniñca sasṛjurjjātamanyavaḥ ॥ 2.2.29 ॥
tānagniradahadvora evamāsīd drumakṣayaḥ ॥ 2.2.30 ॥
bhāryā bhavatu vo hyeṣā somagarbhavivarddhitā ॥ 2.2.34 ॥
yuṣmākaṃ tejaso'rddhena mama cārddhena tejasaḥ।
tataḥ somasya vacanājjagṛhuste pracetasaḥ।
dadau sa daśa dharmāya kaśyapāya trayodaśa।
kālasya nayane yuktāḥ saptaviṃśatimindave ॥ 2.2.41 ॥
ebhyo dattvā tato'nyā vai catasro'riṣṭanemine ।
dve caiva bāhuputrāya dve caivāṅgirase tathā।
manorvaivasvatasyāpi saptamasya prajāpateḥ ॥ 2.2.43 ॥
gandharvopsarasaścaiva jajñire'nyāśca jātayaḥ ॥ 2.2.44 ॥
tataḥ prabhṛti loke'smin prajā maithunasambhavāḥ।
           ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
kathaṃ pracetasatvañca punarlebhe mahātapāḥ ॥ 2.2.47 ॥
          ।।sūta uvāca।।
yuge yuge bhavantyete sarve dakṣādayo dvijāḥ ।
punaścaiva nirudhyante vidvāṃstatra na muhyati ॥ 2.2.50 ॥
vaivasvatasya vakṣyāmi sāmpratasya mahātmanaḥ।
samāsādvyāsataḥ sargaṃ bruvato me nibodhata ॥ 2.2.56 ॥

## 2.3

sūta uvāca।।
saptame tvatha paryāye manorvaivasvatasya ha।



bhṛgostu bhārgavo devo hyaṅgiro'ṅgirasaḥ sutaḥ ।
vaivasvate'ntere hyasmin nityaṃ te chandajāḥ surāḥ ॥ 2.3.4 ॥


atītānāgatā ye ca varttante ye ca sāmpratam।


trailokye yāni sattvāni gatimantyabalāni ca।

etatsarvaṃ pravakṣyāmi bruvato menibodhata ॥ 2.3.9 ॥



bhavatvāddarśanatvācca bhūrloko'yamabhūttataḥ।

bhavatyutpadyamānena kālaśabdo'yamucyate ॥ 2.3.13 ॥

bhavanāttu bhuvarloko niruktajñairnirucyate।
antarikṣaṃ bhuvastasmād dvitīyo loka ucyate ॥ 2.3.14 ॥

utpanne tu bhuvarloke tṛtīyaṃ brahmaṇā punaḥ ।
bhavyeti vyāhṛtaṃ yasmādbhavyo lokastadā'bhavat ॥ 2.3.15 ॥

anāgate bhavya iti śabda eṣa vibhāvyate।
tasmādbhavyo hyasau loko nāmatastu divaṃ smṛtam ॥ 2.3.16 ॥

svarityuktaṃ tṛtīyo'nyo bhāvyo lokastadābhavat।
bhāvya ityeṣa dhāturvai bhāvye kāle vibhāvyate ॥ 2.3.17 ॥


trailokyayuktairvyāhāraistisro vyāhṛtayo'bhavan।

yasmādbhūtasya lokasya bhavyasya bhavatastadā।

manvantareṣu ye devā yajñabhājo bhavanti hi ॥ 2.3.21 ॥





autathyo gautamo vidvāñcharadvānnāma dhārmikaḥ ॥ 2.3.26 ॥

svāyambhuvo'trirbhagavān brahmako śastu pañcamaḥ।

ete saptarṣayaḥ siddhā varttante sāmprate'ntare ॥ 2.3.28 ॥


manorvaivastasyaite nava putrāḥ prakīrttitāḥ ।
kīrttitā vai mayā hyete saptamañcaitadantaram ॥ 2.3.30 ॥



## 2.4

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।
atastṛtīyaṃ papraccha pādaṃ vai śāṃśapāyanaḥ ॥ 2.4.1 ॥
        ।।sūta uvāca।।
pādaṃ samudayādviprā gadato me nibodhata ॥ 2.4.3 ॥
manorvaivasvatasyemaṃ sāmpratasya mahātmanaḥ।
caturyugaikasaptatyā saṅkhyātaḥ pūrvameva tu।
manvādikaṃ bhaviṣyāntamākhyānairbahuvistaram ।
vakṣye vaivasvataṃ sargaṃ namaskṛtya vivasvate ॥ 2.4.7 ॥
svāyambhuve'ntare pūrvaṃ saptāsan ye maharṣayaḥ।
putratve kalpitāścaiva svayameva svayambhuvā ॥ 2.4.10 ॥
prajāsantānakṛdbhistairutpadyadbhirmahātmabhiḥ।
punaḥ pravarttitaḥ sargo yathāpūrvaṃ yathākramam ॥ 2.4.11 ॥
etacchrutvā vacastasya munīnāṃ saṃśayo'bhavat ।
tatastaṃ saṃśayāviṣṭāḥ sūtaṃ saṃśayaniścaye।
satkṛtya paripapracchurmunayaḥ śaṃsitavratāḥ ॥ 2.4.14 ॥
        ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
putratve kalpitāścaiva tanno nigada sattama।
kathaṃ saptarṣayaḥ siddhā ye vai svāyambhuve'ntare।
manvantaraṃ samāsādya punarvaivasvataṃ kila ॥ 2.4.16 ॥
upapannā jane loke sakṛdāgāminastu te ॥ 2.4.17 ॥
ūcuḥ sarve tato'nyonyaṃ janaloke maharṣayaḥ।
ūcureva mahābhāgā vāruṇe vitate kratau ॥ 2.4.18 ॥
svāyambhuve'ntare śaptāḥ saptārthaṃ te bhavena tu।
jajñire vai punaste ha janalokāddivaṃ gatā ॥ 2.4.20 ॥
devasya mahato yajñe vāruṇīṃ bibhratastanum।
brahmaṇo juhvataḥ śukramagnau pūrvaṃ prajepsayā।
brahma vedastathā ghoraiḥ kṛtyāvidhibhiranvitaḥ।
pratyaṅgirasayogaiśca dviśarīraśiro'bhavat ॥ 2.4.27 ॥
devakanyāśca patnyaśca tathā mātara eva ca ॥ 2.4.29 ॥
āyuḥ sarvata evaite devasya yajato mukhe।
svayambhuvastu tā dṛṣṭvā retaḥ samapatadbhuvi।
ājyavajjuhuvāñjakre mantravacca pitāmahaḥ ॥ 2.4.32 ॥
tasyārvāk tejasastasya yajñe lokeṣu taijasam।
saguṇāttejaso nityamākāśe tamasi sthitam।
tamasastejasatvācca sarvabhūtāni jajñire ॥ 2.4.34 ॥
yadā tasminnajāyanta kāle putrāstu karmajāḥ।
jvalanto vapuṣā yuktāḥ sapta vai prasavairguṇaiḥ ॥ 2.4.36 ॥
bhṛgustvamiti hovāca yasmāttasmātsa vai bhṛguḥ ॥ 2.4.37 ॥
mamaiṣa putrakāmasya dīkṣitasya tvayaṃ prabho।
vijajñe'tha bhṛgurddevo mama putro bhavatvayam ॥ 2.4.38 ॥
tathoti samanujñāto mahādevaḥ svayambhuvā।
putratve kalpayāmāsa mahādevastathā bhṛgum।
retodhāstubhyamevāhaṃ dvitīyo'yaṃ mamāstviti ॥ 2.4.41 ॥
evamastviti so'pyukto brahmaṇā sadasaspatiḥ।
marīciḥ prathamastatra marīcibhyaḥ samutthitaḥ।
kratau tasmin suto jajñe yatastasmātsa vai kratuḥ ॥ 2.4.44 ॥
ahaṃ tṛtīya ityarthastasmādatriḥ sa kīrttyate।
vasumadhyātsamutpanno vasumān vasudhāśrayaḥ ॥ 2.4.46 ॥
vasiṣṭha iti tattvajñaiḥ procyate vrahmavādibhiḥ ।
apare pitaro nāma etaireva maharṣibhiḥ।
ete samāsatastāta puraiva tu guṇāstrayaḥ।
pracetaso'riṣṭanemirbahulasva prajāpatiḥ।
ityevamādayo'nye'pi bahavaśca prajeśvarāḥ ॥ 2.4.54 ॥
srotobhyastasya cotpannāvaśvinau rūpasammitau।
vidurjanmākṣarajaso vimalā netrasambhavāḥ 4.57 ।।
raudraṃ lohitamityāhurlohitaṃ kanakaṃ smṛtam।
ādimānasya lokasya brahmā brahmasamudbhavaḥ।
sarvakāmadamityāhustatra kanyāmudāharan ॥ 2.4.61 ॥
brahmā suragurustatra tridaśaiḥ saṃprasīdati।
dvandvaṃ saṃvarddhayāmāsa tava tejovivarddhanam।
anantaṃ brahma satyañca tapaśca paramaṃ bhuvi ॥ 2.4.65 ॥
sarve hi vayamete ca tavaiva prasavaḥ prabho।
tato'bravīllokaguruḥ paramityavicārayan ।
bhāryā bhṛgorapratime uttame'bhijane śubhe।
bhṛgostvajanayaddivyā kāvyaṃ vedavidāṃ varam।
brāhmeṇa tejasā yuktaḥ sa cāto brahmavittamaḥ।
tasyāmeva tu catvāraḥ putrāḥ śukrasya jajñire ॥ 2.4.75 ॥
ijyādharmavināśārthaṃ manumetyābhyayojayan।
tirobhūteṣu teṣmindro dharmapatnīñca cetanām।
graheṇa mocayitvā tu tataḥ so'nusasāra tām ॥ 2.4.80 ॥
suṣvāpa devadevasya vedyāṃ vai dakṣiṇe tataḥ ॥ 2.4.81 ॥
devyāṃ tānsuṣuve sarvānkāvyaścaivātmajānprabhuḥ ॥ 2.4.85 ॥
paulomyajanayatputraṃ brahmiṣṭhaṃ vaśinaṃ vibhum ॥ 2.4.87 ॥
cyavanāccyavanāso'tha cetanastu pracetasaḥ।
jamadagniṛcīkasya satyavatyāṃ vyajāyata।
bhṛgośca ruciparyāye raudravaiṣṇavayostathā ॥ 2.4.92 ॥
jamanādvaiṣṇavasyāgnerjamadagnirajāyata।
reṇukā jamadagnestu śakratulyaparākramam।
brahmakṣatramayaṃ rāmaṃ suṣuve'mitatejasam ॥ 2.4.93 ॥
aurvasyāsītputraśataṃ jamadagnipurāgemam ।
vatso viśvo'śviṣeṇaśca pāṇḍaḥ pathyaḥ saśaunakaḥ।
ityetāṅgirasaḥ patnyastāsu vakṣyāmi santatim ॥ 2.4.97 ॥
avandhyaṃ vāmadevañca utathyamuśijantathā ॥ 2.4.99 ॥
aṅgirasastu saṃvartto devānaṅgirasaḥ śrṛṇu।
bṛhaspateryavīyāṃso devā hyaṅgirasaḥ smṛtāḥ ॥ 2.4.102 ॥
audāryāyurdanurdakṣo darbhaḥ prāṇastathaiva ca।
ayasyastu utathyaśca vāmadevastathośijaḥ।
mārīcaṃ parivakṣyāmi vaṃśamuttamapūruṣam।
yasyānvavāye sambhūtaṃ jagatsthāvarajaṅgamam ॥ 2.4.107 ॥
saṃpūjyate praśastāyāṃ manasā bhāvitā prabhuḥ ॥ 2.4.108 ॥
putraṃ marīcaṃ sūryābhaṃ vadhauveśo vyajījanat ॥ 2.4.110 ॥
saptavarṣasahasrāṇi tataḥ so'pratimo'bhavat ॥ 2.4.111 ॥
apibatsa tadā kaśyaṃ kaśyaṃ madyamihocyate ॥ 2.4.113 ॥
dakṣābhiśaptaḥ kupitaḥ kaśyapastena so'bhavat ॥ 2.4.115 ॥
athābruvan punaḥ sarve munayo romaharṣaṇam।
nisargaḥ sampravṛtto'yaṃ manorvaiva svatasya ha ॥ 2.4.118 ॥
          ।।sūta uvāca।।
sasarja dakṣo bhūtāni gatimanti dhruvāṇi ca ।
upasthite'ntare hyasmin manorvaivasvatasya ha ॥ 2.4.119 ॥
tataḥ pravṛtto dakṣastuprajāḥ sraṣṭuñcaturvidhāḥ।
sambhāvito yogabalairaṇimādyairviśeṣataḥ ॥ 2.4.121 ॥
ātmānaṃ vyabhajan śrīmān manuṣyoragarākṣasān।
devāsurasagandharvān divyasaṃhananaprajān ।
īśvarānātmanastulyān rūpadraviṇatejasā ॥ 2.4.122 ॥
tathaivānyāni mudito gatimanti dhruvāṇi ca।
yayā dhṛtamidaṃ sarvaṃ jagat sthāvarajaṅgamam ॥ 2.4.127 ॥
atha putrasahasraṃ sa vairiṇyāmamitaujasā।
yaḥ sa vai procyatevipraḥ kaśyapasyeti kṛtrimaḥ।
dakṣaśāpabhayādbhīto brahmārṣistena karmaṇā ॥ 2.4.134 ॥
tasmāt sa kaśyapasyātha dvitīyaṃ mānaso'bhavat।
tato'bhisandhitaṃ cakre dakṣastu parameṣṭinā।
kanyāyāṃ nārado mahyaṃ tava putro bhavatviti ॥ 2.4.139 ॥
tadupaśrutya viprāste jātakautūhalāḥ punaḥ ।
apṛcchan vadatāṃ śreṣṭhaṃ sūtaṃ tattvārthadarśinam ॥ 2.4.141 ॥
           ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।
sa tathyaṃ vacanaṃ śrutvā jijñāsāsambhavaṃ śubham ।
antamūrddhvamadhaścaiva kathaṃ srakṣyatha vai prajāḥ ॥ 2.4.145 ॥
kiṃ pramāṇantu medinyāḥ sraṣṭavyāni tathaiva ca।
avijñāyeha sraṣṭavyamanyathā kiṃ nu srakṣyatha।
alpaṃ vāpi bahurvāpi tatra doṣastu dṛśyate ॥ 2.4.146 ॥
vāyuntu samanuprāpya gatāste vai parābhavam ॥ 2.4.147 ॥
adyāpi na nivarttante bhramanto vāyumiśritāḥ ।
evaṃ vāyupathaṃ prāpya bhramante te maharṣayaḥ ॥ 2.4.148 ॥
tacchratvā vacanaṃ sarve kumārāste mahaujasaḥ।
adyāpi na nivarttante samudrebhya ivāpagāḥ ॥ 2.4.152 ॥
tāstadā pratijagrāha patnyarthe kaśyapaḥ prabhuḥ।
dharmaḥ somastu bhagavāṃstathaivānye maharṣayaḥ ॥ 2.4.156 ॥


## 2.5

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।

utpattiṃ vistareṇeha brūhi vaivasvate'ntare ॥ 2.5.1 ॥

       ।।sūta uvāca।।
dharmasya tāvadvakṣyāmi nisargaṃ taṃ nibodhata।

dharmapatnyo daśatvetā dakṣaḥ prācetaso dadau ॥ 2.5.3 ॥



darśaśca paurṇamāsaśca bṛhadyacca rathantaram ।






manvantare bhaviṣyāmastannaḥ śreyo bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.5.12 ॥


naranārāyaṇau tatra jajñāte punareva hi।
vipaścidindro yaścāsīttathā satyo hariśca tau।


prabhavo'tha vibhuścaiva sādhyā dvādaśa jajñire ॥ 2.5.16 ॥





dharasya putro draviṇo hutahavyavahastathā।
dhruvaputro bhavo nāmnā kālo lokaprakālanaḥ ॥ 2.5.21 ॥

somasya bhagavān varcā budhaśca grahabodhanaḥ।
rohiṇyāṃ tau samutpannau triṣu lokeṣu viśrutau ॥ 2.5.22 ॥


agniputraḥ kumārastu śarastambe vyajāyata।

avijñātagatiścaiva dvau putrāvanilasya ca ॥ 2.5.25 ॥

dvau putrau devalasyāpi kṣamāvantau manīṣiṇau ॥ 2.5.26 ॥

yogasiddhā jagatkṛtsnamasaktā vicaratyuta ॥ 2.5.27 ॥









nāmataḥ saṃpravakṣyāmi bruvato me nibodhata ॥ 2.5.36 ॥



tato vedavidaścaitāṃ tithimicchanti parvasu।


prājāpatyastathā aindrastathendro niṛtistathā।



brahmasaumyastathādityo bārhaspatyo'tha vaiṣṇavaḥ।


dakṣiṇottaramadhyāni tāni vidyādyathākramam ॥ 2.5.46 ॥

sthānaṃ jāradgavaṃ madhye tathairāvatamuttaram।

tisrastu vīthayo hyetā uttare mārga ucyate ॥ 2.5.48 ॥


etāstu vīthayastisro madhyame mārga ucyate ॥ 2.5.50 ॥







upasthite'ntare hyasmin punarvaivasvatasya ha।

vaivasvate'ntare hyasmin yogādarddhena cetasaḥ ॥ 2.5.58 ॥


vaivasvate'ntare hyasmin naranārāyaṇau surau ॥ 2.5.60 ॥

teṣāmapi hi devānāṃ nidhanotpattirucyate।
yathā sūryasya loke'smin udayāstamayāvubhau।





tatastvaṣṭā tato viṣṇurajaghanyo'jaghanyajaḥ।


ajaikapādahirbughnyamūrddhvaketuṃ jvaraṃ tathā ॥ 2.5.68 ॥

tapasā tena mahatā surabhī tānajījanat ॥ 2.5.69 ॥

tato duhitarāvanye surabhī dve vyajāyata।

suṣuve kāmadughā tu surūpā tanayadvayam ॥ 2.5.71 ॥

haṃsakīlā nṛpa mṛṣīn bhadrāyāstu vyajāyata।

rudrā devopabāhyāste gandharvayonayo hayāḥ ॥ 2.5.73 ॥

surabhyanumate datto dhvajo māheśvarastu saḥ ॥ 2.5.74 ॥



ete yugasahasrānte jāyante punareva hi ॥ 2.5.77 ॥

eteṣāmapi devānāṃ nirodhotpattirucyate ॥ 2.5.78 ॥

yathā sūryasya loke'smin udayāstamayāvubhau।
ete devanikāyāste sambhavanti yuge yuge ॥ 2.5.79 ॥

           ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
sādhyāśca vasavo viśve rudrādityāstathaiva ca।


yaśca yasmāt prabhavati yaśca yasmin pratiṣṭitaḥ।

etat prabhrūhi naḥ sarvaṃ tvaṃ hi vettha yathāyatham ॥ 2.5.83 ॥

        ।।sūta uvāca।।
atra vo varṇayiṣyehamantaranteṣu yat smṛtam।


guṇavṛddhinibandhatvād dvidhānugrahabandhataḥ ॥ 2.5.86 ॥



rajasā tu samudrikto brahmā sambhavate yadā।


sattvodrekāttu puruṣo yadā bhavati sa prabhuḥ।

kramāttasya nivarttante rūpaṃ nāma ca karma ca।
trailokye varttamānasya sarvānugrahanigrahaiḥ ॥ 2.5.92 ॥

yadā bhavati brahmā ca tadā cāntaramucyate।
yadā ca puruṣo brahmā na caiva puruṣastu saḥ ॥ 2.5.93 ॥

maṇirvibhajate varṇān vicitrān sphaṭike yathā।

tadā guṇavaśāttasya svayambhoranurañjanam।
ekatve ca pṛthaktve ca proktametannidarghanam ॥ 2.5.95 ॥


bhavatyeko dvidhā caiva tridhā mūrtivināśanāt।
eko brahmāntakṛccaiva puruṣaśceti te trayaḥ ॥ 2.5.97 ॥

ekasyaitāḥ smṛtāstisrastanavastu svayambhuvaḥ।
brāhmī ca pauruṣī caiva antakārī ca te trayaḥ ॥ 2.5.98 ॥



trailokye tāḥ smṛtāstisrastanavastu svayambhuvaḥ ॥ 2.5.101 ॥


ekaḥ svayambhuvaḥ kālastribhistrīn vai karoti saḥ।

ityetāḥ kathitā stisrastanavastu svayambhuvaḥ।


ekatve ca pṛthaktve ca tāsu bhinnāḥ prajāstviha ।
idaṃ paramidaṃ neti bruvato bhinnadarśanāḥ ॥ 2.5.106 ॥

kecicchivaṃ paratvena prāhurviṣṇuṃ tathā'pare।


ekaṃ nindati yasteṣāṃ sarvānena sa nindati।
ekaṃ yo vetti puruṣaṃ tamāhurbrahmavādinam ॥ 2.5.109 ॥



idaṃ paramidaṃ neti vadanti bhinnadarśinaḥ ॥ 2.5.112 ॥

ekatvena pṛthaktvena svayambhūrvyavatiṣṭhate ॥ 2.5.113 ॥

guṇamātrātmikābhistu tanubhirmohayan prajāḥ।
teṣvekaṃ yajate yastu sa tadā yajate trayam ॥ 2.5.114 ॥

tasmāddevāstrayo hyete nairantarye vyavasthitāḥ।

guṇātmakatvātrtraikālye tasmādekaḥ sa ucyate ॥ 2.5.116 ॥


manvantare ca kalpe ca āvarttante punaḥ punaḥ ॥ 2.5.118 ॥

tejasā yaśasā buddhyā śrutena ca balena ca ।
jāyante tatsamāścaiva tānapīha nibodhata ॥ 2.5.119 ॥



samprāpte caiva kalpānte saptaraśmirddivākaraḥ।

viṣṇuḥ prajānugṛhṇāti nāmarupaviparyaye ।

tasyāṃśena vijajñe sa iha svāyambhuve'ntare।

tataḥ punaḥ sa vai devo prāpte svārociṣe'ntare।

auttame cāntare caiva tuṣitastu viduḥ sa vai।
vaśavarttibhirutpanno vaśavarttī hariḥ punaḥ ॥ 2.5.125 ॥

satyāyāmabhavatsatyaḥ satyaiḥ saha surottamaiḥ।
tāmasasyāntare cāpi samprāpte punareva hi।



tristriḥ kramairimān lokān jitvā viṣṇururukramam ॥ 2.5.129 ॥

pratyapādayadindrāya devebhyaścaiva sa prabhuḥ।
ityetāsatanavastasya vyatītāḥ sapta saptasu।


ityete brahmaṇaścaiva vāmanasya mahātmanaḥ।


yadyadvibhūtimatsattvaṃ śrīmadūrjitameva vā।
tattadevāvagacchaghvaṃ viṣṇostejoṃ'śasambhavam ॥ 2.5.134 ॥

tato'pare bruvantīmamanyonyāṃśena jāyate ॥ 2.5.135 ॥

yasmānna vidyate bhedo manasaścetasaśca ha।

ekastu prabhuśaktyā vai bahudhā bhavatīśvaraḥ।
bhūtvā yasmācca bahudhā bhavanyekaḥ punastu saḥ ॥ 2.5.137 ॥

tasmātsumanaso bhedājjāyante tejasaśca ha।




caturbhiḥ kāraṇairebhiryathātattvaṃ na vindati ॥ 2.5.142 ॥

tenānyat santamapyarthaṃ dveṣānna pratipadyate ॥ 2.5.143 ॥


imau codāharantyatra ślokau yogeśvaraṃ prati ॥ 2.5.145 ॥

kuryādyogabalamprāpya taiśca sarvaiḥ sahācaret ॥ 2.5.146 ॥

prāpnuyādviṣayāścaiva tathaivogratapaścaran ।


## 2.6

।।ṛṣaya ucuḥ ।।
etacchrutvā vacastasya naimiṣeyāstapasvinaḥ।
saptasviha kathaṃ devā jātā manvantareṣviha।
eva muktastadā sūto vinayī brahmavādibhiḥ।
          ।।sūta uvāca।।
darśaśca paurṇamāsaśca bṛhadyacca rathantaram।
ākūtaḥ prathamasteṣāṃ tatastvākūtireva ca ॥ 2.6.5 ॥
adhīṣṭastu tato jñeyaḥ adhītiścaiva tattvataḥ।
jñeyo dvādaśaputraśca yaścābdena samājayet।
taṃ dṛṣṭvā cābravīdbrahmā jayā devānasūyata ॥ 2.6.7 ॥
dārā grihotrasaṃyoge mithyāmārabhateti ca।
evamuktvā tu taṃ vrahmā tatraivāntaradhīyata ॥ 2.6.8 ॥
tataste nābhyanandanta tadvākyaṃ parameṣṭinaḥ।
jugupsantaḥ prasūtiñca nistandrā nirmamābhavan।
prasūyadhvaṃ jayaghvañcetyuktavānasmi yat purā ॥ 2.6.14 ॥
jugupsamānāḥ svaṃ janma santatiṃ nābhi nandatha ॥ 2.6.15 ॥
mayā parigataṃ sarvaṃ kathamacchandato mama।
loke yadasti kiñcidvai sattvāsattvavyavasthitam।
tathā vicāritaṃ yacca tatsarvaṃ viditaṃ mama ॥ 2.6.21 ॥
mayā sthitamidaṃ sarvaṃ jagat sthāvarajaṅgamam।
āśāmayena tattvena kathaṃ chettumihotsahe ॥ 2.6.22 ॥
iha karmāṇyanārabhya ko me chandādvimokṣyate ॥ 2.6.23 ॥
paribhāṣya tato devān jayān vai naṣṭacetasaḥ।
abravītsa punastān vai dhṛtān daṇḍe prajāpatiḥ ॥ 2.6.24 ॥
yasmātsa viphalo'yanno hyapārastveṣa yaḥ kṛtaḥ।
ātmacchandena vo janma bhaviṣyati surottamāḥ।
manvantareṣu saṃmūḍhāḥ ṣaṭ‌su sarve gamiṣyatha ॥ 2.6.26 ॥
trayīṃ vidyāṃ brahmacaryaṃ prasūtiṃ śrāddhameva ca ।
yajñañcaiva tu dānañca eṣāmeva tu kurvatām।
sa hi sma virajā bhūtvā vasate'nyapraśaṃsayā ॥ 2.6.28 ॥
vaivasvate'ntare'tīte matsamīpamiheṣyatha ॥ 2.6.29 ॥
tato yūyaṃ mayā sārddhaṃ siddhiṃ prāpsyatha śāśvatīm।
evamuktvā tu tān brahmā tatraivāntaradhīyata ॥ 2.6.30 ॥
tato devāstirobhūte īśvare hyakutobhayam।
tatasteṣāntu yāstanvastā'bhavan dvādaśa hradāḥ।
tataḥ svāyambhuve tasmin sargete jajñire surāḥ।
vidhiśca munayaścaiva kṣemo nando'vyayastathā।
punaste tuṣitā devā uttame tvantare svayam ॥ 2.6.36 ॥
te tu satyāḥ punarddevāḥ samprāpte tāmase'ntare ॥ 2.6.38 ॥
harayo nāma te devā yajñabhājastathā'bhavan ॥ 2.6.39 ॥
upasthite manoḥ sarge punarvaivasvatasya ha ॥ 2.6.42 ॥
ādye tretāyugamukhe prāpte vaivasvatasya tu ।
ya imāṃ saptasambhūtiṃ devānāṃ devaśāsanāt ।
          ।।ṛṣa ūcuḥ ।।
utpattiṃ nidhanañcaiva vistarāt kathayasva naḥ ॥ 2.6.48 ॥
kaśyapasyātmajau tau vai sarvebhyaḥ pūrvajau smṛtau।
sūtye'hanyatirātrasya kaśyapasyāśvamedhike ॥ 2.6.50 ॥
           ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।
hiraṇyakaśipornāma janma caiva mahātmanaḥ।
prabhāvañcaiva daityasya vistarādbrūhi naḥ prabho ॥ 2.6.52 ॥
       ।।sūta uvāca।।
ṛṣibhirddevatābhiśca gandharvai rupaśobhitaḥ ॥ 2.6.53 ॥
mukhyārtvijaśca catvārasteṣāntānyupakalpayet ॥ 2.6.55 ॥
śubhaṃ tatrāsanaṃ yattu hoturarthe prakalpitam।
daśa varṣasahasrāṇi garbhastasyā avartata ॥ 2.6.57 ॥
upaklṛptāsanaṃ yattu hoturarthe hiraṇmayam।
sarvāmaratvaṃ viprebhyaḥ sarvabhūtebhya eva ca।
yogāddevān vinirjitya sarvadevatvamāsthitaḥ ॥ 2.6.62 ॥
evamukto'tha brahmā tu tasmai dattvā yathepsitam।
dattvā tasmai varān divyān tatraivāntaradhīyata।
evaṃprabhāvo daityendro hiraṇyakaśipu rdvijāḥ।
nakhaistu tena nirbhinnastataḥ śuddhā nakhāḥ smṛtāḥ ॥ 2.6.66 ॥
utkuraḥ śakuniścaiva kālanābhastathaiva ca ॥ 2.6.67 ॥
prahlādaḥ pūrvajasteṣāmanuhlādastathaiva ca।
saṃhrādaśca hradaścaiva hradaputrānnibodhata ॥ 2.6.70 ॥
hrādo nisundaśca tatā hradaputrau babhūvatuḥ।
sundopasundau vikrāntau nisundatana yāvubhau ॥ 2.6.71 ॥
brahmaghnastu mahāvīryo mūkastu hradadāyinaḥ।
śumbhaścaiva niśumbhaśca viṣvakseno mahaujasaḥ ॥ 2.6.77 ॥
gaveṣṭinaḥ sutā hyete jambhasya śatadundubhiḥ।
tathā dakṣaśca khaṇḍaśca trayastu jambhasūnavaḥ ॥ 2.6.78 ॥
virodhaśca manuścaiva vṛkṣāyuḥ kuśalīmukhaḥ।
brahmajit kṣatrajiccaiva devāntakanarāntakau ।
dhanuko hyasilomā ca nābalaśca sagomukhaḥ।
virocanasya putrastu balirekaḥ pratāpavān।
śakunī pūtanā caiva kanye dve tu baleḥ sute।
bāṇasya candra manaso lauhityamupapadyate ॥ 2.6.85 ॥
sa tu tasyai varaṃ prādāt prārthitaṃ bhagavan prabhuḥ।
kimicchasi mayi śubhre mārīcastāmabhāṣata ॥ 2.6.87 ॥
hataputrāsmi bhagavān ādityaistava sūnubhiḥ ॥ 2.6.88 ॥
śakrahantāramiccheyaṃ putraṃ dīrghatapo'nvitam।
evaṃ bhavatu bhadrante śucirbhava tapodhane।
janayiṣyasi satputraṃ śakrahantāramāhave ॥ 2.6.91 ॥
putraṃ trilokapracaramathatvaṃ janayiṣyasi ॥ 2.6.92 ॥
evamuktvā mahātejāstayā samavasat prabhuḥ ।
gate bharttari sā devī ditiḥ paramaharṣitā।
tapastasyāntu kurvantyāṃ paricaryāñcakāra ha।
nyavedayat sahasrākṣo yaccānyadapi kiñcana ॥ 2.6.96 ॥
evamārādhitā śakramuvācātha ditistathā ॥ 2.6.97 ॥
        ।।ditiruvāca।।
jayalipsāṃ samādhāsye labdhvāhaṃ tādṛśaṃ sutam।
trailokyavijayaṃ pūtra prāpsyāmi saha tena vai ॥ 2.6.99 ॥
tasyāstadantaraṃ labdhvā jahāsa ca mumoda ca ॥ 2.6.101 ॥
abhinatsa pradhānantu kuliśena mahāyaśāḥ ॥ 2.6.102 ॥
kuliśena bibhedendrastato ditirabudhyata ॥ 2.6.104 ॥
na hantavyo na hantavya ityevaṃ ditirabravīt ।
prāñjalirvajrasahito ditiṃ śakro'bhyabhāṣata ॥ 2.6.105 ॥
aśucirdevi suptāsi pādayorgatamūrddhajā।
tadantaramahaṃ labdhvā śakrahantāramāhave।
bhinnavān garbhametante bahudhā kṣantumarhasi ॥ 2.6.106 ॥
tasmistu viphale garbhe ditiḥ paramaduḥkhitā।
priyantu karttumicchāmi śreyo garbhasya me kuru ॥ 2.6.109 ॥
vātaskandhānimān sapta carantu mama putrakāḥ।
pṛthivyāṃ prathamaskandho dvitīyaścaiva bhāskare।
dhruve tu saptamaścaiva vātaskandhaḥ parastu saḥ ॥ 2.6.112 ॥
pṛthivyāṃ prathamaskandho amedhyebhyo ya āvahaḥ।
sūryorddhvantu tataḥ somādudvaho yastu vai smṛtaḥ।
somādūrddhvaṃ tatharkṣebhyaścaturthaḥ suvahastu yaḥ।
yakṣebhyaśca tathaivorddhvamāgrahādvivahastu yaḥ।
pañcamaṃ pañcamaḥ saumyaḥ skandhastu caratāṅgaṇaḥ ॥ 2.6.118 ॥
ūrddhvaṃ grahādṛṣibhyastu ṣaṣṭo yo vai parāhataḥ ।
carantu mama putrāstu tatra ṣaṣṭe gaṇe tu ye ॥ 2.6.119 ॥
saptarṣayastathaivorddhvamādhruvāt saptamastu yaḥ।
vāta skandhaḥ parivahastatra tiṣṭhantu me sutāḥ ॥ 2.6.120 ॥
tatra tena ca nāmnā vai bhavantu marutastvime ॥ 2.6.121 ॥
tatkṛte karmabhiścaiva maruto vai pṛthak pṛthak ॥ 2.6.122 ॥
sattvajyotistathādityaḥ satyajyotistathā'paraḥ।
tiryagjyotiśca sajyotirjjyotiṣmānaparastathā ॥ 2.6.123 ॥
prathamastu gaṇaḥ prokto dvitīyaṃ me nibodhata।
ṛtajitsatyajiccaiva suṣeṇaḥ senajittathā ॥ 2.6.124 ॥
satmitro'bhimitraśca harimitrastathā'paraḥ।
dhvāntaścaiva dhuniścaiva hyugro bhīmastathaiva ca।
vāta skandhaṃ carantvete mama putrāśca putraka।
vicarantu ca bhadrante devaiḥ saha mamātmajāḥ ॥ 2.6.130 ॥
sarvametadyathoktante bhaviṣyati na saṃśayaḥ।
nāvṛṣṭibhayamāpnoti bahvāyuśca bhavettataḥ ॥ 2.6.135 ॥


## 2.7

।।sūta uvāca।।
ata ūrddhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi danuputrānnibodhata।

sarve labdhavarāścaiva sutaptatapasastathā ॥ 2.7.2 ॥



ajāmukho'tha bhagavān śilo vāmanasastathā।
marīcirakṣakaścaiva mahāgārgyo'ṅgirāvṛtaḥ ॥ 2.7.5 ॥

indrajidviśvajiccaiva tathāsura vimarddanaḥ ॥ 2.7.6 ॥





asuraśca virupākṣaḥ supatho'tha mahāsuraḥ।


teṣāmaparisaṅkhyeyaṃ putrapautrādyanantakam ।। 7.13 ।।

svarbhānustu smṛto daityo hyanubhānurdanoḥ sutaḥ।



siṃhikāyāmathotpannā vipracittisutāstvime ।। 7.17 ।।

śatagālaśca balavānnyāsaḥ śāmbastathaiva ca । 7.18 ।।

haraḥ kalpaḥ kālanābho bhaumaśca narakastathā ।। 7.19 ।।



nihato jāmadagnyena bhārgaveṇa balīyasā।
svarbhānostu prabhā kanyā pulomno'tha śacī tathā ।। 7.22 ।।

pulomā kālikā caiva vaiśvānarasute ubhe ।। 7.23 ।।


vaiśvānarasute hyete pulomā kālikā ubhe ।
ubhe hyapi tu te kanye mārīcasya parigrahe । 7.25 ।।



mayasya jātā ye putrāḥ sarve vīraparākramāḥ।


arūrurbālijambhau ca virakṣaśca viṣastathā ।। 7.30 ।।

aruro stanayaḥ krūro dhundhurnāma mahāsuraḥ।
nihataḥ kuvalāśvena uttaṅkavacanāt kila ।। 7.31 ।।

baleḥ putrau mahāvīryau tejasāpratimāvubhau।
kumbhilaścakravarmā ca sa karṇaḥ pūrvajanmani ।। 7.32 ।।

virakṣasyāpi putrau dvau kālakaśca varaśca tau।



prajāsvantargatāḥ sarve nivasanti sudhārmikāḥ ।। 7.36 ।।

pravāhyajanayat putrān yajñe vai gāyanottamān ।। 7.37 ।।


panaścaiva taraṇyaśca sucandro daśamastathā।


## 2.8

।।sūta uvāca।।
citrasenograsenaśca ūrṇāyuranaghastathā ॥ 2.8.1 ॥

yugapattṛṇapatkālirditiścitraratha stathā ॥ 2.8.2 ॥


antarā dāravatyā ca priyamukhyā surottamā ॥ 2.8.4 ॥


suvarā ca subāhuśca pūrṇitā supratiṣṭitā ॥ 2.8.6 ॥

hemāsarā sutī caiva suvṛttā kamalā ca yā ॥ 2.8.7 ॥

subhujā haṃsapādā ca laukikyo'psarasastathā।

tāsāṃ nāmāni sarvāsāṃ kīrttyamānāni me śrṛṇu ॥ 2.8.9 ॥

suyaśā prathamā tāsāṃ gāndharvī tadanantaram ।
vidyāvatī cārumukhī sumukhī ca varānanā ॥ 2.8.10 ॥


kambalo harikeśaśca kapilaḥ kāñcanastathā।
meghamālītu yakṣāṇāṃ gaṇa eṣa udāhṛtaḥ ॥ 2.8.12 ॥

suyaśāyā duhitaraścatasro'psarasaḥ smṛtāḥ।
tāsāṃ nāmāni vai samyag bruvato me nibodhata ॥ 2.8.13 ॥

loheyī tvabhavajjyeṣṭā bharatā tadanantaram।
kṛśāṅgī ca viśālā ca rūpeṇāpratimā tathā ॥ 2.8.14 ॥

tābhyo'pare yakṣagaṇāścatvāraḥ parikīrttitāḥ।
utpāditā viśālena vikrāntena mahātmanā ॥ 2.8.15 ॥

loheyo bharateyaśca kṛśāṅgeyaśca viśrutaḥ ।
viśāleśca yakṣāṇāṃ purāṇe prathitā gaṇāḥ ॥ 2.8.16 ॥

ityetairasurairghorairmahābalaparākramaiḥ।
lokairyakṣagaṇairvyāptā lokāloka vidāṃvarāḥ ॥ 2.8.17 ॥

gandharvāścātha vāleyā vikrāntena mahātmanā।


citrāṅgado mahāvīryaścitravarmā tathaiva ca।
citraketurmahābhāgaḥ somadatto'tha vīryavān।
tisro duhitaraścaiva tāsāṃ nāmāni vakṣyate ॥ 2.8.20 ॥

prathamā tvagnikā nāma kambalā tadanantaram।
tathā vasumatī nāma rūpeṇāpratimaujasaḥ ॥ 2.8.21 ॥

tābhyaḥ pare kumāreṇa gaṇā utpāditāstvime।
trayo gandharvamukhyānāṃ vikrāntā yuddhadurmadāḥ ॥ 2.8.22 ॥

āgneyāḥ kāmbaleyāśca tathā vasumatīsutāḥ।
tairgaṇairvividhairvyāptamidaṃ lokacarācaram ॥ 2.8.23 ॥

vidyāvantaśca tenaiva vikrāntena mahātmanā।
utpāditā mahābhāgā rūpavidyādhaneśvarāḥ ॥ 2.8.24 ॥

teṣāmudīrṇa vīryāṇāṃ gandharvāṇāṃ mahātmanām।

hiraṇyaromā kapilaḥ sulomā māgadhastathā।
candraketuśca vai gāṅgo godaścaiva mahābalaḥ ॥ 2.8.26 ॥

mahāvidyāvadātānāṃ vikrāntānāṃ tapasvinām।
ityevamādirhi gaṇo dve cānye ca sulocane ॥ 2.8.27 ॥

śivā ca sumanāścaiva tābhyāmapi mahātmanā।
utpāditā viśravasā vidyācaraṇagocarāḥ ॥ 2.8.28 ॥

śaiveyāścaiva vikrāntāstathā saumanaso gaṇāḥ।
etairvyāptamidaṃ lokaṃ vidyādharagaṇaistribhiḥ ॥ 2.8.29 ॥

ebhyo'nekāni jātāni ambarāntaracāriṇām।
loke gaṇaśatānyeva vidyāgharaviceṣṭitāt ॥ 2.8.30 ॥

aśvamukhāśca tenaiva vikrāntena mahātmanā।
utpāditā hyaśvamukhāḥ kinnarāṃstānnibodhata ॥ 2.8.31 ॥

samudraḥ senaḥ kālindo mahānetro mahābalaḥ।
suvarṇaghoṣaḥ sugrīvo mahāghoṣaśca vīryavān ॥ 2.8.32 ॥

ityevamādirhi gaṇaḥ kinnarāṇāṃ mahātmanām।
hayānanānāṃ vidvadbhirvistīrṇaḥ parikīrttyate ॥ 2.8.33 ॥

tathāsamutthitenaiva vikrāntena mahātmanā।
utpāditā naramukhāḥ kinnarāḥ śāṃśapāyanāḥ ॥ 2.8.34 ॥

rudradattendradattau ca candradruma mahādrumau ॥ 2.8.35 ॥

binduśca bindusāraśca candravaṃśāśca kinnarāḥ।
ityete kinnarāḥ śreṣṭā loke khyātāḥ suśobhanāḥ ॥ 2.8.36 ॥

nṛtyagītapragalbhānāmeteṣāṃ dvijasattamāḥ।
loke gaṇaśatānyeva kinnarāṇāṃ mahātmanām ॥ 2.8.37 ॥

yakṣā yakṣopaśāntaśca lauheyā rūpaśālinī।
duhītā suravindeti prakāśā siddhasammatā ॥ 2.8.38 ॥

upāyāketanasyāhi svayamutpādito gaṇaḥ ।
karālakena bhūtānāṃ teṣāṃ nāmāni me śrṛṇu ॥ 2.8.39 ॥

bhūtā bhūtagaṇairjñeyā āveśakaniveśakāḥ।
ityevamādirhi gaṇo bhūmigocarakaḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.8.40 ॥

vijñeya iha loke'smin bhūtānāṃ bhūtanāyakaḥ।
ye utkṛṣṭā bhavantyeṣāmambarāntaracāriṇām।
vṛkṣāgramātramākāśaṃ te caranti na saṃśayaḥ ॥ 2.8.41 ॥

tatreme devagandharvāḥ prāyeṇa kathitā mayā।
devopasthānaniratā vijñeyāste yaśasvinaḥ ॥ 2.8.42 ॥

nārāyaṇaṃ suraguruṃ virajaṃ puṣkarekṣaṇam।
hiraṇyagarbhañca tathā caturvaktraṃ svayambhuvam ॥ 2.8.43 ॥

śaṅkarañca mahādevamīśānañca jagatprabhum।
indrapūrvāṃstathā dityān rudrāṃśca vasubhiḥ saha ॥ 2.8.44 ॥

upatasthuḥ sagandharvā nṛttyagītaviśāradāḥ।
tridaśāḥ sarvalokasthā nipuṇā gītavādinaḥ ॥ 2.8.45 ॥

haṃso jyeṣṭhaḥ kaniṣṭo'nyo madhyamau ca hahā huhūḥ।
caturtho dhiṣaṇaścaiva tato vāsirucistathā ॥ 2.8.46 ॥



manovatī sukeśā ca tumburostu sute ubhe।

pramlocetyabhivikhyātānumlocantī tathaiva ca ॥ 2.8.50 ॥


menasya menakā kanyā brahmaṇo hṛṣṭacetasaḥ ।





sendropendraiḥ suragaṇaiḥ rūpātiśayanirmitāḥ ॥ 2.8.57 ॥

śubharūpā mahābhāgā divyā nārī tilottamā।
brahmaṇaścāgnikuṇḍācca devanārī prabhāvatī।
rūpayauvanasampannā utpannā lokaviśrutā ॥ 2.8.58 ॥

ve dītalasamutpannā caturvaktrasya dhīmataḥ।
nāmnā vedavatī nāma suranārī mahāprabhā ॥ 2.8.59 ॥

varahemavibhā hemā devanārī sulocanā ॥ 2.8.60 ॥


samprayoge tu kāntena mādyanti madirāṃ vinā।
tāsāmāpyāyate sparśādānandaśca vivarddhate ॥ 2.8.62 ॥

parvate nārade pūrvaṃ retaḥ skannaṃ prajāpateḥ।
parvatastatra saṃbhūto nāradaścaiva tāvubhau।।
devarukhyo sūryajanma tasminnāradaparvatau।।(pāṭhabhedaḥ)

ṣaṭ‌triṃśattu svasāraśca yavīyasyastu tāḥ smṛtāḥ ॥ 2.8.63 ॥


kadrūrnāgasahasraṃ vai carā caramajījanat ।

sakaṇīraśca jambhaśca añjano vāmanastathā ॥ 2.8.66 ॥

airāvatamahāpajhau kambalāśvatarāvubhau।
ailapatraśca śaṅkhaśca karkoṭakadhanañjayau ॥ 2.8.67 ॥

kumāraḥ puṣpadantaśca sumukho durmukhastathā ॥ 2.8.68 ॥

bindupādaḥ puṇḍarīko nāgaścāpūraṇastathā ॥ 2.8.69 ॥

prahlādaḥ pajhacitraśca gandharvo'tha namasvikaḥ ॥ 2.8.70 ॥


khaśā vijajñe putrau dvau viśrutau puruṣādakau।








nakhinaṃ lohitagrīvaṃ sā kaniṣṭhaṃ prasūyate ॥ 2.8.80 ॥



abravīt so'sakṛttaṃ vai rakṣemāṃ mātaraṃ khaśām।

etasminneva kāle tu prādurbhūtastayoḥ pitā ।


pūrvamācakṣva tattvena tathaivābhyāṃ vyatikramam ॥ 2.8.86 ॥

mātulaṃ bhajate putraḥ pitṝn bhajati kanyakā।

yadvarṇā tu bhavedbhūmistadvarṇaṃ salilaṃ dhruvam।

balaśīlādibhistāsāmaditirdharmatatparā।
dharmaśīlādibhiścaiva prabodhabalaśālinī ॥ 2.8.89 ॥

vinatā tu punarddevī vaihāyasagatipriyā ॥ 2.8.90 ॥

tapomayena śīlena surabhiḥ samalaṃkṛtā।

danāyuṣāyāḥ śīlaṃ vai vairanugraha lakṣaṇam।
tvañca devi mahābhāge krodhaśīlā matāsi me ॥ 2.8.92 ॥

ityetāni saśīlāni svabhāvāllokanānnṛṇām।
karmato yatnato buddhyā rūpato balatastathā।
kṣamātaścaiva bhinnāni bhāvitārthabalena ca ॥ 2.8.93 ॥

rajaḥsattvatamovṛtterviśvarūpāḥ svabhāvataḥ।
mātulantvanuyātāste putrakā guṇavṛttibhiḥ ॥ 2.8.94 ॥


tena dhātvarthayogena tattvadarśī cakāra ha ॥ 2.8.96 ॥

yakṣa ityeṣa dhāturvai khādane kṛṣaṇe ca saḥ।
yakṣayetyuktavān yasmāt tasmādyakṣo bhavatyayam ॥ 2.8.97 ॥



yuvayorhastasaṃsparśo naktamena tu sarvaśaḥ ॥ 2.8.100 ॥

naktāhāravihārau ca divā svapnopabhoginau।

ityuktvā kaśyapaḥ putrau tatraivāntaradhīyata ॥ 2.8.102 ॥

gate pitari tau vīrau nisargādeva dāruṇau।
viparyayeṇa varttantau kimbhakṣau prāṇihiṃsakau ॥ 2.8.103 ॥

mahābalau mahāsattvau mahākāyau durāsadau।

rūpānurūpairāhāraiḥ prabhavetāmubhāvapi ॥ 2.8.105 ॥


bhakṣārthamapi lipsantau sarvatastau niśācarau।
indreṇa tu varau caiva dhṛtau dattvā suvadhyatām ॥ 2.8.107 ॥

yakṣastu na kadācidvai niśīthai hyekakaściram।


asṛṅmāṃsavasāhārau puruṣādau mahābalau।

āhārārthamaṭantau tau kanyābhyāṃ sahitāvubhau ॥ 2.8.111 ॥

te'paśyan rākṣasaṃ tatra kāmarūpaṃ mahābalam।

pitarāvūcatuḥ kanye yuvāmānayata drutam ॥ 2.8.113 ॥

jīvagrāhaṃ vigṛhyainaṃ visphurantaṃ pade pade।
tataḥ samabhisṛtyainaṃ kanye jagṛhatustadā।


tau tuṣṭau karmaṇā tasya kanye dve dadatuḥ sute ॥ 2.8.116 ॥


iyaṃ brahmadhanā nāma mama kanyā hyalomikā ।

jantavo'syā dhanāhārāstāvaśrāvayatāṃ dhanam ॥ 2.8.119 ॥


evaṃ piśācakanye te mithune dve prasūyatām।
tayoḥ prajāvisargañca bruvato me nibodhata ॥ 2.8.121 ॥

hetiḥ prahetirugraśca pauruṣeyo vadhastathā।
visphūrjiścaiva vātaśca āpo vyāghrastathaiva ca ॥ 2.8.122 ॥

sūryasyānucarā hyete saha tena bhramanti ca ॥ 2.8.123 ॥

hetiputrastathā laṅkurlaṅkordvāveva cātmajau।

vadhaputro nikumbhaśca krūro vai brahmarākṣasaḥ।
vātaputro virāgastu āpaputrastu jambukaḥ ॥ 2.8.125 ॥

ityete vai parikrāntāḥ krūrāḥ sarve tu rākṣasāḥ ॥ 2.8.126 ॥




śleṣmātakataruṣvete prāyaśastu kṛtālayāḥ।

tāṃ lipsuścintamānaśca nandanaṃ sa cacāra ha ॥ 2.8.131 ॥

vaibhrājaṃ surabhiñcaiva tathā caitrarathañca yat।

nopāyaṃ vindate tatra tasyā lābhāya cintayan।

mamodvijante bhūtāni bhayāvṛttasya sarvaśaḥ।

kṛtvā rūpaṃ bahumataṃ gandharvasya tu guhyakaḥ।

buddhvā ca suruciṃ taṃ sā bhāvenaivābhyavarttata।

sa tatra siddhakaraṇaḥ sadyo jātaḥ suto'sya vai ।
pariṇāhocchrayairyuktaḥ sadyo vṛtto jvalan śriyā ॥ 2.8.137 ॥


jāte sa tasmin harṣeṇa svarūpaṃ pratyapadyata ॥ 2.8.139 ॥

mriyamāṇāḥ prasuptāśca kruddhā bhītāḥ praharṣitāḥ ॥ 2.8.140 ॥

tato'bravīdapsarasaḥ smayamānaḥ sa guhyakaḥ।
gṛhaṃ me gaccha suśroṇi saputrā varavarṇinī ॥ 2.8.141 ॥

vibhrāntāḥ prādravan bhītāḥ krodhamānā'psarogaṇāḥ ॥ 2.8.142 ॥

gacchantīranvagacchadyā putrastāṃ sāntvayan girā।


jagāma saha putreṇa tato yakṣaḥ svamālayam।
nyagrodharohiṇaṃ nāma guhyakā yatra śerate।

anuhrādasya daityasya bhadrāmativarāṃ sutām।

tayoḥ patnyau bhaginyau tu kratusthalyātmaje śubhe ॥ 2.8.147 ॥



tathā mandaraśobhiñca pajhaṃ candraprabhaṃ tathā ॥ 2.8.150 ॥

maghapūrṇaṃ subhadrañca pradyotañca mahaujasam।
dyutimatketumantau ca mitraṃ maulisudarśanau ॥ 2.8.151 ॥


pūrṇabhadraṃ hemarathaṃ maṇimannandivarddhanau ॥ 2.8.153 ॥

śvetañca vipulañcaiva puṣpavantaṃ bhayāvaham ॥ 2.8.154 ॥

kumudaṃ kṣemakañcaiva varddhamānaṃ tathā damam ॥ 2.8.155 ॥




visphūrjjito vidyujjihvo mātaṅgo dhūmritastatā ॥ 2.8.159 ॥

candrārkaḥ sukaro budhnaḥ kapilomā prahāsakaḥ।







tathaiva naiṛto nāma tryambakānucareṇa ha।






ālambeyena janitā naikāḥ surasikena hi।

bahutvāccaiva sargasya teṣāṃ vaktuṃ na śakyate ॥ 2.8.174 ॥

duhitā svabhāvavikacā mandasattvaparākramā ॥ 2.8.175 ॥

tasyā api virūpeṇa naiṛteneha ca prajāḥ।












yakṣāḥ puṇyatamā nāma tathā ye ke'pi guhyakāḥ।

paulastyā rākṣasā ye ca viśvāmitrāśca ye smṛtāḥ ॥ 2.8.189 ॥

teṣāṃ rājā mahārājaḥ kubero hyalakādhipaḥ ॥ 2.8.190 ॥




devānāṃ tulyadharmāṇāṃ hyasurāḥ sarvaśaḥ smṛtāḥ।
tribhiḥ pādhaistu gandharvā devai rhīnāḥ prabhāvataḥ ॥ 2.8.194 ॥

gandharvebhyastribhiḥ pādairhīnā vai sarvaguhyakāḥ।
prabhāvatulyā yakṣāṇāṃ vijñeyāḥ sarvarākṣasāḥ।


devāsurebhyo hīyante trīn pādān vai parasparam।

         ।।sūta uvāca।।




ityevamādayo'nye'pi irāvatyā nibodhata ॥ 2.8.202 ॥


sāmnā prasūyamānena sadya eva gajo'bhavat।
sa prāyacchadirāvatyai putrārthaṃ sa tu bhauvanaḥ ॥ 2.8.204 ॥


apsujasyaikamūlasya suvarṇābhasya hastinaḥ ।
ṣaḍdantasya hi bhadrasya aupāvāhyaśca vai balaḥ ॥ 2.8.206 ॥

tasya putro'ñjanaścaiva supratīko'tha vāmanaḥ।
padmaścaiva caturtho'bhūddhastinī cābhramustathā ॥ 2.8.207 ॥

diggajāṃstāṃśca catvāraḥ śvetā'janayatāśugān ।

saṅkīrṇo'pyañjano yastu upavāhyo yamasya tu।
bhadro yaḥ supratīkastu haritaḥ sa hyapāmpateḥ ॥ 2.8.209 ॥

padmo mandastu yo gauro dvipo hyailavilasya saḥ।

padmottarastu yaḥ padmau gajo vai varuṇo gaṇaḥ।

udagrabhāvenopetā jāyante tasya cānvaye।







supratīkastu rūpeṇa nāstyasya sadṛśo gajaḥ ।


evaṃ mātā tayoścāpi prathitāyurajaḥsutau ॥ 2.8.221 ॥



samāyavaradāṃścaṇḍān pravṛddhabalinodarān ।
hastiyuddhe priyānnāgān viddhi tasya kulodbhavān ॥ 2.8.224 ॥








añjanasyaikamūlasya prācyānnāgavanantu tat ॥ 2.8.232 ॥

gaṅgodbhedātkarūṣebhyaḥ supratīkasya tadvanam ॥ 2.8.233 ॥

apareṇotkalāccaiva hyāvedibhyaśca pañcamam।
ekabhūtātmanosyaitadvāmanasya vanaṃ smṛtam ॥ 2.8.234 ॥

apareṇa tu lauhityamāsindhoḥ paścimena tu।
yamasyaitadvanaṃ proktamanuparvatameva tat ॥ 2.8.235 ॥



sarovarasamudrādinadīpulinavāsinaḥ।


visṛṣṭākṣān virūpākṣān kṛśākṣānekalocanān।

caṇḍāṃśca vikaṭāṃścaiva viromān romaśāṃstathā।

sarovarasamudrādinadīpulinasevinaḥ।



ghorān krūrāṃśca medhyāṃśca śivān puṇyān savādinaḥ ॥ 2.8.245 ॥

kuśahastān mahājihvān mahākarṇānmahānanān।

dīptāsyān dīptanetrāṃśca citra mālyānuleṣanān ॥ 2.8.247 ॥

naktañcarān suduṣprekṣyān ghorāṃstān vai niśācarān ॥ 2.8.248 ॥

paratve ca bhayaṃ daiva sarve te gatamānasāḥ।

ete sarve mahātmāno bhūtyāḥ putrāḥ prakīrttitāḥ ॥ 2.8.250 ॥



chagalaśchagalī caiva vakro vakramukhī tathā।

sumbhapā traśca kumabhī ca vajradaṃṣṭraśca dundubhiḥ।






piśācāste tu vijñeyāḥ sukalpā iti jajñire।
bībhatsaṃ vikṛtācāraṃ putrapautramanantakam।



















adhārmikā janāste vai ājīvā vihitāḥ suraiḥ।

amṛtopamasattvānāṃ cauraviśvāsaghātinām।
etairanyaiśca bahubiranyāyopārjjitairdhanaiḥ ।

dhūpairhāridrakṛśaraistailabhadraguḍaudanaiḥ ॥ 2.8.281 ॥





ityevamādirhi gaṇo maino vistīrṇa ucyate ॥ 2.8.286 ॥

grāhāścaturvidhā jñeyāstathānujyeṣṭhakā api।
niṣkāṃśca śiśumārāṃśca matā vyajanayatprajāḥ ॥ 2.8.287 ॥





tiryagaṃ hetukamādyādurbahulaṃ vaṃśavistaram।
saṃsvedajavikārāṇi yathā yebhyo bhavanti ha ॥ 2.8.292 ॥

svastipikaśarīrebhyo jāyantyutpādakā dvijāḥ।


tathā gharmāditaptābhyastvadbhayo vṛṣṭibhya eva ca।
naikā mṛgaśarīrebhyo jāyante jantavastvime ॥ 2.8.295 ॥


jalajāḥ svedajāścaiva jāyante jantavastvime।


jambubilvāmrapūgebhyaḥ phalebhyaścaiva jantavaḥ ॥ 2.8.299 ॥

mudgebhyaḥ panasebhyaśca taṇḍulebhyastathaiva ca।
tathā koṭaraśuṣkebhyo nihitebhyo bhavanti hi ॥ 2.8.300 ॥

anyebhyo'pi ca jāyante na hi tebhyaściraṃ sadā।
jantavasturagādibhyo viṣādibhyastathaiva ca ॥ 2.8.301 ॥

bahūnyahāni niḥkṣipte sambhavanti ca gomaye।

kramād‌drumāṇāṃ jāyante vividhā nīlamakṣikāḥ।
tathā śuṣkavikārebhyaḥ putrikāḥ prabhavanti ca ॥ 2.8.303 ॥

kālikā śatikebhyaśca sarpā jāyanti sarvaśaḥ।

gobhyo hi mahiṣebhyaśca jāyante jantavaḥ prabho।


prāyeṇa tu vasantyasminnucchiṣṭodakakarddame ॥ 2.8.307 ॥

tṛṇebhyaścaiva jāyante putrikā putrabhāsakāḥ ॥ 2.8.308 ॥




kecittu yonijā devāḥ kecidevānimittataḥ ॥ 2.8.312 ॥

tūlālāghaśca kolaśca śivā kanyā tathaiva ca।
apatyaṃ saramāyāstu gaṇā vai saramādayaḥ ॥ 2.8.313 ॥

kṛṣṇo dhūmrārūṇaścaiva tūlālāghaśca kadrukāḥ ॥ 2.8.314 ॥

surasātha vijajñe tu śatamekaṃ śiro matam।
sarpāṇāṃ takṣako rājā nāgānāñcāpi vāsukiḥ । dra. garuḍapurāṇe 1.197.12 madhye vāsukeḥ pārthivamaṇḍale evaṃ takṣakasya vāyumaṇḍale sthitiḥ

pulahasyātmajā sargastāmrāyāstannibodhata।


sampātirajanat putraṃ kanyāmekāṃ tathaiva ca।











etāneva vijajñe'tha putrapautramanantakam ॥ 2.8.329 ॥






iti sargaikadeśasya kīrtito'vayavo mayā।







tato'dhaḥ srotasaste vai utpadyante surāsurāḥ ।। 8.342 ।।







## 2.9

।।sūta uvāca।।








gandharvāṇāmadhipatiṃ cakre citrarathaṃ tadā ॥ 2.9.9 ॥






gaṇitasyātha yogasya cakre saṃvatsaraṃ prabhum ॥ 2.9.15 ॥

prajāpatirvai rajasaḥ pūrvasyāndiśi viśrutam।


yathāpradeśamadyāpi dharmeṇa paripālyate ॥ 2.9.18 ॥

nṛpā hyete'bhiṣicyante manavo ye bhavanti vai ॥ 2.9.19 ॥

evamanye'bhiṣicyante prāpte manvantare punaḥ।

rājasūye'bhiṣiktaśca pṛthurebhirnarottamaiḥ।


kaśyapo gotrakāmastu cacāra paramaṃ tapaḥ ।
putrau gotrakarau mahyaṃ bhavetāmityacintayat ॥ 2.9.23 ॥

tasya pradhyāyamānasya kaśyapasya mahātmanaḥ।

vatsāraścāsitaścaiva tāvubhau brahmavādinau।

raibhyasya raibhyā vijñeyā nidhruvasya nibodhata।
cyavanasya sukanyāyāṃ sumedhāḥ samapadyata ॥ 2.9.26 ॥



varaprabhṛtayo devā devalasya prajāstvimāḥ।
caturyuge tvatikrānte manorhyekādaśe prajāḥ।
athāvaśiṣṭe tasmistu dvāpare sampravartate ॥ 2.9.29 ॥

mānasasya cariṣyantastasya putro damaḥ kila।


tasya patnyaścatasrastu paulastyakulavarddhanāḥ ॥ 2.9.32 ॥



divyena vidhinā yuktamārṣeṇaiva śrutena ca।



evaṃvidhaṃ sutaṃ dṛṣṭvā viśvarūpadharaṃ tathā।





rājaseno jayayukto rūpeṇa ca balena ca ॥ 2.9.43 ॥



niyutānyekaṣaṣṭiśca saṅkhyāvidbhirudāhṛtā ॥ 2.9.46 ॥






sarve labdhavarāścaiva putrapautrasamanvitāḥ।











na samastabalaṃ buddhaṃ yato māyākṛtaṃ hi tat ॥ 2.9.63 ॥



anapatyaḥ kratustasmin smṛto vaivasvate'ntare।
tasya patnyaśca sundaryo daśaivāsanpativratāḥ ॥ 2.9.66 ॥




svarbhānunā hate sūrye patamāno divo mahīm।
tamo'bhibhūte loke'smin prabhā yena pravarttitā ॥ 2.9.70 ॥

svasti te'stviti coktaḥ sa patanniha divākaraḥ।
brahmarṣervacanāttasya na papāta divo mahīm ॥ 2.9.71 ॥

yajñeṣvatrighanaścaiva surairyaśca pravarttitaḥ ॥ 2.9.72 ॥



dattātreyastasya jyeṣṭo durvāsāstasya cānujaḥ ।



jajñire ca tvarundhatyāstānnibodhata sattamāḥ ॥ 2.9.78 ॥



atrauṣadhaṃ mūlaphalamoṣadhīśca pravarttayan ।




jananī brahmadattasya patnī sattvaguhasya ca ॥ 2.9.85 ॥


ata ūrddhvaṃ nibodhadhvamindrapratimasambhavam।
kuśītiyaḥ samākhyāta indrapratima ucyate ॥ 2.9.87 ॥

upamanyuḥ sutastasya yasyeme upamanyavaḥ ॥ 2.9.88 ॥





## 2.10

etacchrutvā vacastasya sutasya viditātmanaḥ।
uttaraṃ paripapracchuḥ sūtaputraṃ dvijātayaḥ ॥ 2.10.1 ॥
            ।।śāṃśapāyana uvāca।।
mainākaścaiva dauhitro dauhitrī ca tathā hyumā।
pūrvameva mayoddiṣṭaṃ śrṛṇudhvaṃ mama sarvaśaḥ ॥ 2.10.5 ॥
aneke pitaraścaiva varttante vka ca vā punaḥ।
śrotumicchāmi bhadrante śrāddhasya ca paraṃ vidhim ॥ 2.10.6 ॥
pitaraḥ kathamutpannāḥ kasya putrāḥ kimātmakāḥ ॥ 2.10.7 ॥
svarge tu pitaro'nye ye devānāmapi devatāḥ।
svarge hi ke tu varttante pitaro narake tu ke ॥ 2.10.9 ॥
abhisandhāya pitaraṃ pituśca pitaraṃ tathā।
ke ceha pitaro nāma kān yajāmo vayaṃ punaḥ ।
devā api putṝn svarge yajantīti hi naḥ śrutam ॥ 2.10.12 ॥
etadicchāmi vai śrotuṃ vistareṇa bahuśruta।
spaṣṭābhidhānamarthaṃ vai tadbhavānvaktumarhati ॥ 2.10.13 ॥
ṛṣīṇāntu vacaḥ śrutvā sūtastattvārthadarśivān।
ācacakṣe yathāpraśraṃ ṛṣīṇāṃ mānasaṃ tataḥ ॥ 2.10.14 ॥
       ।।sūta uvāca।।
manvantareṣu jāyante pitaro devasūnavaḥ ॥ 2.10.15 ॥
na sma kiñcidvijānanti tato loke hyamuhyata ॥ 2.10.18 ॥
apṛcchan saṃyatātmāno vidhivacca mitho mithaḥ ॥ 2.10.21 ॥
tasmādyaduktaṃ yuṣmābhistattathā na tadanyathā ॥ 2.10.24 ॥
pitaraste bhaviṣyanti tebhyo'yaṃ dīyatāṃ varaḥ ॥ 2.10.25 ॥
sarvatra varttamānāste pitaraḥ prapitāmaham।
tenaitatsarvathā siddhaṃ dānamadhyayanaṃ tapaḥ ॥ 2.10.33 ॥
ityete pitaro devā devāśca pitaraḥ punaḥ ।
anyonyaṃ pitaro hyete devāśca pitaraśca ha ॥ 2.10.34 ॥
papracchurmunayo bhūyaḥ sūtaṃ tasmādyaduttaram ॥ 2.10.35 ॥
            ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।
varttante devapravarā devānāṃ somavarddhanāḥ ॥ 2.10.36 ॥
        ।।sūta uvāca।।
etadvo'haṃ pravakṣyāmi pitṛsargamanuttamam।
ka ete pitaro nāma kiyantaḥ ke ca nāmataḥ।
kaśca kālo bhaveccrāddhe vidhiḥ kaścānuvarttate ॥ 2.10.42 ॥
etadicchāmi bhagavan vistareṇa yathātatham।
          ।।bṛhaspatiruvāca।।
vinayena yathānyāyaṃ gambhīraṃ praśnamuttamam ॥ 2.10.45 ॥
sūryācandramasau caiva tathāhorātrameva ca ॥ 2.10.46 ॥
na babhūvustadā tāta tamobhūtamidaṃ jagat।
brahmaiko duścaraṃ tatra cacāra paramaṃ tapaḥ ॥ 2.10.47 ॥
śaṃyustamabravīdbhūyaḥ pitaraṃ brahmavittamam।
sarvadaiva vratasnātaṃ sarvajñānavidāṃvaram ॥ 2.10.48 ॥
evamukto bṛhattejā bṛhaspatiruvāca tam ॥ 2.10.49 ॥
sarveṣāṃ tapasāṃ yuktistapoyogamanuttamam।
yogena tapasā yuktaḥ pūrvameva tadā prabhuḥ।
tato devāstato bhūmireṣā lokaparamparā।
loke varttanti te hyasmiṃstebhyaḥ parjanyasambhavaḥ ।
tato yugasahasrānte jāyante brahmavādinaḥ।
pratilabhya punaryogaṃ mokṣaṃ gacchantyamūrttayaḥ ॥ 2.10.61 ॥
vyaktāvyaktaṃ parityajya mahāyogabalena vā ।
naśyantyulkeva gagane kṣīṇavidyutprabheva ca ॥ 2.10.62 ॥
utsṛjya dehajātāni mahāyogabalena ca।
kriyayā gurupūjābhirye ca kurvanti nityaśaḥ।
tābhirāpyāyayantyete pitaro yogavarddhanāḥ ॥ 2.10.64 ॥
āpyāyayanti yogena trailokyaṃ yena jīvati ॥ 2.10.65 ॥
tasmācchrāddhāni deyāni yogibhyo yatnataḥ sadā।
sahasraśastu viprānvai bhojayedyāvadāgatān ।।
ekastu yogavitprītaḥ sarvānarhati tacchṛṇu ॥ 2.10.67 ॥
brahmacārisahasreṇa yogī hyeko viśiṣyate ॥ 2.10.69 ॥
putro vāpyatha vā pautro dhyāninaṃ bhojayiṣyati ॥ 2.10.71 ॥
tadalābhepyudāsīnaṃ gṛhasthamapi bhojayet ॥ 2.10.72 ॥
pūjayeccārghyapātreṇa veśmanā bhojanena ca।
tasmāddadyācca vai dānaṃ viprāyātithaye naraḥ।
aśvamedhasahasreṇa rājasūyaśatena ca।
puṇḍarīkasahasreṇa yogiṣvāvasatho varam ॥ 2.10.77 ॥


## 2.11

।।sūta uvāca।।
teṣāṃ lokavisargantu kīrttayiṣye nibodhata ॥ 2.11.1 ॥


nāmāni lokasargañca teṣāṃ vakṣye samāsataḥ।



jātaḥ sarvaiṣadhidharaḥ sarvaratnākarātmavān।
parvataḥ pravaraḥ puṇyaḥ krauñcastasyātmajo'bhavat ॥ 2.11.6 ॥


āśrite dve hyaparṇā tu aniketā tapo'carat।





etattu trikumārīkaṃ jagatsyāsyati śāśvatam।



mahāyogabalopetā mahādevamupasthitā।



tasyāpi śaṅkhalikhitau smṛtau putrāvayonijau।

anyonyaprītiranayorumāśaṅkarayoryathā ॥ 2.11.20 ॥


anayo rativighnañca tvamācara hutāśana।
sarvatra gata eva tvaṃ na doṣo vidyate tadā ॥ 2.11.22 ॥

ityevamukte tu tathā vahninā ca tathā kṛtam।

tato ruṣitayā devyā śapto'gniḥ śāṃśapāyana।

kṛtavānasyakarttavyaṃ tasmāttvamasi durmate ॥ 2.11.25 ॥




maddhitārthamimaṃ garbhamato dhāraya nimnage।
matprasādācca khedo vai mandastava bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.11.29 ॥


kālaṃ prakṛṣṭaṃ sumahadgharbhadhāraṇatatparā ॥ 2.11.31 ॥

tayā parigataṃ garbhaṃ kukṣau himavataḥ śubhe।
śubhaṃ śaravaṇaṃ nāma citraṃ puṣpitapādapam।



devadundubhayo nedurākāśe madhurasvarāḥ।

jagurgandharvvamukhyāśca sarvvaśastatra tatra ha।




ṣapmukho vyasṛjacchrīmāṃstāsāṃ prītyā mahādyutiḥ ॥ 2.11.40 ॥

śrīmān kamalapatrākṣastaruṇāditya sannibhaḥ।
yena jātena lokānāmākṣepastejasā kṛtaḥ ॥ 2.11.41 ॥


kārttikeya iti khyātastasmādasurasūdanaḥ ॥ 2.11.43 ॥

mukhādvinirgatā tasya svaśaktiraparājitā ॥ 2.11.44 ॥




sendropendrairmahābhāgairdevaraignisutaḥ prabhuḥ ।

devasenāpatistvevaṃ paṭyate naranāyakaḥ।
devāriskandanaḥ skandaḥ sarvalokeśvaraḥ prabhuḥ ॥ 2.11.49 ॥

pramathairvividhairdavaistathā bhūta gaṇairapi ।





yaiḥ kriyante ca karmmāṇi śarīrairiha daivataiḥ ।
taireva tatkarmaphalaṃ prāpnuvantīha devatāḥ ॥ 2.11.55 ॥

sadyaḥ phalanti karmāṇi devatve pretya mānuṣe।
tasmādamāvasvapatyatvaṃ pretya prāpsyase phalam ॥ 2.11.56 ॥

ityuktyā vai pitaraḥ punaste tu prasāditāḥ।




sa vedamekaṃ viprarṣiścaturdhā vai kariṣyati ॥ 2.11.61 ॥

mahābhiṣasya putrau dvau śantanoḥ kīrttivarddhanau।
vicitravīryyaṃ dharmajñaṃ tvamevotpādayiṣyasi ॥ 2.11.62 ॥







traya ete gaṇāḥ proktā dharmamūrtidharāḥ śubhāḥ ॥ 2.11.69 ॥

yoginī yogapatnī ca yogamātā tathaiva ca ॥ 2.11.70 ॥



sa tasyāṃ pitṛkanyāyāṃ yogācāryānpariśrutān ।

brahmadattasya jananī mahiṣī tvaṇuhasya tu ॥ 2.11.74 ॥

etānutpādya dharmātmā putrānyogamavāpya ca।

sarvavyāpi vinirmukto bhaviṣyati mahāmuniḥ ॥ 2.11.76 ॥

amūrttimantaḥ pitaro dharmamūrttidharāstu ye।


sarvakāmasamṛddheṣu dvijā stānbhāvayantyuta ॥ 2.11.79 ॥







samutpannasya pulahādutpannāstasya vai punaḥ ॥ 2.11.86 ॥




mānasā nāma te lokā vahante yatra te divi।

jananī trasaddasyorhi purukutsaparigrahaḥ ॥ 2.11.91 ॥

manvanta rādau śrāddhāni pravarttayati sarvaśaḥ ॥ 2.11.92 ॥



somasyāpyāyanaṃ kṛtvā hyagnervaivasvatasya ca।
uda gāyanañcāgnau ca aśvamedhaṃ tadāmuyāt ॥ 2.11.95 ॥




ityete pitaraścaiva lokā duhitaraśca vai।





pitṝṃstvapūjayansarve nityameva phalārthinaḥ ॥ 2.11.104 ॥


mokṣaṃ yogamayaiśvaryyaṃ prayacchanti pitāmahāḥ ॥ 2.11.106 ॥


aiśvaryyaṃ vihitaṃ yogamaiśvaryyaṃ vittamuttamam।
yogaiśvaryyadṛte mokṣaḥ kathañcinnopapadyate ॥ 2.11.108 ॥

apakṣasyaiva gamanaṃ gagane pakṣiṇo yathā ।

sarvakāmaprasiddhāni prayacchanti pitāmahāḥ ॥ 2.11.110 ॥



haṃsabarhiṇayuktāni muktāvaiḍhūryyavanti ca।


## 2.12

।।bṛhaspatiruvāca।।

rajatasya tathā cāpi darśanaṃ dānameva ca।
pitṝnetena dānena satputrāstārayantyuta ॥ 2.12.2 ॥

svadhādāyārthibhistāta tasmindatte tadakṣayam ॥ 2.12.3 ॥

rakṣoghnaṃ brahmavarccasyaṃ pitṝṃstattadvitārayet ॥ 2.12.4 ॥



sarvatoratnimātrantu caturasraṃ susaṃhitam ॥ 2.12.7 ॥

dhanyamārogyamāyuṣyaṃ balavarṇavivarddhanam ॥ 2.12.8 ॥



payasā hyājagavyena śodhanaṃ vāgbhireva tu।

iha cāmutra ca śrīmānsarvakarmasamanvitaḥ।
mantreṇa vidhivatsamyagaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ॥ 2.12.12 ॥


tasya puṣṭirathaiśvaryyamāyuḥ santatireva ca।
vicitrā bhajate lakṣmīrmokṣaṃ ca labhate kramāt ॥ 2.12.14 ॥

aśvamedhaphalaṃ hyetaddvijaiḥ satkṛtya pūjitam।

devatābhyaḥ pitṛbhyaśca mahāyogibhya eva ca।
namaḥ svadhāyai svāhāyai nityameva bhavantyuta ॥ 2.12.16 ॥

piṇḍanirvapaṇe caiva japedetatsamāhitaḥ।

pitṝṃstatrtriṣu lokeṣu mantro'yaṃ tārayatyuta।

rājyakāmo japedenaṃ sadā mantramatandritaḥ।

prīyante pitaro yena japyena niyamena ca।



tānnamaskṛtya sarvānvai pitṝnkuśaladāyakān ॥ 2.12.23 ॥

nakṣatrāṇāṃ carādīnāṃ pitṝnatha pitāmahān।



pitṛgaṇebhyaḥ saptabhyo namo lokeṣu saptasu।
svayambhuve namaścaiva brahmaṇe yogacakṣuṣe ॥ 2.12.27 ॥

etaduktaṃ sasaptarṣibrahmarṣigaṇapūjitam ।
pavitraṃ paramaṃ hyetacchrīmadrakṣovināśanam ॥ 2.12.28 ॥

anena vidhinā yuktastrīnvarān labhate naraḥ।
annamāyuḥ sutāṃścaiva dadate pitaro bhuvi ॥ 2.12.29 ॥

bhaktyā paramayā yuktaḥ śraddadhāno jitendriyaḥ।
saptārcciṣaṃ japedyastu nityameva samāhitaḥ।

yatkiṃcitpacyate gehe bhakṣyaṃ vā bhojyameva ca।
anivedya na bhoktavyaṃ tasminnāyatane sadā ॥ 2.12.31 ॥



## 2.13

bṛhaspatiruvāca।।
pālāśaṃ brahmagavarcasyamaśvatthe rājyabhāvanā।
sarvabhūtādhipatyaṃ ca plakṣe nityamudāhṛtam ॥ 2.13.1 ॥
saubhāgyamuttamaṃ loke madhuke samudāhṛtam।
varṣedajasraṃ parjanyo veṇupātreṣu kurvvataḥ ॥ 2.13.5 ॥
saṃyuktānmadhusarpirbhyāṃ so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ॥ 2.13.8 ॥
labhate strīṣvapatyāni iha cāmutra cobhayoḥ।
dadyādeva pitṛbhyastu nityameva hyatandritaḥ ॥ 2.13.9 ॥
dīpaṃ pitṛbhyaḥ prayataḥ sadā yastu prayacchati।
sa loke'pratimaṃ cakṣuḥ sadā ca labhate śūbham ॥ 2.13.10 ॥
tejasā yaśamā caiva kāntyā caiva balena ca।
bhuvi prakāśo bhavati bhrājate ca triviṣṭape।
apsarobhiḥ parivṛto vimānāgre sa modate ॥ 2.13.11 ॥
gandhānpuṣpāṇi dhūpāṃśca dadyādājyādutīśca vai।
vastrairannaiḥ pradānaistairbhakṣyapeyaistathaiva ca।
tasmādannena vidhivat pūjayeddvijasattamān ॥ 2.13.15 ॥
gandhadānamalaṅkāramekaikaṃ nirvapedvudhaḥ ॥ 2.13.17 ॥
abhyaṅga darbhapiñjālaistribhiḥ kuryādyathāvidhi ॥ 2.13.18 ॥
apasavyaṃ pitṛbhyaśca dadyādannamanuttamam।
cailamantreṇa piṇḍebhyo dattvā darśanajaṃ hitam ॥ 2.13.21 ॥
piṇḍānparikṣipetsamyagapasavyamatandritaḥ ॥ 2.13.23 ॥
namo vaḥ pitaraḥ saumyāḥ paṭhannityamatandritaḥ ॥ 2.13.27 ॥
ulūkhalasya lekhāyā mudapātrācca sevanam ॥ 2.13.28 ॥
varjayettaddaśāṃ yajñe yadapyahatavastrajām।
dhūpaṃ ca guggulaśreṣṭhaṃ turuṣkaṃ dhūpameva ca ॥ 2.13.32 ॥
javāsumanaso bhaṇḍīrūpakāmakuraṇḍakāḥ।
yāni gandhādapetāni upagandhīni yāni ca।
nihanmi sarvvaṃ yadamedhyavadbhaved hatāśca sarvve suradānavā mayā।
evaṃ pitre dṛṣṭamannaṃ hi yasya tasyāsurā varjayantīha sarvve।
bhavantyeva kṛte śrāddhe nityameva prayatnataḥ ॥ 2.13.48 ॥
kāśaiḥ punarbhavaiḥ kāryyaṃ sarvvameva phalaṃ bhavet ॥ 2.13.50 ॥
atha te pitaro devā devāśca pitaraḥ punaḥ ॥ 2.13.51 ॥
somāya vai pitṛmate svadhā aṅgirase namaḥ।
asvargyaṃ laukikaṃ vāpi juhuyātkarmmasiddhaye ॥ 2.13.53 ॥
antarādhāya samidhaṃ tathā homo vidhīyate।।
samāhitena manasā prayatāgniḥ prayatnataḥ ॥ 2.13.54 ॥
agnaye kavyavāhāya svadhā aṅgirase namaḥ।
yamāya caivāṅgirase svadhā nama iti bruvan ॥ 2.13.55 ॥
etayorantaraṃ nityaṃ juhuyādvai vivasvate।
upacāraṃ svadhākāraṃ tathaivollekhanañca yat ॥ 2.13.57 ॥
aśvamedhaphalenaiva tatsmṛtaṃ mantrapūrvvakam।
bahuhavyatvamevāgnau susamiddhe viśeṣataḥ।
vidhūme lelihāne ca hotavyaṃ karmasiddhaye ॥ 2.13.60 ॥
aprabuddhe sadhūme ca juhuyādyo hutāśane।
yajamāno bhavedandhaḥ so'putra iti naḥ śrutam ॥ 2.13.61 ॥
jvālā dhūmopasevyaśca sa tu vahnirna siddhaye ॥ 2.13.62 ॥
bhūmiṃ vigāhate yatra tatra vidyātparābhavam ॥ 2.13.63 ॥
snigdhaḥ pradakṣiṇaścaiva vahniḥ syātkāryyasiddhaye ॥ 2.13.64 ॥
sthālyudumbarapātrāṇi phalāni samidhastathā।
pavitraṃ vā dvijaśreṣṭha śuddhaye janmakarmmasu।
tadeva kṛtsnaṃ vijñeyaṃ samitsu ca yathākramam।
kṛtvā samāhitaṃ cittamagraye vai karomyaham ॥ 2.13.68 ॥
anujñātaḥ kuruṣveti tathaiva dvijasattamaiḥ।
samidbhiḥ kalkaleyābhirjjuhuyādyo hutāśanam ।
phalaṃ yat karmaṇastasya tanme nigadataḥ śrṛṇu ॥ 2.13.73 ॥
nīpo vibhī takaścaiva vallībhiśca tathaiva ca।
ayajñīyāḥ smṛtā ye ca vṛkṣāṃścaiva tu varjayet ॥ 2.13.75 ॥


## 2.14

।।sūta uvāca।.
devāśca pitaraścaiva tebhyo'nye pitarastathā।

pūjayecca pitṛn pūrvvaṃ devāścāpi viśeṣataḥ।
devebhyo'pi pitṛn pūrvamarcayantīha yatnataḥ ॥ 2.14.2 ॥

dakṣasya duhitā khyātā loke viśveti nāmataḥ।
vidhinā sā tu dharmmajña dattā dharmmāya dharmmataḥ।
tasyāḥ putrā mahātmāno viśvedevā iti śrutiḥ ॥ 2.14.3 ॥


himavacchikhare ramye devagandharvasevite।
sarvvāpsarobhiścaritaṃ devagandharvvasevitam ॥ 2.14.5 ॥


evamukte tu pitṛbhistadā trailokyabhāvanaḥ।
prajānāmadhipo brahmā viśavānitīdamabravīt ॥ 2.14.7 ॥

         ।।brahmovāca।।
tapasā tena suprītaḥ kaṃ kāmaṃ vidadhāmi vaḥ ॥ 2.14.8 ॥


pratyuvāca tato brahmā tānvai tridivapūjitān ॥ 2.14.10 ॥

bhaviṣyatyevameveti kāṃkṣito vo varastu yaḥ।
pitṛbhistu tathetyuktvā evametanna saṃśayaḥ ॥ 2.14.11 ॥

sahāsmābhistu vo bhāvyaṃ yatkiñcit kriyate tviha।

bhaviṣyati manuṣyeṣu satyametadbravīmi te।
mālyairgandhaistathānnena yuṣmānagrerjayiṣyati ॥ 2.14.13 ॥


evaṃ vidhikṛtaḥ samyak sarvvametadbhaviṣyati ॥ 2.14.15 ॥



brahmasthānamavāpnoti sarvakāmapuraskṛtam ॥ 2.14.18 ॥



naivedyaṃ kecidicchanti jīvatyapi prayatnataḥ।
udakpūrvvaṃ baliṃ kuryādudakumbhaṃ tathaiva ca ॥ 2.14.21 ॥


iṣṭenānnena bhakṣyaiśca bhojayeta yathāvidhi।
vidhānaṃ vedavihitametadvakṣyāmi yatnataḥ ॥ 2.14.23 ॥

devadevā mahātmāno hyetepi pitaro hyuta।

taddhi dharmārthakuśalānityuvāca bṛhaspatiḥ ॥ 2.14.25 ॥

somamāpyāyayantyete pitaro yogamāsthitāḥ ॥ 2.14.26 ॥




ādikarma samutsṛjya dātā tatra vinaśyati ॥ 2.14.30 ॥

prajārthī pata (tna)ye dadyānmadhyamaṃ tatra pūrvakam ॥ 2.14.31 ॥

uttamāṃ dyutimanvicchan goṣu nityaṃ prayacchati।

prārthayandīrghamāyuśca vāyasebhyaḥ prayacchati।
saukumāryamathānvicchan kukkuṭebhyaḥ prayacchati ॥ 2.14.33 ॥


anujñāte tu tairviprairvānamudviyatāmiti ॥ 2.14.35 ॥


bhakṣyamannaṃ tathā peyamanuttamaphalāni ca।

snigdhairbhakṣyaiḥ sugandhaiśca tarpayeta rasaistathā।


ataḥ paraṃ vidhiṃ saumyaṃ bhuktavatsu dvijātiṣu।


prāñjaliḥ prayataścaiva anugamya visarjayet ॥ 2.14.42 ॥


## 2.15

।।bṛhaspatiruvāca।।

pretya ca svargalābhāya kāmaiśvaryaṃ suvistaram ।



yatra varṣasahasrāṇi prayutānyarbudāni ca।

yatra mṛtyorgatirnāsti tathaivāsurarakṣasām।

tejasā yaśasā caiva bhrājate sa nagottamaḥ।



dadyātsakṛdapi prājñastasya vakṣyāmi yatphalam ॥ 2.15.10 ॥

antarddhānaṃ ca gacchanti kṣetramāsādya tatsadā ॥ 2.15.11 ॥





sakṛdevārcitāstatra svargamamarakaṇṭake ॥ 2.15.16 ॥

mahendraparvate ramye puṇyaṃ śakraniṣevitam।
tatrāruhya bhavet prītiḥ śrāddhaṃ caiva mahatphalam ॥ 2.15.17 ॥


saptagodāvare caiva gokarṇe ca tapovane।
aśvamedhaphalaṃ tatra snātvā ca labhatenaraḥ ॥ 2.15.19 ॥

rudrastatra tapastepe devadevo maheśvaraḥ ॥ 2.15.20 ॥



divyaiśvandanavṛkṣaiśca pādapairupaśobhitam।
āpaḥ svādanasampṛktā vahanti satataṃyataḥ ॥ 2.15.23 ॥








karmāṇi svaprayuktāni sidhyanti prabhavātyaye ।
duṣprasaktāni pitṛṣu prayuktāni bhavantyuta ॥ 2.15.31 ॥


māṭharasya vane puṇye siddhacāraṇasevite।
antarddhānaṃ na gacchanti sattāstasminmahāgirau ॥ 2.15.33 ॥

vindhye caiva girau puṇye dharmādharmanidarśanam।

spaṣṭā bhavati sā dhārā prāyaśaḥ śubhakarmaṇām ॥ 2.15.35 ॥



vimale ca vipāpe ca satkṛtya prabhave'bhaye।


mahodadhau prabhāse ca tasmādevaṃ vinirddiśet ॥ 2.15.40 ॥


dadyācchrāddhantu yastasmiṃstasya vakṣyāmi yatphalam ॥ 2.15.42 ॥




khyātaṃ hayaśiro nāma tīrthaṃ sadyo varapradam।


ajhatuṅge śubhe tīrthe tarpayetsatataṃ pitṝn।
dṛśyate parvasu cchāyā yatra nityaṃ divaukasām।

dadyācchrāddhantu yastasmistasya vakṣyāmi yatphalam ॥ 2.15.49 ॥

asmiँlloke vaśī yaḥ syātpretya svarge sa modate ॥ 2.15.50 ॥

tatra vyāsasaraḥ puṇyaṃ divyaṃ brahmasarastathā ॥ 2.15.51 ॥


gatvaitān mucyate pāpāddvijo vahniḥ sanātanaḥ ।।


brahmatīrthe mahātīrthe aśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet ॥ 2.15.54 ॥

sindhusāgarasambhede tadā pañcanade'kṣayam ।

mahākūṭe ca vande ca girau trirau trikakude tathā ॥ 2.15.56 ॥




svargyamārgapradaṃ nāma tīrthaṃ sadyo varapradam।

snātvā svargamavāpnoti tasmistīrthottame naraḥ ॥ 2.15.61 ॥

khyātamāyatanaṃ tatra nandisiddha niṣevitam।

kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ te tu gacchantyantarhitā divam ॥ 2.15.63 ॥



arcayedvā pitṝṃstatra satputrastvanṛṇo bhavet ॥ 2.15.66 ॥


sarvataścaiva gaṅgyāṃ maināke ca nagottame ॥ 2.15.68 ॥

yamunāprabhave caiva sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ।


brahmānugahrade snātvā sadyo bhavati brāhmaṇaḥ।
tasmin hi śrāddhamānantyaṃ japahomatapāṃsi ca ॥ 2.15.71 ॥


yayā vai tulitaṃ vipraistīrthānāṃ phalamuttamam ॥ 2.15.73 ॥



acchodakaṃ nāma saro yatrācchodā samucchritā ।
matsyayonau punarjātā niyogādvāraṇena tu ॥ 2.15.76 ॥

tasyāṃ yoga samādhāne dattaṃ yugapadudbhavet ॥ 2.15.77 ॥

kuberatuṅge vyāmocce vyāsatīrthe tathaiva ca।


siddhakṣetraṃ tu vai juṣṭaṃ yatprāpya na nivarttate।



japo homastathā dhyānaṃ yatkicitsukṛtaṃ bhavet ॥ 2.15.83 ॥

evamādīni sadyastāṃ snātvā prāpnoti satphalm ॥ 2.15.84 ॥


śailakīrttipurābhyāśe kāmānāpnoti puṣkalān।




tatra caiva hrade puṇye divyo vai nāgarāḍ yataḥ ॥ 2.15.90 ॥

atipradīptairbhujagairbhoktumannaṃ na śakyate ॥ 2.15.91 ॥

pratyakṣaṃ dṛśyate dharmastīrthayoranayordvayoḥ।
devadāruvane cāpi cārayestaṃ nidarśanam ॥ 2.15.92 ॥

bhāgīrathyāṃ prayāge ca nityamakṣayyamucyate ॥ 2.15.93 ॥



tapo homastathā dhyānaṃ yat kiñcitsukṛtaṃ bhavet।
lauhitye vaitaraṇyāṃ vai svarṇavedyāṃ tathaiva ca ॥ 2.15.96 ॥


himañca patate tatra samantāt pañcayojanam ॥ 2.15.98 ॥


khyāpitaṃ dharmmasarvvasvaṃ lokasyāsya nidarśanam।

unmajjanti tathā lagne ye vai pāpakṛto janāḥ ॥ 2.15.101 ॥



alpenāpyatra kālena naro dharmmaparāyaṇaḥ।

lelihānairmahābhogai rakṣitantu divāniśam ॥ 2.15.105 ॥

nāmnā kanakanandīti tīrthaṃ trailokyaviśrutam।


tīre tu sarasastatra devasyāyatanaṃ mahat।
āruhya tajjapaṃstatra siddho yāti divaṃ tataḥ ॥ 2.15.108 ॥


tasminnirvarttayecchrāddhaṃ yathāśakti yathābalam।



yataḥ pravarttate bhūyaḥ pūrvvasāgaramantimam ॥ 2.15.113 ॥

candrabhāgā ca sindhuśca ubhau mānasasannibhau।




sarvvaṃ puṇyaṃ himavato gaṅgā puṇyā ca sarvvataḥ।

pūto bhavati snātvā nu dattvā dattvā tathaiva ca ॥ 2.15.119 ॥

upahvaranitambeṣu tathā prasravaṇeṣu ca ॥ 2.15.120 ॥

pulineṣvāpagānāṃ ca tathaiva prabhave yuge।

asaṃsṛṣṭopaliptāsu hṛdyāsu surabhīṣu ca।
gomayenopalipteṣu vivikteṣu gṛheṣu ca ॥ 2.15.122 ॥

kuryyācchrāddhamathaiteṣu nityameva yathāvidhi।


traivarṇye vihite sthāne dharmmavarṇāśrame tathā।


tiryagyoniṃ na gacchecca kudeśe na ca jāyate।
svargī bhavati vai vipro mokṣopāyaṃ ca vindati ॥ 2.15.127 ॥

hetudraṣṭā ca pañcaite na tīrthaphalamaśrnute ॥ 2.15.128 ॥



buddhiṃ manasi saṃyamya sarvveṣāṃ tu nivarttanam ॥ 2.15.131 ॥


nigrahādbuddhimanaso ramyā buddhistu jāyate ॥ 2.15.133 ॥

parinirvāti śuddhātmā yathā vahnirnirindhanaḥ ॥ 2.15.134 ॥

viyojayati kṣetrajñaṃ tebhyo yogena yogavit ॥ 2.15.135 ॥

āsannaḥ sadasannaiva naiva kiñcit sthita iti ॥ 2.15.136 ॥


## 2.16

।।bṛhaspatiruvāca।।

himaprapatane kuryyādāharedvā himantataḥ।
agnihotramataḥ puṇyaṃ paramaṃ hi tataḥ smṛtam ॥ 2.16.2 ॥

naktantu varjayecchrāddhaṃ rāhoranyatra darśanāt।
sarvvasvenāpi karttavyaṃ kṣipraṃ vai rāhudarśane ॥ 2.16.3 ॥

uparāge na kuryyādyaḥ paṅke gauriva sīdati।





śyāmākā hastināmā ca paṭolaṃ bṛhatīphalam।
agastyasya śikhā tīvrā kaṣāyāḥ sarvva eva ca ॥ 2.16.9 ॥




purā devāsure yuddhe nirjitasya baleḥ suraiḥ ॥ 2.16.13 ॥

vraṇebhyo visphuranto vai patitā raktabindavaḥ।


durgandhaṃ phenilaṃ caiva tathā vai palvalodakam।
na labhedyatra gaustṛptiṃ naktaṃ yaccaiva gṛhyate ॥ 2.16.16 ॥








nagnādayo na paśyeyuḥ śrāddhameva vyayasthitam।

        ।।śaṃyuruvāca।।
nagnādīn bhagavan samyaṅmamādya paripṛcchataḥ।
kathaya dvijamukhyāgra vistareṇa yathātatham ॥ 2.16.25 ॥

evamukto mahātejā bṛhaspatiruvāca tam।

parityajati yo mohātte vai nagnā dvijottamāḥ।

vṛṣo vedasamastasmin yo vai samyaṅna paśyati ॥ 2.16.28 ॥

purā devāsure yuddhe nirjitairasuraistadā ॥ 2.16.29 ॥

pāṣaṇḍavaikṛtasthāne naiṣā sṛṣṭiḥ svayambhuvā।
dviśrāddhakaśca nirgranthāḥ śākyā puṣṭikalaṃśakāḥ ॥ 2.16.30 ॥

ye dharmmaṃ nānuvarttante te vai nagnādayo janāḥ।

kulandhamā niṣādāśca tathā puṣṭivināśakāḥ ॥ 2.16.32 ॥


dasyavaśca nṛśaṃsāśca darśanenaiva varjitāḥ ॥ 2.16.34 ॥

devadevarṣinindāyāṃ ratāṃścaiva viśeṣataḥ ॥ 2.16.35 ॥



          ।।pitara ūcuḥ ।।



śvā caiva darśanādeva hanti śrāddhaṃ na saṃśayaḥ।

malinaiḥ patitaiścaiva na draṣṭavyaṃ kathañcana।
annaṃ paśyeyurete vai naitatsyāddhavyakavyayoḥ ॥ 2.16.40 ॥






santi vedavirodhena kecidvijñānamāninaḥ ॥ 2.16.46 ॥


saindhavaṃ lavaṇaṃ yacca tathā mānasasambhavam ॥ 2.16.48 ॥

pavitraṃ paramaṃ hyetat pratyakṣamapi varttate।
agnau nikṣipya badhnīyāddhastau prakṣipya yatnataḥ ॥ 2.16.49 ॥

gamayenmastakaṃ caiva brahmatīrthaṃ hi tatsmṛtam।

nidhāya cādbhiḥ siñceta tathaivāpsu niveśanam।
ariṣṭatumule bilvaṃ tviṅgudaśvadanānyapi ॥ 2.16.51 ॥






saṃmārjanaṃ prokṣaṇañca bhūmeścaivopalepanam।


prātargṛhātpaścimadakṣiṇena iṣukṣepañcākṣamātraṃ padañca।

mṛṇmayairbhājanairvāpi tirodhāya vasundharām ॥ 2.16.60 ॥



daśa cāpi punardadyādvāmahastakrameṇa tu ।


kuryyāt sannihitaṃ nityaṃ prājñastīrthe kamaṇḍalum।
asatkāryaṃ kāryametairyathāvatpādadhāvanam ॥ 2.16.65 ॥



trirātramuktaṃ sasnehamekarātramato'nyathā ॥ 2.16.68 ॥


sindhoruttaraparyyantaṃ tathā divyantare śatam।


manovyaktirathāgniśca kāle caivopalepanam।

atonyathā tu yaḥ kuryyānmohācchaucasya saṅkaram।





taistaiḥ prītāḥ prayacchanti pitaro yogavarddhanāḥ।


## 2.17

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।


         ।।sūta uvāca।।







arcayitvā tato dadyādviprāyātithaye naraḥ।

āgniṣṭomaṃ tu payasā prāpnuyādvai tathāśrutam।
sarpiṣā tu śubhaṃ cakṣuḥ ṣoḍaśāhaphalaṃ labhet।
madhunā tvatirātrasya phalaṃ ca samavāpnuyāt ॥ 2.17.11 ॥

tatprāpnuyācchraddadhāno naro vai sarvaiḥ kāmairbhojayedyastu viprān।
sarvārthadaḥ sarvaviprātithau yaḥ phalaṃ bhuṅkte sarvamedhasya nityam ॥ 2.17.12 ॥

yastu śrāddhe'tithiṃ prāpya daive vāpyavamanyate।

devāśca pitaraścaiva vahniścaiva hi tān dvijān ।

sarvasvenāpi tasmādvai pūjayedatithīn sadā ॥ 2.17.15 ॥

vānaprastho gṛhasthaśca gṛhamabhyāgato'thavā।
bālāḥ khinnā yatiścaiva jānīyādatithīn sadā ॥ 2.17.16 ॥

atitheratithiḥ śreṣṭhaḥ so'tithiryoga ucyate ॥ 2.17.17 ॥

na ca santo na samṛddho na sevī nācaro'tithiḥ ॥ 2.17.18 ॥

tasmai satkṛtya dātavyaṃ yajñasya phalamicchatā ॥ 2.17.19 ॥


sarastīrthābhisaṃvedyā nadī navavahāstathā ॥ 2.17.21 ॥

gatvaitān mucyate pāpaiḥ svarge nityaṃ mahīyate।



snātvā sacailo mṛdbhistu dvādaśabhistu śudhyati ॥ 2.17.25 ॥

etadeva bhavecchaucaṃ maithune navabhistathā।
mṛdā prakṣālya hastau tu kuryācchaucavidhinnaraḥ ॥ 2.17.26 ॥

prakṣālya cādbhirhastau ca snātvā caiva mṛdā punaḥ।
mṛdaṃ guhye tato dvistu punareva mṛdaṃ budhaḥ ॥ 2.17.27 ॥

paridadyānmṛdastisro hastapādāvasecanam ॥ 2.17.28 ॥

mṛdastisraḥ pādayostu hastayostisra eva ca ॥ 2.17.29 ॥

anirṇikte mṛdaṃ dadyānmṛdante tvadbhireva tu ॥ 2.17.30 ॥




prokṣitantu haret kiñci cchrāddhe daiva tathā punaḥ।
uttareṇa haredvedyāṃ dakṣiṇena visarjayet ॥ 2.17.34 ॥

vicchinnaṃ syādviparyāse daive pitrye tathaiva ca।



utsṛṣṭasya sumambhāṣe hyaśuciṃ prayatasya ca।


saśabdamaṅgulībhiśca praṇataścāvalo kayan ॥ 2.17.40 ॥

yaścādharme sthito mohādāyānto'pyaśucirbhavet।

pādau prakṣālya hastau tu antarjānurupa spṛśet।



dānāni brahmacaryañca bhavanti saphalāstathā।

vāgbhāvaśuddhanirṇiktamaduṣṭaṃ vāpyaninditam ॥ 2.17.46 ॥

na vaktavyaḥ sadā vipraḥ kṣudhito nāsti kiñcana ॥ 2.17.47 ॥

tasmai satkṛtya yo dadyādayūpo yajña ucyate।
apluṣṭānnaṃ śrṛtānnantu kṛśavṛttimayācakam ॥ 2.17.48 ॥

yo dadātyāntimabhyeśca sa brahmaghno durātmavān ॥ 2.17.49 ॥


niyukto vā'niyukto vā paṃkt‌yā harati duṣkṛtam।

yatistu sarvvaviprāṇāṃ sarvveṣāmagrya utsave।
itihāsapañcamān vedān yaḥ paṭettu dvijottamaḥ ॥ 2.17.52 ॥

anantaraṃ yathāyogyaṃ niyoktavyo vijānatā।
trivedo'nantarastasya dvivedastadanantaraḥ ॥ 2.17.53 ॥

pāvanāyaiva paṃktyā vai tān pravakṣye nibodhata ॥ 2.17.54 ॥





pitarastasya taṃ māsaṃ tasya retasi śerate ॥ 2.17.59 ॥

śrāddhaṃ dattvā ca bhuktvā ca maithunaṃ yo niṣevate।
pitarastasya taṃ māsaṃ retaḥsthā nātra saṃśayaḥ ॥ 2.17.60 ॥

tasmādatithaye deyaṃ bhojayedbrahmacāriṇam।



āśramāḥ pāvanāḥ pañca upadhābhiranāśramāḥ ॥ 2.17.64 ॥





dvijeṣvapi kṛtaṃ nityaṃ śrāddhakarmmaṇi varjayet ॥ 2.17.69 ॥

eteṣu varttate yaśca kṛtsvavarṇaṃ sa gacchati।

śuśrūṣaṇaṃ vāpyaguro raho vā kāryyaṃ naitadvidyate brāhmaṇasya ॥ 2.17.71 ॥


mithyātattvavido varjjyāstathā dambhaviṣūcakāḥ।

vede niyogadātāro lobhamohaphalārthinaḥ।


bhṛto'dhyāpayate yastu bhṛtakādhyāpitastu yaḥ ।

krayavikrayiṇau caiva jīvitārthaṃ vigarhitau।

prāhurvedān vedavido vedān yaścopajīvati।।
ubhau tau nārhataḥ śrāddhaṃ putrikāpatireva ca ॥ 2.17.78 ॥


ātmārthaṃ yaḥ pacedannaṃ na devātithikārakaḥ।



na tadaśnanti pitaro yaśca vāmaṃ samaśnute ॥ 2.17.83 ॥

yo dadyā drāgamohāttu na tadgacchetpitṝnsadā ॥ 2.17.84 ॥




dānaṃ pratigraho homo bhojanaṃ balireva ca।


śikhibhyo vā tridaṇiḍabhyaḥ śrāddhaṃ yatnāt pradāpayet ॥ 2.17.90 ॥

ye tu vrate sthitā nityaṃ jñānino dhyāninastathā।

sarvvaṃ yogeśvarairvyāptaṃ trailokyaṃ vai nirantaram ।
tasmāt paśyanti te sarve yatkiñcijjagatīgatam ॥ 2.17.92 ॥

vyaktāvyaktaṃ vaśīkṛtya sarvvasyāpi ca yatparam।


ṛco hi yo veda sa veda vedān yajūṃṣi yo veda sa veda yajñam।
sāmāni yo veda sa veda brahma yo mānasaṃ veda sa veda sarvvam ॥ 2.17.95 ॥


## 2.18

bṛhaspatiruvāca।।


divyāpsarobhiḥ saṅkīrṇamannado labhate phalam ॥ 2.18.3 ॥


upavītantu yo dadyācchrāddhakāleṣu dharmavit ।



pūrṇasayyāṃ tu yo dadyāt puṣpamālāvibhūṣitām।
prāsādo hyuttamo bhūtvā gacchantamanugacchati ॥ 2.18.8 ॥

bhavanaṃ ratnasampūrṇaṃ saśayyāsanabhojanam।

vāhanāni ca divyāni ayutānyarbbudāni ca ॥ 2.18.10 ॥

sumahajjvalanaprakhyaṃ ratnakāmasamanvitam ।
sūryyacandra prabhaṃ divyaṃ vimānaṃ labhate'kṣayam ॥ 2.18.11 ॥

apsarobhiḥ parivṛtaṃ kāmagaṃ tu manojavam।

divyairgandhaiḥ prasiñcanti puṣpavṛṣṭibhireva ca।
