gandharvāpsarasastatra gāyante vādayanti ca ॥ 2.18.13 ॥

kanyā yuvatayo mukhyāḥ sahitāścāpsarogaṇaiḥ।
susvaraistaṃ vibudhyante satataṃ hi manoramaiḥ ॥ 2.18.14 ॥

aśvadānasahasreṇa rathadānaśatena ca।
dantināṃ ca sahasreṇa yoginyā vasate naraḥ ॥ 2.18.15 ॥

dadyāt pitṛbhyo yogibhyo yastūjjvalanamambhasi।

jīvitasya pradānāddhi nānyaddānaṃ viśiṣyate।

ahiṃsā sarvvadevebhyaḥ pavitrā sarvvadāyinī।
dānaṃ hi jīvitasyāhuḥ prāṇināṃ paramaṃ budhāḥ ॥ 2.18.18 ॥



dattvā tu labhate dātā prakāmaṃ dharmmameva ca ॥ 2.18.21 ॥


śiśireṣu tathā tvagniṃ bahukāṣṭhaṃ tathaiva ca।

surabhīṇi ca mālyāni gandhavanti tathaiva ca ॥ 2.18.24 ॥



śrāddhe'pyetāni yo dadyādaśvamedhaphalaṃ labhet।
śrāddhakālenivedyaṃ ca darśaśrāddha upasthite ॥ 2.18.27 ॥

viprāṇāṃ guṇayuktānāṃ smṛtiṃ medhāṃ ca vindati।

kumbhadohanadhenūnāṃ bahvīnāṃ ca phalaṃ labhet।
asmistu modate loke syandanaiśca suvāhanaiḥ ॥ 2.18.29 ॥

ramyamāvasathaṃ dattvā rājasūyaphalaṃ labhet ॥ 2.18.30 ॥

vanaṃ puṣpaphalopetaṃ dattvā saurabhamaśnute।

dattvaitān modate svarge nityamācandratārakam।

pitarastasya tuṣyanti svargaṃ cānantyamaśnute।
rājabhiḥ pūjyate cāpi dhanadhānyaiśca varddhate ॥ 2.18.33 ॥

ūrṇākauśeyavastrāṇi tathā pravarakambalau।

dānānyatāni viprebhyo bhojayitvā yathāvidhi।
prāpnoti śraddadhānastu vājapeyaśataṃ phalam ॥ 2.18.35 ॥




vāso hi sarvadaivatyaṃ sarvadevaistvabhiṣṭutam।

tāni sarvāṇyavāpnāpnoti yajñavedatapāṃsi ca ॥ 2.18.40 ॥


sarvakāmasamṛddhasya yajñasya phalamaśnute।
bhakṣyān dhānāḥ karambhāṃśca piṣṭakān ghṛtaśarkarāḥ ॥ 2.18.42 ॥

kṛśarānmadhu karkañca payaḥ pāyasameva ca।


ghṛtena bhojayedviprān ghṛtaṃ bhūmau samutsṛjet।

odanaṃ pāyasaṃ sarpirmadhumūlaphalāni ca।
bhakṣyāṃśca vividhāndattvā pretya ceha ca modate ॥ 2.18.46 ॥

yaśca saṃvatsaraṃ prītyā kṛsarairmasureṇa ca ॥ 2.18.47 ॥

śrāddheṣvetāni yo dadyāt pajhāni labhate nidhim ॥ 2.18.48 ॥

navasasyāni yo dadyācchrāddhe satkṛtya yatnataḥ।
sarvabhogānavāpnoti pūjyate ca divaṃ gataḥ ॥ 2.18.49 ॥

sarvaśreṣṭāni yo dadyāt sarvvaśreṣṭo bhavennaraḥ ॥ 2.18.50 ॥



vayañjanaṃ ca tathā snigdhaṃ bhaktyā satkṛtya yatnataḥ ॥ 2.18.53 ॥

annado labhate tisraḥ kalpakoṭīstathaiva ca ॥ 2.18.54 ॥

annādbhūtāni jāyante jīvanti ca na saṃśayaḥ ॥ 2.18.55 ॥

annairjīvati trailokyamannasyaiva hi tatphalam ॥ 2.18.56 ॥

anne lokāḥ pratiṣṭhanti lokadānasya tatphalam ।



tribhirdvābhyāmathaikena dānena tu sukhī bhavet ॥ 2.18.60 ॥

trailokyasyādhipatyaṃ hi dānādeva vyavasthitam ॥ 2.18.61 ॥

rājā tu labhate rājyamadhanaścottamaṃ dhanam।
yān kāmān manasā'rtheta tāṃstasya pitaro viduḥ ॥ 2.18.62 ॥


## 2.19

bṛhaspatiruvāca।।

pūrvapakṣo variṣṭo hi pūrvā citrī udāhṛtā ॥ 2.19.2 ॥




paratreha ca sarvveṣu nityameva sukhī bhavet ।

sarvve puruṣamāyānti nipānamiva dhenavaḥ ॥ 2.19.7 ॥

moghastasya bhavelloko labdhaṃ cāsya vinaśyati ॥ 2.19.8 ॥

devāṃstu dāyino yānti tiryyaggacchantyadāyinaḥ।

pratipaddhanalābhāya labdhaṃ cāsya na naśyati ॥ 2.19.10 ॥




mahāsatramavāpnoti gaṇānāmadhipo bhavet।






sarvvān kāmānavāpnoti svargamānantyamaśnute ।
ṛtaṃ dadyādamāvāsyāṃ somamāpyāyanaṃ mahat ॥ 2.19.20 ॥

evamāpyāyitaḥ somastrīँllokān dhārayiṣyati।

stavaiḥ puṣpairmanojñaiśca sarvvakāmaparicchadaiḥ।

upakrīḍairvvimānaistu pitṛbhaktaṃ dṛḍhavratam।
stuvanti devagandharvvāḥ siddhasaṅghāśca taṃ sadā ॥ 2.19.23 ॥

pratyakṣamarcitāstena bhavanti pitaraḥ sadā ॥ 2.19.24 ॥

pitryaṃ kurvvanti tasyāṃ tu viśeṣeṇa vicakṣaṇaḥ ॥ 2.19.25 ॥

tasmānmaghāṃ vai vāñchanti pitaro nityameva hi ।
pitṝdaivatabhaktā ye te'pi yānti parāṃ gatim ॥ 2.19.26 ॥


## 2.20

bṛhaspatiruvāca।।

agnīnādhāya sāpatyo jāyate sa gatajvaraḥ ॥ 2.20.2 ॥

apatyakāmo rohiṇyāṃ saumyenaujasvitā bhavet ।

kṣetrabhāgī bhavet putrī śrāddhaṃ kurvvan punarvvasau।


śreṣṭo bhavati jñātīnāṃ maghāsu śrāddhamācaran।

sa satsu mukhyo bhavati haste yastarpayetpitṝn ॥ 2.20.7 ॥


anurādhāsu kurvvāṇo naraścakraṃ pravarttayet ॥ 2.20.9 ॥


āṣāḍhābhiścottarābhirvītaśoko bhavennaraḥ।

śrāddhaṃ tvabhijitā kurvan vindate'jāvikaṃ phalam ॥ 2.20.12 ॥

nakṣatre vāruṇe kurvvan bhiṣaksiddhimavāpnuyāt।
pūrvve proṣṭhapade kurvvan vindate'jāvikaṃ phalam ॥ 2.20.13 ॥

uttarāsvanatikramya vindedghā vai sahasraśaḥ।
bahurūpakṛtaṃ dravyaṃ vindet kurvvaṃstu revatīm।

kṛtsnāṃ tu lebhe sa kṛtsnāṃ labdhā ca praśaśaṃsa tam ॥ 2.20.15 ॥


## 2.21

śaṃyuruvāca।।

           bṛhaspatiruvāca।

tilairvrīhiyavairmāṣairadbhirmūlaphalena ca।




śrāddhameva vijānīyādgavyaṃ saṃvatsaraṃ bhavet ॥ 2.21.7 ॥


kṛṣṇacchāgastathā godhā ānantyāyaiva kalpate ॥ 2.21.9 ॥

tāste'haṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yathāvatsannibodhata ॥ 2.21.10 ॥


ājena sarvalohena varṣāsu ca maghāsu ca।

         śaṃyuruvāca।।

           bṛhaspatiruvāca।।


phalaṃ vṛṣasya vakṣyāmi gadato me nibodhata।




tṛptistṛptiḥ pitṝṇāṃ vai tadvṛṣasyādhikocyate ॥ 2.21.20 ॥

       bṛhaspatiruvāca।।



brahmadeya sutaścaiva chandogo jyeṣṭhasāmagaḥ।

ye ca sadyovratā nityaṃ svakarmaniratāśca ye ॥ 2.21.26 ॥

ye cāpi nityaṃ daśasu sukṛteṣu vyavasthitāḥ ॥ 2.21.27 ॥

śraddheyā brāhmaṇā ye tu yogadharmmamanuvratāḥ ॥ 2.21.28 ॥

dharmāśramavariṣṭāste havyakavyeṣu te varāḥ।


prathamaḥ sarvadharmāṇāṃ yogadharmo nigadyate ।



pitrā vivadamānaśca yasya copapatirgṛhe।
abhiśastastathā stenaḥ śilpairyaścopajīvati ॥ 2.21.34 ॥

sūjakaḥ parvakārī ca yastu mitreṣu druhyati।
gaṇayācanakaścaiva nāstiko vedavarjitaḥ ॥ 2.21.35 ॥



yacca vāṇijake caiva neha nāmutra tadbhavet।
nikṣepahāriṇe caiva kitave vedanindake ॥ 2.21.38 ॥

tathā vāṇijake caiva kāruke dharmmavarjite।

bhasma nīva hutaṃ havyaṃ dattaṃ paunarbhave dvije ॥ 2.21.40 ॥

pāparogī sahasrasya dāturnāśayate phalam ॥ 2.21.41 ॥


sopānatkaśca yo bhuṅkte yacca dadyāttiraskṛtam ।



rajasvalānusparśena kruddho yaśca prayacchati।

nadītīreṣu ramyeṣu saritsu ca sarassu ca।
vivikteṣu ca prīyante datteneha pitāmahāḥ ॥ 2.21.47 ॥

na ca kurvīta bhuñjāno hyanyo'nyaṃ matsarastadā ॥ 2.21.48 ॥

apasavye kṛte tena vidhivaddarbhapāṇinā ।

anumatyādito viprānagnau kuryādyathāvidhi।


evamete mahātmāno mahā yogā mahaujasaḥ।

pitṛbhaktirato nityaṃ yogaṃ prāpnotyanuttamam।

yajñahetoryaduddhṛtya mohayitvā jagattadā।

proktaṃ sanatkumāreṇa mahattaddharmmaśāśvatam ॥ 2.21.55 ॥

pitṛbhaktyā prayatnena pitṛbhaktaiśca nityaśaḥ ॥ 2.21.56 ॥

tañca yogaṃ samāsena pitṛbhaktastu kṛtsnaśaḥ।
prayatnātprāpnuyāttatra sarvameva na saṃśayaḥ ॥ 2.21.57 ॥

yasmai śrāddhāni deyāni yacca dattaṃ mahāphalam।
yeṣu ca svargamāpnoti tatte proktaṃ sasaṃgraham ॥ 2.21.58 ॥

          bṛhaspatiruvāca।।
śrutvaivaṃ śrāddhakalpaṃ tu yo'sūyāṃ kurute naraḥ।
sa majjennarake ghore nāstikastamasāvṛtaḥ ॥ 2.21.59 ॥

mahārogāvasāyastu sa yaḥ saṃyatamānasaḥ।
vedāśramānmuktacittaḥ kumbhīkā nadhigacchati।
jihvācchedaṃ stenametya prāpnuyustena caiva ha ॥ 2.21.60 ॥

sīdanti te sāgare loṣṭabhūtā yogadviṣaḥ sthāsyante yāvadurvvī ।
tasmācchrāddhe dharmma uddiṣṭa eṣa nityaṃ kāryaḥ śraddadhānena puṃsā ॥ 2.21.61 ॥

parivādāt kṛmirbhūtvā tatraiva parivarttate ॥ 2.21.62 ॥





na pārago vindati pāramātmanastrilokamadhye carati svakarmabhiḥ।

tattvañcaturviṃśatiyo gapāragaṃ sa pārago yastvayanāntapāragaḥ ॥ 2.21.68 ॥

kṛtsnaṃ yathā tattvavisargamātmanastathaiva bhūyaḥ pralayaṃ sadātmanaḥ।
pratyāharedyogabalena yogavit sa sarvapārakramayānagocaraḥ ॥ 2.21.69 ॥

vedasya veditā yo vai vedyaṃ vindati yogavit।
taṃ vai vedavidaṃ prāhustaṃ prāhurvedapāragam ॥ 2.21.70 ॥

vedyaṃ ca veditavyaṃ ca viditvā vai yathāvidhi।
evaṃ vedavidaṃ prāhustato'nye vedacintakāḥ ॥ 2.21.71 ॥



sa paṅktipāvanaścaiva dvijānāmagrabhug bhavet।

yaścaiva śrṛṇuyānnityamānantyaṃ svargamaśnute।
anasūyo jitakrodho lobhamohavivarjitaḥ ॥ 2.21.75 ॥

mokṣopāyo hyayaṃ śreṣṭhaḥ svargopāyo hyayaṃ paraḥ ।

imaṃ vidhiṃ yo hi paṭedatandritaḥ samāhitaḥ saṃsadi parvasandhiṣu ।
apatyabhāgbhavati pareṇa tejasā divau kasāṃ sa vrajate salokatām ॥ 2.21.77 ॥

yena proktastvayaṃ kalpo namastasmai svayambhuve।
mahāyogeśvarebhyaśca sadā ca praṇato hyaham ॥ 2.21.78 ॥

ityetepitara stāta devānāmapi devatāḥ।
saptasveteṣu te nityaṃ sthāneṣu pitaro'vyathāḥ ॥ 2.21.79 ॥

prajāpatisutā hyete sarve caiva mahātmanaḥ।


devāstvetān yajante vai sarveṣveteṣvavasthitāḥ।


pitṝṃśca yo yajedbhaktyā pitaraḥ pūjayanti tam।






tathā taṃ nayate mantro janturyatrāvatiṣṭhate ॥ 2.21.90 ॥

māna gotraṃ ca mantraśca dattamannaṃ nayanti tam।


ityete pitaro devā devāśca pitaraḥ punaḥ।


proktaṃ sarvaṃ yathānyāyaṃ yathā brahmā'bravīt purā ॥ 2.21.95 ॥

          bṛhaspatiruvāca।।
ityetadaṅgirāḥ prāha ṛṣīṇāṃ śrṛṇvatāṃ tadā।
pṛṣṭastu saṃśayaṃ sarvaṃ pitṝṇāṃ prāha saṃsadi ॥ 2.21.96 ॥

satre vai vitate pūrvaṃ tadā varṣasahasrike ।
yasmin gṛhapatirhyāsīdbrahmā vai devatā prabhuḥ ॥ 2.21.97 ॥

saṃvatsaraśatānyaṃ ca tatropetā iti śrutiḥ।


         sūta uvāca।।
ata ūrddhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi varuṇasya nibodhata ॥ 2.21.100 ॥

iti śrīmahāpurāṇe vāyuprokte śrāddhakalpe bhinnakālikatṛptisādhanadravyaviśeṣagayā śrāddhādiphalabrāhmaṇaparīkṣādikathanaṃ nāmaikaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ ।। 21 ।। *

## 2.22


        ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।



bruvato me nibodhasva ṛṣirāha yathā mama ॥ 2.22.4 ॥

sthitiṃ caiṣāṃ prabhāvañca bruvato me nibedhata ॥ 2.22.5 ॥

tasyāḥ putrau kalirvaidyaḥ sutā ca surasundarī ॥ 2.22.6 ॥

kali putrau mahāvīryau jayaśca vijayaśca ha।
vaidyaputrau ghṛṇiścaiva muniścaiva mahābalau ॥ 2.22.7 ॥




sadramaścaika hasto'bhūdvidhamaścaikapātsmṛtaḥ ॥ 2.22.11 ॥


nākasya śakuniḥ patnī vighnasya ca ayomukhī।

skandasteṣāmadhipatirbrahmaṇo'numate prabhuḥ ॥ 2.22.14 ॥

yogasiddhā jagatkṛtsnamasaktā carate sadā ॥ 2.22.15 ॥




virocanasya bhaginī mātā triśirasastu sā ॥ 2.22.19 ॥

devācāryasya mahato viśvakarmasya dhīmataḥ।


yamau punarasūtāsau yamañca yamunāñca ha ॥ 2.22.22 ॥



         ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
etadveditumicchāmastattvaṃ vibrūhi pṛcchatām ॥ 2.22.25 ॥

         sūta uvāca।।
dṛṣṭvā garbhavadhādbhītaḥ kaśyapo duḥśito'bhavat ॥ 2.22.26 ॥


tasya tadvacanaṃ śrutvā nāmānvarthamudāharat ॥ 2.22.28 ॥


vijajñe savituḥ saṃjñābhāryāyāntu trayaṃ purā ॥ 2.22.30 ॥

manuryavīyān sāvarṇiḥ saṃjñāyāñca tathāśvinau।



gotreṣu pratiruddhaṃ vai nāti kāntamivābhavat ॥ 2.22.34 ॥


tejastvabhyadhikaṃ tasya nityameva vivasvataḥ।

dvau sutau tu mahāvīryau kanyāṃ kālindimeva ca ॥ 2.22.37 ॥

tato yamo yamī caiva yamajau saṃbabhūvatuḥ ॥ 2.22.38 ॥



ahaṃ yāsyāmi bhadrante svameva bhavanaṃ pituḥ ॥ 2.22.41 ॥

tvayeha bhavane mahyaṃ vastavyaṃ nirviśaṅkayā।
imau ca bālakau mahyaṃ kanyā ca varavarṇinī ॥ 2.22.42 ॥

bhartre vai naivamākhyeyamidaṃ bhagavate tvayā।
evamuktābravīt saṃjñāṃ saṃjñāyāḥ pārthivī tu sā ॥ 2.22.43 ॥

ākhyāsyāmi mataṃ tubhyaṃ gaccha devi svamālayam ॥ 2.22.44 ॥


bhartuḥ samīpaṃ gaccha tvaṃ mā jugupsa divākaram ॥ 2.22.46 ॥

saivamuktā tadā pitrā niyuktā ca punaḥ punaḥ।


ādityo janayāmāsa putrāvādityavarcasau ॥ 2.22.49 ॥

pūrvajasya manostulyau sādṛśyena tu tau prabhū ।


manurevābhavatso vai sāvarṇya iti budhyate।

manustaccākṣamatsarvaṃ yamastadvai na cākṣamat ॥ 2.22.53 ॥




manunā saha dharmmātmā pituḥ sarvvaṃ nyavedayat ॥ 2.22.57 ॥


śapto'hamasmiँllokeśa jananyā tapatāṃ vara।
tava prasādo nastrātuṃ hyetasmānmahato bhayāt ॥ 2.22.59 ॥

vivasvānevamuktastu yamaṃ provāca vai prabhuḥ।

yena tvāmāviśatkrodho dharmmajñaṃ satyavādinam।
na śakyametanmithyā tu karttuṃ māturvacastava ॥ 2.22.61 ॥



tulyeṣvapyadhikaḥ sneha ekasmin kriyate tvayā ॥ 2.22.64 ॥

sā tatpariharantī vai nācacakṣe vivasvataḥ।
ātmanā sa samādhāya yogantathyamapaśyata ॥ 2.22.65 ॥

sā tatsarvaṃ yathātattvamācacakṣe vivasvataḥ ॥ 2.22.66 ॥

vivasvānatha tacchrutvā kruddhastvaṣṭāramabhyayāt।



anukūlaṃ bhavedevaṃ yadi syāt samayo mataḥ ॥ 2.22.70 ॥

rūpaṃ vivasvatastvāsīttiryagūrdhvamadhastathā ॥ 2.22.71 ॥



tattu nirbhāsitantejastejasāpahṛtena tu ॥ 2.22.74 ॥







dharmmeṇa rañjayāmāsa dharmmarājastatastu saḥ ।
so'labhat karmaṇā tena śubhena paramadyutiḥ ॥ 2.22.81 ॥


bhāvyasau nāgate tasmin manuḥ sāvarṇike'ntare।
merupṛṣṭe suramye vai adyāpi carate prabhuḥ ॥ 2.22.83 ॥

bhrātā śanaiścarastatra grahatvaṃ sa tu labdhavān।
mahā'pratihataṃ yuddhe dānava prativāraṇe ॥ 2.22.84 ॥


yastu jyeṣṭo mahātejāḥ sargo yasya tu sāmpratam।
vistaraṃ tasya vakṣyāmi manorvaivasvatasya ha ॥ 2.22.86 ॥



## 2.23

sūta uvāca।।
tato manvantare'tīte cākṣuṣe daivataiḥ saha।

tasya vaivasvato vakṣye sāmpratasya mahātmanaḥ।

manorvaivasvatasyeha sargamādāya sāmpratam ।
manoḥ prathamajasyāsan navaputrāstu tatsamāḥ ॥ 2.23.3 ॥


brahmaṇā tu manuḥ pūrvaṃ coditastu nibodhata।
sraṣṭuṃ pracakrame kāmaṃ niṣphalaṃ samavarttata ॥ 2.23.5 ॥



anugacchāmi bhadrante tamilā pratyuvāca ha ॥ 2.23.8 ॥

dharmmayuktamidaṃ vācyaṃ putrakāmaṃ prajāpatim।

saivamuktvā punarddevī tayorantikamāgamat ॥ 2.23.10 ॥


manunaivāhamuktāsmi anugacchasva māmiti।

anena tava dharmajñe praśrayeṇa damena ca ॥ 2.23.13 ॥

satyena caiva suśroṇi prītau svo varavarṇini।

sudyumna iti vikhyātastriṣu lokeṣu pūjitaḥ।
jagatpriyo dharmmaśīlo manorvaṃśa vivarddhanaḥ ॥ 2.23.15 ॥

mānavaḥ sa tu sudyumnaḥ strībhāvamagamat prabhuḥ।

budhenāntaramāsādya maithunāyopamantritā।


utkalaśca gayaścaiva vinatāśvastathaiva ca।

daśadhā taddadhat kṣetramakarot pṛthivīmimām ॥ 2.23.20 ॥


pratiṣṭā dharmarājasya sudyumnasya mahātmanaḥ ॥ 2.23.22 ॥

mānavaḥ sa tu sudyumnaḥ strībhāvamagamat punaḥ ॥ 2.23.23 ॥

etacchrutvā tu ṛṣayaḥ papracchustadanantaram ।
mānavaḥ sa tu sudyumnaḥ strībhāvamagamat katham ॥ 2.23.24 ॥

      sūta uvāca।।





## 2.24

sūta uvāca।।
pṛṣadhro hisayitvā tu gurorgāvamabhakṣayat ॥ 2.24.1 ॥




prajānerabhavat putraḥ khanitro nāma vīryavān।


karandhamastasya putrastretāyugamukhe'bhavat ॥ 2.24.7 ॥


marutto nāma dharmātmā cakravarttisamo nṛpaḥ।



maruttaścakravarttī sa nariṣyantamavāptavān।


kevalastasya putrastu bandhumān kevalātmajaḥ।
atha bandhumataḥ putro dharmātmā vegavān nṛpaḥ ॥ 2.24.14 ॥

budho vegavataḥ putra stṛṇabindurbudhātmajaḥ।


viśālasya samutpannā viśālā nayanirmitā।

sucandra iti vikhyāto hemacandrādanantaram।



somadattasya rājarṣeḥ sutobhūjjanamejayaḥ।
janamejayātmajaścaiva pramatirnāma viśrutaḥ ॥ 2.24.21 ॥




revasya raivataḥ putraḥ kakujhī nāma dhārmikaḥ।

kanyayā saha śrutvā ca gandharvaṃ brahmaṇo'ntike।
muhūrttaṃ devadevasya mārtyaṃ bahuyugaṃ vibhoḥ ॥ 2.24.26 ॥


bhojavṛṣṭyandhakairguptāṃ vasudevapurogamaiḥ।

dattvā jagāma śikharaṃ merostapasi saṃsthitaḥ ॥ 2.24.29 ॥


        ṛṣya ūcuḥ ।।

kathaṃ bahuyuge kāle vyatīte sūtanandana।
na jarāṃ revatī prāptā palitañca kutaḥ prabho ॥ 2.24.31 ॥

meruṃ gatasya vā tasya śaryyāteḥ santatiḥ katham।

kiyanto vā suragaṇā gandharvvāstatra kīdṛśāḥ।
yacchrutvā raivataḥ kālān muhūrttamiva manyate ॥ 2.24.33 ॥

             sūta uvāca।।
na ca rogaḥ prabhavati brahmalokagatasya hi ॥ 2.24.34 ॥




syātkalopabalopetā caturthī śuddhamadhyamā ॥ 2.24.38 ॥


śuddhaṣaḍjā tathā caiva jānīyāt saptamāṃ ca tām ॥ 2.24.40 ॥


pañcamaṃ rājasūyaṃ va ṣaṣṭhaṃ cakrasuvarṇakam ॥ 2.24.42 ॥

brahmadānañca navamaṃ prājāpatyamanantaram ॥ 2.24.43 ॥


sūryyakrāntaṃ vareṇyañca mattakokilavādinam।
sāvitramarddhasāvitraṃ sarvvato madrameva ca ॥ 2.24.45 ॥

suvarṇañca sutandrañca viṣṇuvaiṣṇuvarāvubhau।
sāgaraṃ vijayañcaiva sarvabhūtamanoharam ॥ 2.24.46 ॥

haṃsaṃ jyeṣṭhaṃ vijānīmastumburupriyameva ca।
manoharamadhātryañca gandharvānugataśca yaḥ ॥ 2.24.47 ॥



tathā pañcadaśecchanti gāndhāragrāmasaṃsthitān ।

uttarādisvarasyaiva brahmā vai devatā'tra ca।
mūrcchanā hariṇāsyaiva asyā indro'dhidaivatam ॥ 2.24.51 ॥

karopanītavitatā marudbhiḥ svaramaṇḍale।
sā kalopanatā tasmānmārutaścātra daivatam ॥ 2.24.52 ॥

manudeśasamutpannā mūrcchanā śuddhamadhyam।
madhyamo'tra svaraḥ śuddho gandharvvaścātra devatā ॥ 2.24.53 ॥

mṛgaiḥ saha sañcarate siddhānāṃ mārgadarśane।


tāla uttaramandrāṃśaḥ ṣaḍjadaivatakāṃ viduḥ।
tasmāduttaramandro'yaṃ devatāsya dhruvo dhruvam ॥ 2.24.56 ॥


upatiṣṭhanti tasmāttaṃ jānīyācchuddhaṣaḍ‌jikam ॥ 2.24.58 ॥

yaḥ satāṃ mūrcchanāṃ kṛtvā pañcamasvarako bhavet ।

bhavantīva hṛtā hyete brahmaṇā nāgadevatāḥ।
ahīnāṃ mūrcchanā hyeṣā varuṇaścātra devatā ॥ 2.24.60 ॥

śakuntakānāṃ kṛtvā ca upamāṃ yānti kinnarāḥ ।
uttamāṃ mūrcchanā tasmāt pakṣirājo'tra devatā ॥ 2.24.61 ॥

gāndhārarāgaśabdena gāṃ ca dhārayate'rthataḥ।
tasmādviśuddhagāndhārī gandharvaścādhidaivatam ॥ 2.24.62 ॥

gāndhārānantaraṃ gatvā sṛṣṭeyaṃ mūrcchanā yataḥ।
tasmāduttaragāndhārī vasavaścātra devatāḥ ॥ 2.24.63 ॥

seyaṃ khalu mahābhūtā pitāmaha mupasthitā।
ṣaḍjeyaṃ mūrcchanā tasmāt smṛtā hyanaladevatā ॥ 2.24.64 ॥

divyeyaṃ cāyatā tena mandaṣaṣṭā ca mūrcchane।
nivṛttaguṇanāmānaṃ pañcamañcātra dhaivatam ॥ 2.24.65 ॥

pūrṇāḥ saptasvarā hyevaṃ mūrcchanāḥ saṃprakīrttitāḥ।


## 2.25







tatraikaḥ saṃcarasthāyī sacarāstacarībhavan।




kumāramaparaṃ vidyādvistaraṃ vamanaṃ gatam।

śyenastvekāntare jātaḥ kalāmātrāntare sthitaḥ।

śyenastu aparohastu uttaraḥ parikīrttitaḥ।
kalākalapramāṇācca sa bindurnāma cāyate ॥ 2.25.13 ॥

viparyayasvaro'pi syādyasya durghaṭito'pi na ॥ 2.25.14 ॥


ākṣiptamavarohyāpi prokṣamadyantathaiva ca ॥ 2.25.16 ॥

preṅkholitamalaṅkāramevaṃ svarasamanvitam ॥ 2.25.17 ॥

svarasaṃkrāmakāccaiva tataḥ proktantu puṣkalam।
prakṣiptameva kalayā pādānītarayorbhavet ॥ 2.25.18 ॥

dvikalaṃ vā yathā bhūtaṃ yattad hrāsitamucyate।

yastu syādavaroho vā tārato mandrato'pi vā।
ekāntarahitā hyete tameva svaramantataḥ ॥ 2.25.20 ॥


caturvidhamidaṃ jñeyamalaṅkāraprayojanam ॥ 2.25.22 ॥

yathātmano hyalaṅkāro viparyasto'tigarhitaḥ।

varṇasya caivālaṅkāro viparyasto'tigarhitaḥ ॥ 2.25.24 ॥

evameva hyalaṅkāro viparyasto vigarhitaḥ ॥ 2.25.25 ॥

yathoddiṣṭasya mārgasya karttavyasya vidhīyate ॥ 2.25.26 ॥

lakṣaṇaṃ paryyavasthāpi varṇikābhiḥ pravarttanam।
yāthātathyena vakṣyāmi māsedbhavamukhodbhave ॥ 2.25.27 ॥

trayoviṃśatyaśītistu teṣāmetadviparyayaḥ।
ṣaḍjapakṣo'pi tattvādau madhyo hīnasvaro bhavet ॥ 2.25.28 ॥

ṣaḍjamadhyamayoścaiva grāmayoḥ paryyapastathā।
mānoyottaramandrasya ṣaḍevātrāvikasya ca ॥ 2.25.29 ॥

svarālaṃpratyayaścaiva sarvveṣāṃ pratyayaḥ smṛtaḥ।
anugamya bahirgītaṃ vijñātaṃ pañcadaivatam ॥ 2.25.30 ॥


paryyayaḥ saṃpravartteta saptasvarapadakramam ॥ 2.25.32 ॥

gāndhārāṃśena gīyante catvāri mandrakāṇi ca।
pañcamo madhyamaścaiva dhaivate tu niṣādajaiḥ।

dve cāparāntike vidyāddhayaśullāṣṭakasya tu।

padasya tu trayaṃ rūpaṃ saptarūpantu kauśikam।

tattu saptasvaraṃ kāryyaṃ saptarūpañca kauśikam ॥ 2.25.36 ॥

aṅgadarśanamityāhurmāne dve samake tathā।
dvitīyabhāvācaraṇā mātrā nābhipratiṣṭitā ॥ 2.25.37 ॥




arddhena pādasāmyasya pādabhāgācca pañcake।

caturthamuttare caiva madravatyāṃ ca madrake।
madrake dakṣiṇasyāpi yathoktā varttate kalā ॥ 2.25.42 ॥

pūrvvamevānuyogantu dvitīyā buddhiriṣyate।

ekatvamupayogasya dvayoryaddhi dvijottama।

kuśatyanuttaraḥ satyaṃ sapta sattvasvaraṃ tu yaḥ ॥ 2.25.45 ॥


## 2.26

sūta uvāca।।
kakujhinastu taṃ lokaṃ raivatasya gatasya ha।











          rājovāca।।


āgate sa vikukṣau tu samāṃse sahasainike।

tatheti codito rājñā vidhivatsamupasthitaḥ।



ikṣvākustu tataḥ kruddho vikukṣimidamabravīt।


prāptaḥ paramadharmmātmā sa cāyodhyādhipo'bhavat ॥ 2.26.20 ॥




indrasya vṛṣabhūtasya kakutstho jāyate purā ॥ 2.26.24 ॥

pūrvvamāḍībake yuddhe kakutsthastena sa smṛtaḥ ।
anenāstu kakutsthasya pṛthuromā ca sa smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.26.25 ॥




         ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।

dhundhuvadhaṃ mahāprājña śrotumicchami vistarāt।

           sūta uvāca।।



prayātaṃ tamuttaṅkastu brahmarṣiḥ pratyavārayat ॥ 2.26.33 ॥

        uttaṅka uvāca।।
bhavato rakṣaṇaṃ kāryyaṃ tattāvat kartumarhati।
nirudvignastapaḥ kartuṃ na hi śaknomi pārthiva ॥ 2.26.34 ॥

mamāśramasamīpeṣu sameṣu marudhanvasu।

antarbhūmigatastatra vālukāntarhito mahān ॥ 2.26.36 ॥


saṃvatsarasya paryyante sa niḥśvāsaṃ pramuñcati।

tasya niḥśvāsavātena raja uddhūyate mahat।



lokāḥ svasthā bhavantvadya tasmin vinihate'sure ।
tvaṃ hi tasya vadhāyādya samarthaḥ pṛthivīpate ॥ 2.26.42 ॥

viṣṇunā ca varo datto mama pūrvvaṃ tato'nagha।
na hi dhundhurmahāvīryyastejasālpena śakyate ॥ 2.26.43 ॥

vīryyaṃ hi sumahattasya devairapi durāsaham ॥ 2.26.44 ॥

evamuktastu rājarṣiruttaṅkena mahātmanā।

rājā saṃnyastaśastro'hamayantu tanayo mama।

sa taṃ vyādiśya tanayaṃ dhundhumāraṇamudyatam।
jagāma parvvatāyaiva tapase śaṃsitavrataḥ ॥ 2.26.47 ॥

kubalāśvastu dharmmātmā piturvacanamāsthitaḥ।
prāyāduttaṅka sahito dhundhostasya nivāraṇe ॥ 2.26.48 ॥


tasmin prayāte durddharṣe divi śabdo mahānabhūt।
adyaprubhṛtyeṣa nṛpo dhundhumāro bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.26.50 ॥

sagatvā puruṣa vyāghrastanayaiḥ saha vīryyavān ॥ 2.26.51 ॥


babhūvātibalo bhūya uttaṅkasya vaśe sthitaḥ।
tasya putraiḥ khanadbhiśca vālukāntarhitastadā ॥ 2.26.53 ॥

mukhajenāgninā kruddho lokānudvarttayanniva ॥ 2.26.54 ॥

vāri śuśrāva yogena mahodadhirivodaye।
somasya somapaśreṣṭha dhārormikalilo mahān ॥ 2.26.55 ॥

tataḥ sa rājātibalo dhundhubandhunibarhaṇaḥ ॥ 2.26.56 ॥

tasya vārimayaṃ vegamapibat sa narādhipaḥ।



dharmme ratiñca satataṃ svarge vāsaṃ tathākṣayam।


haryaśvasya nikumbho'bhūt kṣatradharmarataḥ sadā ॥ 2.26.62 ॥


vikhyātā triṣu lokeṣu putrastasyāḥ prasenajit ॥ 2.26.64 ॥

atyantadhārmiko gaurī tasya patnī pativratā ॥ 2.26.65 ॥

tasyāstu gaurikaḥ putra ścakravarttī babhūva ha ॥ 2.26.66 ॥


yāvatsūrya udayati yāvacca pratitiṣṭhati।




purukutsamambarīṣaṃ mucukundañca viśrutam।



rāvaṇena hato yena trilokīvijaye purā ॥ 2.26.75 ॥

trasadaśvo'naraṇyasya haryyaśvastasya cātmajaḥ ।

tasya putro'bhavadrājā tridhanvā nāma dhārmmikaḥ।

tasya satyavrato nāma kumāro'bhūnmahābalaḥ।
tena bhāryyā vidarbhasya hṛtā hatvā divaukasaḥ ॥ 2.26.78 ॥



tamadharmmeṇa saṃyuktaṃ pitā trayīguṇo'tyajat।
apadhvaṃseti bahuśo'vadat krodhasamanvitaḥ ॥ 2.26.81 ॥

pitā cainamathovāca śvapākaiḥ saha varttaya ॥ 2.26.82 ॥

ityuktaḥ sa nirākrāmannagarādvacanādvibho ॥ 2.26.83 ॥

sa tu satyavrato dhīmāñchvapākāvasathāntikam।



tasya patnī gale baddhvā madhyamaṃ putramaurasam।


satyavrato mahābuddhirbharaṇaṃ tasya cākarot ।
viśvāmitrasya tuṣṭyarthamanukampārthameva ca ॥ 2.26.89 ॥

so'bhavadghālavo nāma gale baddho mahātapāḥ।

tasya vratena bhaktyā ca kṛpayā ca pratijñayā।
viśvāmitrakalatrañca babhāra vinaye sthitaḥ ॥ 2.26.91 ॥


piturniyogātabhajannṛpe tu vanamāsthite ॥ 2.26.93 ॥


satyavratastu bālyāttu bhāvino'rthasya vai balāt।


evaṃ satyavratastān vai kṛtavān saptame pade ॥ 2.26.97 ॥

jānan dharmmān vasiṣṭhastu na ca mantrānihecchati।

iti satyavrate roṣaṃ vasiṣṭo manasākarot ।। 26.98 ।।

na tu satyavrato buddhyā upāṃśuvratamasya vai ।। 26.99 ।।


kulasya niṣkṛtiḥ svasya kṛteyañca bhavediti ।। 26.101 ।।

tato vasiṣṭo bhagavān pitrā tyaktaṃ nyavārayat।





pātaye krūra he krūra tava śaṃkumayomayam ।

pituścāparitoṣeṇa gurordogdhrīvadhena ca।
aproṣitopayogācca trividhaste vyatikramaḥ ।। 26. 108 ।।





miṣatastu vasiṣṭhasya tadadbhutamivābhavat।


śanairyātyabalā ramyā hemante candramaṇḍitā।
alaṃkṛtā tribhirbhāvaistriśaṅkugrahabhūṣitā ।। 26.115 ।।



hariścandrasya tu suto rohito nāma vīryyavān।
harito rohitasyātha caṃcuhārīta ucyate ।। 26.118 ।।

vijayaśca sudevaśca caṃcuputrau babhūvatuḥ।
jetā sarvvasya kṣatrasya vijayastena sa smṛtaḥ ।। 26.119 ।।

rurukastanayastatra rājā dharmmārthakovidaḥ।

haihayaistālajaṅghaiśca nirasto vyasanī nṛpaḥ।
śakairyavanakāmbojaiḥ pāradaiḥ pahlavaistathā ।। 26.121 ।।

nātyarthaṃ dhārmmiko'bhūt sa dharmmye satyayuge tathā।
sagarastu suto bāhorjajñe saha gareṇa vai।



         ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
dharmmān kulocitān kruddho rājā nirasadacyutaḥ ।। 26.125 ।।

             sūta uvāca।।


hṛtaṃ rājyaṃ balīyobhirebhiḥ kṣatriyapuṅgavaiḥ ।
vanaṃ praviśya dharmmātmā saha patnyā tapo'carat ।। 26.128 ।।

kasyacittvatha kālasya toyārthaṃ prasthito nṛpaḥ।
vṛddhatvāddurbbalatvācca antarā sa mamāra ca ।। 26.129 ।।

sapatnyā tu garastasyai datto garbhajighāṃsayā ।। 26.130 ।।




jāmadagnyāttadāgneyamasurairapi duḥsaham।
sa tenāstrabalenaiva balena ca samanvitaḥ ।



vasiṣṭhastān tathetyuktvā samayena mahāmuniḥ ।








āseduśca tatastasmiṃstadantaste mahārṇave ।। 26.145 ।।


tasya cakṣuḥ samāsādya tejastat pratipadyate।

barhiketuḥ saketuśca tathā dharmmaratastrayaḥ।


sāgaratvaṃ ca lebhe sa karmaṇā tena tasya vai ।। 26.150 ।।



        ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।.

            sūta uvāca।।
dve patnyau sagarasyāstāṃ tapasā dagdhakilbiṣe।



munestu vacanaṃ śrutvā keśinī putramekakam।

mahātmanastu jagrāha sumatiḥ poṣaṇe nṛpaḥ ।। 26.158 ।।


sumatistvapi jajñe vai garbhantumbaṃ yaśasvinī।


tato navasu māseṣu samuttasthuryathāsukham।

kālena mahatā caiva cauvanaṃ pratipedire।

sa tu jyeṣṭo naravyāghraḥ sagarasyātmasambhavaḥ।
asamañja iti khyāto barhiketurmahābalaḥ ।। 26.164 ।।


tasya putrastu dharmātmā dilīpa iti viśrutaḥ।

ījā'nena samudrādvai duhitṛtvena kalpitā।



ambarīṣaḥ sutastasya sindhudvīpastato'bhavat।

babhūva vasudhātyarthaṃ tāpatrayavivarjitā ।। 26.171 ।।

ayutāyuḥ sutastasya sindhudvīpasya vīryavān।



sudāsastasya tanayo rājā haṃsamukho'bhavat।


aśmakasyorakāmastu mūlakastatsuto'bhavat।



putro viśvamahattasya putrīkasya vyajāyata ।। 26.180 ।।

yena svargādihāgamya muhūrttaṃ prāpya jīvitam।
trayo'bhisaṃhitā lokā buddhyā satyena caiva hi ।। 26.181 ।।

dīrgabāhuḥ sutastasya raghustasmādajāyata।


śatrughnena purī tasya mathurā sanniveśitā ।। 26.184 ।।


himavatparvatābhyāśe sphītau janapadau tayoḥ ।। 26.186 ।।

candraketostu mallasya candravaktā purī śubhā ।। 26.187 ।।

bharatasyātmajau vīrau takṣaḥ puṣkara eva ca।
gāndhāraviṣaye siddhe tayoḥ puryau mahātmanoḥ ।। 26.188 ।।








evameva mahābāhurikṣvākukulanandanaḥ ।

lavaścānyo mahāvīryastayordeśau nibodhata ।। 26.197 ।।

ramyā niveśitā tena vindhyaparvatasānuṣu ।। 26.198 ।।


kuśasya putro dharmātmā hyatithiḥ supriyātithiḥ।
atitherapi vikhyāto niṣadho nāma pārthivaḥ ।। 26.200 ।।

niṣadhasya nalaḥ putro nabhaḥ putro nalasya tu।



auṅko nāma sa dharmātmā balaputro babhūva ha।


hiraṇyanābhaḥ kauśalyo vasiṣṭhastatsuto'bhavat।
pautrasya jaimineḥ śiṣyaḥ smṛtaḥ sarveṣu śarmasu ।। 26.206 ।।

tasmādadhigato yogo yājñavalkyena dhīmatā ।। 26.207 ।।

puṣyastasya suto vidvān dhruvasandhiśca tatsutaḥ।
sudarśanastasya suto agnivarṇaḥ sudarśanāt ।। 26.208 ।।

ekonaviṃśaprayuge kṣatraprāvarttakaḥ prabhuḥ ।। 26.209 ।।

prasuśruto manoḥ putraḥ susandhistasya cātmajaḥ।



prajāvāneti sāyujyaṃ manorvaivasvatasya saḥ ।। 26.213 ।।



## 2.27

।।sūta uvāca।।
anujasya vikukṣestu nimervaṃśaṃ nibodhata।
yo'sau niveśayāmāsa purandevapuropamam ॥ 2.27.1 ॥

jayantamitivikhyātaṃ gautamasyāśramābhitaḥ।

nemirnāma sudharmātmā sarvasatvanamaskṛtaḥ।

sa śāpena vasiṣṭhasya videhaḥ samapadyata।
tasya putro mithirnāma janitaḥ parvabhistribhiḥ ॥ 2.27.4 ॥

nāmnā mithiriti khyāto jananājjanako'bhavat ॥ 2.27.5 ॥

mithirnāma mahāvīryo yenāsau mithilābhavat।

udāvasoḥ sudharmātmā janito nandivarddhanaḥ।

suketorapi dharmātmā devarāto mahābalaḥ।



haryaśvasya maruḥ putro maroḥ putraḥ pratitvakaḥ।

devamīḍhasya vibudho vibudhasya suto dhṛtiḥ ॥ 2.27.12 ॥



hrasvaromātmajo vidvān sīradhvaja iti śrutiḥ।

        śāṃśapāyana uvāca।।

           sūta uvāca।।
vidhinā suprayuktena tasmātsā tu samutthitā ॥ 2.27.17 ॥


tasya bhānumataḥ putraḥ pradyumnaśca pratāpavān।


suśrutasya jayaḥ putro jayasya vijayaḥ sutaḥ ॥ 2.27.21 ॥


ityete maithilāḥ proktāḥ somasyāpi nibodhata ॥ 2.27.23 ॥


      vāyūmahāpurāṇam -
       uttarārddham - 28
       ।।sūta uvāca ।।

so'ti tasthau sarvalokānbhagavāntsvena tejasā ।। 28.1 ।।


suduścaraṃ nāma tapo yena taptaṃ mahatpurā।

tasyorddhvaretasastatra sthitasyānimiṣaspṛham।
somatvantanurāpede mahābuddhiḥ sa vai dvijaḥ ।। 28.4 ।।

ūrddhvamācakrame tasya somatvaṃ bhāvitātmanaḥ।
somaḥ susrāva netrābhyāṃ daśa vā dyotayan diśaḥ ।। 28.5 ।।


sa tābhyaḥ sahasaivātha digbhyo garbhaḥ prabhānvitaḥ।


patantaṃ somamālokya brahmā lokapitāmahaḥ।

sa hi devamayo viprā dharmmārthī satyasaṅgaraḥ।
yukto vājisahasreṇa siteneti hi naḥ śrutam ।। 28.10 ।।

tasminnipatite devāḥ putre'treḥ paramātmani।

tatraivāṅgirasastasya bhṛgoścaivātmajastathā।
ṛgbhiryajurbhirbahubhiratharvāṅgirasairapi ।। 28.12 ।।


samena rathamukhyena sāgarāntāṃ vasundharām।

tasya yaccāpi tattejaḥ pṛthivīmanvapadyata।


sa labdhatejāstapasā saṃstavaistaiśca karmmabhiḥ।


tatastasmai dadau rājyaṃ brahmā brahmavidāṃ varaḥ।




hiraṇyagarbhaścodgātā brahmā brahmatvameyivān।
sadasyastatra bhagavān harirnārāyaṇaḥ prabhuḥ ।
sanatkumārapramukhairādyairbrahmarṣibhirvṛtaḥ ।। 28.23 ।।

tebhyo brahmarṣimukhyebhyaḥ sadasyebhyaśca vai dvijāḥ ।। 28.24 ।।



sa vibhramamatirviprā vinaye vinayo hataḥ ।। 28.27 ।।




tena snehena bhagavān rudrastasya bṛhaspateḥ।

tena brahmarṣimukhyebhyaḥ paramāstraṃ mahātmanā।

tatra tadyuddhamabhavat pratyakṣantārakāmayam।



garbhamutsṛjase na tvaṃ vipraḥ prāha bṛhaspatiḥ ।। 28.36 ।।

atho nāvasṛjattantu kumāraṃ dasyuhantamam ।। 28.37 ।।


satyaṃ brūhi sutaḥ kasya somasyātha bṛhaspateḥ ।। 28.39 ।।


yadatra tathyantad‌brūhi tāre kasya sutastvayam ।। 28.41 ।।

somasyeti mahātmānaṃ kumārandasyuhantamam ।। 28.42 ।।

budha ityakaronnāma tasya putrasya dhīmataḥ ।। 28.43 ।।

pratipūrvvañca gamane samabhyuttiṣṭhate budhaiḥ।




etatsomasya vai janma kīrttitaṃ dvijasattamāḥ।

somasya janma śrutvaiva sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ।। 28.49 ।।

      vāyūmahāpurāṇam -
        uttarārddham - 29
        sūta uvāca ।।

somasya tu budhaḥ putro budhasya tu purūravāḥ।


atīva putro lokeṣu rūpeṇāpratimo'bhavat ।। 29.3 ।।



vane caitrarathe ramye tathā mandākinītaṭe।


eteṣu vanasukhyeṣu surairācariteṣu ca।

          ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।

      sūta uvāca।।
ailaṃ tu taṃ varārohā samayena vyavasthitā ।। 29.10 ।।

anagnadarśanañcaiva akāmāta saha maithunam ।। 29.11 ।।





         gandharvvā ūcuḥ।

tayā tu samayastatra kriyamāṇo mato'naghaḥ ।। 29.17 ।।

tadahaṃ vacmi vaḥ sarvaṃ yathā tyakṣyati sā nṛpam ।। 29.18 ।।



rājānamabravītsā tu putro me hriyateti vai ।। 29.21 ।।

evamukto viniścitya nagnastiṣṭhati vai nṛpaḥ ।
nagnaṃ drakṣyati māṃ devī samayo vitatho bhavet ।। 29.22 ।।


putrau mama hṛtau rājannanāthāyā iva prabho।
evamuktastadotthāya nagno rājā pradhāvitaḥ ।। 29.24 ।।

meṣābabhyāṃ padavīṃ rājan gandharvvairvyutthitāmatha।

prakāśitantu sahasā tato nagnamavekṣya sā।

tirobhūtāntu tāṃ jñātvā gandharvāstatra tāvubhau।
meṣau tyaktvā ca te sarve tatraivāntarhitābhavan ।। 29.27 ।।



plakṣatīrthe puṣkariṇyāṃ vigāḍhenāmbunāplutām।

apaśyatsā tataḥ subhrū rājānamavidūrataḥ।

tata āvirbabhūvustāḥ pañcacūḍāpsarāstu tāḥ ।। 29.32 ।।

dṛṣṭvā tu rājā tāṃ prītaḥ pralāpān kurute bahūn।


niśāmekāntu vai rājā hyavasattu tayā saha ।। 29.35 ।।




vṛṇe nityaṃ hi sālokyaṃ gandharvāṇāṃ mahātmanām।
tathetyuktvā varaṃ vavre gandharvāśca tathāstviti ।। 29.39 ।।

sthālīmagneḥ pūrayitvā gandharvvāśca tamabruvan।

tamādāya kumārantu nagarāyopacakrame।
niḥkṣipya tamaraṇyāñca sa putrantu gṛhaṃ yayau ।। 29.41 ।।

punarādāya dṛśyāgnimaśvatthaṃ tatra dṛṣṭavān।

gandharvvebhyastathākhyātumagninā gāṃ gatastu saḥ ।


vāsāya ca sa gandharvvastretāyāṃ sa mahārathaḥ।

deśe puṇyatame caiva maharṣibhiralaṃkṛte ।। 29.46 ।।


tasya putrā babhūvurhi ṣaḍindropamatejasaḥ।



suhotrasyābhajjahnuḥ keśikāgarbhasambhavaḥ।
pratigatya tato gaṅgā vitato yajñakarmmaṇi ।। 29.51 ।।


sauhotrirvaradaḥ kruddho gaṅgāṃ saṃraktalocanaḥ।
asya gaṅge'valepasya sadyaḥ phalamavāpnuhi ।। 29.53 ।।

etatte viphalaṃ sarvvaṃ pītamambhaḥ karomyaham।



jahnuśca dayitaṃ putraṃ suhotraṃ nāma dhārmikam।



kuśastambastapastepe putrārthī rājasattamaḥ।
pūrṇe varṣasahasre vai śatakratumapaśyata ।। 29.60 ।।


putratvaṃ kalpayāmāsa svayameva purandaraḥ।

paurukutsābhavadbhāryā gādhistasyāmajāyata।

tasyāṃ putrastu vai bharttā bhārgavo bhṛgunandanaḥ।
putrārthe sādhayāmāsa caruṃ gādhestathaiva ca ।। 29.64 ।।


ajeyaḥ kṣatriyairyuddhe kṣatriyarṣabhasūdanaḥ ।। 29.66 ।।


tapasyābhirato nityamaraṇyaṃ praviveśa ha ।। 29.68 ।।


bhartturvacanamavyagrā hṛṣṭā mātre nyavedayat ।। 29.70 ।।

mātā tu tasyai daivena duhitre svaṃ caruṃ dadau ।

atha satyavatī garbhaṃ kṣatriyāntakaraṃ śubham।


mātuḥ siddhyati te bhadre caruvyatyāsahetunā।

viśvaṃ hi brahma tapasā mayā tatra samarpitam ।। 29.75 ।।

brāhmaṇāpasadastvanya ityukto munirabravīt ।। 29.76 ।।


punaḥ satyavatī vākyamevamuktābravīdidam।

kāmamevaṃvidhaḥ putro mama syāttu vada prabho ।। 29.79 ।।

mayyanyathā na śakyaṃ vai kartumeva dvijottama।
tataḥ prasādamakarot sa tasyāstapaso balāt ।। 29.80 ।।

putre nāsti viśeṣo me pautre vā varavarṇini।
tvayā yathoktaṃ vacanaṃ tathā bhadre bhaviṣyati ।। 29.81 ।।

tapasyabhiratandāntaṃ jamadagniḥ śamātmakam ।। 29.82 ।।

yamanādvaiṣṇavasyāgnerjamadagnirajāyata ।। 29.83 ।।






jamadagnistato vīryyājjajñe brahmavidāṃ varaḥ।


viśvāmitrasya putrastu śunaḥśepho'bhavanmuniḥ।
hariścandrasya yajñe tu paśutve niyutaḥ savai।
devairdattaḥ sa vai yasmāddevarātastato'bhavat ।। 29.91 ।।

madhucchando nayaścaiva kṛtadevau dhruvāṣṭakau ।। 29.92 ।।


udumbarā udumlānāstārakā yamamuñcatāḥ ।। 29.94 ।।

babhravaḥ pāṇinaścaiva dhyāvajapyāstathaiva ca ।। 29.95 ।।



pauroravasya puṇyasya brahmarṣeḥ kauśikasya tu ।। 29.98 ।।

aṣṭakasya suto yo hi prokto jahnugaṇomayā ।। 29.99 ।।

        ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।



evamuktastato vākyamabravīdidamarthavat।
anyāyopagatairdravyairāhṛtya yajane dhiyā।
dharmābhikāṃkṣī yajate na dharmaphalamaśnute ।। 29.102 ।।



tena dattāni dānāni aphalāni bhavantyuta।
tasya dharmmapravṛttasya hiṃsakasya durātmanaḥ ।। 29.105 ।।

evaṃ labdhvā dhanaṃ mohāddadato yajataśca ha।

kāmānanabhisandhāya yajate ca dadāti ca ।। 29.107 ।।

sa dānaphalamāpnoti tacca dānaṃ sukhodayam।
dānena bhogānāpnoti svargaṃ satyena gacchati ।। 29.108 ।।




kapeśca purukutsaśca satyaścānṛhavānṛthuḥ

kakṣīvaścaiva śijayastathānye ca mahārathāḥ।

## 2.29

sūta uvāca ।।

mātuḥ siddhyati te bhadre caruvyatyāsahetunā।

viśvaṃ hi brahma tapasā mayā tatra samarpitam ॥ 2.29.75 ॥

brāhmaṇāpasadastvanya ityukto munirabravīt ॥ 2.29.76 ॥


punaḥ satyavatī vākyamevamuktābravīdidam।

kāmamevaṃvidhaḥ putro mama syāttu vada prabho ॥ 2.29.79 ॥

mayyanyathā na śakyaṃ vai kartumeva dvijottama।
tataḥ prasādamakarot sa tasyāstapaso balāt ॥ 2.29.80 ॥

putre nāsti viśeṣo me pautre vā varavarṇini।
tvayā yathoktaṃ vacanaṃ tathā bhadre bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.29.81 ॥

tapasyabhiratandāntaṃ jamadagniḥ śamātmakam ॥ 2.29.82 ॥

yamanādvaiṣṇavasyāgnerjamadagnirajāyata ॥ 2.29.83 ॥






jamadagnistato vīryyājjajñe brahmavidāṃ varaḥ।


viśvāmitrasya putrastu śunaḥśepho'bhavanmuniḥ।
hariścandrasya yajñe tu paśutve niyutaḥ savai।
devairdattaḥ sa vai yasmāddevarātastato'bhavat ॥ 2.29.91 ॥

madhucchando nayaścaiva kṛtadevau dhruvāṣṭakau ॥ 2.29.92 ॥


udumbarā udumlānāstārakā yamamuñcatāḥ ॥ 2.29.94 ॥

babhravaḥ pāṇinaścaiva dhyāvajapyāstathaiva ca ॥ 2.29.95 ॥



pauroravasya puṇyasya brahmarṣeḥ kauśikasya tu ॥ 2.29.98 ॥

aṣṭakasya suto yo hi prokto jahnugaṇomayā ॥ 2.29.99 ॥

        ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।



evamuktastato vākyamabravīdidamarthavat।
anyāyopagatairdravyairāhṛtya yajane dhiyā।
dharmābhikāṃkṣī yajate na dharmaphalamaśnute ॥ 2.29.102 ॥



tena dattāni dānāni aphalāni bhavantyuta।
tasya dharmmapravṛttasya hiṃsakasya durātmanaḥ ॥ 2.29.105 ॥

evaṃ labdhvā dhanaṃ mohāddadato yajataśca ha।

kāmānanabhisandhāya yajate ca dadāti ca ॥ 2.29.107 ॥

sa dānaphalamāpnoti tacca dānaṃ sukhodayam।
dānena bhogānāpnoti svargaṃ satyena gacchati ॥ 2.29.108 ॥




kapeśca purukutsaśca satyaścānṛhavānṛthuḥ

kakṣīvaścaiva śijayastathānye ca mahārathāḥ।



## 2.30

sūta uvāca।।



putro gṛtsamadasyāpi śunako yasya śaunakaḥ।



dharmmaśca dīrghatapaso vidvān dhanvantaristataḥ।

       ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।

kathaṃ dhanvantarirddevo mānuṣeṣviha jajñivān।
etadveditumicchāmastato brūhi priyaṃ tathā ॥ 2.30.8 ॥

           sūta uvāca।।

ajastvamiti hovāca tasmādajastu saḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.30.10 ॥

vidhatsva bhāgaṃ sthānañca mama loke surottama ॥ 2.30.11 ॥

kṛto yajñavibhāgastu yajñiyaurhe suraistathā ॥ 2.30.12 ॥

vedeṣu vidhiyuktañca vidhihotraṃ maharṣibhiḥ।
na śakyamiha homo vai tulyaṃ karttuṃ kadācana ॥ 2.30.13 ॥

arvāksuto'si he deva nāmamantro'si vai prabho।

aṇimādiyutā siddhirgarbhasthasya bhaviṣyati।
tenaiva ca śarīreṇa devatvaṃ prāpsyasi prabho।
cārumantrairghṛtairgandhairyakṣyanti tvāṃ dvijātayaḥ ॥ 2.30.15 ॥

atha ca tvaṃ punaścaiva āyurvedaṃ vidhāsyasi।

tasmāt tasmai varaṃ dattvā viṣṇurantardaghe tataḥ ॥ 2.30.17 ॥

putrakāma stapastepe nṛpo dīrghatapāstathā ॥ 2.30.18 ॥

vareṇa cchandayāmāsa prīto dhanvantarirnṛpam ॥ 2.30.19 ॥

        nṛpa uvāca।।
bhagavan yadi tuṣṭastvaṃ putro me dhṛtimān bhava।
tatheti samanujñāya tatraivāntaradhīyata ॥ 2.30.20 ॥

tasya gehe samutpanno devo dhanvantaristadā।


dhanvantarisutaścāpi ketumāniti viśrutaḥ।
atha ketumataḥ putro vibho bhīmaratho nṛpaḥ।
divodāsa iti khyāto vārāṇasyadhipo'bhavat ॥ 2.30.23 ॥




          ṛṣa ūcuḥ।।

             sūta uvāca।।

etasminneva kāle tu kṛtadāro maheśvaraḥ।


jugupsate sā nityañca devaṃ devīṃ tathaiva ca ॥ 2.30.31 ॥

daridraḥ sarva eveha akliṣṭaṃ laḍate'naghe ॥ 2.30.32 ॥



tathoktastu mahādevaḥ sarvāṃllokānavekṣya ha।






pūjā tu mahatī caiva nityameva prayujyate।
gandhairdhūpaiśca mālyaiśca prekṣaṇīyaistathaiva ca ॥ 2.30.41 ॥

annapradānayuktaiśca atyadbhutamivābhavat।
evaṃ sampūjyate tatra nityameva gaṇeśvaraḥ ॥ 2.30.42 ॥




na prayacchati putrāṃstu nikumbhaḥ kāraṇena tu।
rājā yadi tat krudhyeta tataḥ kiñcit pravarttate ॥ 2.30.46 ॥



na dadāti ca putraṃ me kṛtaghno bahubhojanaḥ ॥ 2.30.49 ॥






ramate tatra vai devī ramamāṇe maheśvaraḥ ॥ 2.30.55 ॥

na ratiṃ tatra vai devī labhate gṛhavismayāt।

prahasyaināmathovāca avimuktaṃ hi me gṛham ॥ 2.30.57 ॥

nāhaṃ devi gamiṣyāmi gacchasveha ramāmyaham।
tasmāttadavimuktaṃ hi proktaṃ devena vai svayam ॥ 2.30.58 ॥

yasmin vasati vai devaḥ sarvadevanamaskṛtaḥ।

antarddhānaṃ kalau yāti tatpurantu mahātmanaḥ।
antarhite pure tasmin purī sā vasate punaḥ ॥ 2.30.60 ॥



bhadraśreṇyasya putrastu durdamo nāma nāmataḥ।

tena putreṇa bālena prahṛtaṃ tasya vai punaḥ ॥ 2.30.64 ॥

pratarddanasya putrau dvau vatso gargaśca viśrutaḥ ॥ 2.30.65 ॥

vatsaputro hyalarkastu sannatistasya cātmajaḥ ।





suketutanayaścāpi dharmaketuriti śrutiḥ।
dharmaketostu dāyādaḥ satyaketurmahārathaḥ ॥ 2.30.71 ॥

satyaketusutaścāpi vibhurnāma prajeśvaraḥ।
suvibhustu vibhoḥ putraḥ sukumārastataḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.30.72 ॥


gārgyasya gargabhūmistu vātsyo vatsasya dhīmataḥ ॥ 2.30.74 ॥


ityete kāśyapāḥ proktā rajerapi nibodhata।
rājeyamiti vikhyātaṃ kṣatramindrabhayāvaham ॥ 2.30.76 ॥

tadā daivāsure yuddhe samutpanne sudāruṇe।

āvayorbhagavān yuddhe vijetā ko bhaviṣyati।
brūhi naḥ sarvvalokeśa śrotumicchāmahe vayam ॥ 2.30.78 ॥

       brahmovāca।।

rajiryatastato lakṣmīryato lakṣmīstato dhṛtiḥ।
yato dhṛtistato dharmo yato dharmastato jayaḥ ॥ 2.30.80 ॥

abhyayurjayamicchantaḥ stuvanto rājasattamam ॥ 2.30.81 ॥


       rajiruvāca।।
indro bhavāmi dharmātmā tato yotsyāmi saṃyuge ॥ 2.30.83 ॥

         dānavā ūcuḥ।।
asmākamindraḥ prahlādastasyārthe vijayāmahe।

sa tatheti bruvanneva devairapyabhicoditaḥ।
bhaviṣyasīndro jitveti devairapi nimantritaḥ ॥ 2.30.85 ॥


taṃ tathā tu rajhiṃ tatra devaissaha śatakratuḥ ॥ 2.30.87 ॥

rajiputro'hamityuktvā punarevābravīdvacaḥ।
yasyāhamindraputraste khyātiṃ yāsyāmi śatruhan ॥ 2.30.88 ॥



samakrāmanta bahudhā svargalokaṃ triviṣṭapam ॥ 2.30.91 ॥



hataujā durbalo mūḍho rajiputraiḥ prasīda me ॥ 2.30.94 ॥

        bṛhaspatiruvāca।।

prayatiṣyāmi devendra taddhitārthaṃ mahādyute।

tataḥ karmma cakārāsya tejaḥsaṃvarddhanaṃ mahat ॥ 2.30.97 ॥

teṣāñca buddhisaṃmohamakarodbuddhisattamaḥ।

tato lebhe suraiśvaryamaindrasthānaṃ tathottamam ॥ 2.30.99 ॥



## 2.31

।।ṛṣayaḥ ūcuḥ।।

         ।।sūta uvāca।।
āhavan taṃ marutsomamannakāmaḥ prajeśvaram।

tena te marutastasya marutsomena toṣitāḥ।

annaṃ tasya sakṛtpakvamahorātre na kṣīyate।


yatidharmamavāpyeha brahmabhūyāya te gatāḥ ॥ 2.31.6 ॥

anapāyastato jātastadā dharmapradattavān।

sañjayasya jayaḥ putro vijayastasya jagmivān ॥ 2.31.8 ॥

vijayasya jayaḥ putrastasya haryandvataḥ smṛtaḥ ।
haryandutastato rājā sahadevaḥ pratāpavān ॥ 2.31.9 ॥

sahadevasya dharmātmā adīna iti viśrutaḥ।
adīnasya jayatsenastasya putro'tha saṃkṛtiḥ ॥ 2.31.10 ॥

ityete kṣatra dharmāṇo nahuṣasya nibodhata ॥ 2.31.11 ॥





yaduṃ ca turvasuṃ caiva devayānirvyajāyata ॥ 2.31.16 ॥


rathantasmai dadau rudraḥ prītaḥ paramabhāsvaram।

yuktaṃ mano javairaśvairyena kanyāṃ samudvahat।
sa tena rathamukhyena jigāya ca tato mahīm ॥ 2.31.19 ॥

yayātiryudhi durddharṣo devadānavamānavaiḥ ।

yovatsudeśaprabhavaḥ kauravo janamejayaḥ।

durbuddhirhiṃsayāmāsa lohagandhaṃ narādhipam ॥ 2.31.22 ॥

sa lohagandho rājarṣiḥ paridhāvannitastataḥ।
paurajānapadaistyakto na lebhe śarma karhicit ॥ 2.31.23 ॥

tataḥ sa duḥkhasantapto nālabhatsaṃvidaṃ kvacit।

yojayāmāsa cendrotaḥ śaunako janamejayam।

sa lohagandho vyanaśattasyāvasathametya ha।
sa ca divyo rathastasmādvasoścedipatestathā ॥ 2.31.26 ॥

tato hatvā jarāsandhaṃ bhīmastaṃ rathamuttamam।
pradadau vāsudevāya prītyā kauravanandanaḥ ॥ 2.31.27 ॥



tvaṃ yado pratipadyasva pāpmānaṃ jarayā saha।


tasmājjarānna te rājan grahītumahamutsahe ॥ 2.31.32 ॥


aśaktaḥ kāryakaraṇe paribhūtastu yauvane।

santi te bahavaḥ putrā mattaḥ priyatarā nṛpa।

sa evamukto yadunā tīvrakopasamanvitaḥ।

māmanādṛtya durbuddhe yadāttha navadeśika ॥ 2.31.37 ॥

yastvaṃ me hṛdayājjāto vayaḥ svaṃ na prayacchasi ॥ 2.31.38 ॥

turvaso pratipadyasva pāpmānaṃ jarayā saha ॥ 2.31.39 ॥

        turvasuruvāca।।
tasmājjarāṃ na te rājan grahītumahamutsahe ॥ 2.31.40 ॥

       yayātiruvāca।।
yastvaṃ me hṛdayājjāto vayaḥ svanna prayacchasi।
tasmāt prajā samucchedaṃ turvaso tava yāsyati ॥ 2.31.41 ॥


guru dāraprasakteṣu tiryagyonigateṣu vā।

          sūta uvāca।

druhyo tvaṃ pratipadyasva varṇarūpavināśinīm।
jarāṃ varṣasahasraṃ vai yauvanaṃ svandadasva me ॥ 2.31.45 ॥

pūrṇe varṣasahasre te pratidāsyāmi yauvanam।

       druhyuruvāca।।


              yayātiruvāca।।
yastvaṃ me hṛdayājjāto vayaḥ svanna prayacchasi।
tasmād druhyo priyaḥ kāmo na te sampatsyate kvacit ॥ 2.31.48 ॥

nauplavottarasañcārastatra nityaṃ bhaviṣyati।

ano tvaṃ pratipadyasva pāpmānaṃ jarayā saha।
evaṃ varṣasahasrantu careyaṃ yauvanena te ॥ 2.31.50 ॥

       anuruvāca।।

          yayātiruvāca।।
yastvaṃ me hṛdayājjāto vayaḥ svanna prayacchasi।
jarādoṣastvayokto'yaṃ tasmātte pratipatsyate ॥ 2.31.52 ॥

agnipraskandanaparastvaṃ cāpyeva bhaviṣyasi ॥ 2.31.53 ॥




             sūta uvāca।।
evamuktaḥ pratyuvāca putraḥ pitaramañjasā।
yathānumanyase tāta kariṣyāmi tathaiva ca ॥ 2.31.57 ॥


jarayāhaṃ praticchanno vayorūpadharastava।
yauvanaṃ bhavate dattvā cariṣyāmi yathārtavat ॥ 2.31.59 ॥

          yayātiruvāca।।

           sūta uvāca।।


dharmmāvirodhādrājendro yathārhati sa eva hi ॥ 2.31.63 ॥

devānatarpayadyajñaiḥ pitṝñchrāddhaistathaiva ca।


dharmmeṇa ca prajāḥ sarvvā yathāvadanurañjayan।

avirodhena dharmasya cacāra sukhamuttamam ॥ 2.31.67 ॥

viśvāsaheto reme vai vaibrāje nandane vane ॥ 2.31.68 ॥




sevitā viṣayāḥ putra yauvanena mayā tava ॥ 2.31.72 ॥





yadurjyeṣṭhastava suto jātastamanu turvasuḥ।


          yayātiruvāca।।

mātā pitrorvacanakṛtsa hi putraḥ praśasyate ।


yadunāhamavajñātastathā turvasunāpi ca।


putro yastvānuvartteta sa rājā te mahāmate ॥ 2.31.84 ॥


varadānena śukrasya na śakyaṃ vaktumuttaram ॥ 2.31.86 ॥




yathāpradeśaṃ dharmajñairdharmmeṇa pratipālyate ॥ 2.31.90 ॥





nālamekasya tatsarvamiti paśyanna muhyati ॥ 2.31.95 ॥


yadā parānna bibheti yadā tvasmānna bibhyati।
yadā necchati na dveṣṭi brahma sampadyate tadā ॥ 2.31.97 ॥

yā dustyajā durmmatibhiryā na jīryati jīryyataḥ।


yaccākāmasukhaṃ loke yacca divyaṃ mahat sukham।

pālayitvā vrataśataṃ tatraiva svargamāpnuyāt ॥ 2.31.101 ॥




## 2.32

।।sūrya uvāca।।
vistareṇānupūrvyeṇa gadato me nibodhata ॥ 2.32.1 ॥



haihayasya tu dāyādo dharmmatattva iti śrutiḥ ॥ 2.32.4 ॥

dharmmatantrastu kīrttistu saṃjñeyastasya cātmajaḥ।


durmadasya tato dhīmān kanko nāma viśrutaḥ ॥ 2.32.7 ॥



dattamārādhayāmāsa kārttavīryo'trisambhavam ॥ 2.32.10 ॥

tasmai datto varān prādāccaturo bhūritejasaḥ।
pūrvvaṃ bāhusahasrantu sa vavre prathamaṃ varam ॥ 2.32.11 ॥

adharmme dīyamānasya sadbhistasmānnivāraṇam।



tasya bāhusahasrantu yuddhyataḥ kila dhīmataḥ।
yauddho dhvajo rathaścaiva prādurbhavati māyayā ॥ 2.32.15 ॥



gandharvvairapsarobhiśca nityamevopaśobhitāḥ ॥ 2.32.18 ॥


yajñairdānaistapobhiśca vikrameṇa śrutena ca ॥ 2.32.20 ॥



sapta sapta vārān samrāṭ cakravarttī babhūva ha ॥ 2.32.23 ॥

sa eva paśupālo'bhūt kṣetrapālastathaiva ca।
sa eva vṛṣṭyā parjanyo yogitvādarjjuno'bhavat ॥ 2.32.24 ॥

sa vai bāhusahasreṇa jyāghātakaṭinena ca ।




purā sa tāmanusarannavagāḍho mahārṇavam।
cakāroddhṛttya velāntaṃ sa kālaḥ prāvṛṇodvanam ॥ 2.32.29 ॥


patitā viddhaphenaughamāvarttakṣiptadussaham ॥ 2.32.31 ॥





tato gatvā pulastyastu arjunaṃ ca prasādayat।
mumoca rājā paulastyaṃ pulastyenānupālitam ॥ 2.32.36 ॥

tasya bāhusahasrasya babhūva jyātalasvanaḥ।

aho mṛdhe mahāvīryaṃ bhārgavo yasya so'cchinat।





saṃlebhe varuṇaḥ putraṃ purā bhāsvinamuttamam।

yasmānna varjitamidaṃ vanaṃ te mama haihaya ॥ 2.32.44 ॥

tasmāt te duṣkaraṃ karmma kṛtamanyo haniṣyati।
arjuno nāma kaunteyo na ca rājā bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.32.45 ॥

chittvā bāhusahasraṃ vai pramathya tarasā balīṃ ॥ 2.32.46 ॥




jayadhvajasya putrastu tālajaṅghaḥ pratāpavān ॥ 2.32.50 ॥



durjayastasya putrastu babhūvāmitradarśanaḥ ॥ 2.32.53 ॥


kārtavīryasya yo janma kathayediha dhīmataḥ ॥ 2.32.55 ॥

vittavān bhavatyatraiva dharmmaścāsya vivarddhate।


## 2.33

ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
kimarthaṃ bhuvanaṃ dagdhamapavasyaṃ mahātmanām।


       sūta uvāca।।

         rājovāca।।
bhagavan kena te tuṣṭirbhavedbrūhi divākara।

         sūrya uvāca।।
tena tṛpto bhaveyaṃ vai na tuṣye'nyena pārthiva ॥ 2.33.5 ॥

          rājovāca ।।


          āditya uvāca।।


tataḥ śarānathādityastvarjunāya prayacchati।
tataḥ saṃprāpya sumahatsthāvaraṃ sarvvameva hi ॥ 2.33.9 ॥

tapovanāni ramyāṇi vanānyupavanāni ca ॥ 2.33.10 ॥


etasminneva kāle tu apo nilayamāśritaḥ।


           sūta uvāca।।


ghṛtamprasūtamicchanti raśādoragryamātmajam ॥ 2.33.16 ॥

citraiścitrarathastasya putra karmmabhiranvitaḥ ॥ 2.33.17 ॥


cakravarttī mahāsattvo mahāvīryo bahuprajaḥ।



antaraḥ sa purā yastu yajñasya tanayo'bhavat ॥ 2.33.22 ॥

uśanā sutadharmmātmā avāpya pṛthivīmimām।

vīraḥ kambalabarhistu maruttatanayaḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.33.24 ॥

putrastu rukmakavaco vidvān kambalavarhiṣaḥ।
nihatya rukmakavacaḥ purā kavacino raṇe ॥ 2.33.25 ॥



parighañca hariñcaiva videhe sthāpayatpitā ॥ 2.33.28 ॥

tebhyaḥ pravrajito rājyā jjyāmagho'bhavadāśrame ॥ 2.33.29 ॥





yaste janiṣyate putrastasya bhāryā bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.33.34 ॥


putrau vidarbho'janayacchūrau raṇaviśāradau ॥ 2.33.36 ॥


krathorvidarbhaputrastu kuntistasyātmajo'bhavat ॥ 2.33.38 ॥


tasya putro daśārhastu mahābalaparākramaḥ।
daśarhasya suto vyomā tato jīmūta ucyate ॥ 2.33.40 ॥

atha bhīmarathasyāsīt putro rathavaraḥ kila ॥ 2.33.41 ॥

tasya putro navarathastato daśarathaḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.33.42 ॥

tasmāt karambhako dhanvī devarāto'bhavattataḥ ॥ 2.33.43 ॥

devakṣatrasuto jajñe devanaḥ kṣatranandanaḥ ॥ 2.33.44 ॥

devanāt sa madhurjajñe yasya medhārthasambhavaḥ।


jajñe purudvataḥ putro bhadravatyāṃ purūdvahaḥ।



## 2.34

sūta uvāca।।


śrṛñjayasya sute dve tu bāhyakaste udāvahat ॥ 2.34.3 ॥

tasya bhārye bhaginyau te prāsūteti sutān bahūn।

ayutāyutasāhasra śatajidatha vāmakaḥ ॥ 2.34.5 ॥


putraḥ sarvaguṇopeto mama bhūyāditi sma ha।

kalyāṇañca narapatestasya sā nimnagottamā ॥ 2.34.8 ॥


bhavetsarvaguṇopeto rājño devāvṛdhasya hi।
tasmādasya svayaṃ cāhaṃ bhavāmyadya sahavratā।
jajñe tasyāḥ svayaṃ hasto bhāvastasya yatheritaḥ ॥ 2.34.10 ॥


tasyāmādhatta garbhaṃ sa tejasvinamudāradhīḥ।







anamitraṃ sutañcaiva tāvubhau puruṣottamau।
anamitrasuto nighno nighnasya dvau babhūvatuḥ ॥ 2.34.19 ॥




atha rājā vivasvantamuvāca sthitamagrataḥ।
yathaiva vyomni paśyāmi tvāmahaṃ jyotiṣāmpate ॥ 2.34.23 ॥

tejomaṇḍalinañcaiva tathaivāpyagrataḥ sthitam।

etacchrutvā sa bhagavān maṇiratnaṃ syamantakam।

tato vigrahavantaṃ taṃ dadarśa nṛpatistadā।

tamatiprasthitaṃ bhūyo vivasvantaṃ sa śakrajit।

sa tamābaddhya nagaraṃ praviveśa mahīpatiḥ ॥ 2.34.28 ॥


taṃ prasenijite divyaṃ maṇiratnaṃ syamantakam।
dadau bhrātre narapatiḥ premṇā śakrajiduttamam ॥ 2.34.30 ॥

syamantako nāma maṇiryasya rāṣṭre sthito bhevat ।

lipsāṃ cakre prasenāttu maṇiratnaṃ syamantakam।
govindo na ca taṃ lebhe śakto'pi na jahāra ca ॥ 2.34.32 ॥





sa tu prasenamṛgayāmacarattatra cāpyatha।

ṛkṣavantaṃ girivaraṃ vindhyañca nagamuttamam।




         ।।dhātryuvāca।।
sukumāraka mārodīstava hyeṣa syamantakaḥ ॥ 2.34.42 ॥

apaśyacca vilābhyāśe prasenamavadāritam॥ 2.34.43 ॥


yuyudhe vāsudevastu bile jāmbavatā saha।
bāhubhyāmeva govindo divasānekaviṃśatim ॥ 2.34.45 ॥


vāsudevastu nirjitya jāmbavantaṃ mahābalam।

bhagavattejasā grasto jāmbavān prasabhaṃ maṇim।

maṇiṃ syamantakaṃ caiva jagrāhātma viśuddhaye।

dadau satrājite taṃ vai maṇiṃ sātvatasannidhau ॥ 2.34.50 ॥


veda mithyābhiśasteḥ sa nābhiśasyati karhicit ॥ 2.34.52 ॥

daśa svasṛbhyo bhāryyābhyaḥ śatrajittaḥ śataṃ sutāḥ।
vīro vratapatiścaiva hyapasvāntaśca supriyaḥ ॥ 2.34.53 ॥



yattat satrājite kṛṣṇo maṇiratnaṃ syamantakam।

akrūro ratnamanvicchan maṇiñcaiva syamantakam ॥ 2.34.57 ॥


samayaṃ kāraṇaṃ cakre bodhyo nānyaistvayetyuta ॥ 2.34.59 ॥


prayayau rathamāruhya nagaraṃ vāraṇāvatam ॥ 2.34.61 ॥

satyabhāmā tu tadvṛttaṃ bhojasya śatadhanvanaḥ।



hataḥ prasenaḥ siṃhena satrājicchatadhanvanā।
syamantakamahaṃ mārge tasya prahara he prabho ॥ 2.34.65 ॥






pravṛddhavegā vaḍavā tvadhvanāṃ śatayojanam।
dṛṣṭā rathagatistasya śatadhanvānamarddayat ॥ 2.34.71 ॥

tatastasya hayāste tu śramāt khedācca vai dvijāḥ।


padbhyāmeva tato gatvā śatadhanvānamacyutaḥ।
mithilādhipatiṃ taṃ vai jaghāna paramāstravit ॥ 2.34.74 ॥

syamantakaṃ na cāpaśyaddhatvā bhojaṃ mahābalam।

dhikchabdamasakṛt pūrvvaṃ pratyuvāca janārddanam ॥ 2.34.76 ॥


praviveśa tato rāmo mithilāmarimarddanaḥ।
sarvvakāmairupahṛtairmaithilenaiva pūjitaḥ ॥ 2.34.78 ॥

etasminneva kāle tu babhrurmatimatāṃvaraḥ।




atha duryyodhano rājā gatvā'tha mithilāṃ prabhuḥ।

yuddhe hatvā tu śatrugnaṃ saha bandhumatā balī ॥ 2.34.84 ॥

bhaṅgakārasya tanayo viśrutau sumahābalau ॥ 2.34.85 ॥

jajñāte'ndhakamukhyasya śatrughno bandhumāṃśca tau।



pravavarṣa sahasrākṣaḥ kukṣau jalanidhestataḥ ॥ 2.34.89 ॥



yacca ratnaṃ maṇivaraṃ tava hastagataṃ prabho।



tata ārjjavasaṃprāptababhruhastādarindamaḥ।


veda mithyābhi śastiṃ sa na vrajecca kathañcana ॥ 2.34.97 ॥


satyavāk satyasampannaḥ satyakastasya cātmajaḥ।
sātyakiryuyudhānasya tasya bhūtiḥ suto'bhavat ॥ 2.34.99 ॥


śvaphalkastu mahārājo dharmātmā yatra varttate।




vasati sma na vai jajñe sarbhasthāntāṃ pitābravīt ॥ 2.34.105 ॥

provāca cainaṃ garbha sthā sā kanyā gaurdine dine ॥ 2.34.106 ॥

yadi dattā tadā syāṃ hi yadi syāmīhatāṃ pitaḥ।



dharmabhṛcca śrṛṣṭacayo vargamocastathāparaḥ।

akrūrādugrasenyāntu sutau dvau kulanandinau।

citrakasyābhavan putrāḥ pṛthurvipṛthureva ca।



kapotaromā tasyātha revato'bhavadātmajaḥ ॥ 2.34.115 ॥

tasyāsīttumburusakhā vidvān putro'bhavatkila।
khyāyate yasya nāmnā sa candanodakadundubhiḥ ॥ 2.34.116 ॥

tasmāccābhijitaḥ putra utpannastu punarvasuḥ।
aśvamedhantu putrārthe ājahāra narottamaḥ ॥ 2.34.117 ॥

tasya madhye'tirātrasya sadomadhyātsamutthitam।
tatastu vidvān dharmajño dātā yajvā punarvasuḥ ॥ 2.34.118 ॥





aśītiyuktaniyutānyāhukapratimo'vrajat ॥ 2.34.123 ॥



āhukāndhasya duhitā dvau putrau sambabhūvatuḥ ॥ 2.34.126 ॥

devakaścograsenaśca devagarbhasamāvubhau ।

devānāmapi devaśca sudevo devarañjitā।





ugraseno mahāpatyo vikhyātaḥ kukurodbhavaḥ।

bhajamānasya putrastu rathimukhyo vidūrathaḥ।



svayambhojaḥ svayambhojāddhṛdikaḥ sambabhūva ha।


sudāntaśca dhiyāntaśca nakavān kanakodbhavaḥ।
devārhasya suto vidvān jajñe kambalabarhiṣaḥ ॥ 2.34.139 ॥

ajāvaputrāya tataḥ pradadāvasamaujase।




jajñe tasya prasūtasya dundubhiḥ prāṇadaddivi।

manuṣyaloke kṛtsne'pi rūpe nāsti samo bhuvi ॥ 2.34.145 ॥








nakulaḥ sahadevaśca rūpasattvaguṇānvitau ॥ 2.34.153 ॥


cekitānabṛhatkṣatrau tathaivānyau mahābalau ॥ 2.34.155 ॥

vindānuvindāvāvantyau bhrātarau sumahābalau।


yaduśravānujastasya rujakanyo'nujastathā।
patnyastu vasudevasya trayodaśa varāṅganāḥ ॥ 2.34.158 ॥


sugandhirvanarājī ca dve cānye paricārike।।



pautrau rāmasya jajñāte vijñātau niśitotsukau।

mandabāhyo'tha rāmāṇagirikau gira eva ca।
śuklagulmeti gulmaśca daridrāntaka eva ca ॥ 2.34.164 ॥


bhadrāśvo bhadraguptiśca bhadravighnastathaiva ca ॥ 2.34.166 ॥

bhadrabāhurbhadraratho bhadrakalpastathaiva ca।

nandopanantadau mitraśca kukṣimitrastathācalaḥ ॥ 2.34.168 ॥

citropa citre kanye ca sthitaḥ puṣṭirathāparaḥ।
madirāyāḥ sutā hyete sudevo'tha vijajñire ॥ 2.34.169 ॥

upabimbo'tha bimbaśca sattvadantamahaujasau।


tadayo bhadrasenaśca yajudāyaśca pañcamaḥ।



vasudevasya bhāryāsu mahābhāgāsu saptasu।

ato'sya saha devāyāṃ śūro jajñe'bhayāsakhaḥ।

upasaṅgaṃ vasuñcāpi tanayau devarakṣitau।

vijayaṃ rojanañcaiva varddhamānaṃ tathaiva ca।
etān sarvvān mahābhāgānupadevā vyajāyata ॥ 2.34.178 ॥

āgāhī ca svasā caiva surūpā śiśirāyiṇī ॥ 2.34.179 ॥


śaibyāyāmadadacchauriḥ putraṃ kauśikamavyayam ॥ 2.34.181 ॥

puṇḍraśca kapilaścaiva vasudevātmajau hi tau।

tasyāṃ samabhavadvīro vasudevātmajo balī।

vikhyāto devarātasya mahābhāgaḥ suto'bhavat।




tantijastantimālaśca svaputrau kanakasya tu।
vastāvanestvaputrāya vasudevaḥ pratāpavān।

tapāśca kodhanuścaiva virajāḥ śyāmasṛñjimau।
anapatyo'bhavacchyāmaḥ śyāmakastu vanaṃ yayau।




avyakto'vyaktaliṅgasthaḥ sa eva bhagavān prabhuḥ ॥ 2.34.193 ॥


aditerapi putratvametya yādavanandanaḥ।





muhūrtto vijayo nāma yatra jāto janārdanaḥ ॥ 2.34.200 ॥

jāyate smaiva bhagavān nayanairmohayan prajāḥ ॥ 2.34.201 ॥

gīrbhirmaṅgalayuktābhiḥ stuvanto madhusūdanam।
maharṣayaḥ sagandharvā upatasthuḥ sahasraśaḥ ॥ 2.34.202 ॥

vasudevastu taṃ rātrau jātaṃ putramadhokṣajam।

bhīto'haṃ kaṃsatastāta etadeva bravīmyaham।

ugrasenamate tiṣṭhan yaśodāyai tadā dadau ॥ 2.34.205 ॥

yaśodā nandagopasya patnī sā nandagopateḥ ॥ 2.34.206 ॥



dattvainaṃ nandagopasya rakṣamāmiti cābravīt।
ayaṃ sa garbho devakyā asmatkleśān haniṣyati ॥ 2.34.209 ॥




putravatparipālyanto devā devān yathā tadā ।


          ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।

kimarthaṃ vasudevasya bhojaḥ kaṃso narādhipaḥ।
jaghāna putrān bālān vai tanno vyākhyātumarhasi ॥ 2.34.215 ॥

           sūta uvāca।।


uktaṃ hi kila devakyā vasudevasya dhīmataḥ ।


asyā yaḥ saptamo garbhaḥ sa te mṛtyurbhaviṣyati ॥ 2.34.220 ॥



nastriyaṃ kṣatriyo jātu hantumarhati kaścana।

yo'syāḥ sambhavate garbhaḥ saptamaḥ pṛthivī pate।
tamahante prayacchāmi tatra kuryyā yathākramam ॥ 2.34.224 ॥




        ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।


           sūta ūvāca।।






patnyarthaṃ vāsudevasya utpannā rājaveśmasu।


sānurbhānustathākṣaśca rohito mantrayastathā ॥ 2.34.237 ॥

jarāndhakastāmravakṣā bhaumariśca jarandhamaḥ।

bhānurbhaumarikā caiva tāmraparṇī jarandhamā।

bhadraśca bhadraguptaśca bhadravindrastathaiva ca।

saṃgrāmajicca śatajit ta na ca sahasrajit।




bṛhatī nartakonneyī sunaye saṅgatā tathā ।



tumbaśca tumbabāṇaśca janastambaśca tāvubhau।


tasyāmaśvasuto jajñe vajro nāma mahāyaśāḥ।
vajrasya pratibāhustu sucārustasya cātmajaḥ ॥ 2.34.250 ॥




sarvvamakakulaṃ yadvadvarttate vaiṣṇave kule ॥ 2.34.254 ॥

nideśasthāyibhistasya baddhyante sarvamānuṣāḥ ॥ 2.34.255 ॥



## 2.35

śālakī badaraścaiva vidvān dhanvantaristathā।
           ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।
brahmakṣetre yugānteṣu kimarthamiha jāyate ।
vasudevakule dhīmān vāsudevatvamāgataḥ ॥ 2.35.8 ॥
devalokaṃ samutsṛjya martyalokamihāgataḥ ॥ 2.35.9 ॥
sa kathaṃ gāṃ gato viṣṇurgopamanvakarotprabhuḥ ॥ 2.35.12 ॥
yena lokān kramairjittvā tribhistrīṃstridaśepsayā।
sthāpitā jagato mārgāstrivargapravarāstrayaḥ ॥ 2.35.14 ॥
pūrvvadaityo mahāvīryyo hiraṇyakaśipurhataḥ ॥ 2.35.17 ॥
sahasraśirasaṃ devaṃ yamāhurvai yuge yuge ॥ 2.35.19 ॥
ekārṇavagate loke tatpaṅkajamapaṅkajam ॥ 2.35.20 ॥
sarvvadevamayaṃ kṛtvā sarvāyudhadharaṃ vapuḥ ॥ 2.35.21 ॥
garuḍasthena cotsiktaḥ kālanemirnipātitaḥ।
agnimāhavanīyañca vediñcaiva kuśasravam ॥ 2.35.25 ॥
atha trīniha yaścakre havyabhāga pradānmakhe ॥ 2.35.26 ॥
yaḥ parantapasaḥ prāhuryaḥ paramparamātmavān।
ādityādistu yo devo yaśca daityāntako vibhuḥ ॥ 2.35.39 ॥
seturyo lokasetūnāṃ medhyo yo medhyakarmmaṇām ॥ 2.35.40 ॥
vedyo yo vedaviduṣāṃ prabhuryaḥ prabhavātmanām।
vinayo nayatṛptānāṃ tejastejasvināmapi ॥ 2.35.42 ॥
vigraho vigrahāṇāṃ yo gatirgatimatāmapi।
māṃsāttu medaso janma medaso'sthi nirūpyate।
asthnā majjā samabhavanmajjātaḥ śukrasambhavaḥ ॥ 2.35.45 ॥
śukrādgharbhaḥ samabhavadrasamūlena karmmaṇā।
garbhoṣmasambhavo jñeyo dvitīyo rāśirucyate।
kaphavarge'bhavacchukraṃ pittavarge ca śoṇitam ॥ 2.35.48 ॥
dehasya madhye hṛdayaṃ sthānantu manasaḥ smṛtam ॥ 2.35.49 ॥
manaḥ prajāpatirjñeyaṃ kaphaḥ somo vibhāvyate ॥ 2.35.50 ॥
evaṃ pravarttito garbho varttate'mbudasannibhaḥ ॥ 2.35.51 ॥
sa pañcadhā śarīrastho vidyate varddhayet punaḥ ॥ 2.35.52 ॥
prāṇo'sya paramātmānaṃ varddhayan parivarttate ॥ 2.35.53 ॥
vyāno vyānasyate yena samānaḥ sarvvasandhiṣu ॥ 2.35.54 ॥
bhūtāvāptistatastasya jāyatendriyagocarā।
naidhane'smin kathaṃ loke naratvaṃ viṣṇurāgataḥ ॥ 2.35.58 ॥
viṣṇorutpattimāścaryyaṃ kathayasva mahāmate।
           ।।sūta uvāca।।
saptasaptatapaḥ proktā bhṛguśāpena mānuṣe।
jāyate ca yugānteṣu devakāryyarthasiddhaye ॥ 2.35.64 ॥
tasya divyatanuṃ viṣṇorgadato me nibodhata।
yugadharmme parāvṛtte kāle ca śithile prabhuḥ ॥ 2.35.65 ॥
            ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
etadveditumicchāmo vṛttaṃ daivāsuraṃ katham ॥ 2.35.67 ॥
             ।।sūta uvāca।।
daivāsuraṃ yathāvṛttaṃ bruvatastannibodhata।
hiraṇyakaśipurdaityastrailokyaṃ prāk praśāsati ॥ 2.35.68 ॥
nideśasthāyinaścaiva tayordevāsurābhavan ॥ 2.35.70 ॥
hiraṇyakaśipurdaityo narasiṃhena sūditaḥ।
vāmanena balirvaddhastrailokyākramaṇe kṛte ॥ 2.35.77 ॥
hiraṇyākṣo hato dvandve prativāde tu daivataiḥ।
prāhlādo nirjito yuddhe indreṇāmṛtamanthane ॥ 2.35.79 ॥
virocanastu prāhlādirnityamindravadhodyataḥ।
indreṇaiva sa vikramya nihatastārakāmaye ॥ 2.35.80 ॥
aśaknuvanto deveṣu puraṃ goptuṃ tridaivatam ।
nihatā dānavāḥ sarve tripurastryambakeṇa tu ॥ 2.35.82 ॥
aṣṭame tvasurāścaivaṃ rākṣasāścāndhakārakāḥ।
jitadevamanuṣyaistu pitṛbhiścaiva saṅgatān ॥ 2.35.83 ॥
hato dhvajo mahendreṇa māyācchannaśca yodhayan।
dhvaje lakṣyaṃ samāviśya vipravarttirmahābhujaḥ ॥ 2.35.85 ॥
rajiḥ kolāhale sarvān devaiḥ parivṛto'jayat।
daityasaṃsthamidaṃ sarvamāsīddaśayugaṃ kila ॥ 2.35.91 ॥
trailokyamavyayamidaṃ mahendreṇa tu pālyate ॥ 2.35.92 ॥
prahlādasya tataścādastrailokyaṃ kālaparyayāt ।
tato'surān parityajya yajñe devā upāgaman ।
yajñe devānatha gate kāvyaṃ te hyasurābruvan ॥ 2.35.94 ॥
jadhnustai rvadhyamānāste kāvyamevābhidudruvuḥ ॥ 2.35.100 ॥
trailokyaṃ vijitaṃ sarvaṃ vāmanena tribhiḥ kramaiḥ ।
balirbaddho hato jambho nihataśca virocanaḥ ॥ 2.35.103 ॥
agnimāpyāyayeddhotā mantraireva bṛhaspatiḥ ॥ 2.35.106 ॥
tato yāsyāmyahaṃ devaṃ mantrārthe nīlalohitam।
na vai devā vadhiṣyanti yāvadāgamanaṃ mama ॥ 2.35.108 ॥
yotsyāmahe punardevāṃstataḥ prāpsyatha vai jayam ॥ 2.35.109 ॥
nyastavādā vayaṃ sarve lokān yūyaṃ kramantu vai ॥ 2.35.110 ॥
nyastaśastreṣu daityeṣu svān vai jagmuryathāgatān ॥ 2.35.112 ॥
praṇamyenasuvācātha jagatprabhavamīśvaram ॥ 2.35.114 ॥
mantrānicchāmyahaṃ deva me nasanti bṛhaspatau।
evamukto'bravīddevo mantrānicchasi vai dvija।
yadyupāsyasi bhadrante matto mantramavāpsyasi ॥ 2.35.117 ॥
tathokto deva devena sa śukrastu mahātapāḥ।
tato niyukto devena kuṇḍadhāro'sya dhūmakṛt ॥ 2.35.119 ॥
mantrārthaṃ tatra vasati brahmacarye maheśvaraḥ ॥ 2.35.120 ॥
tasmiñchidre tadāmarṣā devāstān samabhidravan।
utpetuḥ sahasā sarve santrastāste tato'bhavan ॥ 2.35.122 ॥
nyastaśastre jaye datte ācāryavratamāsthite।
santyajya. samayaṃ devāste sapatnajighāṃsavaḥ ॥ 2.35.123 ॥
jñāpayāmastatamidaṃ yāvadāgamanaṃ guroḥ।
vinivṛtte tataḥ kāvye yotsyāmo yudhi tān surān ॥ 2.35.126 ॥
prāpadyanta tato bhītāstadā caiva tadā'bhayam ॥ 2.35.127 ॥
na bhetavyaṃ na bhetavya bhayantyajata dānavāḥ ॥ 2.35.128 ॥
matsannidhau vartatāṃ vo na bhīrbhavitumarhati।
abhijagmuḥ prasahyaitānavicārya balābalam।
devī kruddhābravīdenānanindratvaṃ karomyaham।
evamuktastato viṣṇuṃ praviveśa purandaraḥ।
kathaṃ mucyeva sahitau viṣṇurindramabhāṣata ॥ 2.35.136 ॥
indro'bravījjahi hyenāṃ yāvannau na dahedvibho।
abhidhyāya tataścakramāpannaḥ satvaraṃ prabhuḥ ॥ 2.35.138 ॥
kruddhastadastramāviddhya śiraściccheda mādhavaḥ ॥ 2.35.139 ॥
tasmāttvaṃ saptakṛtvo vai mānuṣeṣu prapatsyasi ॥ 2.35.141 ॥
tena satyena jīvasva yadi satyaṃ bravīmyaham ॥ 2.35.144 ॥
taistairmanonukūlaiśca hyupacārairatandritā ॥ 2.35.151 ॥
pūrṇe dhūmavrate cāpi ghore varṣasahasrike ।
evaṃ bruvaṃstvayaikena cīrṇaṃ nānyena kenacit।
tasmāttvaṃ tapasā buddhyā śrutena ca balena ca ॥ 2.35.156 ॥
tejasā cāpi vibudhān sarvānabhibhaviṣyasi।
yacca kiñcinmama brahman vidyate bhṛgunandana ॥ 2.35.157 ॥
pratibhāsyati te sarvaṃ taccādyantaṃ na kasyacit ॥ 2.35.158 ॥
ajeyatvaṃ dhaneśatvamavadhyatvaṃ ca vai dadau।
harṣāt prādurbabhau tasya devastotraṃ maheśvaram।
tadā tiryaksthitastvevaṃ tuṣṭuve nīlalohitam ॥ 2.35.161 ॥
ririhāṇāya lopāya vatsarāya jagatpate ॥ 2.35.162 ॥
kapardine hyūrddhvaromṇe hayāya karaṇāya ca।
vasuretāya rudrāya tapase cīravāsase ॥ 2.35.164 ॥
hrasvāya muktakeśāya senānye rohitāya ca ॥ 2.35.165 ॥
suvṛttāya suhastāya dhanvine bhārgavāya ca ॥ 2.35.166 ॥
sahasrabāhave caiva sahasrāmalacakṣuṣe।
sahasrakukṣaye caiva sahasracaraṇāya ca ॥ 2.35.167 ॥
sahasraśirase caiva bahurūpāya vedhase।
pinākine ceṣumate citrāya rohitāya ca ॥ 2.35.171 ॥
dundubhyāyaikapādāya arhāya buddhaye tathā।
kapilāyaikavīrāya mṛtyave tryambakāya ca ॥ 2.35.173 ॥
tigmāyudhāya medhyāya siddhāya ca pulastaye ॥ 2.35.183 ॥
aghaghnāya makhghnāya mṛtyave yajñiyāya ca।
kṣipregave sudhanvāya pramedhyāya priyāya ca ॥ 2.35.186 ॥
vibhrāntāya mahantāya antaye durgamāya ca ॥ 2.35.187 ॥
hiraṇyabāhave caiva satyāya śamanāya ca।
vṛtadhanvane kavicine rathine ca varūthine।
bhūtabhavyabhavāyaiva tubhyaṃ kālātmane namaḥ।
viśvāya marute caiva tubhyandevātmane namaḥ।
agnisomartvigijyāya paśumantrauṣadhāya ca ॥ 2.35.196 ॥
tapase caiva satyāya tyāgāya ca śamāya ca ॥ 2.35.197 ॥
janastapāya satyāya tubhyaṃ lokātmane namaḥ ॥ 2.35.199 ॥
avyaktāyātha mahate bhūtāyaivondriyāya ca ।
śuddhāya vibhave caiva tubhyaṃ nityātmane namaḥ ॥ 2.35.201 ॥
satyānteṣu mahānteṣu caturṣu ca namo'stu te ॥ 2.35.202 ॥
namaḥstotre mayā hyasmin sadasadvyāhṛtaṃ vibho।
madbhakta iti brahmaṇya sarvantat kṣantumarhasi ॥ 2.35.203 ॥


## 2.36

sūta uvāca।।
brahmeti praṇatastasmai prāñjalirvākyamabravīt ॥ 2.36.1 ॥


tataḥ so'ntarhite tasmin deveśānucare tadā।


anayā satataṃ bhaktyā praśrayeṇa damena ca।
snehena caiva suśroṇi prīto'smi varavarṇini ॥ 2.36.5 ॥

kimicchasi varārehe kaste kāmaḥ samṛdhyatām।
taṃ te saṃpūrayāmyadya yadyapi syāt sudurlabham ॥ 2.36.6 ॥



adṛśyaṃ sarvabhūtaistu saṃprayogamihecchasi ॥ 2.36.9 ॥

devendrānalavarṇābhe varārohe sulocane।

tataḥ svagṛhamāgamya jayantyā sahitaḥ prabhuḥ ॥ 2.36.11 ॥



dākṣiṇyaṃ tasya tadbudhvā pratijagmuryathāgatam ॥ 2.36.14 ॥


buddhvā tadantaraṃ so'tha daityānāmiva coditaḥ।


tataste hṛṣṭamanaso vidyārthamupapedire ॥ 2.36.18 ॥

yayau ca samakālaṃ sa sadyotpannamatistadā ॥ 2.36.19 ॥


           ।।śukra uvāca।।
vibhrāntaprekṣite sādhvi trivarṇāyatalocane ॥ 2.36.21 ॥

evamuktā'bravīddevī bhaja bhaktān mahāvrata।
eṣa brahman satāṃ dharmo na dharmaṃ lopayāmi te ॥ 2.36.22 ॥

           ।।sūta uvāca।।


prekṣante sma hyubhau tatra sitāsitaśucismitau ॥ 2.36.25 ॥


anugacchata māṃ sarve tyajatainaṃ bṛhaspatim ॥ 2.36.27 ॥



śrutvā tasya tataste vai saṃmantryārthavaco'bruvan ॥ 2.36.30 ॥

eṣa vai gururasmākamantarepsurayaṃ dvijaḥ ॥ 2.36.31 ॥



sthitā vayaṃ nideśe'sya gacchatvaṃ sādhu mā ciram ॥ 2.36.34 ॥

evamuktvāsurāḥ sarve prāpadyanta bṛhaspatim।
yadā na pratipadyante tenoktaṃ tanmahaddhitam ॥ 2.36.35 ॥

cukopa bhārgavasteṣāmavalepena vai tadā।



aho dhig vañcitāḥ smeha parasparamathābruvan ॥ 2.36.39 ॥


tato'surāḥ paritrastā devebhyastvaritā yayuḥ।


tatastenāvalepena gatā yūthaṃ parābhavam ॥ 2.36.43 ॥

prahlādastamathovāca mānaṃ tvaṃ tyaja bhārgava।


yadi nastvaṃ na kuruṣe prasādaṃ bhṛgunandana।
apadhyātā stvayā hyadya pravekṣyāmo rasātalam ॥ 2.36.46 ॥

           sūta uvāca।।
evamukto'nunītaḥ sa stutaḥ kopaṃ nyayacchata ॥ 2.36.47 ॥

uvācedanna bhetavyaṃ na gantanyaṃ rasātalam।
avaśyambhāvī hyartho'yaṃ prāpto vo mayi jāgrati ॥ 2.36.48 ॥


matprasādācca yuṣmābhirbhuktaṃ trailokyamūrjjitam ॥ 2.36.50 ॥


sāvarṇike punastubhyaṃ rājyaṃ kila bhaviṣyati।

evaṃ kilamahaṃ proktaḥ pautraste brahmaṇā svayam।
tathāhṛteṣu lokeṣu tapo'sya na kilābhavat ॥ 2.36.53 ॥

tasmādajena prītena dattaṃ sāvarṇike'ntare ॥ 2.36.54 ॥


prītena cāmaratvaṃ vai dattaṃ tubyaṃ svayambhuvā।
tasmānnirutsu kastvaṃ vai paryyāyaṃ saha mākulaḥ ॥ 2.36.56 ॥

na ca śakyaṃ mayā tubhyaṃ purastādvai visarpitum ।

imau ca śiṣyau dvau mahyaṃ tulyāvetau bṛhaspateḥ ।




ajayannasurā devān bhagnā devā amantrayan ॥ 2.36.62 ॥

             ।।devā ūcuḥ ।।


tajjñānāpahṛtāvetau kṛtvā jeṣyāmahe'surān ।

yajñe samāhvayiṣyāmastyajatamasurān dvijau ।



vadhyamānāstadā devairviviśuste rasātalam।
tataḥ prabhṛti śāpena bhṛgunaimittikena ca ॥ 2.36.68 ॥

karttuṃ dharmmavyavasthānamadharmmasya ca nāśanam ॥ 2.36.69 ॥

prahlādasya nideśe tu ye'surā na vyavasthitāḥ।

yajñaṃ pravartayāmāsa caitye vaivasvate'ntare ॥ 2.36.71 ॥

prādurbhāve tadānyasya brahmaivāsīt purohitaḥ।

sambhūtaḥ sa samudrāntarhiraṇyakaśiporvadhe।

daityaistrailokya ākrānte tṛtīyo vāmano'bhavat ॥ 2.36.74 ॥

yajamānantu daityendramadityāḥ kulanandanaḥ।

trailokyasya bhavān rājā tvayi sarvvaṃ pratiṣṭitam।
dātumarhasi me rājan vikramāṃstrīniti prabhuḥ ॥ 2.36.76 ॥

vāmanantaṃ ca vijñāya tato'numuditaḥ svayam ॥ 2.36.77 ॥

tribhiḥ kramairviśvamidaṃ jagadākrāmata prabhuḥ ॥ 2.36.78 ॥


saputrapautrānasurān pātālatalamānayat ॥ 2.36.80 ॥



tasya gātre jagatsarvamātmānamanupaśyati ।

tadvai rūpamupendrasya devadānavamānavāḥ।

virocana kulaṃ sarvaṃ pātāle sanniveśitam ॥ 2.36.85 ॥

tataḥ sarvāmaraiśvaryaṃ dattvendrāya mahātmane।


tretāyuge tu daśame dattātreyo babhūva ha।

māndhātuścakravarttitve tasthau tathyapuraḥsaraḥ ॥ 2.36.89 ॥




devakyāṃ vasudevāttu brahnagārgyapuraḥsaraḥ ॥ 2.36.93 ॥

aprameyo niyojyaśca yatra kāmacaro vaśī।
krījate bhagavāँ lloke bālaḥ krījanakairiva ॥ 2.36.94 ॥

paraṃ paramametasmādviśvarūpānna vidyate ॥ 2.36.95 ॥










anukarṣan sarvasenāṃ hastyaśvarathasaṅkulām।

nātyarthaṃ dhārmikā ye ca ye ca dharmadviṣaḥ vkacit।



pravṛttacakrobalavān mlecchānāmantakṛdbalī।


gātreṇa vai candrasamaḥ pūrṇe kaliyuge'bhavat।




tataḥ sa vai tadā kalkiścaritārthaḥ sasainikaḥ।
karmaṇā nihatā ye tu siddhāste tu punaḥ svayam ॥ 2.36.115 ॥


tato vyatīte kalkau tu sāmānyaiḥ saha sainikaiḥ ॥ 2.36.117 ॥



hrasvā alpāyuṣaścaiva vanaukasa ime smṛtāḥ ॥ 2.36.120 ॥





turvasostu pravakṣyāmi pūrordruhyoranostathā ॥ 2.36.125 ॥


## 2.37

।।sūta uvāca।।
turvasostu suto vahnirvahnergobhānurātmajaḥ।
gobhānostu suto vīrastrisānuraparājitaḥ ॥ 2.37.1 ॥
karandhamastrisānostu maruttasya na cātmajaḥ।
anavatyo maruttastu sa rājāsīditi śrutam।
duṣkṛtaṃ pauravaṃ cāpi sarve putramakalpayan ॥ 2.37.3 ॥
pāṇḍyaśca keralaścaiva colaḥ kulyastathaiva ca।
druhyostu tanayau vīrau babhruḥ setuśca viśrutau।
aruddhaḥ setuputrastu bābhravo ripurucyate ॥ 2.37.7 ॥
yauvanāśvena samiti kṛcchreṇa nihato balī।
gāndhāraputro dharmmastu ghṛtastasya suto'bhavat ॥ 2.37.10 ॥
ghṛtasya durdamo jajñe pracetāstasya cātmajaḥ ।
sabhānaraśca pakṣaśca parapakṣastathaiva ca।
janamejayo mahā satvaḥ purañjayasuto'bhavat।
mahāmanāstu putrau dvau janayāmāsa viśrutau ॥ 2.37.17 ॥
vṛṣadarbhaḥ suvīrastu kekayo madrakastathā ॥ 2.37.22 ॥
uśadratho mahābāhustasya hemaḥ suto'bhavat ॥ 2.37.24 ॥
saṃgrāme cāpyajeyatvaṃ dharme caiva prabhāvanā ॥ 2.37.29 ॥
trailokyadarśanañcaiva prādhānyaṃ prasave tathā।
bale cāprati matvaṃ vai dharmatattvārthadarśanam ॥ 2.37.30 ॥
caturo niyatān varṇān tvaṃ vai sthāpayiteti ca।
           ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।
           sūta uvāca।।
bṛhaspatirbṛhattejā mamatāṃ so'bhyapadyata ॥ 2.37.36 ॥
uvāca mamatā tantu bṛhaspatimanicchatī।
ayaṃ hi me mahāgarbho rodate'ti bṛhaspate।
amogharetāstvañcāpi na māṃ bhajitamarhasi।
asminneva gate kāle yathā vā manyase prabho ॥ 2.37.39 ॥
evamuktastayā samyag bṛhattejā bṛhaspatiḥ।
utsṛjantaṃ tadā reto garbhasthaḥ so'bhyabhāṣata ॥ 2.37.41 ॥
no snātako nyaseddhyasmin dvayornehāsti sambhavaḥ।
māmevamuktavān mohāttamo dīrghaṃ pravekṣyasi ॥ 2.37.44 ॥
godharmaṃ saurabheyāttu vṛṣabhācchrutavān prabho ॥ 2.37.46 ॥
sa tena nigṛhītastu na cacāla padātpadam।
na mayāsāditastāta balavaṃstvadvidhaḥ kvacit।
tryambakaṃ vahatā devaṃ yato jāto'smi bhūtale ॥ 2.37.50 ॥
evamukto'bravīdenaṃ jīvastvaṃ me kva yāsyasi ॥ 2.37.51 ॥
kāryākāryaṃ na vai vijho gamyāgamyaṃ tathaiva ca।
prasādite gate tasmin godharmaṃ bhaktitastu tam।
tato yavīyasaḥ patnīmautathyasyābhyamanyata।
provāca dīrghatamasaṃ krodhāt saṃrakta locanaḥ।
durvṛttastvaṃ tyajāmyeva gaccha tvaṃ svena karmaṇā।
yasmāttvamandho vṛddhaśca bharttavyo duranuṣṭitaḥ।
tenāsi tvaṃ parityakto durācāro'si me matiḥ ॥ 2.37.61 ॥
          sūta uvāca।।
karmaṇyasmistataḥ krūre tasya buddhirajāyata।
nirbhatsya caiva bahuśo bāhubhyāṃ parigṛhya ca।
koṣṭe samudre prakṣipya gaṅgāmbhasi samutsṛjat ॥ 2.37.62 ॥
taṃ sastrīko balirnāma rājā dharmārthatattvavit।
antaḥpure jugopainaṃ bhakṣyairbhojyaiśca tarpayan ॥ 2.37.64 ॥
prītaḥ sa vai vareṇātha cchandayāmāsa vai balim।
            ।।baliruvāca।।
putrān dharmārthasaṃyuktānutpādayitumarhasi ॥ 2.37.66 ॥
evamuktastu tenarṣistathāstvityuktavān hi tam ।
janayāmāsa dharmātmā putrāvetau mahojasau ॥ 2.37.69 ॥
prādhītau vidhivatsamyagīśvarau brahmavādinau ॥ 2.37.70 ॥
siddhau pratyakṣadharmāṇau buddhau śreṣṭhatamāvapi।
mamaitāviti hovāca balirvairocanastvṛṣim ॥ 2.37.71 ॥
netyuvāca tatastantu mamaitāviti cābravīt।
utpannau śūdrayonau tu bhavacchadmā surottamau ॥ 2.37.72 ॥
lihiṣyasyajugupsantī hyāpādatalamastakam ॥ 2.37.76 ॥
tatastvaṃ prāpsyase devi putrāṃśca manasepsitān।
apānañca samāsādya jugupsantī nyavarjayat।
           ।।ṛṣiruvāca।।
vaṅgastasmātkaliṅgastu puṇḍro brahmastathaiva ca ॥ 2.37.84 ॥
surabhirdīrghatamasamatha prīto vaco'bravīt ॥ 2.37.87 ॥
vicārya yasmādgodharmmaṃ tvamevaṃ kṛtavānasi ।
tena nyāyena mumuce hyahaṃ prītosmi tena te ॥ 2.37.88 ॥
tasmāttava tamo dīrghaṃ nistudāmyadya paśya vai।
bārhaspatyañca yatte'nyatpāpaṃ santiṣṭhate tanau ॥ 2.37.89 ॥
gavā dīrghatamāḥ so'tha gautamaḥ samapadyata ॥ 2.37.91 ॥
yathoddiṣṭaṃ hi pitrarthe cacāra vipulaṃ tapaḥ ॥ 2.37.92 ॥
tato'bravīt pitā cainaṃ putravānasmyahaṃ prabho।
ityeṣa dīrghatamaso balervairocanasya vai ॥ 2.37.95 ॥
putro divirathasyāsīdvidvān dharmaratho nṛpaḥ ॥ 2.37.99 ॥
sa vai dharmarathaḥ śrīmān yena viṣṇupade girau।
śrṛṇu dharmmarathasyāpi rājā citraratho'bhavat।
haryyaṅgasya tu dāyādo rājā bhadrarathaḥ kila।
atha bhadrarathasyāsīdbṛhatkarmā prajeśvaraḥ ॥ 2.37.103 ॥
bṛhanmanāstu rājendraṃ janayāmāsa vai sutam ॥ 2.37.104 ॥
             ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।
etadicchāmahe śrotumatyarthaṃ kuśalo hyasi ॥ 2.37.107 ॥
           ।।sūta uvāca।।
tasya patnīdvayaṃ cāsīccaidyasyobhe ca te sute ॥ 2.37.108 ॥
jayadrathastu rājendro yaśodevyāṃ vyajāyata ॥ 2.37.109 ॥
vijayasya dhṛtiḥ putra stasya putro dhṛtavrataḥ ॥ 2.37.110 ॥
satyakarmasutaścāpi sūtastvadhirathastu vai ॥ 2.37.111 ॥
etadvaḥ kathitaṃ sarvaṃ karṇe yadvai pracoditam ॥ 2.37.112 ॥
           sūta uvāca।।
aviddhastu sutastasya yaḥ prācīmajayaddiśam ॥ 2.37.114 ॥
aviddhataḥ pravīrastu manasyurabhavatsutaḥ।
rājātho jayado nāma manasyorabhavatsutaḥ ॥ 2.37.115 ॥
dhundhorbahu gavī putraḥ sañjātistasya cātmajaḥ ॥ 2.37.116 ॥
sañjāteratha raudrāśvastasya putrānnibodhata।
rajeyuśca kṛte yuśca vakṣeyuḥ sthaṇḍileyu ca।
ghṛteyuśca jaleyuśca sthaleyuścaiva saptamaḥ ॥ 2.37.118 ॥
dharmeyuḥ sannateyuśca vaneyurddaśamastu saḥ।
anādṛṣṭastu rājarṣī riveyustasya cātmajaḥ ॥ 2.37.121 ॥
rantirnāraḥ sarasvatyāṃ putrānajanayacchubhān।
trasuṃ tathā pratirathaṃ dhruvañcaivātidhārmikam ॥ 2.37.123 ॥
dhuryaḥ pratirathasyāpi kaṇṭhastasyābhavat sutaḥ ॥ 2.37.124 ॥
trasuḥ sudayitaṃputraṃ malinaṃ brahmavādinam।
upadātaṃ tato lebhe caturastviti sātmajān ॥ 2.37.126 ॥
suṣmantamatha duṣyantaṃ pravīramanaghantathā।
cakravarttī tato jajñe dauṣyantirnṛpasattamaḥ ॥ 2.37.127 ॥
bharasva putraṃ duṣyantaṃ satyamāha śakuntalā ॥ 2.37.129 ॥
retodhāḥ putraṃ nayati naradeva yamakṣayāt।
tatastasya narendrasya vitataṃ putrajanma tat ॥ 2.37.132 ॥
tato marudbhirānīya putrastu sa bṛhaspateḥ।
saṅkrāmito bharadvājo marudbhiḥ kratubhirvibhuḥ ॥ 2.37.133 ॥
tatraivodārahantīdaṃ bharadvājasya dhīmataḥ।
janmasaṅkramaṇañcaiva marudbhirbharatāya vai ॥ 2.37.134 ॥
alaṃkṛtya tanuṃ svāntu maithunaṃ dehi me śubhe ॥ 2.37.135 ॥
evamuktā'bravīdena mantarvatnī hyahaṃ vibho।
amogharetāstvañcāpi dharmaścaiva vigarhitaḥ।
vinayo nopadeṣṭavyastvayā mama kathañcana।
evamuktaḥ sa garbheṇa kupitaḥ pratyuvāca ha ॥ 2.37.140 ॥
pratiṣedhasitattasmāt tamo dīrghaṃ pravekṣyasi ॥ 2.37.141 ॥
pādābhyāntena tacchannaṃ māturdvāraṃ bṛhaspateḥ।
tadretastu tayormadhye nivāryaḥ śiśuko'bhavat ॥ 2.37.142 ॥
evamuktvā gatāyāṃ sa putrantyajati tatkṣaṇāt।
bharasva bāḍhamityukto bharadvājastato'bhavat ॥ 2.37.144 ॥
tasmin kāle tu bharato marudbhiḥ kratubhiḥ kramāt।
kāmyanaimittikairyajñairyajate putralipsayā ॥ 2.37.146 ॥
yajñaṃ tato marutsomaṃ putrārthe punarāharat ॥ 2.37.147 ॥
tena te marutastasya marutsomena toṣitāḥ।
bharadvājaṃ tataḥ putraṃ bārhaspatyaṃ manīṣiṇam ॥ 2.37.148 ॥
bharatastu bharadvājaṃ putraṃ prāpya tadābravīt।
pūrvantu vitathaṃ tasya kṛtaṃ vai putrajanma hi।
tataḥ sa vitatho nāma bharadvājastathā'bhavat ॥ 2.37.150 ॥
tasmāddivyo bharadvājo brāhmaṇyāt kṣatriyo'bhavat।
dvimukhyāyananāmā sa smṛto dvipitṛkastu vai ॥ 2.37.151 ॥
tato'tha vitathe jāte bharataḥ sa divaṃ yayau।
vitathasya tu dāyādo bhuvamanyurbabhūva ha ॥ 2.37.152 ॥
narasya sāṃkṛtiḥ putrastasya putrau mahaujasau।
dāyādāścāpi gāgrasya śinibaddhāt babhūva ha।
mahāvīryasutaścāpi bhīmastasmādubhakṣayaḥ।
trayyāruṇiṃ puṣkariṇaṃ tṛtīyaṃ suṣuve kapim।
bharadvājaprasādena śrṛṇudhvaṃ tasya vistaram।
bṛhatkarmā sutastasya putrastasya bṛhadrathaḥ ॥ 2.37.164 ॥
viśvajittanayastasya senajittasya cātmajaḥ।
kāmpilye samaro nāma sa ceṣṭasamaro'bhavat ॥ 2.37.169 ॥
samarasya paraḥ pāraḥ satvadaśca iti trayaḥ।
jajñe sarvaguṇopeto vibrājastasya cātmajaḥ ॥ 2.37.171 ॥
yogasūnuḥ sutastasya viṣvakseno'bhavanṛpaḥ ॥ 2.37.173 ॥
viṣvaksenasya putrastu udakseno babhūva ha ॥ 2.37.174 ॥
bhallāṭastasya dāyādo yena rājā purāhataḥ।
            ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।
ugrāyudhaḥ kasya sutaḥ kasmin vaṃśe ca kīrtyate।
          ।।sūta uvāca।।
dhṛtimāṃstasya putrastu tasya satyadhṛtiḥ sutaḥ ॥ 2.37.177 ॥
tasyānvaye ca mahati mahatpauravanandanaḥ।
mahatpauravaputrastu rājā rukmarathaḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.37.180 ॥
sumaterapi dharmmātmā rājā sannatimān prabhuḥ।
tasyāsītsanatirnāma kṛtastasya suto'bhavat ॥ 2.37.182 ॥
śiṣyo hiraṇyanābhestu kauthumasya mahātmanaḥ।
kārtirugrāyudhaḥ so'tha vīraḥ pauravanandanaḥ ॥ 2.37.184 ॥
babhūva yena vikramya pṛṣatasya pitāmahaḥ।
nīlasya tapasogreṇa suśāntirabhyajāyata ॥ 2.37.187 ॥
tatastu rikṣadāyādā bhedāśca tanayāstvime ॥ 2.37.188 ॥
indrasenā yato garbhaṃ badhyaśvaṃ pratyapadyata।
putraḥ satyadhṛtirnāma dhanurvedasya pāragaḥ ॥ 2.37.195 ॥
pracaskande śarastambe maithunaṃ samapadyata ॥ 2.37.196 ॥
ata ūrddhvaṃ pravakṣyāmi divodāsasya santatim ॥ 2.37.198 ॥
maitreyastu tato jajñe smṛtā ete'pi saṃśritāḥ ॥ 2.37.199 ॥
rājāpi cyavano vidvāṃstataḥ pratiratho'bhavat ॥ 2.37.200 ॥
atha vai cyavanāddhīmān sudāsaḥ samapadyata।
saudāsaḥ sahadevaśca somakastasya cātmajaḥ ॥ 2.37.201 ॥
somakasya suto janturhate tasmiñchataṃ vibho ॥ 2.37.202 ॥
hutāgnyanidrā hyabhavat pavitramitabhojanā ॥ 2.37.205 ॥
kṛṣyamāṇe tadā śakrastatrāsya varado babhau।
kurostu dayitāḥ putrāḥ sudhanvā jahnureva ca ॥ 2.37.210 ॥
cyavanastasya putrastu rājā dharmārthakovidaḥ।
vidyoparicarājjajñe girikā sapta sūnavaḥ।
mahāratho bhagadharo viśruto yo bṛhadrathaḥ ॥ 2.37.214 ॥
māthailyaśca lalitthaśca matsyakālaśca saptamaḥ ॥ 2.37.215 ॥
ūrjjasya nabhasaḥ putrastasmājjajñe sa vīryavān ॥ 2.37.218 ॥
śakale dve sa vai jāto jarayā sandhitastu saḥ।
jarāsandho mahābāhurjarayā sandhitastu saḥ ॥ 2.37.219 ॥
sarvakṣatrasya jetā'sau jarāsandho mahābalaḥ।
jarāsandhasya putrastu sahadevaḥ pratāpavān ॥ 2.37.220 ॥
        ।।sūta uvāca।।
parikṣitasya dāyādo babhūva janamejayaḥ।
jahnustvajanayatputraṃ surathaṃ nāma bhūmipam।
sārvabhaumā jjayatsena ārādhistasya cātmajaḥ ॥ 2.37.224 ॥
devāpistu pravavrāja vanaṃ dharmmaparīpsayā ॥ 2.37.229 ॥
cyavano'sya hi putrastu iṣṭakaśca mahātmanaḥ ॥ 2.37.230 ॥
yaṃ yaṃ rājā spṛśati vai jīrṇaṃ samayato naram।
punaryuvā sa bhavati tasmātte śantanuṃ viduḥ ॥ 2.37.232 ॥
tato'sya śantanutvaṃ vai prajāsviha pariśrutam।
sa upayeme dharmmātmā śantanu rjāhnavīṃ nṛpaḥ ॥ 2.37.233 ॥
kāle vicitravīryantu śantanu rjanayatsutam।
śantanordayitaṃ putraṃ prajāhitakaramprabhum।
dhṛtarāṣṭrāttu gāndhārī putrāṇāṃ suṣuve śatam ॥ 2.37.236 ॥
indrāddhanañjayo jajñe śakratulyaparākramaḥ।
aśvibhyāṃ saha devaśca nakulaścāpi mādrijau ॥ 2.37.239 ॥
asapatnaṃ tadāmarṣādvaiśampāyana eva tu।
na sthāsyatīha durbuddhe tavaitadvacanaṃ bhuvi ॥ 2.37.245 ॥
yāvatsthā syāmyahaṃ loke tāvannaitatpraśasyate।
abhitaḥ saṃsthitaścāpi tataḥ sa janamejayaḥ ॥ 2.37.246 ॥
vijñāya saṃsthito'paśyattadvadhīṣṭāṃ vibhormakhe ॥ 2.37.247 ॥
parikṣittanayaśvāpi pauravo janamejayaḥ ।
dviraśvamedhamāhṛtya tato vājasaneyakam ।
pravarttayitvā tadbrahma trikharvvī janamejayaḥ ॥ 2.37.248 ॥
kharvvamaśvakamukhyānāṃ kharvvamaṅganivāsinām।
sarvvañca madhyadeśānāṃ trikharvvī janamejayaḥ ।
tasya putraḥ śatanīko balavān satyavikramaḥ ।
tataḥ sutaṃ śatānīkaṃ viprāstamabhyaṣecayat ॥ 2.37.250 ॥
putro'śvamedha datto'bhūcchatānīkasya vīryyavān।
putro'śvamedhadattādvai jātaḥ parapuraṃjayaḥ ॥ 2.37.251 ॥
adhisāmakṛṣṇo dharmātmā sāmprato'yaṃ mahāyaśāḥ।
           ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।
sūta sārddhaṃ nṛpairbhāvyaṃ vyatītaṃ kīrttitaṃ tvayā ॥ 2.37.254 ॥
yattu saṃsthāsyate kṛtyamutpatsyanti ce ye nṛpāḥ।
kālaṃ yugapramāṇañca guṇadoṣān bhaviṣyataḥ।
sa evamukto munibhiḥ sūto buddhimatāṃ varaḥ।
          ।।sūta uvāca।।
anāgatāni sarvāṇi bruvato me nibodhata।
ailāṃścaiva tathekṣvākūn saudyumnāṃścaiva pārthivān।
tebhyaḥ pare ca ye cānye utpatsyante mahīkṣitaḥ ॥ 2.37.261 ॥
adhisāmakṛṣṇaḥ so'yaṃ sāmprataṃ pauravānnṛpaḥ।
adhisāmakṛṣṇaputro nirvakre bhavitā kila।
gaṅgayāpahṛte tasminnagare nāgasāhvaye।
bhaviṣyaduṣṇastatputra uṣṇāccitrarathaḥ smṛtaḥ।
suṣeṇo vai mahāvīryo bhaviṣyati mahāyaśāḥ।
rucaḥ sutīrthādbhavitā tricakṣo bhavitā tataḥ।
pariplutasutaścāpi bhavitā sunayo nṛpaḥ ॥ 2.37.269 ॥
medhāvī sunayasyātha bhaviṣyati narādhipaḥ।
pañcaviṃśanṛpā hyete bhaviṣyāḥ pūrvavaṃśajāḥ ॥ 2.37.271 ॥
brahmakṣatrasya yo yonirvaṃśo devarṣisatkṛtaḥ ॥ 2.37.272 ॥
tataḥ kṣayaḥ sutastasya vatsavyūhastataḥ kṣayāt ॥ 2.37.275 ॥
vatsavyūhātprativyūhastasya putro divākaraḥ।
yaśca sāṃpratamadhyāsta ayodhyāṃ nagarīṃ nṛpaḥ ॥ 2.37.276 ॥
sahadevasya dāyādo bṛhadaśvo bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.37.277 ॥
sahadevaḥ sutastasya sunakṣatraśca tatsutaḥ ॥ 2.37.279 ॥
kinnarastu sunakṣatrādbhaviṣyati paraṃtapaḥ।
bhavitā cāntarikṣastu kinnarasya suto mahān ॥ 2.37.280 ॥
antarikṣātsuparṇastu suparṇāccāpyamitrajit।
putrastasya bharadvājo dharmī tasya sutaḥ smṛtaḥ।
prasenajittato bhāvyaḥ kṣudrako bhavitā tataḥ ॥ 2.37.283 ॥
sumitraḥ surathasyāpi antyaśca bhavitā nṛpaḥ ॥ 2.37.284 ॥
bṛhadbalānvaye jātā bhavitāraḥ kalau yuge।
prādhānyataḥ pravakṣyāmi gadato me nibodhata ॥ 2.37.288 ॥
saṃgrāme bhārate tasmin sahadevo nipātitaḥ।
somādhistasya tanayo rājarṣiḥ sagirivraje ॥ 2.37.289 ॥
sa vai praṇatasāmanto bhaviṣye nayavarjjitaḥ ।
ekatriṃśatsamā rājyamajakasya bhaviṣyati।
bhaviṣyati samā viṃśattatsuto varttivarddhanaḥ ॥ 2.37.305 ॥
utpatsyate mahāpadmaḥ sarvakṣatrāntare nṛpaḥ ॥ 2.37.318 ॥
ekarāṭ sa mahāpadma ekacchatro bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.37.319 ॥
sarvakṣatrahṛtoddhṛtya bhāvino'rthasya vai balāt ॥ 2.37.320 ॥
ṣaḍviṃśattu samā rājā hyaśoko bhavitā nṛṣu ॥ 2.37.324 ॥
ityete nava bhūpā ye bhokṣyanti ca vasundharām।
saptatriṃśacchataṃ pūrṇaṃ tebhyastu gaurbhaviṣyati ॥ 2.37.328 ॥
puṣpamitrastu senānīruddhṛtya vai bṛhadratham।
tato dhrukaḥ samā dve tu bhaviṣyati sutaśca vai ॥ 2.37.331 ॥
bhaviṣyanti samāstasmāttisra eva pulindakāḥ।
tato vai vikramitrastu samā rājā tataḥ punaḥ।
apārthivasudevantu bālyādvyasaninaṃ nṛpam ॥ 2.37.335 ॥
bhūtimitraḥ sutastasya caturvviṃśadbhaviṣyati।
caturastuṅgakṛtyāste nṛpāḥ kaṇṭāyanā dvijāḥ ॥ 2.37.338 ॥
śrṛṅgāṇāṃ cāpi yacchiṣṭaṃ kṣayayitvā balaṃ tadā।
śrīsātakarṇirbhavitā tasya putrastu vai mahān।
āpādabaddho daśa vai tasya putro bhaviṣyati।
śatānyarddhacaturthāni bhavitārastrayodaśa।
tacchannena ca kālena tataḥ kolikilā vṛṣāḥ ॥ 2.37.356 ॥
tataḥ kolikilebhyaśca vindhyaśaktirbhaviṣyati।
bhūtinandastataścāpi vaideśe tu bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.37.360 ॥
aṅgānāṃ nandanasyānte madhunandirbhaviṣyati।
tasyānvaye bhaviṣyanti rājānaste trayastu vai।
tasya putrāstu catvāro bhaviṣyanti narādhipāḥ ॥ 2.37.364 ॥
supratīko nabhīrastu samā bhokṣyati triṃśatim ॥ 2.37.365 ॥
meghā iti samākhyātā buddhimanto navaiva tu।
viśvasphānirmahāsattvo yuddhe viṣṇusamo balī ॥ 2.37.371 ॥
utsādayitvā kṣatrantu kṣatramanyat kariṣyati ॥ 2.37.372 ॥
jāhnavītīramāsādya śarīraṃ yasyate balī ॥ 2.37.373 ॥
tulyakālaṃ bhaviṣyanti sarve hyete mahīkṣitaḥ ॥ 2.37.379 ॥
bhaviṣyantīha yavanā dharmataḥ kāmato'rthataḥ ॥ 2.37.380 ॥
strīṇāṃ balavadhenaiva hatvā caiva parasparam।
bhokṣyanti kaliśeṣe tu vasudhāṃ pārthivāstathā ॥ 2.37.382 ॥
uditoditavaṃśāste uditāstamitāstathā ।
vihīnāstu bhaviṣyanti dharmmataḥ kāmato'rthataḥ।
viparyyayena varttante nāśayiṣyanti vai prajāḥ।
kalkinopahatāḥ sarve mlecchā yāsyanti sarvaśaḥ।
saritparvatasevinyo bhaviṣyanti prajāstadā ॥ 2.37.393 ॥
araṇyānyabhipatsyanti hyāryyā mlecchajanaiḥ saha।
svayaṃ kṛtvā vivatsyanti yathā munijanāstathā ॥ 2.37.397 ॥
pārthivān vyavahāreṇa vibādhantaḥ parasparam ॥ 2.37.399 ॥
evaṃ bhaviṣyanti narāstadādharmme vyavasthitāḥ ॥ 2.37.400 ॥
vṛttyarthamabhilipsantaścariṣyanti vasundharām।
kṣīṇe kaliyuge tasmin divye varṣasahasrake।
ekarātre bhariṣyanti tadā kṛtayugaṃ bhavet ॥ 2.37.405 ॥
