etadvarṣasahasrantu jñeyaṃ pañcādaśaduttaram ॥ 2.37.407 ॥
saptaviṃśatiparyyante kṛtsne nakṣatramaṇḍale।
tebhyaḥ pravarttate kālo divyaḥ saptarṣibhistu taiḥ ॥ 2.37.412 ॥
tato madhyena ca kṣetraṃ dṛśyate yatsamaṃ divi ॥ 2.37.413 ॥
andhrāṃśe sa caturvviśe bhaviṣyanti mate mama ॥ 2.37.415 ॥
upasthāsyanti tān viprāstadā vai vṛttilipsavaḥ ।
pratipannaḥ kaliyugastasya saṅkhyāṃ nibodhata।
divye varṣasahasrantu tatsandhyāṃśaṃ prakīrttitam।
punaruktā bahutvācca na mayā parikīrttitā।
vaivasvate'ntare hyasmin nimivaṃśaḥ samāpyate ॥ 2.37.427 ॥
tathā hi kathayiṣyāmi gadato me nibodhata ॥ 2.37.428 ॥
suvarccāḥ somaputrastu ikṣvākostu bhaviṣyati।
etau kṣatrapraṇetārau caturviṃśe caturyuge ॥ 2.37.430 ॥
devāpirasapatnastu ailādirbhavitā nṛpaḥ ॥ 2.37.431 ॥
kṣatraprāparttakau hyetau bhaviṣyete caturyuge।
kṣīṇe kaliyuge tasmin bhaviṣye tu kṛte yuge।
saptarṣibhistu taiḥ sārddhamādye tretāyuge punaḥ ॥ 2.37.433 ॥
kāle kṛtayuge caiva kṣīṇe tretāyuge punaḥ।
bījārthante bhaviṣyanti brahmakṣatrasya vai punaḥ ॥ 2.37.435 ॥
kṣatrasyaiva samucchedaḥ sambandho vai dvijaiḥ smṛtaḥ।
paramparā yugānāñca brahmakṣatrasya codbhavaḥ।
etena kramayogena ailekṣvākvanvayā dvijāḥ ॥ 2.37.439 ॥
ailasyekṣvākunandasya prakṛtiḥ parivarttate।
manorvaivasvatasyāsmin varttamāne'ntare tu ye ।
nu śakyaṃ vistaraṃ teṣāṃ santānānāṃ paramparā।
tatpūrvāparayogena vaktuṃ varṣaśatairapi ॥ 2.37.450 ॥
punaruktaṃ bahutvācca na śakyantu yugaiḥ saha ॥ 2.37.453 ॥
labhate ca vareṇyañca durlabhāniha laukikān।
dhāraṇācchravaṇāccaiva te lokān dhārayantyuta।
vistareṇānupūrvvī ca kimbhūyo varttayāmyaham ॥ 2.37.456 ॥


## 2.38


      ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।


vistarāvayavañcaiva nisargasya mahātmanaḥ।
vistareṇānupūrvyā ca sarvameva bravīhi me ॥ 2.38.4 ॥

         sūta uvāca।।
bhavatāṃ kathayiṣyāmi sarvametadyathātatham।

manorvaivasvatasyemaṃ sāṃpratasya mahātmanaḥ।

pralayañcaiva lokānāṃ bruvato me nibodhata ॥ 2.38.7 ॥

etānyuktāni vai samyak saptasaptasu vai mayā।

sāvarṇasya pravakṣyāmi manorvaivasvatasya ha।
bhaviṣyasya bhaviṣyanti samāsāttu nibodhata ॥ 2.38.9 ॥





ritastapaśca śukraśca dyutirjyotiḥ prabhākarau ॥ 2.38.14 ॥


prabhurvibhurvibhāsaśca jetā hantā'rihā rituḥ।


damo dātā vidaḥ somo vittavaidyau yamo nidhiḥ।


sāmpratasya bhaviṣyanti sāvarṇasyāntare manoḥ ॥ 2.38.20 ॥

teṣāmindro bhaviṣyastu balirvairocanaḥ purā।

sāvarṇasya manoḥ putrā bhaviṣyanti navaiva tu ॥ 2.38.22 ॥





          ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
bhavettu brahmaṇaścaiva dharmeṇa ca mahātmanaḥ ॥ 2.38.27 ॥

         sūta uvāca।।

jajñire manavaste hi bhaviṣyānāgatāntare ॥ 2.38.29 ॥

prācetasasya dakṣasya dauhitrā manavastu ye।


vaivasvate'ntare prāpte samutpattistayoḥ śubhā।



prajābhistapasā caiva vistaraṃ teṣu vakṣyate।

manvantarādhikāreṣu vartante ca sakṛt sakṛt।




punaruktā bahutvāttu vakṣye na teṣu vistaram।
manvantareṣu bhāvyeṣu bhūteṣvapi tathaiva ca ॥ 2.38.40 ॥


sarvakanyāvaśiṣṭā tu śreṣṭā dharmaparā sutā।

vairājastamupāsīnaṃ dharmeṇa ca bhavena ca।

dakṣa kanyā taveyaṃ vai janayiṣyati suvratā।

brahmaṇo vacanaṃ śrutvā dakṣo dharmo bhavastadā।


upabhogasamarthaiśca sadyojātaiḥ śarīrakaiḥ ॥ 2.38.47 ॥

saṃrabdhā vai vyakarṣanta mama putro mametyuta ॥ 2.38.48 ॥

abhidhyānānmanotpannānūcurvai te parasparam।
yo yasya vapuṣā tulyo bhajatāṃ sa tu taṃ sutam ॥ 2.38.49 ॥


dhruvaṃ rūpaṃ pituḥ putraḥ so'nurudhyati sarvadā।



ruceḥ prajāpateḥ putro raucyo nāmābhavatsutaḥ ॥ 2.38.54 ॥

bhūtyāmutpādito yastu bhautyo nāmābhavatsutaḥ।
vaivasvate'ntare rājā dvau manū tu vivasvatoḥ ॥ 2.38.55 ॥




prathamaṃ merusāvarṇerdakṣaputrasya vai manoḥ।
sambhūtāśca mahātmānaḥ sarve vaivasvate'ntare ॥ 2.38.59 ॥

dakṣaputrasya putrāste rohitasya prajāpateḥ।


vājiyo vājijiccaiva prabhṛtiśca kakudyatha।

tejasmānnathavo dvau tu dvādaśaite marīcayaḥ।

varṇastathāpyaṅgaviśvau muraṇyo vrajano mataḥ।
amito dravaketuśca jambhosthājasraśakrakāḥ ॥ 2.38.64 ॥




sutapāḥ paulavaścaiva saptaite rohitāntare।


daśame tvatha paryāye dharmaputrasya vai manoḥ।
dvitīyasya tu sāvarṇerbhāvyasyaivāntare manoḥ ॥ 2.38.70 ॥


devāste vai bhaviṣyanti dharmaputrasya vai manoḥ ॥ 2.38.72 ॥











paulaho hyagnitejāśca devā hyekādaśe'ntare ॥ 2.38.83 ॥


dvādaśe tvatha paryāye rudraputrasya vai manoḥ ॥ 2.38.85 ॥



aruntijo hariścaiva vidvān yaśca sahasraśaḥ ॥ 2.38.88 ॥


rajaścaiva tu rājaśca svarṇapādastathaiva ca ॥ 2.38.90 ॥


devān sumanaso viddhi sukarmāṇo nibodhata।

vikramaśca kramaścaiva nibhṛtaḥ kānta eva ca।।
ete sukarmaṇo devāḥ sutāṃścaiṣāṃ nibodhata ॥ 2.38.93 ॥

varyyoditastathā jiṣṭo varcasvī dyutimān haviḥ।




ete saptarṣayaḥ siddhā anye sāvarṇike'ntare।

mitravān mitrabinduśca mitraseno hyamitrahā।




devairdevāstrayastriṃśatpṛthaktvena nibodhata ॥ 2.38.103 ॥


divaspatirmahāsattvasteṣāmindro bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.38.105 ॥


paulahastattva darśī ca bhārgavaśca nirutsakaḥ।
niṣprakampastathātreyo nirmohaḥ kaśyapastathā ॥ 2.38.107 ॥

citraseno vicitraśca tapo dharmadhṛto bhavaḥ ॥ 2.38.108 ॥

anekakṣatrabaddhaśca suraso nirbhayaḥ pṛthaḥ।
raucyasyaite manoḥ putrā hyantare tu trayodaśe ॥ 2.38.109 ॥

caturddaśe tu paryāye bhautasyāpyantare manoḥ ।


saptaiva tāṃstān bhāgāṃśca viddhi cākṣuṣasaṃjñakān।


trailokye yāni sattvāni gatimanti dhruvāṇi ca।
sarvaśaḥ svairguṇaistāni indrāste'bhibhavanti vai ॥ 2.38.114 ॥


ojasvī subalaścaiva bhautyasyaite manoḥ sutāḥ ॥ 2.38.116 ॥

eko vaivasvataścaiva sāvarṇo manurucyate ॥ 2.38.117 ॥

raucyo bhautyaśca yau tau tu manoḥ paulahabhārgavau।
bhautyasyaivādhipatye tu pūrṇaḥ kalpastu pūryyate ॥ 2.38.118 ॥

           sūta uvāca।।
ante'nekayuge tasmin kṣīṇe saṃhāra ucyate ॥ 2.38.119 ॥

saptaite bhārgavā devā ante manvantare tadā।
bhuktvā trailokyamadhyasthā yugākhyāṃ hyekasaptatim ॥ 2.38.120 ॥

pitṛbhirmanubhiścaiva sārddhaṃ saptarṣibhistu ye।
yajvānaścaiva te'pyanye tadbhāktāścaiva taiḥ saha ॥ 2.38.121 ॥

maharlokaṃ gamiṣyanti tyaktvā trailokyamīśvarāḥ।
tatasteṣu gateṣūrddhvaṃ kṣīṇe manvantare tadā।
anādhāramidaṃ sarvaṃ trailokyaṃ vai bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.38.122 ॥


tatasteṣu vyatīteṣu trailokyasyeśvareṣviha।

jitādyāśca gaṇā hyatra cākṣuṣāntāścaturdaśa।
manvantareṣu sarvveṣu devāste vai mahaujasaḥ ॥ 2.38.125 ॥

sametya devāste sarve prāpte saṃkalane tadā ॥ 2.38.126 ॥

maharlokaṃ parityajya gaṇāste vai caturddaśa।




tatra yugasahasrāntamaharyardbrahmaṇo viduḥ।

naimittikaḥ prākṛtiko yaścaivātyantiko'rthataḥ।

brāhmo naimittikastasya kalpadāhaḥ prasaṃyamaḥ।


aharante prakurute sargasya pralayaṃ punaḥ।

tato yugasahasrānte saṃprāpte ca yugakṣaye।


tānyevātra pralīyante bhūmitvamupayānti ca।

asahyaraśmirbhagavān pibannambho gabhastibhiḥ।

bhūya eva vivartante vyāpnuvanto vanaṃ śanaiḥ।

tasmādudakaṃ sūryyasya tapato'ti hi kathyate।

tasmādapaḥ piban yā vai dīpyate ravirambare ॥ 2.38.142 ॥

tasya te raśmayaḥ sapta pibantyambho mahārṇavāt।

caturlokamimaṃ sarvaṃ dahanti śikhinastadā ॥ 2.38.144 ॥


khaṃ samāvṛtya tiṣṭhanti nirdahanto vasundharām ॥ 2.38.146 ॥


adhaścordhvaśca tiryak ca saṃruddhaṃ sūryaraśmibhiḥ ॥ 2.38.148 ॥

ekatvamupayātānāmekajvālaṃ bhavatyuta ॥ 2.38.149 ॥

caturlokamidaṃ sarvaṃ nirdahatyāśu tejasā ॥ 2.38.150 ॥



tatastāni pralīyante bhūmitvamupayānti ca ॥ 2.38.153 ॥

sarvvaṃ tadbhasmasāccakre sarvvātmā pāvakastu saḥ ॥ 2.38.154 ॥

samudrebhyo nadībhyaśca pātālebhyaśca sarvataḥ।
pibannapaḥ samiddho'gniḥ pṛthivīmāśrito jvalan ॥ 2.38.155 ॥

lokān saṃharate dīpto ghoraḥ saṃvarttako'nalaḥ ॥ 2.38.156 ॥



udatiṣṭhañchikhāstasya bahvayaḥ saṃvarttakasya tu।
tadā dahati sandīpto golakaṃ caiva sarvvaśaḥ ॥ 2.38.159 ॥

bhūrlokantu bhuvarlokaṃ svarlokañca mahastathā।






indragopanibhāḥ keciduttiṣṭhanti ghanā divi।


tadā jaladharāḥ sarve pūrayanti nabhaḥsthalam ॥ 2.38.168 ॥


tataste jaladā varṣaṃ muñcanti ca mahodyamam।


plāvayanti jagatsarvaṃ bṛhajjālaparisravaiḥ ॥ 2.38.172 ॥

anye tu salilaughaistu velāmabhibhavantyapi।
sādrirdvīpāntaraṃ pṛthvī hyadbhiḥ saṃchādyate tadā ॥ 2.38.173 ॥

tasya vṛṣṭyā ca toyaṃ tatsarvvaṃ hi parimaṇḍitam।
praviśatyudadhau viprāḥ pītaṃ sūryyasya raśmibhiḥ ॥ 2.38.174 ॥



tatastu sahasodbhrāntaḥ payodāṃstānnabhastale।



prasarantyo vrajantyekaṃ salilākhyāṃ bhajantyuta ॥ 2.38.180 ॥


bhasma sarvvamanuprāpya tasmādambho nirucyate ॥ 2.38.182 ॥

nānātve caiva śīghre ca dhāturvai ara ucyate।

tasmin yugasahasrānte divase brahmaṇo gate।


sahasrabāhuḥ prathamaḥ prajāpatistrīyapathe yaḥ puruṣo nirucyate ॥ 2.38.186 ॥

hiraṇyagarbhaḥ puruṣo mahān vai saṃpadyate vai tamasaḥ parastāt ॥ 2.38.187 ॥

caturyugasahasrānte sarvataḥ salilaplute।


janalokavivarttantastapasā labdhacakṣuṣaḥ।




tataste vasate rātriṃ tamasyekārṇave jale ॥ 2.38.194 ॥

tato rātrikṣaye prāpte pratibuddhaḥ prajāpatiḥ।

evaṃ saloke nirvṛtte upaśānte prajāpatau।
brahmanaimittike tasmin kalpite vai prasaṃyame ॥ 2.38.196 ॥

devarṣimanuvaryyeṣu tasmin saṅkalane tadā ॥ 2.38.197 ॥

kalpādāvaprataptāni janamevāśrayanti vai ॥ 2.38.198 ॥

tiryagyonīni satvāni nārakeyāni yānyapi।
jane tānyupapadyante yāvatsaṃplavate jagat ॥ 2.38.199 ॥



yathā sūryasya loke'sminnudayāstamanaṃ smṛtam।



pratyāhāre ca sarge ca gatimanti dhruvāṇi ca ॥ 2.38.205 ॥


vyaktā vyakto mahādevastasya sarvamidaṃ jagat ॥ 2.38.207 ॥

pūrvaprayātena tathā hyapo'nyāstenaiva tenaiva tu saṃvrajanti ॥ 2.38.208 ॥

yathā śūbhena tvaśubhena caiva tatraiva tatraiva vivarttamānāḥ।
martyāstu dehāntarabhāvitattvādravervaśādūrddhvamadhaścaranti ॥ 2.38.209 ॥


saha prajñānisargeṇa saha devaiścaturddaśa ॥ 2.38.211 ॥


etadbrāhmamaho jñeyaṃ tasya saṃkhyāṃ nibodhata।



muhūrttāstu punastriṃśadahorātramiti sthitiḥ।




māgadhenaiva mānena jalaprastho vidhīyate।

samāhani ca rātrau ca muhūrtto vai dvinālikau ॥ 2.38.221 ॥



etaddivyamahorātramiti śāstraviniścayaḥ।



etacchrutvā tu ṛṣayo vismayaṃ paramādbhutam।

        ।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ ।।



ete rātryahanī pūrvaṃ kīrttite tviha taukike।




salilenāplute loke naṣṭe sthāvarajaṅgame ॥ 2.38.234 ॥

nirāloke pradagdhe tu naiśena tu samāvṛte ।

ahorātrastathaivāsya krameṇa parivarttate।

trailokye yāni satvāni gatimanti dhruvāṇi ca।

agre bhūtaḥ prajānāntu tasmādbhūtaḥ prajāpatiḥ।

śāśvate cāmṛtatve ca śabde cābhūtasaṃplavaḥ।

etāvān sthitikālastu hyajasyeha prajāmateḥ।
sthityante pratisargasya brahmaṇaḥ parameṣṭinaḥ ॥ 2.38.240 ॥

tathaivaṃ pratisargeṇa brahmā samupaśāmyati ॥ 2.38.241 ॥


mahatpralīyate'vyakte guṇasāmyaṃ tato bhavet ॥ 2.38.242 ॥

brahmanaimittiko hyeṣa saṃprakṣālanasaṃyamaḥ ॥ 2.38.243 ॥



## 2.39

vāyuruvāca||

te devaiḥ saha tiṣṭhanti maharlokanivāsinaḥ|

ṛṣibhi rdaivataiścaiva saha gandharvarākṣasaiḥ || 39.3 ||



taistathyakāribhiryuktaiḥ śraddhāvadbhiradarpitaiḥ|

          |.ṛṣaya ūcuḥ||
maharloketi yatproktaṃ mātariśvaṃstvayā vibho|
pratiloke ca karttavyamanekaiḥ samadhiṣṭitāḥ || 39.7 ||

yāvantaścaiva te lokā dahyante ye na teprabho|
etannaḥ kathaya prītyā tvaṃ hi vettha yathā tatham || 39.8 ||

evamuktastato vāyurmunibhirvinayātmabhiḥ|
provāca madhuraṃ vākyaṃ yathātattvena tattvavit || 39.9 ||

          vāyuruvāca||






vyaktāni tu pravakṣyāmi sthānānyetāni sapta vai|


satyantu saptamo loko nirālokastataḥ param|

dvitīyaṃ bhuva ityukta antarikṣaṃ tato'bhavat|

vyāhāraistribhiretaistu brahmalokamakalpayat|
tato bhūḥ pārthivo loka antarikṣaṃ tato'bhavat|

svarloko vai divaṃ hyetatpurāṇe niścayaṃ gatam|

vāyurbhuvasyādhipatistena vāyurbhuvapatiḥ|
bhavyasya sūryo'dhipatistena sūryo divaspatiḥ || 39.22 ||

maheti vyāhṛtenaivaṃ maharlokastato'bhavat|

janastu pañcamo lokastasmājjāyanti vai janāḥ |

kalpadagdhe tadā loke pratiṣṭhanti tadā tapaḥ || 39.25 ||


brahmalokastataḥ satyaṃ saptamaḥ sa tu bhāskaraḥ || 39.27 ||

svarlokavāsinaḥ sarve devā bhuvinivāsinaḥ || 39.28 ||

aniketāntarikṣāste bhuvarlaukyā divaukasaḥ || 39.29 ||




maharlokaścaturthastu tasmiṃste kalpavāsinaḥ || 39.33 ||

tān sarvān sapta sūryāste arccirbhirnidahanti vai || 39.34 ||

bhṛguḥ pulastyaḥ pulahaḥ kraturityevamādayaḥ || 39.35 ||

prajānāṃ patayaḥ sarve varttante tatra taiḥ saha|
niḥsattvā nirmamāścaiva tatra te hyūrddhvaretasaḥ || 39.36 ||


ṣaṣṭe kāle nivarttante tattadāharviparyaye || 39.38 ||

satyantu saptamo loko hyapunarmārgagāminām|


sūryadhruvāntaraṃ yacca svargaloko divaḥ smṛtaḥ|
dhruvājjanāntaraṃ yacca maharlokastaducyate || 39.41 ||


bhuve svarge ca ye sarve somapā ājyapāśca te|
caturthe ye'pi varttante maha rlokaṃ samāśritāḥ || 39.43 ||

sadyaścotpadyate teṣāṃ manasā sarvvamīpsitam || 39.44 ||

ete daivā yajante vai yajñaiḥ sarvaiḥ parasparam|

nivarttatītyāsambandho'tīte devagaṇe tataḥ || 39.46 ||



          vāyuruvāca||
pulastyaḥ pulahaścaiva kraturityevamādayaḥ || 39.49 ||




maharlokeṣu dīpteṣu janamevāśrayanti te|
sarve sūkṣmaśarīrāste tatrasthāstu bhavanti te || 39.53 ||

jana loke vivarttante yāvatsaṃplavate jagat || 39.54 ||

aharādau prasūyante pūrvavatkramaśāstviha || 39.55 ||




bhāvino'rthasya ca balāt puṇyākhyātibalena ca || 39.60 ||

nivṛttavṛttayaḥ sarve svasthāḥ sumanasastathā|
vairāje tūpapadyante lokamutsṛjya tajjanam || 39.61 ||

tato'nyenaiva kālena nityayuktāstapasvinaḥ|
kathanāccaiva dharmasya teṣāṃ te jajñire'nvaye || 39.62 ||

devatve ca ṛṣitve ca manuṣyanve ca sarvaśaḥ || 39.63 ||


brahmaloke vivarttaṃte pūrvvapūrvvakrameṇa tu || 39.65 ||

etasmin brahmaloke tu kalpe vairājake gate|
vairājaṃ punarapyeke kalpasthānamakalpayan || 39.66 ||

evaṃ pūrvvānupūrvyeṇa brahmalokagatena vai|
evaṃ teṣu vyatīteṣu tapasā parikalpite|
vairāje tūpapadyante daśakṛtvo nivarttate || 39.67 ||


           ||sūta uvāca|.
evameva na sandeho yathāvatkathitaṃ mayā || 39.69 ||


            ṛṣaya ūcuḥ||
tiṣṭhanti caiva yatkālaṃ tanno brūhi yathātatham || 39.71 ||


tataḥ prāpyanta te sarvve śuddhiśuddhatamāśca ye|


khyātiṃ naimittikīṃ teṣāṃ paryyāye samupasthite || 39.75 ||

sthānatyāge manaścāpi yugapatsaṃpravarttate|
ūcuḥ sarvve tadānyo'nyaṃ vairājāñchuddhabuddhayaḥ || 39.76 ||

brahmaloke pravarttānastannaḥ śreyo bhaviṣyati || 39.77 ||

evamuktvā tadā sarve brahmānte vyavasāyinaḥ|
yojayitvā tadā sarvve varttante yogadharmmiṇaḥ || 39.78 ||

brahmakāyamavarttanta punarāvṛttidurllabham ||. 39.79 ||

lokaṃ taṃ samanuprāpya sarvve te bhāvanāmayam|

vairājebhyastathaivorddhvamantare ṣaḍguṇe tataḥ|
brahmalokaḥ samākhyāto yatra brahmā purohitaḥ || 39.81 ||

te sarve praṇavātmāno buddhaśuddhatapāstathā|

dvandvaiste nābhibhūyante bhāvatrayavivarjjitāḥ|

prabhāvavijayaiśvaryyasthitivairāgyadarśanaiḥ|
tebrahmalaukikāḥ sarve gatiṃ prāpya vivarttanīm || 39.84 ||

brahmaṇā saha devaiśca samprāpte pratisañcare|

ityetadamṛtaṃ śukraṃ nityamakṣayamavyayam|
devarṣayo brahmasatraṃ sanātanamupāsate || 39.86 ||




akāmayuktairye vīrāstapobhirddagdhakilbiṣāḥ|

etadbrahmapadaṃ divyaṃ paramaṃ vyomni bhāsvaram|
gatvā na yatra śocanti hyamarā brahmaṇā saha || 39.91 ||

           ṛṣaya ūcuḥ||
etadveditumicchāmastanno nigada sattama || 39.92 ||

          sūta uvāca||

ekaṃ śatasahasrantu niyutaṃ procyate budhaiḥ || 39.94 ||




koṭīnāṃ sahasramayutamityayaṃ madhya ucyate|
koṭi sahasra niyutā sa cānta iti saṃjñitaḥ || 39.98 ||



arbudaṃ nirbudañcaiva kharbudañca tataḥ smṛtam|
kharvañcaiva nikharvañca śaṅkuḥ padmaṃ tathaiva ca || 39.101 ||

samudraṃ madhyamañcaiva parārddhamaparaṃ tataḥ|


tāvaccheṣo'pi kālo'sya tasyānte pratisṛjyate|


paraṃ brahma paraṃ jñānaṃ paramaiśvaryameva ca|
tasmātparataraṃ bhūtaṃ brahmaṇo'nyanna vidyate || 39.106 ||

pare sthito hyeṣa paraḥ sarvārtheṣu tataḥ paraḥ|




etacchrutvā tu te sarve naimiṣeyāstapasvinaḥ|

papracchurmātariśvānaṃ sarvve te brahmavādinaḥ|
brahmalokastu bhagavan yāvanmātrāntaraḥ prabho || 39.112 ||



           vāyuruvāca||
avyaktādvyaktabhāgo vai mahāsthūlo vibhāṣyate || 39.115 ||


yadabhedyatamaṃ loke vijñeyaṃ paramāṇu tat || 39.117 ||


trasareṇuḥ samākhyātastatpadmaraja ucyate || 39.119 ||







dhanuḥsahasre dve cāpi gavyūtirupadiśyate|

etena dhanuṣā caiva yojanaṃ tu samāpyate|





tasyorddhvañcarate śukrastasmādūrddhvaṃ ca lohitaḥ || 39.132 ||



yo'sau tārāmaye divye vimāne hrasvarūpake|
uttānapādaputro'sau meḍhibhūto dhruvo divi || 39.135 ||

trailokyasyaiṣa utsedho vyākhyāto yojanairmayā|
manvantareṣu devānāmijyā yatraiva laukikī || 39.136 ||

varṇāśramebhya ijyā tu loke'sminyā pravarttate|

trailokyametadvayākhyātamata ūrddhvaṃ nibodhata|
dhruvā dūrddhvaṃ maharloko yasmiste kalpavāsinaḥ|

dve koṭyau tu maharlokādyasmiṃste kalpavāsinaḥ|

caturguṇottarādūrddhvaṃ janalokāttapaḥ smṛtam|





gacchanti ghorakarmmāṇaḥ prāṇino yatra karmmabhiḥ || 39.145 ||

narako rauravo rodhaḥ sūkarastāla eva ca|
taptakumbho mahājvālaḥ śabalo'tha vimocanaḥ || 39.146 ||





rodhe goghno brūṇahā ca hyagnidātā purasya ca|
sūkare brahmahā majjetsurāpaḥ svarṇataskaraḥ || 39.152 ||

tāle patetkṣatriyahā hatvā vaiśyañca durgatim|


sādhvīvikrayakarttā ca yastu bhaktaṃ parityajet|

vedo vikrīyate yena vedaṃ dūṣayate ca yaḥ|

agamyagāmīca naro narakaṃ śabalaṃ vrajet|
vimohe patite caure maryādāṃ yo bhinatti vai || 39.157 ||

duradhvaṃ kurute yastu kīṭalohaṃ prapadyate|
ratnaṃ dūṣayate yastu kṛmibhakṣyaṃ prapadyate || 39.158 ||

lālābhakṣe sa patati durgandhe narake gataḥ || 39.159 ||


nakṣatrairjīvito yaśca naro gacchatyadhomukham || 39.161 ||

evamādīni vikrīṇanghoro pūyavahe patet || 39.162 ||





patanti narake ghore viṅ‌bhuje nātra saṃśayaḥ || 39.167 ||



asi patravane chedī tathā hyaurabhrikāśca ye|

āśramapratyavasitā agnijvāle patanti vai|

skandante yadi vā svapne vratino brahmacāriṇaḥ || 39.172 ||

tesarve narakaṃ yānti niyatantu śvabhojane || 39.173 ||

karmāṇi ye tu kurvanti sarve nirayagāminaḥ || 39.174 ||


evamādi krameṇaiva varṇyamānānnibodhata|

adharmasūnavaste syurandhatāmisrakādayaḥ|
rauravaḥ prathamasteṣāṃ mahā raurava eva ca || 39.177 ||




apratiṣṭe sthitirnāsti bhramastasminsudāruṇaḥ || 39.181 ||



duḥkhotkarṣastu sarveṣu hyadharmasya nimittataḥ || 39.184 ||

ūrddhvaṃ lokaiḥ samāvetau nirālokau ca tāvubhau|

upabhogasamarthaistu sadyo jāyanti karmabhiḥ|

duḥkhotkarṣastu sarveṣu hyadharmasya nimittataḥ || 39.187 ||

tiryagyonau prasūyante karmaśeṣe gate tataḥ|

dharmādharmanimittena sadyo jāyanti mūrttayaḥ|
upabhogārthamutpattiraupapattikakarmataḥ || 39.189 ||

paśyanti nārakāndevā hyadhovakrān hyadhogatān|

tasmādūrddhvamadhobhāvo lokāloke na vidyate || 39.191 ||


        ṛṣaya ūcuḥ||


            vāyuruvāca||
na śakyā jantavaḥ kṛtsnāḥ prasaṃkhyātuṃ kathañcana|
anādyantāśca saṃkīrṇā hyapyūhena vyavasthitāḥ|
gaṇanā vinivṛttaiṣāmānantyena prakīrttitāḥ || 39.195 ||




audakā jantavaḥ sarve niścayāttadvicāritam || 39.199 ||



bhāgena dvipadā jñeyā laukike'smiṃstu sarvvaśaḥ || 39.202 ||

yaḥ sahasratamo bhāgo bhāge tu dvipadāṃ punaḥ|
dhārmmikāstena bhāgena vijñeyāḥ sammitāḥ punaḥ || 39.203 ||

sahasreṇaiva bhāgena dhārmmikebhyo divaṅgatāḥ|


uṣṇastu rauravo jñeyastejo ghorarasātmakaḥ || 39.206 ||



         ṛṣaya ūcuḥ|
maho janastapaḥ satyaṃ bhūto bhāvyo bhavastathā|
lokāntarañca yādṛgvai tanno brūhi yathātatham || 39.209 ||

         vāyuruvāca||






vibutvātkhalu yogāgnirbrahmaṇo'nugraherataḥ|

akṣaraṃ dhruvamavyagramaṣṭamantvaupasargikam|


vistareṇānupūrvyā ca bruvato me nibodhata|
trayodaśaiva koṭyastu niyutā daśa pañca ca|
bhūrlokādbrahmaloko vaiyojanaiḥ samprakīrttyate || 39.220 ||


eṣorddhvagapracārastu gatyantañca tataḥ smṛtam |




vidhiranyairanaupamyaḥ paramāṇurmaheśvaraḥ|

yadaṇḍamāsītsauvarṇaṃ prathamantvaupasargikam|


viburlokasya sṛṣṭyarthaṃ lokasaṃsthānameva ca|
sannisargaḥ sa tanvā ca lokadhāturmahātmanaḥ || 39.229 ||

tayormadhye puraṃ divyaṃ sthānaṃ yasya manomayam || 39.230 ||

tadvigrahavataḥ sthānamīśvarasyāmitaujasaḥ|


madhyāhnārkaprakāśena paratejo'bhimardinā|


na tatra kramate mṛtyurna tapo na jarā śramāḥ || 39.235 ||

na hi tasya purasyānyairupamāṃ kartumarhati|

tatpuraṃ govṛṣāṅkasya tejasā vyāpya tiṣṭhati|
bhāvena manaso bhūmirvinyastā kanakāmayī || 39.237 ||

ratnavālukayā tatra vinyastā śuśubhe'dhikam|
śāradendurpakāśāni bālasūryyanibāni ca || 39.238 ||

arddha śvetārddharaktāni sauvarṇāni tathaiva ca|
rathacakrapramāṇāni nālairmarakataprabhaiḥ || 39.239 ||

saukumāreṇa rūpeṇa gandhināpratimena ca|
tatra divyāni padmāni vaneṣūpavaneṣu ca || 39.240 ||

bhṛṅgapatranikāśāni tapanīyāni yāni ca|
arddhakṛṣṇārddharaktāni sukumārāntarāṇi ca || 39.241 ||

ātapatra pramāṇāni paṅkajaiḥ saṃvṛtāni ca|
bhūyaḥ sapta mahānadyastāsānnāmāni bodhata || 39.242 ||

varamā varabhadrā ca ramyāstasminpurottame || 39.243 ||

padmotpaladalonmiśraṃ phenādyāvarttavigraham|
jalaṃ maṇidalaprakhyamāvahanti saridvarāḥ || 39.244 ||

na tu brahmarṣayo devā nāsurāḥ pitaras tathā|
na khalvanye'prameyasya vidurīśasya tatpuram || 39.245 ||

tatra ye dhyānamavyagrāḥ suyuktā vijitendriyāḥ|

madhye puravarendrasya tasyāpratimatejasaḥ|
sumahānmerusaṅgāśo divyo bhadraśriyā vṛtaḥ || 39.247 ||

anupameyai ratnaiśca sarvataḥ sa vibhūṣitaḥ || 39.248 ||


rājataiścāpi śuśubhe indranīlamayaiḥ śubhaiḥ|
dṛḍhairvajramayaiścaiva ityevaṃ sumahāhitaiḥ || 39.250 ||

jalaiśca vividhākārairdīpyadbhiradhivāsitam|

rukmaghaṇṭāninādaiśca nityapramuditotsavaḥ|
kinnarāṇāmadhīvāsaiḥ sandhyābhrākārarājitaiḥ || 39.252 ||

parivārasamantāttu hemapuṣpodakaprabhaiḥ|
yathā hi meruśailendro hemaśrṛṅgairvirājate || 39.253 ||

cāmīkaramayībhistu patākābhistathā puram |
evaṃ prāsādarājo'sau bhūmikābhirvirājate || 39.254 ||

vasantapratimā yatra tryambakasya niveśane|
lakṣmīḥ śrīśca vapurmmāyā kīrttiḥ śobhā sarasvatī || 39.255 ||

devyā vai sahitā hyetā rūpagandhasamanvitāḥ|
bhūṣaṇaṃ sarvaratnānāṃ yonyaḥ kāntivilāsayoḥ || 39.256 ||

koṭiśataṃ mahābhāgā vibhajyātmānamātmanā|
bhagavantaṃ mahātmānaṃ pratimodantyatandritāḥ| || 39.257 ||

tāsāṃ sahasraśaścānyāḥ pṛṣṭhataḥ paricārikāḥ|
rūpiṇyaśca śriyā yuktāḥ sarvāḥ kamalalocanāḥ || 39.258 ||

līlāvilāsasaṃyuktairbhāvairatimanoharaiḥ|
gaṇaistāḥ saha modante śailābhaiḥ pāvakopamaiḥ || 39.259 ||

kubjā kāmanikāmaiśca varagātrā hayānanāḥ |
puṇḍrāśca vikaṭāścaiva karalāścipiṭānanāḥ || 39.260 ||


gajānanāstathaivānyāḥ siṃha vyāghrānanāstathā|
lohitākṣā mahāstanyaḥ subhagāścārulocanāḥ || 39.262 ||

anyāśca kāmarūpiṇyo nānāveṣadharāḥ striyaḥ || 39.263 ||

abhyantarapariskandhā devāvāsagṛhocitāḥ|

nandinā ca gaṇaiḥ sārddhaṃ viśvarūpairmahātmabhiḥ|
tathā rudragaṇaiścāpi tulyaudāryyaparākramaiḥ || 39.265 ||

pāvakātmajasaṅkāśairyūpadaṃṣṭrotkaṭānanaiḥ|

sarvartukusumāṃ mālāṃ jighramāṇorasi sthitān |

īṣatkarāllamboṣṭhaṃ tīkṣṇadaṃṣṭrā gaṇāñacitam|
ṣaḍūrddhvanetraṃ duṣprekṣyaṃ rucirañcīravāsasam || 39.268 ||

āhaveṣvaparikliṣṭaṃ devānāmarināśanam|
bāhunā bāhumāveśya pārśve savye'ntare sthitam || 39.269 ||

rarājāpadiśantasya vāmāgrakaragocaram|
mahābhairavanirghoṣaṃ balenāpratimaujasam|
daśavarṇadhanuścaiva vicitraṃ śobhate'dhikam || 39.270 ||

triśūlaṃ vidyutābhāsamamoghaṃ śatrunāśanam|
jājvalyamānaṃ vapuṣā paramaṃ tattviṣā yutam || 39.271 ||

asiścaivaujasāṃ śreṣṭhaḥ śītaraśmiḥ śaśī tathā|
tejasā vapuṣā kāntyā deveśasya mahātmanaḥ|
śuśubhe'bhyadhikaṃ tatra vedyāmagniśikhā iva || 39.272 ||

sthitaḥ purastāddevasya śātakaumbhamayo mahān|
śuśubhe ruciraḥ śrīmānsodakaḥ sakamaṇḍaluḥ || 39.273 ||

asimāveśya cāṅgeṣu pāṇḍurāmbara dhāriṇī|
uraśchadena mahatā mauktikena virājitā|
caturbhujā mahābhāgā vijayā lokasammatā || 39.274 ||

devyā ādyaḥ pratīhārī śrīrivāpratimā parā|
vibhrājatī sthitā caiva kṛtvā devasya cāñjalim || 39.275 ||

tasyāḥ pṛṣṭānugāścānyāḥ striyo'psarogaṇānvitāḥ|
tāḥ khalvabhinavaiḥ kāntairupatiṣṭhanti śaṅkaram || 39.276 ||

upagāyanti deveśaṃ gaṇā gandharvayonayaḥ || 39.277 ||

abhyunnato mahoraskaḥ śaranmeghasamadyutiḥ|
śobhate nandamānaśca gopatistasya veśmani || 39.278 ||

skandaśca saparīvāraḥ putro'syāmitavīryyavān|
raktāmbaradharaḥ śrīmānvarāmbujadalekṣaṇaḥ || 39.279 ||

vyapetavyasanākrūrāḥ prajānāṃ pālane ratāḥ || 39.280 ||

taiḥ sārddhaṃ sa mahāvīryyaḥ śobhate śikhivāhanaḥ |
vyālakrīḍanakaistatra krīḍate viśvatomukhaḥ || 39.281 ||

ye nṛpā vibhudhendrāṇāṃ kāñcanasya pradāyinaḥ|
ye ca svāyatanā viprā gṛhasthā brahmavādinaḥ || 39.282 ||

gūḍhasvādhyāya tapasastathā caivoñchavṛttayaḥ|
ete sabhāsadastasya deveśasya ca sammatāḥ || 39.283 ||

manvantarāṇyanekāni vyavarttanta punaḥ punaḥ|

vyāghrāścaivānugāstatra kāñcanābhāstarasvinaḥ|
svacchandacāriṇaḥ sarve svayaṃ devena nirmmitāḥ|| 39.285 ||

mṛtyormṛtyusamāste tu yamadarpāpahāriṇaḥ |
vibhūtimapyasaṃkhyeyāṃ ko na khalvabhidhāsyate || 39.286 ||

ataḥparamidaṃ bhūyo bhavenādbhutamuttamam|

mandarādiprakāśānāṃ balenāpratimaujasām|
hārakundenduvarṇānāṃ vidyudvananinādinām || 39.288 ||

cūḍāmaṇidharāṇāṃ vai meghasannibhavāsasām|
śrīvatsāṅkitavajrāṇāmaṅgulīśūlapāṇinām || 39.289 ||

evaṃ diśānāṃ devānāṃ rūpeṇottamaśālinām|
tasya prāsādamukhyasya stambheṣūttamaśobhiṣu || 39.290 ||

saṃyatāgnimayībhistu śrṛṅkhalābhiḥ pṛthakpṛthak|
māyāsahasraṃ siṃhānāṃ sukhaṃ tatra nivāsinām || 39.291 ||

stambhe'pyapāsṛtāṣaṣṭaṃ(?) tryambakasya niveśane|
atha tatpratisaṃpūjya vāyorvākyaṃ suvismitāḥ|

bhagavansarvabhūtānāṃ prāṇa sarvatraga prabho|
ke te siṃhamahābhūtāḥ kva te jātāḥ kimātmakāḥ || 39.293 ||

siṃhāḥ kenāparādhena bhūtānāṃ prabhaviṣṇunā|
vaiśvānaramayaiḥ pāśaiḥ saṃruddhāstu pṛthakpṛthak || 39.294 ||

vyapanīya svakāddehātkrodhāste siṃhavigrahāḥ || 39.295 ||

bhūtānāmabhayaṃ dattvā purābaddhāgnibandhane|
yajñabhāganimittaṃ ca īśvarasyājñayā tadā || 39.296 ||

teṣāṃ vidhānamuktena siṃhenaikena līlayā|

niḥsṛtā ca mahādevyā mahākālī maheśvarī|

vīrabhadro'prameyātmā devyā manyupramārjjanaḥ || 39.299 ||

tasya veśma surendrasya sarvaguhyatamasya vai|
sanniveśastvanaupamyo mayā vaḥ parikīrttitaḥ || 39.300 ||


sarvakāmasamṛddheṣu vanopavanaśobhiṣu || 39.302 ||





anaupamyairvarai ratnaiḥ sarvataḥ paribhūṣitaḥ || 39.307 ||



        ṛṣaya ūcuḥ||
anugrāhyatamāḥ samyak pramodante purottame|

         vāyuruvāca||
śrūyatāṃ devadevasya bhaktiryairanukalpitā|

madhyāhārāśca mātrāśca hyātmārāmā jitendriyāḥ|
jitadvandvā mahotsāhāḥ saumyā vigatamatsarāḥ || 39.312 ||

bhāvasthāḥ sarvvabhūtānāmavyāpārā anākulāḥ|
ananyamanaso bhūtvā prapannā ye maheśvaram || 39.313 ||






krīḍante vividhairbhāvairbhogān prāpya sudurlabhān || 39.319 ||

svacchandagatayaḥ siddhāḥ siddhaiścānyaurvibodhitāḥ|

ebhiḥ saha mahātmā hi devadevo maheśvaraḥ|




         ṛṣaya ūcuḥ||
īśvarasyottamaṃ puṇyamaṣṭamantvaupasargikam || 39.325 ||

yo gandhena samṛddhaṃ vai paramaṃ paramātmanaḥ || 39.326 ||

svena māhātmyayogena sahasrasyāmitaujasaḥ || 39.327 ||

yasya bhakteṣvasaṃmoho hyanukampārthameva ca|

jyotsnayā vyāpya khaṃ candraṃ vinyastā viśvarūpadhṛk |
vibhūtirbhrājate'tyarthaṃ devadevasya veśmani || 39.329 ||

tatsarvaṃ nikhilenedaṃ vaktrādamṛtanisravam || 39.330 ||

apītvā khalu sarvasya bhaktyāsmābhistu suvratāḥ|
praśnaṃ devavara prāṇa yathāvadvaktumarhasi || 39.331 ||

           sūta uvāca||
sa khalūvāca bhagavānkiṃ bhūyo varttayāmyaham|

          ṛṣaya ūcuḥ||

kimavasthā bhavantyete tanno brūhi yathārthavat || 39.334 ||

ete ye vai tvayā proktāḥ siṃhavyāghragaṇaiḥ saha|
ye cānye siddhisamprāptā mātariśvā jagāda ha || 39.335 ||

idañca paramaṃ tattvaṃ samākhyāsyāmi śrṛṇvatām|



mahābhūtavināśānte pralaye pratyupasthite || 39.339 ||







viṣṇunā saha saṃyuktaṃ karoti vikaroti ca|
sa rudro yaḥ sāmamayastathaiva ca yajurmayaḥ || 39.346 ||



ananyamanaso bhūtvā prapadyante maheśvaram || 39.349 ||


prapadya parayā bhaktyā jñānayuktena cetasā|


labhate rudrasālokyaṃ bhaktimānvigatajvaraḥ|
amadyapaśca yaḥ śūdro bhavabhakto jitendriyaḥ || 39.353 ||


madyapo madyapaiḥ sārddhaṃ bhūtasaṅghaiśca modate|



## 2.40

।।sūta ūvāca।।


upasthite mahāghore hyapratyakṣe tu kasyacit ॥ 2.40.3 ॥

ante drumasya yamprāpte paścimasya manostadā ।
ante kaliyuge tasminkṣīṇe saṃhāra ucyate ॥ 2.40.4 ॥

samprakṣāle tadā vṛtte pratyāhāre hyupasthite।

svabhāvakārite tasminpravṛtte pratisañcare ॥ 2.40.6 ॥

āttagandhā tato bhūmiḥ pralayatvāya kalpate ।
praviṣṭe gandhatanmātre toyāvasthā dharā bhavet ॥ 2.40.7 ॥

sarvamāpūrayitvedaṃ tiṣṭhanti vicaranti ca ॥ 2.40.8 ॥

apāmasti guṇo yastu jyotiṣe līyate rasaḥ।

graste ca salile tejaḥ sarvvatomukhamīkṣyate ॥ 2.40.10 ॥

athāgniḥ sarvato vyāpta ādatte tajjalantadā।
sarvamāpūryyate'rcirbhistadā jagadidaṃ śanaiḥ ॥ 2.40.11 ॥

arcirbhiḥ santate tasmiṃstiryagūrddhvamadhastataḥ ।
pralīyate tadā tasmindīpārciriva mārute ॥ 2.40.12 ॥

upaśāmyati tejo hi vāyunā dhūyate mahat ॥ 2.40.13 ॥

nirāloke tadā loke vāyubhūte ca tejasi।


arūpamarasasparghamagandhaṃ na ca mūrtimat ॥ 2.40.16 ॥



bhūtendriyeṣu yughapadbhūtādau saṃsthiteṣu vai।


buddhirmanaśca liṅgaśca mahānakṣara eva ca।

svātmanyeva sthite caiva kāraṇe lokakāraṇe ॥ 2.40.22 ॥

vinivṛtte tadā sarge prakṛtyāvasthitena vai ।

āgatāgatikatvācca grahaṇaṃ tanna vidyate ॥ 2.40.24 ॥

bhāvagrāhyānumānācca cintayitvedamucyate।
sthite tu kāraṇe tasminnitye sadasadātmike ॥ 2.40.25 ॥

evaṃ saptādayo'bhyastātkramātprakṛtayastu vai ॥ 2.40.26 ॥

pratyāhāre tadā sarge praviśyanti parasparam।

saptadvīpasamudrāntaṃ saptalokaṃ saparvatam।



etau saṃhāravistārau brahmāvyaktau tataḥ punaḥ ॥ 2.40.31 ॥

sṛjate grasate caiva vikārānsargasaṃyame।


avyaktaṃ kṣetramityāhurbrahma kṣetrajña ucyate।




avyūhā śaṅkarāccaiva jyotirvacca vyavasthitaḥ।
yasmātpratiśarīraṃ hi sukhaduḥkhopalabdhitā।


nivarttate tadā tasya sthitirāgaḥ svayambhuvaḥ।
sahasā yojyakaiḥ sarvairbrahmaloka nivāsibhiḥ ॥ 2.40.40 ॥







tattvaprasaṃyamo hyeṣa smṛto hyāvarttako dvijāḥ ॥ 2.40.47 ॥

           sūta uvāca।।
ūrddhva bhāvo hyadhobhāvo sukhaduḥkhe priyāpriye ॥ 2.40.48 ॥

sarvametatprayātasya guṇamātrātmakaṃ smṛtam।

yonyavasthā svabhāve ca dehināṃ tu niṣicyate ॥ 2.40.50 ॥

ye jayanti punardehe dehānyatve tathaiva ca ॥ 2.40.51 ॥

karaṇaiḥ svaiḥ pracīyete kāyatveneha jantubhiḥ ॥ 2.40.52 ॥

sarge ca pratisarge ca saṃsāre caiva jantavaḥ।
saṃyujyante viyujyante karaṇaiḥ sañcaranti ca ॥ 2.40.53 ॥


tayoḥ pravarttakaṃ madye ihaivāvarttakaṃ rajaḥ ॥ 2.40.55 ॥

ityevaṃ parivarttante trayaḥ srotoguṇātmakāḥ।


tama sābhibhavājjanturyāthātathyaṃ na vindati।
atatddarśanātso'tha trividhaṃ badhyate tataḥ ॥ 2.40.58 ॥

prākṛtena ca bandhena tathā vaikārikena ca।
dakṣiṇābhistṛtīyena baddho'tyantaṃ vivarttate ॥ 2.40.59 ॥

anitye nityasaṃjñā ca duḥkhe ca sukhadarśanam ॥ 2.40.60 ॥


karmmajastu punardeho mahāduḥkhaṃ pravarttate ॥ 2.40.62 ॥


tailapālīkavajjīvastatraiva pari varttate ॥ 2.40.64 ॥


jñānādvijayate sarvaṃ tyāgād‌buddhirvirajyate।
vairāgyācchuddhyate cāpi śuddhaḥ sattvena mucyate ॥ 2.40.66 ॥



brahmādau sthāvarānte vai saṃsāre hyādhibhautike।


eṣa mārgo hi niradhitiryagyonau ca kāraṇam।
tiryagyonigatañcaiva kāraṇaṃ sa nirucyate ॥ 2.40.71 ॥


anaiśvaryyantu tatsarvaṃ pratighātātmakaṃ smṛtam ।

sattvasthamātrakaṃ cittaṃ yathā sattvapradarśanāt।


tena baddhasya vai bandho mokṣo muktasya tena ca ।
saṃsāre vinivṛtte tu mukto liṅgena mucyate ॥ 2.40.76 ॥

niḥsambandho hyacaitanyaḥ svātmanyevāvatiṣṭhate।
svātmavyavasthitaścāpi virūpākhyena likhyate ॥ 2.40.77 ॥

sa cāpi trividhaḥ prokto mokṣo vai tattvadarśibhiḥ ॥ 2.40.78 ॥


nirañjanatvācchuddhastu tato netā na vidyate।





anityamaśivaṃ duḥkhamiti buddhyānucintya ca।

tataḥ prayāṇakāle hi doṣairnaimittikaistathā ॥ 2.40.86 ॥


śaityātprakupito vāyurūrddhvantu kramate tataḥ ॥ 2.40.88 ॥



śarīraṃ prajahaṃso vai nirucchvāsastato bhavet।

yatheha loke khadyotaṃ nīyamānamitastataḥ ।
rañjanaṃ tadvadhe yattu netā netā na vidyate ॥ 2.40.92 ॥









tiryyagūrddhvamadhastācca dhāvato'pi yathākramam।

paryyāyavācakaiḥ śabdairekārthaiḥ so'bhilikhyate।


yatheṣṭaṃ pariniryyāti bhinne dehe sunirvṛte ॥ 2.40.103 ॥



prakṛtiṃ satyamityāhu rvikāro'nṛtamucyate ॥ 2.40.106 ॥


kṣetrapratyayato yasmātkṣetrajñaḥ śubha ucyate ॥ 2.40.108 ॥




tameva ca vikārantu yasmādvai kṣarate punaḥ।

sukhaduḥkhamohabhāvā bhojyamityabhidhīyate।
acetatvāddhi viṣayastaddhi dharmmavibhuḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.40.113 ॥

na kṣīyate na kṣarati vikāraprasṛtantu tat।

yasmātpuryyanuśete ca tasmātpuruṣa ucyate।
purapratyayiko yasmātpūruṣetyabhidhīyate ॥ 2.40.115 ॥

puruṣaṃ kathayasvātha kathantajjñairvibhāṣyate।

asti nāstīti so'nyo vā baddho mukto gataḥ sthitaḥ।
nairhetikāntanirddeśyasūktastasminna vidyate ॥ 2.40.117 ॥

bhāvagrāhyamanumānyaṃ cintayanna pramuhyate ॥ 2.40.118 ॥


evaṃ jñātvā sa vijñātā tataḥ śāntiṃ niyacchati।
kāryye ca kāraṇe caiva buddhyādau bhautike tadā ॥ 2.40.120 ॥

saṃprayukto viyukto vā jīvato vā mṛtasya ca।




yajjñātvā na nivarttante kṣetrajñe tu nirañjane।

apratarkyamacintyatvādavāpyatvācca sarvaśaḥ।
nābhilimpati tattattvaṃ samprāpya manasā saha ॥ 2.40.126 ॥

vyape(ye)tasukhaduḥkhe ca niruddhe śāntimāgate ॥ 2.40.127 ॥

nirātmake punastasminvācyāvācyo na vidyate।
etau saṃhāravistārau vyaktāvyaktau tataḥ punaḥ ॥ 2.40.128 ॥

sṛjate grasate caiva grastaḥ paryavatiṣṭhate।

adhiṣṭānapravṛttena tasya te buddhipūrvakam।
sādharmyavaidharmyakṛtasaṃyogo vidhitastayoḥ।

yāvacca sargapratisargakālastāvacca tiṣṭhati susannirudhya।
pūrvaṃ hitavye tadabuddhipūrvaṃ pravarttate tatpuruṣārthameva ॥ 2.40.131 ॥

anādyanantā hyabhimānapūrvakaṃ vitrāsayantī jagadabhyupaiti ॥ 2.40.132 ॥

ukto hyasmiṃstadātyantaṃ kavibhistatpramucyate ॥ 2.40.133 ॥

ityeṣa pratisargovastrividhaḥ kīrttito mayā।।


## 2.41

।।ṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।


atyadbhutāni karmmāṇi vidhimāndharmmaniścayaḥ।
vicitrāśca kathāyogā janma cāgryamanuttamam ॥ 2.41.3 ॥


papracchuḥ satriṇaḥ sarve punaḥ sargapravarttanam ॥ 2.41.5 ॥

bandheṣu sampralīneṣu guṇasāmye tamomaye ॥ 2.41.6 ॥

apravṛttau brahmaṇastu mahāsāyujyagaistadā ।
kathaṃ prapatsyate sargastannaḥ prabrūhi pṛcchatām ॥ 2.41.7 ॥

vyākhyātumupacakrāma punaḥ sargapravarttanam ॥ 2.41.8 ॥

ahaṃ vau varttayiṣyāmi yathā sargaḥ prapatsyate।

tasmādvāco nivarttante hyaprāpya manasā saha ॥ 2.41.10 ॥

avyaktavat parokṣatvādghrahaṇaṃ taddurāsadam।


sattvamātrātmako dharmmo guṇasattve pratiṣṭitaḥ।
tamomātrātmako'dharmmo guṇe tamasi tiṣṭhati ॥ 2.41.13 ॥

avibhāgavatāvetau guṇasāmyasthitāvubhau।
sarvakāryye buddhipūrvaṃ pradhānasya prapatsyate ॥ 2.41.14 ॥

evaṃ tānabhimānena prapatsyeta purastadā ॥ 2.41.15 ॥

yadā pravarttitavyantu kṣetrakṣetrajñayordvayoḥ।
bhojyabhoktṛtvasambandhaṃ prapatsyete yutāvubhau ॥ 2.41.16 ॥


tataḥ prapatsyate vyaktaṃ kṣetrakṣetrajñayordvayoḥ।

kṣetrajñasya pradhānasya puruṣasya prapatsyate ॥ 2.41.19 ॥

brahmāṇḍe prathamaḥ so'tha bhavitā ceśvaraḥ punaḥ।
tato jñeyasya kṛtsnasya sarvabhūtapatiḥ śivaḥ ॥ 2.41.20 ॥

īśvaraḥ sarvvamuktānāṃ brahmā brahmamayo mahān।

anādyau svayamutpannāvubhau sūkṣmau tu tau smṛtau ।
anādisaṃyogayutau sarvakṣetrajñameva ca ॥ 2.41.22 ॥

abuddhi pūrvakaṃ yuktau maśakau tu varau tadā।
apratyayamanādyaṃ ca sthitāvudakamapsyaśaḥ ॥ 2.41.23 ॥

pravṛtte pūrvataḥ pūrvaṃ punaḥ sarge prapatsyate।
ajñāguṇaiḥ pravarttante rajaḥsattvatamātmakam ॥ 2.41.24 ॥


satyābhidhyāyinastasya dhyāyinaḥ sannimittakam।
rajaḥsattvatamā vyaktā vidharmmāṇaḥ parasparam ॥ 2.41.26 ॥

saṃsiddhakāryyakaraṇā utpadyante'bhimāninaḥ ॥ 2.41.27 ॥

sarve satvāḥ prapadyante hyavyaktātpūrvameva ca।

saṃsarantastu te sarve stānaprakaraṇaiḥ saha।
kāryyāṇi pratipatsyante utpadyante punaḥ punaḥ ॥ 2.41.29 ॥

guṇamātrātmakāścaiva dharmmādharmmau parasparam।

guṇāstatpratidhāvante tasmāttattasya rocate ॥ 2.41.31 ॥


tadbhāvitāḥ prapadyante tasmāttattasya rocate ॥ 2.41.33 ॥


samāsādeva vakṣyāmi brahmaṇo'tha samudbhavam ॥ 2.41.35 ॥



yugapat sampravarttante bhūtānyevendriyāṇi ca।
bhūtabhedāśca bhūtebhya iti sargaḥ pravarttate ॥ 2.41.38 ॥






prakriyā prathame pāde kathāvastuparigrahaḥ।


naimiṣe satramāsādya munibhyo munisattamāḥ ।
tatprasādādasaṃdigdhaṃ bhūtotpattilayāni ca ॥ 2.41.46 ॥

samyagviditvā medhāvī na mohamadhigacchati ॥ 2.41.47 ॥


brahmasāyujyago bhūtvā brahmaṇā saha mokṣyate ॥ 2.41.49 ॥



sa sarvairmucyate pāpaiḥ puṇyañca mahadāpnuyāt ॥ 2.41.52 ॥

dhūtapāpmā jitasvargo brahmabhūyāya kalpate ॥ 2.41.53 ॥


niruktamasya yo veda sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate ॥ 2.41.55 ॥

itihāsamimaṃ śrutvā dharmāya vidadhe matim ॥ 2.41.56 ॥

brahmasāyujyago bhūtvā daivataiḥ saha modate ॥ 2.41.57 ॥


bṛhaspatistu prevāca savitre tadanantaram ॥ 2.41.59 ॥


sārasvatastridhāmne ca tridhāmā ca śaradvate।


gautamāya bharadvājaḥ so'pi niryyantare punaḥ ॥ 2.41.63 ॥

niryantarastu provāca tathā vājaśravāya ca।



        śāṃśapāyana uvāca ।।

mayā vai tatpunaḥ proktaṃ putrāyāmitabuddhaye।

namaskāryyaśca guravaḥ prayatnena manīṣibhiḥ।


nāhitāya pradātavyaṃ pavitramidamuttamam ॥ 2.41.70 ॥

avyaktaṃ vai yasya yoniṃ vadanti vyaktaṃ dehaṃ kālamantargatañca ।

sarvāṇi cāṅgāni tathaiva tāni vidyāssarvā yasya pucchaṃ vadanti ॥ 2.41.72 ॥



## 2.42

।।śaunakādiṛṣaya ūcuḥ।।
vyāsaprasādādhigataśāstrasambodhanena ca ॥ 2.42.1 ॥
upakramopasaṃhāra vidhinoktāni kṛtsnaśaḥ ॥ 2.42.2 ॥
caturdaśasahasraṃ ca mātsyaṃ proktamatisphuṭam।
tatsaṃkhyakaṃ bhaviṣyañca proktaṃ pañcaśatādhikam ॥ 2.42.3 ॥
mārkaṇḍeyaṃ mahāramyaṃ proktaṃ navasahasrakam।
kathitaṃ brahmavaivarttamaṣṭādaśasahasrakam ॥ 2.42.4 ॥
śatottarañca brahmāṇḍaṃ sūryasaṃkhyāsahasrakam ।
atha bhāgavataṃ divyamaṣṭādaśasahasrakam ॥ 2.42.5 ॥
sahasrāṇi daśaivoktaṃ purāṇaṃ brahmanāmakam।
ayutaślokaghaṭitaṃ purāṇaṃ vāmanābhidham ॥ 2.42.6 ॥
tathaivāyuta saṃkhyātaṃ ṣaṭśatādhikamādikam।
ekonaviṃśasāhasraṃ vainateyamudāhṛtam ॥ 2.42.8 ॥
sahasrapañcapañcāśatproktaṃ pādmaṃ suvistaram।
saptadaśasahasrantu kūrmaṃ proktaṃ manoharam ॥ 2.42.9 ॥
caturviṃśati sāhasraṃ saukaraṃ paramādbhutam।
ekāśītisahasrāṇi skāndamuktaṃ suvistṛtam ॥ 2.42.10 ॥
evamaṣṭādaśoktāni purāṇāni bṛhanti ca।
purāṇeṣveṣu bahavo dharmāste vinirūpitāḥ ॥ 2.42.11 ॥
gaṅgādyā yā mahānadyo yajñavratatapāṃsi ca ॥ 2.42.13 ॥
anekavidhadānāni yamāśca niyamaiḥ saha।
yogadharmā bahuvidhāḥ sāṃkhyā bhāgavatāstathā ॥ 2.42.14 ॥
bhaktimārgā jñānamārgā vairāgyānilanīrajāḥ।
upāsanavidhiścoktaḥ karmasaṃśuddhicetasām ॥ 2.42.15 ॥
ṣaḍdarśanāni coktāni svabhāvaniyatāni ca ॥ 2.42.16 ॥
etadanyacca vividhaṃ purāṇeṣu nirupitam।
ataḥ paraṃ kimapyasti na vā boddhavyamuttamam ॥ 2.42.17 ॥
na jñāyeta yadi vyāso gopayedatha vā bhavān ।
          sūta uvāca।।
atigopyataraṃ divyamanākhyeyaṃ pracakṣate ॥ 2.42.19 ॥
parāśarasuto vyāsaḥ kṛtvā paurāṇikīṃ kathām।
sarvavedārthaghaṭitāṃ cintayāmāsa cetasi ॥ 2.42.20 ॥
varṇāśramavatāṃ dharmo mayā samyagudāhṛtaḥ।
muktimārgā bahuvidhā uktā vedāvirodhataḥ ॥ 2.42.21 ॥
jīveśvarabrahmabhedo nirastaḥ sūtranirṇaye।
nirūpitaṃ paraṃ brahma śrutiyuktavicārataḥ ॥ 2.42.22 ॥
yadarthaṃ brahmacaryādivānaprasthayativratam ॥ 2.42.23 ॥
ācaranti mahāprājñā dhāraṇāñca pṛthagvidhām।
dhyānaṃ samādhiretāni yamaiśca niyamaiḥ saha।
aṣṭāṅgāni yadarthañca caranti munipuṅgavāḥ ॥ 2.42.25 ॥
yadarthaṃ karma kurvanti vedājñāmātratatparāḥ।
parārpaṇadhiyā samyag niṣkāmāḥ kalilojjhitāḥ ॥ 2.42.26 ॥
yajjñaptaye nirākarttuṃ pāpācaraṇamātmanaḥ।
gaṅgāditīrthacaryāṇi niṣevante śucivratāḥ ॥ 2.42.27 ॥
tadbrahma paramaṃ śuddhamanādyantamanāmayam।
nityaṃ sarvagataṃ sthāṇu kūṭasthaṃ kūṭavirjitam ॥ 2.42.28 ॥
sarvendriyacarābhāsaṃ prākṛtendriyavarjitam।
dikkālādyanavacchinnaṃ nityaṃ cinmātramavyayam ॥ 2.42.29 ॥
adhyāstaṃ sarpavadyatra viśvametatprakāśate।
viśvasminnapi cānveti nirvikāraśca rajjuvat ॥ 2.42.30 ॥
samyagvicāritaṃ yadvatphenormibudbudodakam।
tathā vicāritaṃ brahma viśvasmānna pṛthagbhavet ॥ 2.42.31 ॥
sarvaṃ brahmaiva nānātvaṃ nāstīti nigamā jaguḥ।
yasmādbhavanti brahmāṇḍa koṭayo na bhavanti ca ॥ 2.42.32 ॥
bhavetāṃ yā parā śaktiryadādhāratayā sthitā ॥ 2.42.33 ॥
yasminnidaṃ yataścedaṃ yenedaṃ yadidaṃ smṛtam।
yadajñānājjagadbhāti yasmin jñāte jaganna hi ॥ 2.42.34 ॥
asatyaṃ yajjaḍaṃ duḥkhamavastviti nirūpitam।
viparītamato yadvai saccidānandamūrttikam ॥ 2.42.35 ॥
jīve jāgrati viśvākhyaṃ svapne yattaijasaṃ smṛtam ।
suṣuptau prājñasaṃjñaṃ tatsarvāvasthāsu saṃsmṛtam ॥ 2.42.36 ॥
yaccakṣuṣāṃ cakṣuratha śrotrāṇāṃ śrotramasti ca।
tvak tvacāṃ rasanaṃ tasya prāṇaṃ prāṇasya yadviduḥ ॥ 2.42.37 ॥
buddhirjñānena ca prāṇāḥ kriyāśaktyā nirantaram।
yanneśiri samabhyetuṃ jñātuṃ ca paramārthataḥ ॥ 2.42.38 ॥
rajjāvahirmarau vāri nīlimā gagane yathā।
asadviśvamidaṃ bhāti yasminnajñānakalpitam ॥ 2.42.39 ॥
ghaṭāvacchinna evāyaṃ mahākāśo vibhidyate।
kāryopādhiparicchinnaṃ tadvadyajjīvasaṃjñikam ॥ 2.42.40 ॥
māyayā citrakāriṇyā vicitraguṇaśīlayā ।
brahmāṇḍaṃ citramatulaṃ yasmin bhittāvivārpitam ॥ 2.42.41 ॥
dhāvato'nyānatikrāntaṃ vadato vāgagocaram ।
vedavedāntasiddhāntairvinirṇītaṃ tadakṣaram ॥ 2.42.42 ॥
ityevaṃ śrūyate vede bahudhāpi vicārite ॥ 2.42.43 ॥
akṣarasyātmanaścāpi svātmarūpatayā sthitam।
paramānandasandoharūpamānanda vigraham ॥ 2.42.44 ॥
līlāvilāsarasikaṃ ballavīyūthamadhyagam।
śikhipicchakirīṭena bāsvadratnacitena ca ॥ 2.42.45 ॥
ullasadvidyudāṭopakuṇḍalābhyāṃ virājitam।
karṇopāntacarannetrakhañjarīṭamanoharam ॥ 2.42.46 ॥
kuñjakuñjapriyāvṛndavilāsaratilampaṭam।
adharāmṛta saṃsiktaveṇunādena vallavīḥ।
mohayantañcidānandamanaṅgamadabhañjanam ॥ 2.42.48 ॥
koṭikāmakalāpūrṇaṃ koṭicandrāṃśunirmalam।
trirekhakamṭhavilasadratnaguñjāmṛgā kulam ॥ 2.42.49 ॥
yamunāpuline tuṅge tamālavanakānane।
kadambacampakāśokapārijātamanohare ॥ 2.42.50 ॥
rādhāvilāsarasikaṃ kṛṣṇākhyaṃ puruṣaṃ param।
śrutavānasmi vedebhyo yatastadgocaro'bhavat ॥ 2.42.52 ॥
evaṃ brahmaṇi cinmātre nirguṇe bhedavarjite।
golokasaṃjñike kṛṣṇo dīvyatīti śrutaṃ mayā ॥ 2.42.53 ॥
nātaḥ parataraṃ kiñcinnigamāgamayorapi।
tathāpi nigamo vakti hyakṣarāt parataḥ paraḥ ॥ 2.42.54 ॥
golokavāsī bhagavānakṣarātpara ucyate।
tasmādapi paraḥ ko'sau gīyate śrutibhiḥ sadā ॥ 2.42.55 ॥
uddiṣṭo veda vacanairviśeṣo jñāyate katham।
śrutervārtho'nyathā bodhyaḥ paratastvakṣarāditi ॥ 2.42.56 ॥
vicārayāmāsa ciraṃ na prapede yathātatham ॥ 2.42.57 ॥
          sūta uvāca।।
vicārayannapi munirnāpa vedārthaniścayam।
vedo nārāyaṇaḥ sākṣādyatra muhyanti sūrayaḥ ॥ 2.42.58 ॥
tathāpi mahatīmārttiṃ satāṃ hṛdayatāpinīm।
punarvicārayāmāsa kaṃ vrajāmi karomi kim ॥ 2.42.59 ॥
paśyāmi na jagatyasminsarvajñaṃ sarvadarśanam।
ajñātvā'nyatamaṃ loke sandehavinivarttakam ॥ 2.42.60 ॥
meroḥ kuhariṇīṃ gatvā cacāra paramaṃ tapaḥ।
yatra kārttasvarakphūrjajjyotsnājālairnirantaram ॥ 2.42.61 ॥
sadā prabādhate viṣvaktamaḥstomaṃ dṛśantudam।
cakāste yatra paramaṃ kāntāramatisundaram ॥ 2.42.62 ॥
nānādrumalata ---tpakṣinināditam।
kṣutpipāsābhayakrodhatāpaglānivivarjitam ॥ 2.42.63 ॥
jātarūpaśilānaddhataṭasañcārapākṣa ॥ 2.42.64 ॥
yuktamambhoja pavanaiḥ sevyamānaṃ samantataḥ।
śivairadhyāsitambhāvairhiṃsraiḥ sattvaiḥ samujjhitam ॥ 2.42.65 ॥
nirjanaṃ divyalatikāpriyakhaṇḍavarājitam।
śūkaiḥ pārāvatai rhṛdyairunmadanmattakokilam ॥ 2.42.66 ॥
utpatatpadmarajasāṃ paṭalāmodadiṅ‌mukham।
tatrāpi kāñcinī divyā guhā paramaśobhanā ॥ 2.42.67 ॥
tāṃ praviśya jitā hāro jitacitto jitāsanaḥ।
sasmāra vedāṃścaturastadekāgramanā muniḥ ॥ 2.42.68 ॥
trayī jagāma śaradāṃ śatasya smarato'sya hi ।
prādurāsaṃstato vedāścatvāraścārudarśanāḥ ॥ 2.42.69 ॥
sphuratpadmapalāśākṣā jaṭāmukuṭadhāriṇaḥ।
kuśamuṣṭikarāmbhojā mṛgatvaṅmaṇḍitāṃsakāḥ ॥ 2.42.70 ॥
svaraiḥ ṣoḍaśabhiḥ klṛpta vadanāḥ praṇavāntarāḥ।
kacavargodbhavairvarṇaiḥ pañcāvayavapāṇayaḥ ॥ 2.42.71 ॥
pavargadakṣacaraṇā vāmapādāstavargataḥ।
ātarantyantavarṇāśca yeṣāṃ kukṣidvayātmakau ॥ 2.42.72 ॥
nābhinidrāḥ kāntapṛṣṭā modarā yaralavotkacāḥ।
agnidakṣāṃśarucirā dharāgrīvā bhṛtāsakāḥ ॥ 2.42.73 ॥
antasthasandhisaṃsthānā vaikharīvāgvijṛmbhitāḥ।
apaśyanmathurāmeṣāṃ hṛdayāmbhojakalpitām ॥ 2.42.74 ॥
harerbhagavataḥ sākṣādāvirbhāvasthalī hi sā।
kāśīmapaśyadbhrūmadhye māyāmādhārasaṃsthitām ॥ 2.42.75 ॥
liṅgadeśe tataḥ kāñcīmavantīṃ nābhimaṇḍale ।
kaṇṭhasthāṃ dvārakāmeṣāṃ prayāgaṃ prāṇagaṃ tathā ॥ 2.42.76 ॥
savyāpasavyayosteṣāṃ gaḍgā'pi yamunā nadī ।
madhye sarasvatī sākṣādgayākṣetraṃ tathānane ॥ 2.42.77 ॥
hanugrīvāmadhyagataṃ prabhāsakṣetramuttamam।
badaryyāśramameteṣāṃ brahmarandhre dadarśa ha ॥ 2.42.78 ॥
pauṇḍravardhananepālapīṭhaṃ nayanayoryuge।
pīṭhaṃ pūrṇagiriṃnāma lalāṭe samadṛśyata ॥ 2.42.79 ॥
kaṇṭe ca mathurāpīṭhaṃ kāñcīpīṭhaṃ kaṭisthitam।
jālandharaṃ tathā pīṭhaṃ stanadeśeṣvadṛśyata ॥ 2.42.80 ॥
bhṛgupīṭhaṃ karṇadeśe hyayodhyāṃ nāsikāpuṭe।
brahmarandhre sthitaṃ brāhyaṃ śaivaṃ sīmantasīmani ॥ 2.42.81 ॥
śāktaṃ jihvāgra dhiṣaṇaṃ vaiṣṇavaṃ hṛdayāmbuje।
sauraṃ cakṣuḥpradeśasthaṃ bauddhañchāyāsu saṅgatam ॥ 2.42.82 ॥
sautrāmaṇiṃ kaṇṭhadeśe paśubandhamathorasi।
vājapeyaṃ kaṭitaṭe hyagnihotraṃ tathānane ॥ 2.42.83 ॥
aśvamedhaṃ kaṭitaṭe naramedhamathodare।
rājasūyaṃ śirodeśe āvasathyaṃ tathā'dhare ॥ 2.42.84 ॥
ūrddvoṣṭe dakṣiṇāgniñca gārhapatyaṃ mukhāntare।
havyaṃ śrutau mantrabhedāṃstathā romasvavasthitān।
bhṛtyairiva mahārājaṃ purāṇairnyāyamiśritaiḥ ॥ 2.42.85 ॥
saṃhitābhiśca tantraiśca pṛthakpṛthagupāsitān।
karma jñānopāsanābhirjanānugrahakārakān ॥ 2.42.86 ॥
dṛṣṭvā suvismitamanā muniḥ kṛṣṇo babhūva tān ।
brahmatejomayāndivyāṃstapato'rkānivacyutān।
jlato'gnī nivodarkānkoṭīndusamadarśanān ॥ 2.42.87 ॥
vavande sahasotthāya daṇḍavatpatito muniḥ।
kṛtārtho'haṃ kṛtārtho'haṃ kṛtārtho'hamitīrayan ॥ 2.42.88 ॥
adya me saphalaṃ janma adya me saphalaṃ manaḥ।
adya me saphalañcāyuryadbhavanto'kṣigocarāḥ ॥ 2.42.89 ॥
alaukikaṃlaukikañca yat kiñcidapi vidyate।
na tadvo'viditaṃ vedyaṃ bhūta bhavyaṃ bhavacca yat ॥ 2.42.90 ॥
na pravṛttiphalā yūyaṃ darśayanto'pi tānsadā।
yadṛcchākarasaṅkocavidhānāyeha rāgiṇām ॥ 2.42.91 ॥
prapañcasyāpi mithyātve brahmatve vā nidhītarau।
na mṛṣārāgaviṣayau tatsaṅkocavidhikṣayau ॥ 2.42.92 ॥
ato lokahitairnūnaṃ paramārthanirūpaṇe।
svoktāḥ svargādiviṣayāḥ naśvarā iti ninditāḥ ॥ 2.42.93 ॥
adhikārivibhedena karmajñānopadeśataḥ।
trātaṃ sarvaṃ jagannūnaṃ śabdabrahmātmamūrttibhiḥ ॥ 2.42.94 ॥
ato'haṃ praṣṭumicchami bhavantaścetkṛpālavaḥ ।
karmaṇāṃ phalamādiṣṭaṃ sargaḥ kāmaikacetasām ॥ 2.42.95 ॥
īśārpitadhiyāṃ puṃsāṃ kṛtasyāpi ca karmaṇaḥ।
cittaśuddhistato jñānaṃ mokṣaśca tadanantaram ॥ 2.42.96 ॥
mokṣo brahmaikyamityevaṃ saccidānandameva yat।
sarvaṃ samāpyate tasmiñjñāte yaddhi kṛtākṛtam ॥ 2.42.97 ॥
yanniḥsaṅgaṃ cidākāśaṃ jñānarūpamasaṃvṛtam।
nirīhamacalaṃ śuddhamaguṇaṃ vyāpakaṃ smṛtam ॥ 2.42.98 ॥
vikāreṣu vinaśyatsu nirvikāraṃ na naśyati।
yathāndhatamasā vyāptalokasya ravirojasā ॥ 2.42.99 ॥
lohasyeva maṇistadvadaraṇiścānale yathā।
yadābhāsena sā sattāṃ pratipadya vijṛmbhate ॥ 2.42.100 ॥
jīveśvarādirūpeṇa viśvākāreṇa cāpyaho।
tasyāmapi pralīnāyāṃ kūṭasthañca yadekalam ॥ 2.42.101 ॥
bhavadbhirevaṃ nirṇītaṃ tattathaiva na saṃśayaḥ।
tathāpi mama jijñāsā varttate kevalaṃ hṛdi ॥ 2.42.102 ॥
ato'pi paramaṃ kiñcidvarttate kila vā na vā।
tadvadantu mahābhāgā bhavantastattvadarśanāḥ ॥ 2.42.103 ॥
yacchravaḥ phalameveha januṣo me kṛtārthatā।
evaṃ bruvantamanaghaṃ vyāsaṃ satyavatīsutam।
sādhu sādhviti saṅkīrtya pratyūcu rnigamā vacaḥ ॥ 2.42.104 ॥
         vedā ūcuḥ ।।
sādhu sādhu mahāprājñā viṣṇurātmā śarīriṇām।
ajo'pi janma sampadya lokānugrahamīhase ॥ 2.42.105 ॥
anyathā te na ghaṭate saṃsārakarmmabandanam।
aspṛṣṭo māyayā devyā kadācijjñānagūhayā ॥ 2.42.106 ॥
bibharṣi svecchayā rūpaṃ svecchayaiva nigūhase।
asmatsammata evārtho bhavatā sampradarśitaḥ ॥ 2.42.107 ॥
purāṇeṣvitihāseṣu sūtreṣvapi ca naikadhā।
tasyātmano'pyātma bhāvatayā puṣpasya gandhavat।
rasavadvā sthitaṃ rūpamavehi paramaṃ hi tat ॥ 2.42.109 ॥
anubhūtaṃ tadasmābhirjāte prākṛtike laye।
akṣarātparatastasmādyatparaṃ kevalo rasaḥ ।
na ca tatra vayaṃ śaktāḥ śabdātīte tadātmakāḥ ॥ 2.42.110 ॥


## 2.43

।।vāyuruvāca।।
yacchrutvā sarvvapāpebhyo mucyate nātrasaṃśayaḥ ॥ 2.43.1 ॥

           ।।sūta uvāca।।
sanakādyairmmahābhāgairdevarṣiḥ sa ca nāradaḥ।
sanatkumāraṃ papraccha praṇamya vidhipūrvvakam ॥ 2.43.2 ॥

      nārada uvāca।।
sanatkumāra me brūhi tīrthaṃ tīrthottamottamam।

sanatkumāra uvāca।।
vakṣye tīrthavaraṃ puṇyaṃ śrāddhādau sarvatārakam।
gayātīrthaṃ sarvadeśe tīrthebhyo'pyadhikaṃ śrṛṇu ॥ 2.43.4 ॥

gayāsurastapastepe brahmaṇā kratave'rthitaḥ।
prāptasya tasya śirasi śilāṃ dharmo hyadhārayat ॥ 2.43.5 ॥

tatra brahmā'karodyāgaṃ sthitaścāpi gadādharaḥ।
phalgutīrthādirūpeṇa niścalārthamaharniśam।
gayāsurasya viprendrabrahmādyairdaivataiḥ saha ॥ 2.43.6 ॥

kṛtayajño dadau brahmā brāhmaṇebhyo gṛhādikam।
svetakalpe tu vārāhe gayāyāgamakārayat ॥ 2.43.7 ॥

gayānāmnā gayā khyātā kṣetraṃ brahnamabhikāṃkṣitam।


no cetpañcadaśāhaṃ vā saptarātriṃ trirātrikam ॥ 2.43.10 ॥

piṇḍaṃ dadyācca pitrāderātmano'pi tilairvinā ॥ 2.43.11 ॥


ātmajo'pyanyajo vāpi gayābhūmau yadā tadā।


vāsena kiṃ kurukṣetre yadi putro gayāṃ vrajet ॥ 2.43.15 ॥

gayāyāṃ sarvvakāleṣu piṇḍaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ।
adhimāse janmadine cāste'pi guruśukrayoḥ ॥ 2.43.16 ॥

na tyaktavyaṃ gayāśrāddhaṃ siṃhasthe'pi bṛhaspatau।
tathā daivapramādena prahateṣu vraṇeṣu ca।
punaḥ karmmādhikārī ca śrāddhakṛdbrahmalokabhāk ॥ 2.43.17 ॥

durlabhaṃ kiṃ punarnnityamasminneva vyavasthitiḥ ॥ 2.43.18 ॥

pramādānmriyate kṣetre brahmādermuktidāyake।

kīṭakādimṛtānāñca pitṝṇāṃ tāraṇāya ca।
tasmātsarvvaprayatnena karttavyaṃ suvicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥ 2.43.20 ॥

brahmaprakalpitānviprānhavyakavyādinā'rccayet।


daṇḍaṃpradarśayedbhikṣurgayāṃ gatvā na piṇḍadaḥ।
daṇḍaṃ nyastā viṣṇupade pitṛbhiḥ saha mucyate ॥ 2.43.23 ॥

na daṇḍī kilbiṣaṃ dhatte puṇyaṃ vā paramārthataḥ।
ataḥ sarvvāṃ kriyāṃ tyaktvā viṣṇuṃ dhyāyati bhāvukaḥ ॥ 2.43.24 ॥

saṃnyasetsarvvakarmmāṇi vedamekaṃ na saṃnyaset।


tanmadhye sarvvatīrthāni trailokye yāni santi vai।
śrāddhakṛdyo gayākṣetre pitṝṇāmanṛṇo hi saḥ ॥ 2.43.27 ॥

śirasi śrāddhakṛdyastu kulānāṃ śatamuddharet।

pade pade'śvamedhasya yatphalaṃ gacchato gayām।
tatphalañca bhavennūnaṃ samagraṃ nātra saṃśayaḥ ॥ 2.43.29 ॥

pāyasenāpi caruṇā saktunā piṣṭakena vā।
taṇḍulaiḥ phalamūlādyairgayāyāṃ piṇḍapātanam ॥ 2.43.30 ॥

tilakalkena khaṇḍena guḍena saghṛtena vā।
kevalenaiva dadhnā vā ūrjena madhunā'tha vā ॥ 2.43.31 ॥

piṇyākaṃ saghṛtaṃ khaṇḍaṃ pitṛbhyo'kṣayamityuta।
ijyate vārttavaṃ bhojyaṃ haviṣyānnaṃ munīritam ॥ 2.43.32 ॥

ekataḥ sarvvavastūni rasavanti madhūni hi।
smṛtvā gadādharāṅaghryabjaṃ phalgutīrthāmbu caikataḥ ॥ 2.43.33 ॥

piṇḍāsanaṃ piṇḍadānaṃ punaḥ pratyavanejanam।
dakṣiṇā cānna saṅkalpastīrthaśrāddheṣvayaṃ vidhiḥ ॥ 2.43.34 ॥

nāvāhanaṃ na digbandho na doṣo dṛṣṭisambhavaḥ।
sakāruṇyena karttavyaṃ tīrthaśrāddhaṃ vicakṣaṇaiḥ ॥ 2.43.35 ॥

anyatrāvāhitāḥ kāle pitaro yāntyamuṃ prati।
tīrthe sadā vasantyete tasmādāvāhanaṃ na hi ॥ 2.43.36 ॥

tīrthaśrāddhaṃ prayacchadbhiḥ puruṣaiḥ phalakāṅkṣibhiḥ।
kāmaṃ krodhaṃ tathā lobhaṃ tyaktvā kāryyā kriyā'niśam ॥ 2.43.37 ॥

brahmacāryyakabhojī ca bhūśāyī satyavākchuciḥ।
sarvvabhūtahite raktaḥ sa tīrthaphalamaśnute ॥ 2.43.38 ॥

tīrthānyanusarandhīraḥ pāṣaṇḍaṃ pūrvvatastyajet।
pāṣaṇḍhaḥ sa ca vijñeyo yo bhavetkāmakārataḥ ॥ 2.43.39 ॥

tīrtheṣu ye narā dhīrāḥ karmma kurvvanti tadghatāḥ।
yathā brahmavido vedyaṃ vastu cānanyacetasaḥ।
praviśanti pareśākhyaṃ brahma brahmaparāyaṇāḥ ॥ 2.43.40 ॥

snāto godo vaitaraṇyāṃ triḥsaptakulamuddharet ॥ 2.43.41 ॥

tathā'kṣayavaṭaṃ gatvā viprānsantoṣayiṣyati।
brahmaprakalpitānviprānhavyakavyādinā'rccayet।

sānnidhyaṃ sarvvatīrthānāṃ gayātīrthaṃ tato varam ॥ 2.43.43 ॥

mīne meṣe sthite sūryye kanyāyāṃ kārmuke ghaṭe।

makare varttamāne ca grahaṇe candrasūryyayoḥ ।
durllabhaṃ triṣu lokeṣu gayāśrāddhaṃ sudurllabham ॥ 2.43.45 ॥

na tacchakyaṃ mayā vaktuṃ kalpakoṭiśatairapi ॥ 2.43.46 ॥


## 2.44

nārada uvāca।।
gayāsuraḥ kathaṃ jātaḥ kiṃprabhāvaḥ kimātmakaḥ ।
tapastaptaṃ kathaṃ tena kathaṃ dehapavitratā ॥ 2.44.1 ॥

        sanatkumāra uvāca।।
prajāḥ sasarja saṃproktaḥ pūrvvaṃ devena viṣṇunā ॥ 2.44.2 ॥

āsureṇaiva bhāvena hyasurānasṛjatpurā।
saumanasyena bhāvena devānsumanaso'sṛjat ॥ 2.44.3 ॥


sthūlaḥ ṣaṣṭiryojanānāṃ śreṣṭo'sau vaiṣṇavaḥ smṛtaḥ।
kolāhalagirivare tapastepe sudāruṇam ॥ 2.44.5 ॥

bahuvarṣasahasrāṇi nirucchvāsaṃ sthiro'bhavat।

brahmalokaṃ gatā devāḥ procuste'tha pitāmaham।
gayāsurādrakṣa deva brahmā devāṃstato'bravīt ॥ 2.44.7 ॥

vrajāmaḥ śaṅkaraṃ devā brahmādyāśca gatāḥ śivam।
kailāse cābruvannatvā rakṣa deva mahāsurāt ॥ 2.44.8 ॥

brahmādyānabravīcchambhurvrajāmaḥ śaraṇaṃ harim।
kṣīrābdhau devadeveśaḥ sa naḥ śreyo vidhāsyati।
brahmā maheśvaro devā viṣṇuṃ natvā pratuṣṭuvuḥ ॥ 2.44.9 ॥

         devā ūcuḥ ।।

ॐ namo viṣṇavo bhartre sarvveṣāṃ prabhaviṣṇave।
rociṣṇave jiṣṇave ca rākṣasādigrasiṣṇave ॥ 2.44.10 ॥

dhariṣṇave'khilasyāsya yogināṃ pārayiṣṇave ।
varddhiṣṇave hyanantāya namo bhrājṣṇive namaḥ ॥ 2.44.11 ॥

            sanatkumāra uvāca।।
evaṃ stuto vāsudevaḥ surāṇāṃ darśanaṃ dadau।
kimarthamāgatā devā viṣṇunoktāstamabruvan ॥ 2.44.12 ॥

gayāsurabhayāddeva rakṣāsmānabravīddharim।
brahmādyā yāntu taṃ daityamāgamiṣyāmyahaṃ tataḥ ॥ 2.44.13 ॥

keśave garuḍārūḍho varaṃ dātuṃ gayāsure।
sarvve svaṃ svaṃ samāsthāya yayurvāhanamuttamam ॥ 2.44.14 ॥

ūcustaṃ vāsudevādyāḥ kimarthaṃ tapyate tvayā।
santuṣṭāḥ svāgatāḥ sarve varaṃ brūhi gayāsura ॥ 2.44.15 ॥

        gayāsura uvāca।।
sarvvadevadvijātibhyo yajñatīrthaśiloccayāt ॥ 2.44.16 ॥

devebhyo'tipavitro'hamṛṣibhyo'pi śivāvyayāt।
mantrebhyo devadevebhyo yogibhyaścāpi sarvvaśaḥ ॥ 2.44.17 ॥

nyāsibhyaścāpi karmmibhyo dharmmibhyaśca tathā punaḥ।
jñātibhyo'tipavitrebhyaḥ pavitraḥ syāṃ sadā surāḥ ॥ 2.44.18 ॥

pavitramastu taṃ devā daityamuktvā yayurdivam।
daityaṃ dṛṣṭvā ca spṛṣṭvā ca sarvve haripuraṃ yayuḥ ॥ 2.44.19 ॥

śūnyaṃ lokatrayaṃ jātaṃ śūnyā yamapurī hyabhūt।
yama indrādibhiḥ sārddhaṃ brahmalokaṃ tato'gamat ॥ 2.44.20 ॥

brahmāṇamūcire devā gayāsuravilopitāḥ।
tvayā datto'dhikāro vai gṛhāṇa tvaṃ pitāmaha ॥ 2.44.21 ॥

brahmābravīttato devānvrajāmo viṣṇumavyayam।
brahmādayo'bruvanviṣṇuṃ tvayā dattavare'sure ॥ 2.44.22 ॥

taddarśanādyayuḥ svargaṃ śūnyaṃ loka trayaṃ hyabhūt।
devairukto vāsudevo brahmāṇaṃ sa vaco'bravīt ॥ 2.44.23 ॥

gatvāsuraṃ prārthayasva yajñārthaṃ dehi dehakam।
viṣṇūktaḥ sasuro brahmā gatvā'paśyanmahāsuram ॥ 2.44.24 ॥

gayāsuro'bravīddṛṣṭvā brahmāṇaṃ tridaśaiḥ sahaḥ।
saṃpūjyotthāya vidhivatpraṇtaḥ śraddhayānvitaḥ ॥ 2.44.25 ॥

         ।।gayāsura uvāca।।
adya me saphalaṃ janma adya me saphalaṃ tapaḥ।
yadāgato'tithirbrahmā sarvvaṃ prāptaṃ mayādya vai ॥ 2.44.26 ॥

yoginyogāṅgavitsarvvalokasvāminpitarguro।
yadarthamā gato brahmanstatkāryyaṃ karavāṇyaham ॥ 2.44.27 ॥

       ।।brahmovāca।।
yajñārthaṃ na tu te tāni pavitrāṇi śarīrataḥ ॥ 2.44.28 ॥

tvayā dehe pavitratvaṃ prāptaṃ viṣṇuprasādataḥ।
ataḥ pavitraṃ dehaṃ tvaṃ yajñārthaṃ dehime'sura ॥ 2.44.29 ॥

       ।।gayāsura uvāca।।
dhanyo'haṃ deva deveśa yaddehaṃ prārthyate tvayā।
pitṛvaṃśaḥ kṛtārtho me dehe yāgaṃ karoṣi cet ॥ 2.44.30 ॥

tvayaivotpādito dehaḥ pavitrastu tvayā kṛtaḥ।
sarvveṣāmupakārāya yāgo'vaśyaṃ bhavatviti ॥ 2.44.31 ॥

ityuktvā so'patadbhūmau śvetakalpe gayāsuraḥ।
naiṛtīṃ diśamāśritya tadā kolāhale girau ॥ 2.44.32 ॥

śiraḥ kṛtvottare daityaḥ pādau kṛtvā tu dakṣiṇe।
brahmā sambhṛtasambhāro mānasānṛtvijo'sṛjat ॥ 2.44.33 ॥

agniśarmāṇammṛtaṃ śaunakaṃ yāñjaliṃ mṛdum।
kumuthiṃ veda kauṇḍilyaṃ hārītaṃ kāśyapaṃ kṛpam ॥ 2.44.34 ॥

gargaṃ kauśikavāsiṣṭau muniṃ bhārgavamavyayam।
vṛddhaṃ pārāśaraṃ kaṇvaṃ māṇḍavyaṃ śrutikevalam ॥ 2.44.35 ॥

śvetaṃ sutālaṃ damanaṃ suhotraṃ kaṅkameva ca।

dadhipañcamukhaṃ vipramṛṣabhaṃ karkameva ca।
kātyāyanaṃ gobhilañca munimugramagāvratam ॥ 2.44.37 ॥

supālakaṃ gautamañca tathā vedaśirovratam।
jaṭāmālinamavyagraṃ cāṭuhāsañca dāruṇam ॥ 2.44.38 ॥

ātreyaṃ cāpyaṅgirasamaupamanyuṃ mahā vratam।

etānanyāṃśca viprendrānvedhā lokapitāmahaḥ ।
parikalpyākarodyāgaṃ gayāsuraśarīrake ॥ 2.44.40 ॥

agniśarmāpi pañcāgnīnmukhādetānathāsṛjat।

satyāvasathyau devarṣe yeṣu yajñāḥ pratiṣṭitāḥ।

hutvā pūrṇāhutiṃ brahnā snātvā cāvabhṛthena tu।
yajñayūpaṃ suraiḥ sārddhaṃ samānīya vyaropayat ॥ 2.44.43 ॥

brahmaṇaḥ sahasā śreṣṭe sarasyevāśritaṃ śubham।
calitaścakito brahmā dharmmarājamabhāṣata ॥ 2.44.44 ॥

jātā gṛhe tava śilā samānīyāvicārayan।
daityasya śīghraṃ śirasi tāṃ dhāraya mamājñayā ॥ 2.44.45 ॥

niścalārthaṃ yamaḥ śrutvā dhārayanmastake śilām।
śilāyāṃ dhāritāyāntu saśilaścāsuro'calat ॥ 2.44.46 ॥

devānūce'tha rudrādīñchilāyāṃ niścalāḥ kila।
tiṣṭhantu devāḥ sakalāstathetyuktvā ca te sthitāḥ ॥ 2.44.47 ॥

devāḥ pādairlakṣayitvā tathāpi calito'suraḥ।
brahmātha vyākulo viṣṇuṃ gataḥ kṣīrābdhiśāyinam।
tuṣṭāva praṇato bhūtvā natvā cādṛtya taṃ prabhum ॥ 2.44.48 ॥

        ।.brahmovāca।।
brahmāṇḍasya pate nātha namāmi jagatāṃ patim।

viṣvakseno'bravīdviṣṇuṃ deva tvāṃ stauti padmajaḥ।
harirāhānaya tvaṃ taṃ viṣṇūktaḥ sa tamānayat।
ajamūce hariḥ kasmādāgato'si vadasva tat ॥ 2.44.50 ॥

       ।।brahmovāca।।
devadeva kṛte yoge pracacāla gayāsuraḥ।
śilāyāṃ devarūpiṇyāṃ nyastāyāṃ tasya mastake ॥ 2.44.51 ॥

rudrādiṣu ca deveṣu saṃsthiteṣvasuro'calat।
idānīṃ niścalārthaṃ hi prasādaṃ kuru mādhava ॥ 2.44.52 ॥

mūrttiṃ dadau niścalārthaṃ brahmaṇe bhagavānhariḥ ॥ 2.44.53 ॥

ānīya mūrttiṃ brahmāpi śilāyāṃ samadhārayat।
tathāpi calitaṃ vīkṣya punardevamathāhvayat ॥ 2.44.54 ॥

āgatya viṣṇuḥ kṣīrābdheḥ śilāyāṃ saṃsthito'bhavat।
janārddanābhidhānena puṇḍarīketi nāmataḥ।

niścalārthaṃ pañcadhāsīcchilāyāṃ prapitāpahaḥ।
pitāmaho'tha phalgvīśaḥ kedāraḥ kanakeśvaraḥ ॥ 2.44.56 ॥

brahmā sthitaḥ svayaṃ tatra gajarūpī vināyakaḥ।
gayādityaścottarārko dakṣiṇārkastridhā raviḥ ॥ 2.44.57 ॥

lakṣmīḥ sītābhidhānena gaurī ca maṅgalāhvayā।

indro bṛhaspatiḥ pūṣā vasavo'ṣṭau mahābalāḥ।
viśvedevāścāśvineyau māruto viśvanāyakaḥ।
sayakṣoragagandharvvāstasthurdevāḥ svaśāktibhiḥ ॥ 2.44.59 ॥

ādyayā gadayā cāsau yasmāddaityaḥ sthirīkṛtaḥ।
sthita ityeva hariṇā tasmādādigadādharaḥ ॥ 2.44.60 ॥

ūce gayāsuro devānkimarthaṃ vañcito hyaham।
yajñārthaṃ brahmaṇe dattaṃ śarīramamalaṃ mayā।

yatsuraiḥ pīḍito'tyarthaṃ gadayā hariṇā tathā।
pīḍito yadyahaṃ devāḥ prasannāḥ santu sarvadā ॥ 2.44.62 ॥

gadādharādayastuṣṭāḥ procuḥ sārddhaṃ gayāsuram।
varaṃ brūhi prasannāḥ smo devānūce gayāsuraḥ ॥ 2.44.63 ॥

yāvatpṛthvī parvatāśca yāvaccandrārkatārakāḥ।
anye ca sakalā devā mannāmnā kṣetramastu vai ॥ 2.44.64 ॥

tanmadhye sarvatīrthāni prayacchantu hitaṃ nṛṇām ॥ 2.44.65 ॥

snānāditarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā piṇḍadānātphalādhikam।
mahātmā vai sahasrañca kulānāṃ coddharennaraḥ ॥ 2.44.66 ॥

vyaktāvyaktasvarūpeṇa yūyaṃ tiṣṭhata sarvadā।
gadādharaḥ svayaṃ lokādbhūyātsarvāghanāśanāt ॥ 2.44.67 ॥

śrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍakaṃ yeṣāṃ brahmalokaṃ prayāntu te।

naimiṣaṃ puṣkaraṃ gaṅgā prayāgaścāvimuktikam।
etānyanyāni tīrthāni divi bhuvyantarikṣataḥ।
samāyāntu sadā nṝṇāṃ prayacchantu hitaṃ surāḥ ॥ 2.44.69 ॥

kiṃ bahūktyā suragaṇā yuṣmāsvekāpi devatā।
cenna tiṣṭedahaṃ cāpi samayaḥ pratipālyatām ॥ 2.44.70 ॥

gayāsuravacaḥ śrutvā procurviṣṇvādayaḥsurāḥ।
tvayā yatprārthitaṃ sarvvaṃ tadbhaviṣyatyasaṃśayam ॥ 2.44.71 ॥

asmatpādānarccayitvā yāsyanti paramāṃ gatim।
devairdattavaro daityo harṣito niścalo'bhavat ॥ 2.44.72 ॥

sthiteṣu caiva deveṣu brāhmaṇebhyo dadāvajaḥ।
grāmāṃśca pañcapañcāśatpañcakrośīṃ gayāṃ tathā।
gṛhānkṛtvā dadau divyānsarvvopaskarasaṃyutān ॥ 2.44.73 ॥

kāmadhenuṃ kalpavṛkṣaṃ pārijātādikāṃstarūn।
mahānadīṃ kṣīravahāṃ ghṛtakulyāstathaiva ca ॥ 2.44.74 ॥

madhuśravāṃ madhukulyāṃ dadhyādyāḍhyasarāṃsi ca।
suvarṇadīrghikāṃ caiva bahūnannādiparvvatān ॥ 2.44.75 ॥

na yācayadhvaṃ viprendrā anyānuktvā dadāvajaḥ ॥ 2.44.76 ॥

dattvā yayau brahmalokaṃ natvā hyādigadādharam ।
dharmmāraṇye tatra dharmmaṃ yājayitvā yayācire ॥ 2.44.77 ॥

dharmmayāge ca lobhādvai pratigṛhya dhanādikam।
tato brahmā samāgatya brāhmaṇāṃstāñchaśāpa ha ॥ 2.44.78 ॥

kṛtavanto yato lobhaṃ maddatteṣvakhileṣvapi।
tasmādṛṇādhikā yūyaṃ bhaviṣyanti sadā dvijaḥ ॥ 2.44.79 ॥

nadyādayo vārivahā mṛnmayāśca yathā gṛhāḥ ॥ 2.44.80 ॥

kāmadhenuḥ kalpavṛkṣo mallokamupatiṣṭhatām।
evaṃ śaptā brahmaṇā te prārthayanto'bruvannajam ॥ 2.44.81 ॥

tvayā yaddattamakhilaṃ tatsarvaṃ śāpato gatam।
jīvanārthaṃ prasādaṃ no bhagavānkarttumarhasi ॥ 2.44.82 ॥

tacchrutvā brāhmaṇānbrahmā provācedaṃ dayānvitaḥ।


ākrāntaṃ daityajaṭharaṃ dharmmeṇa virajādriṇā।
nābhikūpasamīpe tu devī yā virajā sthitā।
tatra piṇḍādikaṃ kṛtvā triḥsaptakulamuddharet ॥ 2.44.85 ॥

mahendragiriṇā tasya kṛtau pādau suniścalau।
itatra piṇḍādikṛtsapta kulānyuddharate naraḥ ॥ 2.44.86 ॥


## 2.45

।।nārada uvāca।।
kathaṃ śilā samutpannā yayākrānto gayāsuraḥ ।
kiṃ rūpaṃ kiṃ ca māhātmyaṃ tasyāḥ kiṃ vada nāma ca ॥ 2.45.1 ॥

           ।ṣanatkumāra uvāca।।
viśvarupā ca tatpatnī bharttṛvrataparāyaṇā ॥ 2.45.2 ॥

tasyāṃ dharmmavratāyāstu kanyā dharmmavratā satī।

tasyāṃ ye tu guṇā hyāsaṃste tiṣṭhanti jagatrtraye।
dharmmo dharmmavratāyāstu triṣu lokeṣu mārgayan ॥ 2.45.4 ॥

nānu rūpaṃ varaṃ lebhe dharmmo'tha varasiddhaye।
tapaḥ kuru varārthaṃ tvaṃ tathetyuktvā vanaṃ yayau ॥ 2.45.5 ॥

kanyā sā ca tapastepe sarvveṣāṃ duṣkarañca yat।
vāyubhakṣā śvetakalpe yugānāmayutaṃ purā ॥ 2.45.6 ॥

paryyaṭanpṛthivīṃ sarvvāṃ kanyāratnaṃ dadarśa saḥ ॥ 2.45.7 ॥

rūpayauvanasampannāṃ parame tapasi sthitām।
papracchātha marīcistāṃ kā tvaṃ kasyāpi tadvada ॥ 2.45.8 ॥

rūpeṇānena māṃ bhīru vimohayasi suvrate ।
brahmātmajo'haṃ vikhyāto marīcirvvedapāragaḥ ॥ 2.45.9 ॥

ahaṃ dharmmavratā nāma dharmmaputrī tapo'nvitā ॥ 2.45.10 ॥

pativratārthaṃ viprendra carāmi paramaṃ tapaḥ।
dharmmavratāṃ marīcistāmuvāca prītipūrvvkam ॥ 2.45.11 ॥

pativratā darśanānme bhaviṣyasi śubhavrate।
pativratecchayā pṛthvīṃ vicarāmi hyaharniśam ॥ 2.45.12 ॥

tvañcetpativratā jātā bhaje tvāṃ bhaja māṃ varam।
loke na tvādṛśī kanyā mama tulyo na te varaḥ ॥ 2.45.13 ॥

dharmmavrate dharmmapatnī tasmāttvaṃ bhava me'dhunā।
dharmmavratā muniṃ prāha dharmmaṃ yācaya suvrata ॥ 2.45.14 ॥

tacchrutvā dharmmamagamanmuniṃ dharmo dadarśa ha।
tejaḥpuñjaṃ varaṃ natvā āsanārghyādinārccayat ॥ 2.45.15 ॥

kimartamāgataḥ pṛṣṭo marīcirdharmmamabravīt।
kanyārthaṃ bhramatā pṛthvī dṛṣṭā te kanyakā varā ।
mahyaṃ kanyāñca tāṃ dehi śreyastava bhaviṣyati ॥ 2.45.16 ॥

arghyādinā samabhyarcya dharmmaṃ proce tatheti tam।
dharmmavratāṃ samānīya dattavāṃstāṃ marīcaye ॥ 2.45.17 ॥

varaṃ ca dattavāṃstasmai tadvākyaṃ yattathā kṛtam।
agnihotreṇa sahitāṃ svāśramaṃ tāṃ dvijo'nayat ॥ 2.45.18 ॥

reme munistayā sārddhaṃ yathā viṣṇuḥ śriyā saha।
pārvvatyā ca yathā śambhuḥ sarasvatyā yathā hyajaḥ ॥ 2.45.19 ॥

jajñe putraśataṃ tasyāṃ marīcerviṣṇunopamam।
marīciḥ phalapuṣpārthaṃ vanaṃ gatvā samāgataḥ ॥ 2.45.20 ॥

śrāntaḥ kadācittāṃ patnīmuvāceti pativratām।

dharmmavratā tathetyuktvā śayanasthasya sā muneḥ।
pādasaṃvāhanaṃ cakre ghṛte nābhyajya tatparā ॥ 2.45.22 ॥

nidrāyamāṇe'tha munau brahmā taṃ deśamāgataḥ।
ityeṣa dṛṣṭvā brahmāṇaṃ manasārccayituṃ prabhum ॥ 2.45.23 ॥

ityākulā samuttasthau matvā sā taṃ gurorgurum ॥ 2.45.24 ॥

arghyapādyādikaṃ dattvā brahmāṇaṃ samapūjayat।
satkṛtāyāntu śayyāyāṃ viśrāmamakarodajaḥ ॥ 2.45.25 ॥

etasminnantare bharttā samuttasthau ca talpataḥ।
dharmmavratāmapaśyansa vipraḥ kruddhaḥ śaśāpa tām ॥ 2.45.26 ॥

pādasaṃvāhanaṃ tyaktvāyasmādājñāṃ vihāya me ।
gatānyatra tataḥ pāpācchāpadagdhā śilā bhava ॥ 2.45.27 ॥

bhartrā dharmmavratā śaptā marīciṃ prāha sā ruṣā।
śayāne tvayi saṃprāpte brahmā tvajjanako guruḥ ॥ 2.45.28 ॥

tvayotthāya hi karttavyaṃ svaguroḥ pūjanaṃ sadā।
mayā tu dharmmacāriṇyā tava kāryye kṛte mune ॥ 2.45.29 ॥

adoṣāhaṃ yataḥ śaptā tasmācchāpaṃ dadāmi te।
tvañca śāpaṃ mahādevādbharttaḥ prāpsyasyasaṃśayam ॥ 2.45.30 ॥

vyākulaṃ taṃ patiṃ dṛṣṭvā vyākulāgātprajāpatim।
natvā śayānaṃ brahmāṇamagniṃ prajvālya cendhanaiḥ ॥ 2.45.31 ॥

gārhapatye sthitā cakre tapaḥ paramaduṣkaram।
tathā śapto marīciśca tapastepe sudāruṇam ॥ 2.45.32 ॥

pativratāyāstapasā marīcestapasā yathā।
indrādayaśca santaptā gatāste śaraṇaṃ harim ॥ 2.45.33 ॥

ūcuḥ kṣīrāmbūdau suptaṃ santaptāstapasā hare।
pativratāyāśca munestrailokyaṃ rakṣa keśava ॥ 2.45.34 ॥

indrādīnāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā viṣṇurdharmmavratāṃ yayau।
etasminneva kāle tu prabuddho bhagavānajaḥ।
ūcurdharmmarvatāṃ devā agnisthāṃ tāṃ sakeśavāḥ ॥ 2.45.35 ॥

agnimadhye tapaḥ karttuṃ kasya śaktiḥ pativrate।
tvayā kṛtaṃ tatparamaṃ sarvvalokabhayaṅkaram ॥ 2.45.36 ॥

varaṃ varaya dharmmajñe asmatto yadabhīpsitam।
viṣṇvādīnāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā devāndharmmavratābravīt ।। 45.37
bhartṛśāpamaśaktāhaṃ nivarttayitumojasā।
datto marīcinā śāpo mahyaṃ sa hyapagacchatu ॥ 2.45.37 ॥

dharmmavratāvacaḥ śrutvā procuretāṃ surāḥ punaḥ ।
dharmmavrate dharmmaputri śāpo'yaṃ paramarṣiṇā ॥ 2.45.38 ॥

dattaste na nirākarttuṃ śakyo devadvijātibhiḥ।
tasmādanyaṃ varaṃ brūhi yato dharmmasya saṃsthitiḥ ॥ 2.45.39 ॥

bhavedvai triṣu lokeṣu vedoktasya śubhavrate।

bharttuḥ śāpānmocayituṃ na śaktāśca yadāmarāḥ।
mahyaṃ varaṃ prayacchadhvamevaṃvidhamanuttamam ॥ 2.45.41 ॥

śilāhaṃ hi bhaviṣyāmi brahmāṇḍe pāvanī śubhā।
nadīnadasarastīrthadevādibhyo'tipāvanī ॥ 2.45.42 ॥

ṛṣyādibhyo munubhyaśca mukhyadevebhya eva ca।
trailokye yāni liṅgāni vyaktāvyaktātmakānyapi।
tāni tiṣṭhantu maddehe tīrtharūpeṇa sarvvadā ॥ 2.45.43 ॥

tīrthānyapi ca sarvvāṇi nakṣatrapramukhāstathā।
tiṣṭhantu devāḥ sakalā devyaśca munayastathā ॥ 2.45.44 ॥

pañcāgnayaḥ kumārādyā bahurupeṇa saṃsthitāḥ ॥ 2.45.45 ॥

mūrttāmūrttasvarūpeṇa padarūpeṇa devatāḥ।
śilāyāṃ krośamātreṇa mūrttirūpāḥ sthitā bhuvi ॥ 2.45.46 ॥

tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvvalokaśca mahāpātakanāśinīm।
pūto dharmmādhikārī ca śrāddhakṛdbrahmalokabhāk ॥ 2.45.47 ॥

śilāsthiteṣu tīrthaṣu snātvā kṛtvātha tarpaṇam।
śrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍakaṃ yeṣāṃ brahmalokaṃ prayāntu te ॥ 2.45.48 ॥

gadādharo dṛśyatīrthaṃ sarvvatīrthottamottamam।
sthāsyanti ca mariṣyanti yāntu brahmapurīṃ narāḥ।

gaṅgāsāgarasaṃjñañca nityaṃ tiṣṭhatu phalguni।
gadādharādhiṣṭitaṃ tatsarvvatīrthottamottamam।
muktirbhavet pitṝṇāṃ ca mṛtānāṃ śrāddhataḥ sadā ॥ 2.45.50 ॥

tyaktvā dehaṃ śilāyānte yāntu viṣṇusvarūpatām ॥ 2.45.51 ॥

yathārccite harau sarvve yajñāḥ pūrṇā bhavanti hi।
tathā śrāddhaṃ tarpaṇañca snānaṃ cākṣayamastviha ॥ 2.45.52 ॥

mama dehe sureśānāṃ ye japanti śrutādikam।
acireṇāpi te siddhāḥ siddhibhājo bhavantu vai ॥ 2.45.53 ॥

pitṝṇāṃ kulasāhasramātmanā sahitaṃ naraḥ।
śrāddhādinā samuddhṛtya viṣṇulokaṃ nayed‌dhruvam ॥ 2.45.54 ॥

yāvatyo hi saricchreṣṭā gaṅgādyāśca hradāḥ śubhāḥ।
samudrādyāḥ saromukhyā mānasādyāḥ sureśvarāḥ।
nṛṇāṃ śrāddhaṃ vidadhato muktaye nivasantu me ॥ 2.45.55 ॥

śarīreṇa samāyāntu kvacinno yāntu devatāḥ।
eko viṣṇustridhāmūrttiryāvatsaṅkīrtyate budhaiḥ ॥ 2.45.56 ॥

tāvacchilāyāṃ sarvvāṇi tīrthāni saha daivataiḥ।
sadā tiṣṭhantu munayo gandharvvāṇāṃ gaṇāśca ye ॥ 2.45.57 ॥

sarvvadevasvarūpā ca nāmeyaṃ devarūpiṇī।
yāvattiṣṭhati brahmāṇḍaṃ tāvattiṣṭhatu vai śilā।
mama dehe'śmarūpe ca ye japanti tapanti ca ॥ 2.45.58 ॥

juhotyagnau ca teṣāṃ vai tadakṣayyopatiṣṭhatām ।
akṣayantu bhavecchrāddhaṃ japahomatapāṃsi ca।
śilāparvvatarūpeṇa mayi tiṣṭhatu sarvvadā ॥ 2.45.59 ॥

pativratāvacaḥ śrutvā devāḥ procuḥ pativratām।
tvayā yatprārthitaṃ sarvvaṃ tadbhaviṣyatyasaṃśayam ॥ 2.45.60 ॥

gayāsurasya śirasi bhaviṣyasi yadā sthirā।
tadā pādādirūpeṇa sthāsyāmastvayi susthirāḥ।
varaṃ śilāyai dattvaivaṃ tatraivāntarddadhuḥ surāḥ ॥ 2.45.61 ॥


## 2.46

।।sanatkumāra uvāca।।
vakṣye śilāyā māhātmyaṃ śrṛṇu nārada muktidam।
yasyā gāyanti devāśca māhātmyaṃ munipuṅgavāḥ ॥ 2.46.1 ॥

śilā sthitā pṛthivyāṃ sā devarūpātipāvanī।

tasyāḥ saṃsparśanāllokāḥ sarve haripuraṃ yayuḥ।
śūnye lokatraye jāte śūnyā yamapurī hyabhūt ॥ 2.46.3 ॥

yama indrādibhirgatvā ūce brahmāṇamadbhutam।
adhikāraṃ gṛhāṇātha yamadaṇḍaṃ pitāmaha ॥ 2.46.4 ॥

yamamūce tato brahmā svagṛhe dhārayasva tām।
brahmokto dharmarājastu gṛhe tāṃ samadhārayat ॥ 2.46.5 ॥

yamo'dhikāraṃ svaṃ cakre pāpināṃ śāsanādikam।
evaṃvidhā gurutarā śilā jagati viśrutā ॥ 2.46.6 ॥

brahmāṇḍe ca yathā meru statheyaṃ devarūpiṇī ॥ 2.46.7 ॥

gayāsurasya śirasi gurutvāddhāritā yataḥ ।
ataḥ pavitrayoryogaḥ pitṝṇāṃ mokṣadāyakaḥ ॥ 2.46.8 ॥

pavitrayordvayoryoge hayamedha majo'karot।
bhāgārthamāgatāndṛṣṭvā viṣṇvādīnabravīcchilā ॥ 2.46.9 ॥

śilāsthitipratijñāṃ tu kurvantu pitṛmuktaye।
tathetyuktvā śilāyānte devā viṣṇvādayaḥ sthitāḥ ॥ 2.46.10 ॥

śilārūpeṇa mūrttyā ca padarūpeṇa devatāḥ।
mūrttāmūrtasvarūpeṇa sthitāḥ pūrvapratijñayā ॥ 2.46.11 ॥

daityasya muṇḍa pṛṣṭe tu yasmātsā saṃsthitā śilā।

ācchāditaḥ śilāpādaḥ prabhāsenādriṇā yataḥ।
bhāsito bhāskareṇeti prabhāsaḥ parikalpitaḥ ॥ 2.46.13 ॥

prabhāsaṃ hi vinirbhidya śilāṅguṣṭo vinirgataḥ।
aṅguṣṭotthita īśo'pi prabhāseśaḥ prakīrttitaḥ ॥ 2.46.14 ॥

śilāṅguṣṭaikateśo yaḥ sā ca pretaśilā smṛtā।
piṇḍadānādyatastasyāṃ pretatvānmucyate naraḥ ॥ 2.46.15 ॥

mahānadīprabāsādyoḥ saṅgame snānakṛnnaraḥ।
rāmo devyā saha snāto rāmatīrthaṃ tataḥ smṛtam ॥ 2.46.16 ॥

prārthito'tra mahānadyā rāma snāto bhaveti ca।


mantreṇānena yaḥ snātvā śrāddhaṃ kurvīta mānavaḥ।
rāmatīrthe piṇḍadastu viṣṇulokaṃ prayātyasau।
tathetyuktvā sthito rāmaḥ sītayā bharatāgrajaḥ ॥ 2.46.19 ॥

tvāṃ namasye'tra deveśaṃ mama naśyatu pātakam ॥ 2.46.20 ॥

mantreṇānena yaḥ snātvā śrāddhaṃ kuryātsapiṇḍakam ।
pretatvāttasya pitaro vimuktāḥ pitṛtāṃ yayuḥ ॥ 2.46.21 ॥

āpastvamasi deveśa jyotiṣāṃ patireva ca।
pāpa nāśaya me deva manovākkāyakarmajam ॥ 2.46.22 ॥

namaskṛtya prabhāseśaṃ bhāsamānaṃ śivaṃ vrajet।
tañca śambhuṃ namaskṛtya kuryādyamabaliṃ tataḥ ॥ 2.46.23 ॥

pitṛpiṇḍādikaṃ kṛtvā rāmaṃ saṃsthāpya tatra ca ॥ 2.46.24 ॥

rāmaṃ sītāṃ lakṣmaṇañca munīnsthāpitavānprabhuḥ।
bharatasyāśrame puṇye nityaṃ puṇyatamairvṛtam।

sthāpitaṃ dharmasarvasvaṃ lokasyāsya nidarśanāt।
mataṅgasya pade śrāddhī sarvāṃstārayate pitṝn ॥ 2.46.26 ॥

rāmeśvaraṃ praṇamyātha na dehī jāyate punaḥ ॥ 2.46.27 ॥

śilāyā jaghanaṃ bhūyaḥ samākrāntaṃ nagena tu।
dharmarājena saṃprokto na gaccheti nagaḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.46.28 ॥

yamarājadharmarājau niścalārthaṃ vyavasthitau।

dvau śvānau śyāmaśabalau vaivasvatakulodbhavau।

vāyasāḥ pratigṛhṇantu bhūyo piṇḍaṃ mayā sthitam ॥ 2.46.31 ॥

yamo'si yamadūto'si vāyaso'si mahābala।

rāme vanaṃ gate śailamāgatya bharatena hi।
pituḥ piṇḍādikaṃ kṛtvā rāmeśaḥ sthāpito'tra vai ॥ 2.46.33 ॥

snātvā natvā ca rāmeśaṃ rāmasītā samanvitam।
tatra śrāddhaṃ sapiṇḍañca kṛtvā viṣṇupuraṃ vrajet।
pitṛbhiḥ saha dharmātmā kulānāñca śataiḥsaha ॥ 2.46.34 ॥

rāmaṃ sītāṃ lakṣmaṇañca munīnsthāpitavānprabhuḥ।
bharatasyāśrame puṇye nityaṃ puṇyatamairvṛtam।

vahnirdvau varuṇau rudrāścatvāraḥ pitṛmokṣadāḥ।
bharatāśramamāsādya tānnametpūjayennaraḥ ॥ 2.46.36 ॥

pāpebhyaścopapāpebhyo mucyate pitṛbhiḥ saha।
yatra kutrāpi devarṣe bharatasyāśrame naraḥ ।
snātaḥ śrāddhādikaṃ kuryāttatkalpe'pi na hīyate ॥ 2.46.37 ॥

sarvamānantyamāhurvai yaddattaṃ bharatāśrame ॥ 2.46.38 ॥

caturyugasvarūpeṇa catasro ravimūrttayaḥ ।
dṛṣṭāḥ spṛṣṭāḥ pūjitāstāḥ pitṝṇāṃ muktidāyakāḥ ॥ 2.46.39 ॥

muktirvāṃmana ityeva tārakākhyo vidhiḥ paraḥ।
saṃsārārṇavataptānāṃ nāvāvetau sureśvarau।
tārakaṃ brahma viśveṣāṃ mṛtānāṃ jīvatāmidam ॥ 2.46.40 ॥

trivikramañca brahmāṇaṃ yaḥ paśyetpuruṣottamam।

śilāyā vāmapāde'pi tathābhyudyantako giriḥ ।
sthāpitaḥ piṇḍadastatra pitṝnbrahmapuraṃ nayet ॥ 2.46.42 ॥

naimiṣāraṇyapārśve tu īce brahmā suraiḥ saha।
mukhyasaṃjñaṃ hi tattīrthaṃ devāstatra pade sthitāḥ ॥ 2.46.43 ॥

triṣu teṣu padeṣveva tīrtheṣu munisattama।

tannaimiṣavanaṃ puṇyaṃ sevitaṃ puṇyapūruṣaiḥ।
tatra vyāsaḥ śukaḥ pailaḥ kaṇvo vedhāḥ śivo hariḥ ॥ 2.46.45 ॥

vāmahaste śilāyāstu tathā codyantako giriḥ ॥ 2.46.46 ॥

sa parvataḥ samānīto hyagastyena mahātmanā।
tatra brahmā haraścaiva tapaścograñca cakratuḥ ॥ 2.46.47 ॥

tatrāgastyasya hi varaṃ kuṇḍaṃ trailokyadurlabham।
yatra munyaṣṭakaṃ siddhaṃ tapastaptvā śivaṃ gatam।
kuṇḍe munyaṣṭakaṃ natvā pitṝnbrahmapuraṃ nayet ॥ 2.46.48 ॥

agastyenātha devarṣe hyudayādrermahātmanā।
śilāyā vāmahaste'pi sthāpito girirāṭ śubhaḥ।
vāditrādyairdivyagītairāḍhyo vāditrako giriḥ ॥ 2.46.49 ॥

sameto'dyāpi kītāni divyāni saha gīyate ॥ 2.46.50 ॥

mohanaśca sunīthaśca śailūjo mohanottamaḥ।
parvato nāradadhyānī saṅgītī puṣpadantakaḥ।
hāhā hūhūprabhṛtayo gītadānaṃ prajakrire ॥ 2.46.51 ॥

tathā citraratho nāma sarvagandharvasaṃvṛtaḥ।
gāyati madhurāṇyeva gītānyadrau mahotsavam ॥ 2.46.52 ॥

ataḥ sa parvato devaiḥ sevyate'dyāpi nityaśaḥ।
dharmajāstatra deveśo haro bhasmāṅgarāgavān ॥ 2.46.53 ॥

pārvatyā sahito rudraḥ parvate gītanādite।
modate pūjitodyeyaḥ pitṝṇāṃ paramāṃ gatim ॥ 2.46.54 ॥

gayāyāṃ paramātmā hi gopatirvā gadādharaḥ।
hīyate vaiṣṇavī māyā tathā rudrārccayā mune ॥ 2.46.55 ॥

śilāyā dakṣiṇe haste bhasmakūṭo girirdhṛtaḥ।
dharmmarājena tatrāste hyagastyaḥ saha bhāryyayā ॥ 2.46.56 ॥

agastyasya pade snātaḥ piṇḍado brahmalokagaḥ।
brahmaṇastu varaṃ lebhe māhātmyaṃ bhuvi durlabham ॥ 2.46.57 ॥

lopāmudrāṃ tathā bhāryyāṃ pitṝṇāṃ paramāṃ gatim।

agastyañca sabhāryyañca pitṝnbrahmapuraṃ nayet।
daṇḍinātha tapastepe sītādrerdakṣiṇe girau ॥ 2.46.59 ॥

vaṭo vaṭeśvarastatra sthitaśca prapitāmahaḥ।
tadagre rukmiṇīkuṇḍaṃ paścime kapilā nadī।
kapileśo nadītīre hyamāsomasamāgame ॥ 2.46.60 ॥


tatrāgnidhārā girivarādāgatodyantakādanu।
tatra sārasvataṃ kuṇḍaṃ sarasvatyā prakalpitam ॥ 2.46.62 ॥

śukrastatra sutaiḥ sārddhaṃ ṣaṇḍāmarkkādibhiḥ prabhuḥ।
tatra tatra munīndrāṇāṃ padeṣu munisattama।
śrāddhapiṇḍādikṛtsnātaḥ pitṝṃstārayate naraḥ ॥ 2.46.63 ॥

śilāyā vāmahaste'pi gṛdrakūṭo girirdhṛtaḥ।
gṛdhrarūpeṇa saṃsiddhāstapastaptvā maharṣayaḥ ॥ 2.46.64 ॥

ato girirgṛdhrakūṭastatra gṛdhreśvaraḥ sthitaḥ।
dṛṣṭvā gṛdhreśvaraṃ natvā yāyācchambhoḥ padaṃ naraḥ ॥ 2.46.65 ॥

tatra gṛdhre guhāyāñca piṇḍadaḥ śivalokabhāk ।
tatra gṛdhre vaṭaṃ natvā prāptakāmo divaṃ vrajet ॥ 2.46.66 ॥

ṛṇamokṣaṃ pāpamokṣaṃ śivaṃ dṛṣṭvā śivaṃ vrajet।
śūrakṣetrañca tatrāste piṇḍadaḥ svarnayetpitṝn ॥ 2.46.67 ॥

ādipālena giriṇā samākrāntaṃ śilodaram।
tatrāste gajarūpeṇa vighreśo vignanāśanaḥ।
taṃ dṛṣṭvā mucyate vighnaiḥ pitṝn brahmapuraṃ nayet ॥ 2.46.68 ॥

nitambe muṇḍapṛṣṭhasya devadāruvanaṃ tvabhūt।
muṇḍapṛṣṭāravindrādrī dṛṣṭvā pāpaṃ vināśayet।
gayānābhau suṣumnāyāṃ piṇḍadaḥ svarnayetpitṝn ॥ 2.46.69 ॥

śilāyā vāmapāde tu sthāpitaḥ pretaparvataḥ।
dharmarājena pāpebhyo giriḥ pretaśilāhvayaḥ ॥ 2.46.70 ॥

pādena dūre nikṣiptaḥ śilāyāḥ pādabhārataḥ।
gataḥ śilāyāḥ saṃsargātpretakūṭaḥ pavitratām ॥ 2.46.71 ॥

pretakuṇḍañca tatrāste devāstatra pade sthitāḥ।
tatra kuṇḍādikaṃ kṛtvā pretatvānmocayetpitṝn ॥ 2.46.72 ॥

pṛthaksthitāśca bahavo vighnakāriṇa eva te।
śrāddhādikāriṇāṃ nṝṇāṃ tīrthe pitṛvimuktaye।
pretā dhānuṣkarūpeṇa karagrahaṇakārakāḥ ॥ 2.46.73 ॥

pādāṅkitāṃ muṇḍapṛṣṭāṃ mahādevanivāsinīm।
tāṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvalokaśca muktaḥ pāpopapātakaiḥ ॥ 2.46.74 ॥

gayāśirasi puṇye ca sarvapāpairvivarjite।
gretādivarjitaṃ yasmāttato'tipāvanaṃ varam ॥ 2.46.75 ॥


vaikuṇṭo lohadaṇḍaśca gṛdhrakūṭaśca śoṇakaḥ।
atra śrāddhādinā sarvānpitṝnbrahmapuraṃ nayet ॥ 2.46.77 ॥

krauñcarūpeṇa hi munirmuṇḍapṛṣṭe tapo'karot।
tasya pādāṅkito yasmātkrauñcapādastataḥ smṛtaḥ ॥ 2.46.78 ॥

snāto jalāśaye tatra nayetsvargaṃ svakaṃ kulam।
baliḥ kākaśilāyāñca kākebhya ṛṇamokṣadaḥ ॥ 2.46.79 ॥

muṇḍapṛṣṭhasya sānau hi lomaśo lomaharṣaṇaḥ।
dvāvetau paramaṃ taptvā tapaḥsiddhiṃ parāṃ gatau ॥ 2.46.80 ॥

āhūtāstu saricchreṣṭā lomaśena mahānadī।

kāverī sindhurīrā ca candanā ca saridvarā।
vāsiṣṭī sarayūrgaṅgā yamunā gaṇḍakīndirā ॥ 2.46.82 ॥

mahāvaitaraṇī nāmnā nikṣarā ca divaukasaḥ ।
sāvavyalakanandā ca hyudīcī kankāhvayā ॥ 2.46.83 ॥

kauśikī brahmadā jyeṣṭā sarvasyāghavimocinī।
kṛṣṇavalvā carmmavatī dve nadyau muktidāyike ॥ 2.46.84 ॥

āhūte saritāṃ śreṣṭe lomahrarṣeṇa sāhasāt ।
tapasastu prabhāveṇa narmmadā munipuṅgava।
tāsu sarvāsu yaḥ snātvā piṇḍadaḥ svarnayetpitṝn ॥ 2.46.85 ॥

brahmayoniṃ praviśyātha nirgacchedyastu mānavaḥ।
paraṃ brahma sa yātīha vimukto yonisaṅkaṭāt ॥ 2.46.86 ॥

nikṣarāyāṃ puṣkariṇyāṃ snātaḥ śrāddhādikaṃ naraḥ।
kuryyātkrauñcapade divye niyamādvāsaratrayam।
sarvānpitṝnnayetsvargaṃ pañcapāpina eva ca ॥ 2.46.87 ॥

janārdano bhasmakūṭe tasya haste tu piṇḍadaḥ।
ātmano'pyathavānyeṣāṃ savyenāpi tilairvinā।
jīvatāṃ dadhisaṃmiśraṃ sarve te viṣṇulokagāḥ ॥ 2.46.88 ॥

yastu piṇḍo mayā dattastava haste janārdana।
yaduddiśya tvayā deyastasminpiṇḍo mṛte prabho ॥ 2.46.89 ॥

eṣa piṇḍo mayā dattastava haste janārdana।
antakāle gate mahyaṃ tvayā deyo gayāśire ॥ 2.46.90 ॥

janārdana namastubhyaṃ namaste pitṛmokṣada।
pitṛpate namaste'stu namaste pitṝūpiṇe ॥ 2.46.91 ॥


lakṣmīkānta namaste'stu pitṝṇāṃ mokṣado bhava ॥ 2.46.93 ॥

vāmajānu susaṃpātya natvā bhīmo janārdanam।
pitṛbhiḥ saha dharmātmā kulānāñca śatena ca ॥ 2.46.94 ॥

śilāyāṃ vyaktarūpeṇa vyaktāvyaktātmanā sthitaḥ।
lakṣmīśo vibhudhaiḥ sārddhaṃ tasmāddevamayī śilā ॥ 2.46.95 ॥


## 2.47

।ṇārada uvāca।।
kathaṃ vyaktasvarūpeṇa vyaktāvyaktātmanā sthitaḥ ॥ 2.47.1 ॥

kathaṃ gadā samutpannā yathā hyādigadādharaḥ।

           sanatkumāra uvāca।।
gado nāmāsuro hyāsīdvajrādvajrataro dṛḍhaḥ।
tapasā dāruṇenāsau brahmalabdhavaraḥ purā।
prārthito brahmaṇe prādātsvaśarīrāsthi dustyajam ॥ 2.47.3 ॥

brahmokto viśvakarmmāpi gadāṃ cakre'dbutāṃ tadā।
tadasthi vajraniṣpeṣaiḥ kundaiḥ svarge hyadhārayat ॥ 2.47.4 ॥

atha kālena mahātā manau svāyambhuve kvacita।
hetī rakṣo brahmaputraḥ tapastepe sudāruṇam ॥ 2.47.5 ॥

unmukhaścorddhvabāhuśca pādāṅguṣṭhabhareṇa ha ॥ 2.47.6 ॥

ekenātiṣṭadavyagraḥ śīrṇaparṇānilāśanaḥ।
brahmādīṃstapasā tuṣṭānvaraṃ vavre varapradān ॥ 2.47.7 ॥

devairdaityaiśca śastrairvividhairmanujādibhiḥ।
kṛṣṇeśānādicakrādyairavadhyaḥ syāṃ mahābalaḥ ॥ 2.47.8 ॥

tathetyuktvāntarhitāste hetirdevānathājayat।
indratvamakaroddhetirbhītā brahmaharādayaḥ ॥ 2.47.9 ॥

hariṃ te śaraṇaṃ jagmurūcurhetiṃ jahīti tān ।
ūce hariravadhyo'yaṃ hetirdevāsuraiḥ surāḥ ॥ 2.47.10 ॥

mahāstraṃ me prayacchadhvaṃ hetiṃ hanmi hi yena tam।
ityuktāste tato devā gadāṃ tāṃ haraye daduḥ ॥ 2.47.11 ॥

dadhāra tāṃ gadāmādau devairukto gadādharaḥ ।
gadayā hetimāhatya devaiḥ sa tridivaṃ yayau ॥ 2.47.12 ॥

gadāmādāvavaṣṭabhya gayāsuraśiraḥśilām।

śilāsau muṇḍapṛṣṭādriḥ prabhāso nāmaparvvataḥ ॥ 2.47.14 ॥

udyanto gītanādaśca bhasmakūṭo girirmmahān।
gṛdhra kūṭaḥ pretakūṭaścādipālo'ravindakaḥ ॥ 2.47.15 ॥

pañcalokaḥ saptaloko vaikuṇṭo lohadaṇḍakaḥ ।
krauñcapādo'kṣayavaṭaḥ phalgutīrthaṃ madhuśravāḥ ॥ 2.47.16 ॥

dadhikulyā madhukulyā devikā ca mahānadī।
vaitaraṇyādirūpeṇa vyakta ādigadādharaḥ ॥ 2.47.17 ॥

viṣṇoḥ padaṃ rudrapadaṃ brahmaṇaḥ padamuttamam।
kāśyapasya padaṃ divyaṃ dvau hastau yatra nirgatau ॥ 2.47.18 ॥

pañcāgnīnāṃ padānyatra indrāgastyapade pare।
raveśca kārttikeyasya krauñcamātaṅgayorapi ॥ 2.47.19 ॥

mukhyaliṅgāni sarvvāṇi vyaktāvyaktātmanā sthitaḥ।
ādyo gadādharaścaiva vyaktaḥ śrīmān gadādharaḥ ॥ 2.47.20 ॥

gayādityaścottarārko dakṣiṇārko'pi naimiṣaḥ ॥ 2.47.21 ॥

śvetārko gaṇanāthaśca vasavo'ṣṭau munīśvarāḥ।
rudrāścaikādaśaivātha tathā saptarṣayo'pare ॥ 2.47.22 ॥

somanāthaśca siddheśaḥ kaparddīśo vināyakaḥ।
nārāyaṇo mahālakṣmīrbrahmā śrīpuruṣottamaḥ ॥ 2.47.23 ॥

mārkaṇḍeyeśaḥ kauṭīśo hyaṅgireśaḥ pitāmahaḥ।
janārdano maṅgalā ca puṇḍarīkākṣa uttamaḥ ॥ 2.47.24 ॥

ityādivyaktarūpeṇa sthitaścādigadādharaḥ।
hetiryo rākṣasastasminhate viṣṇuḥ puraṃ gataḥ ॥ 2.47.25 ॥

brahmaṇā saha rudrādyaiḥ kārite niścale'sure ।
tuṣṭāvādyagadāpāṇiṃ vedhā harṣeṇa nirvṛtaḥ ॥ 2.47.26 ॥

           brahmovāca।।
gadādharaṃ vyapagatakālakalmaṣaṃ gayāgataṃ viditaguṇaṃ guṇātigam।
guhāgataṃ girivaragauragehagaṃ gaṇārccitaṃ varadamahaṃ namāmi ॥ 2.47.27 ॥

ahaḥśriyaṃ tridaśagaṇādisuśriyaṃ bhavaśriyaṃ ditibhavadāraṇaśriyam।
kaliśriyaṃ kalimalamardanaśriyaṃ gadādharaṃ naumi tamāśritaśriyam । 47.28 ।।

dṛḍhādṝḍhaṃ parivṛḍhagāḍhasaṃstutaṃ kāmādbhutaṃ sudṛḍhamarūḍhirūḍhigam।
tamāḍhyagaṃ dṛḍhaduritādyaḍhaukitaṃ svaḍhaukṛtaṃ dṛḍhataragotrasūktibham ॥ 2.47.28 ॥

videhakaṃ karaṇakalāvivarjitaṃ vijanmakaṃ divakaravedibhūṣitam।
gadādharaṃ dhvanimukhavarjitaṃ paraṃ namāmyahaṃ satata manādimīśvaraṃ harim ॥ 2.47.29 ॥

mano'tigaṃ matigativarjitaṃ paraṃ sadādvayaṃ stutiśirasi saṃstutaṃ budhaiḥ।
cidātmakaṃ kaligatakāraṇātigaṃ gadādharaṃ hṛdayagataṃ namāmi tam ॥ 2.47.30 ॥

            sanatkumāra uvāca।।
devaiḥ sārddhaṃ brahmaṇaivaṃ stutaścādigadādharaḥ ।
ūce varaṃ vṛṇīṣva tvaṃ varaṃ brahmā tamabravīt ॥ 2.47.31 ॥

śilāyāṃ devarūpiṇyāṃ na tiṣṭāmastvayā vinā।
sthāsyāmo'tra tvayā sārddhaṃ nityaṃ vyaktādirūpiṇā ॥ 2.47.32 ॥

evamastu śriyā sārddhaṃ sthitaścādigadādharaḥ।
lokānāṃ rakṣaṇārthāya jagatāṃ muktihetave।
suvyaktaḥ puṇḍarīkākṣo janārdana iti śrutaḥ ॥ 2.47.33 ॥

vedairagamyā yā mūrttirādibhūtā sanātanī।
suvyaktā śvetakalpe sā bhaviṣyati tathā punaḥ।
vārāhakalpe hyavyaktā vyaktimapyagamatpurā ॥ 2.47.34 ॥


ye drakṣyanti sadā bhaktyā devamādigadādharam।
kuṣṭharogādinirmmuktā yāsyanti harimandiram ॥ 2.47.36 ॥

ye drakṣyanti sadā bhaktyā devamādigadādharam।
te prāpsyanti dhanaṃ dhānyamāyurārogyameva ca ॥ 2.47.37 ॥

kalatraputrapautrādiguṇakīrttisukhāni ca।
śraddhayā ye namasyanti rājyaṃ brahmapuraṃ tathā।
bhuktvā vrajeyuḥ satataṃ puṇyapuñjaphalaṃ narāḥ ॥ 2.47.38 ॥

gandhadānena gandhāḍhyaḥ saubhāgyaṃ puṣpadānataḥ।
dhūpadānena rājyāptirdīpāddīptirbhaviṣyati ॥ 2.47.39 ॥

dhvajadānātpāpahāniryātrākṛdbrahmalokabhāk।
śrāddhapiṇḍaprado yastu viṣṇuṃ neṣyati vai pitṝn ॥ 2.47.40 ॥

śraddhayā ye namasyanti satotreṇādigadādharam।
stoṣyanti ca samabhyarcya pitṝnneṣyanti mādhavam।
śivo'pi parayā prītyā tuṣṭāvādigadādharam ॥ 2.47.41 ॥

         ।।śiva uvāca।।
avyaktarūpo yo devo muṇḍapṛṣṭādirūpataḥ ।
phalgutīrthādirūpeṇa namāmyādigadādharam ॥ 2.47.42 ॥

vyaktāvyaktasvarūpeṇa padarūpeṇa saṃsthitaḥ।
mukhaliṅgādirūpeṇa namāmyādigadādharam ॥ 2.47.43 ॥

avyaktarūpo yo devo janārdanasvarūpataḥ।
muṇḍapṛṣṭe svayaṃ jāto nāmāmyādigadā dharam ॥ 2.47.44 ॥

śilāyāṃ devarūpiṇyāṃ sthitaṃ brahmādibhiḥ suraiḥ।
pūjitaṃ satkṛtaṃ devaistaṃ namāmi gadādharam ॥ 2.47.45 ॥

yaṃ ca dṛṣṭvā tataḥ spṛṣṭvā pūjayitvā praṇamya ca ।
śrāddhādau brahmalokāptirnamāmyādigadādharam ॥ 2.47.46 ॥

mahadādeśca jagato vyaktasyaikaṃ hi kāraṇam।
avyaktajñānarūpaṃ taṃ namāmyādigadādharam ॥ 2.47.47 ॥

dehendriyamanobuddhiprāṇāhaṅkāravarjitam।
jāgratsvapnavinirmuktaṃ namāmyādigadādharam ॥ 2.47.48 ॥

nityānityavinirmuktaṃ satyamānandamavyayam।
turīyaṃ jyotirātmānaṃ namāmyādigadādharam ॥ 2.47.49 ॥

           ।।sanatkumāra uvāca।।
evaṃ stuto maheśena prīto hyādigadādharaḥ।
sthito devaḥ śilāyāṃ sa brahmādyairdaivataiḥ saha ॥ 2.47.50 ॥

saṃsthitaṃ muṇḍapṛṣṭādrau devamādigadādharam।
stuvanti pūjayantīha brahmalokaṃ prayāntu te ॥ 2.47.51 ॥


vandhyā ca labhate putraṃ vedavedāṅgapāragam।
rājā vijayamāpnoti śūdraśca sukhamāpnuyāt ॥ 2.47.53 ॥

putrārthī labhate putrānabhyarcyādigadādharam।
manasā prārthitaṃ sarvaṃ pūjādyaiḥ prāpnuyāddhareḥ ॥ 2.47.54 ॥


## 2.48

।।sanatkumāra uvāca।।
gayāyātrāṃ pravakṣyāmi śrṛṇu nārada muktidām।
niṣkṛtiḥ śrāddhakartṝṇāṃ brahmaṇā gīyate purā ॥ 2.48.1 ॥


tataḥ pratidinaṃ gacchetpratigrahavivarjitaḥ ॥ 2.48.3 ॥
